# Brown-Driver-Briggs Hebrew Lexicon

## ב (id: BDB00974)

ב, בּ, *Bêth*, 2nd letter; post B Heb. \= numeral 2 (and so in margin of printed MT); Bêth (with diaresis) \= 2000; no evidence of this usage in OT times.



## I בְּ (id: BDB00975)

I. בְּ, **prep.** in (Moab. בּ, Syr. ܒ, Ar. ب, Eth. በ፡) before tone\-syllables in certain cases (Ges§ 102\. 2\) בָּ, with suff. בִּי; בְּךָ ([Ex 7:29](https://ref.ly/Exod7:29), [2 S 22:30](https://ref.ly/2Sam22:30), [Psalm 141:8](https://ref.ly/Ps141:8) בְּכָה), in pause and fem. בָּךְ; בּוֺ (once, [Je 17:24](https://ref.ly/Jer17:24) Kt בֹּה), בָּהּ; בָּנוּ; בָּכֶם, \[בָּכֶן]; בָּהֶם, בָּם \[also בָּהֵ֫מָּה †[Ex 30:4](https://ref.ly/Exod30:4), [36:1](https://ref.ly/Exod36:1), Hb [1:16](https://ref.ly/Hab1:16)], בָּהֶן †[1 S 31:7](https://ref.ly/1Sam31:7), [Is 38:16](https://ref.ly/Isa38:16), Ez [42:14](https://ref.ly/Ezek42:14) \[15 times בָּהֵן (FrMM 235\), and thrice, [Lv 5:22](https://ref.ly/Lev5:22), [Nu 13:19](https://ref.ly/Num13:19), [Je 5:17](https://ref.ly/Jer5:17), בָּהֵ֫נָּה]. Prep. denoting properly *in*, Gk. ἐν, but applied in many derived and fig. significations. The senses expressed by בְּ are grouped by the Rabbis in three classes, בֵּית הַכְּלִי *Beth vasculi*, בֵּית הַדִּיבִּוּק וְהַנְּגִיָ עה *Beth coniunctionis et viciniae*, בֵּית הָעֵזֶר *Beth auxilii;* and the same arrangement may be followed here, though the limits between the three classes are not clearly defined, and they sometimes overlap one another.

 **I.** *In:* **1\.** strictly, of position *in* a place (which often is expressed more precisely by בְּקֶרֶב, בְּתוֺח), as בַּבַּיִת *in* the house, בָּעִיר *in* the city, בַּדּוּד *in* the pot, בָּאָרֶץ *in* the land, etc. constantly. Heb. idiom also says בָּהָר *in* the mountain [Ex 24:18](https://ref.ly/Exod24:18) etc., even in cases where we could hardly avoid saying *on*, as [1 K 11:7](https://ref.ly/1Kgs11:7), [19:11](https://ref.ly/1Kgs19:11): so בְּחֹרֵב [Dt 1:16](https://ref.ly/Deut1:16) al.; בְּראֹשׁ הָהָר [Ex 24:17](https://ref.ly/Exod24:17), [Psalm 72:16](https://ref.ly/Ps72:16). Preceded by a verb of motion (esp. עָבַר) בְּ \= *through*, as [Gn 12:6](https://ref.ly/Gen12:6) and Abram passed through בָּאָרֶץ *in* the land \= passed through it, [13:17](https://ref.ly/Gen13:17), [2 S 24:2](https://ref.ly/2Sam24:2) \+; *in* (\= *through*) a gate, [Is 62:10](https://ref.ly/Isa62:10), [Je 17:19](https://ref.ly/Jer17:19), [Mi 2:13](https://ref.ly/Mic2:13). Fig. to speak בְּאָזְנֵי *in* the ears of …; to be good (*or* evil, etc.) בְּעֵינֵי *in* the eyes of … **2\.** of presence in the midst of a multitude, *among*, [Ex 14:28](https://ref.ly/Exod14:28) there was not left בָּהֶם *among* them even one, [Lv 26:36](https://ref.ly/Lev26:36) [2 S 15:31](https://ref.ly/2Sam15:31) Ahitophel בַּקֹּשְׁרִים is *among* the conspirators, [2 K 18:5](https://ref.ly/2Kgs18:5) בְּכֹל מַלְכֵי יְהוּדָה. So בְּךָ *in* thee (of Israel, coll.) [Dt 7:14](https://ref.ly/Deut7:14), [15:4](https://ref.ly/Deut15:4), [15:7](https://ref.ly/Deut15:7), [18:10](https://ref.ly/Deut18:10), [23:11](https://ref.ly/Deut23:11), [28:54](https://ref.ly/Deut28:54) (diff. from עִמָּח *beside* thee [Lv 25:35](https://ref.ly/Lev25:35)).—Spec. **a.** of an individ., implying eminency among: [Jos 14:15](https://ref.ly/Josh14:15), [Je 46:18](https://ref.ly/Jer46:18) Tabor *among* the mountains, [49:15](https://ref.ly/Jer49:15), [Pr 30:30](https://ref.ly/Prov30:30), Ct [1:8](https://ref.ly/Song1:8) הַיָּפָה בַנָּשִׁים the fair one (\= the fairest) *among* women, [La 1:1](https://ref.ly/Lam1:1): cf. [Luke 1:42](https://ref.ly/Luke1:42). On [1 S 17:12](https://ref.ly/1Sam17:12) v. Dr. **b.** hence with some verbs, when the action refers to only a part of the object, as הִכָּהַ בְּ to smite *among*… i.e. to smite some of… (diff. from הִכָּה with accus.); הָרַג בְּ [Psalm 78:31](https://ref.ly/Ps78:31); נָשָא בְּ to bear *in*, i.e. to share in bearing, [Nu 11:17](https://ref.ly/Num11:17) al.; בָּנָה בְּ to build *in* or *at* [Zc 6:15](https://ref.ly/Zech6:15), [Ne 4:4](https://ref.ly/Neh4:4); עָשָה בְּ [Ex 5:9](https://ref.ly/Exod5:9); עָמֵל בְּ to labour *on* [Jon 4:10](https://ref.ly/Jonah4:10); אָכַל בְּ, שָׁתָה בְּ to eat or drink *of* Ju [13:16](https://ref.ly/Judg13:16), [Pr 9:5](https://ref.ly/Prov9:5); חָלַק בְּ to give a share *in* [Jb 39:17](https://ref.ly/Job39:17). **c.** specifying the parts of which a whole consists (esp. in P) [Gn 7:21](https://ref.ly/Gen7:21), [8:17](https://ref.ly/Gen8:17), [9:10](https://ref.ly/Gen9:10), [9:16](https://ref.ly/Gen9:16), [17:23](https://ref.ly/Gen17:23), [Ex 12:19](https://ref.ly/Exod12:19), [Nu 31:11](https://ref.ly/Num31:11), [31:26](https://ref.ly/Num31:26), [Ho 4:3](https://ref.ly/Hos4:3). **3\.** with ref. to the limits enclosing a space, *within:* [Ex 20:10](https://ref.ly/Exod20:10) בִּשְׁעָרֶיךָ *within* thy gates, [Is 56:5](https://ref.ly/Isa56:5) בְּחוֺמוֺתַי *within* my walls. **4\.** often pregn. with verbs of motion, when the movement *to* a place results in rest *in* it, *into:* after בָּא [Gn 19:8](https://ref.ly/Gen19:8), [Is 19:23](https://ref.ly/Isa19:23); נָתַן [Gn 27:17](https://ref.ly/Gen27:17); שָׁלַח *to send* [Lv 16:22](https://ref.ly/Lev16:22); *in among* [Jos 23:7](https://ref.ly/Josh23:7), [23:12](https://ref.ly/Josh23:12), [1 K 11:2](https://ref.ly/1Kgs11:2).—[Ho 12:7](https://ref.ly/Hos12:7) (an extreme case) שׁוּב בְּ to return (and rest) *in* thy God, [1 S 16:3](https://ref.ly/1Sam16:3) (unless לַזָּבַָח should be read, as v:[7](https://ref.ly/1Sam16:7)).—עַיִן בְּעַיִן (with) eye (looking) *into* eye † [Nu 14:14](https://ref.ly/Num14:14), [Is 52:8](https://ref.ly/Isa52:8); פָּנִים בְּפָנִים †[Dt 5:4](https://ref.ly/Deut5:4); בְּ ´עֵינֵי פְ the eyes of … are *upon*, both in favourable ([Dt 11:12](https://ref.ly/Deut11:12), [Psalm 101:6](https://ref.ly/Ps101:6)) and hostile ([Am 9:8](https://ref.ly/Amos9:8), [Jb 7:8](https://ref.ly/Job7:8)) sense. **5\.** applied to *time*, as [Gn 1:1](https://ref.ly/Gen1:1) בְּרֵאשִׁית *in* the beginning; [2:2](https://ref.ly/Gen2:2) בַּיּוֺם הַשְּׁבִיעִי *on* the seventh day; Ju [10:8](https://ref.ly/Judg10:8) בַּשָּׁנָה הַהִיא *in* that year; \& constantly. **6\.** of a state or condition, whether material or mental, *in* which an action takes place: so oft., בְּשָׁלוֺם *in* peace [Gn 15:15](https://ref.ly/Gen15:15); בַּצָּרָה *in* distress [Psalm 91:15](https://ref.ly/Ps91:15); הָלַח בְּתֻמּוֺ to walk *in* his integrity; [Ex 5:19](https://ref.ly/Exod5:19) בְּרָע *in* evil case. **7\.** בְּ introduces the *predicate*, denoting it as that *in* which the subj. consists, or *in* which it shews itself (the *Beth essentiae*,—common in Arabic, esp. with a ptcp. or adj. and in a negative sentence: Qor 2:79 وَمَ الَّهُ بِغَافِلِ and God (appears) not *as* one remiss; 2:7 وَمَ هُم بِمُوءمِنِينَ and they are not believers \[comp. French *en—en honnête homme*]; v. WAG ii. § 56 a): viz. **a.** a primary pred., [Ex 18:4](https://ref.ly/Exod18:4) the God of my fathers בְּעֶזְרִי was my help, [Psalm 146:5](https://ref.ly/Ps146:5), [Ho 13:9](https://ref.ly/Hos13:9) (rd. :כִּי מִי בְעֶזְרֶֽךָ with LXX SyrVer Che al.); [Psalm 68:5](https://ref.ly/Ps68:5) בְּיָהּ שְׁמוֺ his name consists in Yah, [Jb 23:13](https://ref.ly/Job23:13), [37:10](https://ref.ly/Job37:10). With the pred. in the pl. (as *pl. maj.*) [Psalm 118:7](https://ref.ly/Ps118:7) בְּעֹזְרָי ´י J. is my great helper, [54:6](https://ref.ly/Ps54:6) (v. Che), Ju [11:35](https://ref.ly/Judg11:35). **b.** a secondary pred., [Ex 6:3](https://ref.ly/Exod6:3) and I appeared unto them בְּאֵל שַׁדָּי *as* God Almighty, [Nu 26:53](https://ref.ly/Num26:53) בְּנַחֲלָה *as* an inheritance, [34:2](https://ref.ly/Num34:2), Ez [46:16](https://ref.ly/Ezek46:16), [47:14](https://ref.ly/Ezek47:14), [Dt 10:22](https://ref.ly/Deut10:22), [26:14](https://ref.ly/Deut26:14) I have not put away therefrom בְּטָמֵא *as* one unclean \= while unclean, [28:62](https://ref.ly/Deut28:62), [Is 40:10](https://ref.ly/Isa40:10) בְּחָזָק יָבוֺא he cometh *as* a strong one, [Psalm 35:2](https://ref.ly/Ps35:2) (where v. De) and rise up *as* my help, v:[16](https://ref.ly/Ps35:16), [39:7](https://ref.ly/Ps39:7), [55:19](https://ref.ly/Ps55:19), [Pr 3:26](https://ref.ly/Prov3:26), [Jb 36:32](https://ref.ly/Job36:32) (De). **c.** a pred. as accus., [Is 48:10](https://ref.ly/Isa48:10) I have refined thee וְלאֹ בְכָסֶף but not *as though* silver, Ez [20:41](https://ref.ly/Ezek20:41); [Nu 18:26](https://ref.ly/Num18:26), [36:2](https://ref.ly/Num36:2), [Jos 13:6](https://ref.ly/Josh13:6), [13:7](https://ref.ly/Josh13:7), [23:4](https://ref.ly/Josh23:4), Ez [45:1](https://ref.ly/Ezek45:1), [47:22](https://ref.ly/Ezek47:22), [47:22](https://ref.ly/Ezek47:22) (rd. יַפִּלוּ) [48:29](https://ref.ly/Ezek48:29) (v. LXX Co) all בְּנַחֲלָה (cf. **b.**), [Psalm 78:55](https://ref.ly/Ps78:55) \& allotted it בְּחֶבֶל נַחֲלָה *as* a line of inheritance (i.e. as a measured inheritance). [Is 26:4](https://ref.ly/Isa26:4) ´כִּי בְּיָהּ יהוה חוּר עוֺלָמִים י is different—for *in* Yah there is a rock of ages (cf. Qor 4:7, 4:47 there is sufficiency *in* God as a patron). **d.** in comparisons, [Psalm 37:20](https://ref.ly/Ps37:20) consume away בֶּעָשָׁן *in the form of, as,* smoke [78:33](https://ref.ly/Ps78:33); [102:4](https://ref.ly/Ps102:4), [Jb 34:36](https://ref.ly/Job34:36), [36:14](https://ref.ly/Job36:14). **II.** Denoting proximity—**1\.** *at, by* (not very common): [1 S 29:1](https://ref.ly/1Sam29:1) בָּעַיִן *by* the spring; Ez [10:15](https://ref.ly/Ezek10:15), [10:20](https://ref.ly/Ezek10:20) בִּנְהַר כְּבָר *by* the river Chebar ([1:3](https://ref.ly/Ezek1:3) עַל). **2\.** *on:* [Gn 8:20](https://ref.ly/Gen8:20), [Nu 23:2](https://ref.ly/Num23:2) בַּמִּזְבֵּחַ *on* the altar; Ju [8:21](https://ref.ly/Judg8:21) *on* the necks of the camels; [1 K 2:5](https://ref.ly/1Kgs2:5) al. בְּמָתְנָיו *on* his loins; [Is 59:17](https://ref.ly/Isa59:17) a helmet בְּראֹשׁוֺ *on* his head. **3\.** often with verbs of *touching, approaching, taking hold of, cleaving,* etc., as אָחַז, דָּבֵק, חָשַׁק, נָגַע, נָגַשׁ, קָרַב, פָּגַּע, תָּמַח, תָּפַש (see these words). **4\.** with words expressing or implying an act of hostility—**a.** *against:* [Gn 16:12](https://ref.ly/Gen16:12) יָדוֺ בַכֹּל וְיַד כֹּל בּוֺ his hand *against* all, and the hand of all *against* him; בּ ´הָֽיְתָה יַד פְּ [Dt 13:10](https://ref.ly/Deut13:10), [1 S 5:9](https://ref.ly/1Sam5:9), [18:17](https://ref.ly/1Sam18:17) \+ oft. Hence after verbs (q.v.) of *fighting* (נִלְחַם, רִיב), *going up* to invade (עָלָה), *being angry* (הִתְעַבֵּר, אָנַף, חָרָה אַף), *sinning* or *acting treacherously* (חָטָא, בָּגַד, מָרַד, מָרָה, מָעַל, פָּשַׁע, שִׁקֵּר, כִּזֵּב, כִּחֵשׁ), *testifying* (עָנָה, הֵעִיד), *mocking* (הִלְעִיג, הֵתֵל), *feeling loathing* (קוּץ), *rebuking* (גָּעַר: prop. to protest loudly), *speaking* (דִּבֶּר: [Nu 12:8b](https://ref.ly/Num12:8)), etc., \& even (an extreme case) [Ho 7:14](https://ref.ly/Hos7:14) יָסוּרוּ בִי they turn aside (so as to be) *against* me. In a weaker sense מַשְׁתִּין בַּקִּיר mingens *ad* parietem [1 S 25:22](https://ref.ly/1Sam25:22) al. **b.** *down to, upon* (*super* with accus.), esp. in such phrases as דָּמָיו בּוֺ [Lv 20:9](https://ref.ly/Lev20:9) al. his blood be *upon* him; דָּמוֺ בְראֹשׁוֺ his blood be *upon* his head [Jos 2:19](https://ref.ly/Josh2:19); בְּראֹשׁוֺ (´הֵשִׁיב י)שׁוּב Ju 8:57 (רָעָה), [1 K 2:33](https://ref.ly/1Kgs2:33) (דָּם), v:[44](https://ref.ly/1Kgs2:44) (רָעָה), [Psalm 7:17](https://ref.ly/Ps7:17) (עֲמָלוֺ); ´בְּראֹשׁוֺ נָתַן דֶּרֶח פּ [1 K 8:32](https://ref.ly/1Kgs8:32) \& oft. in Ez, as [9:10](https://ref.ly/Ezek9:10) [11:21](https://ref.ly/Ezek11:21). **III. 1\.** *With*—**a.** of accompaniment: [Nu 20:20](https://ref.ly/Num20:20) בְּעַם כָּבֵד *with* much people, [Jos 22:8](https://ref.ly/Josh22:8), Ju [11:34](https://ref.ly/Judg11:34), [1 K 10:2](https://ref.ly/1Kgs10:2), [2 K 5:9](https://ref.ly/2Kgs5:9), [Je 41:15](https://ref.ly/Jer41:15); [Ex 21:22](https://ref.ly/Exod21:22) and he shall give בִּפְלִלִים *with arbitrators* (arbitrators being employed), [Is 8:16](https://ref.ly/Isa8:16) :בְּלִמֻּדָֽי *with* my disciples, i.e. having them present; [Ex 8:1](https://ref.ly/Exod8:1), [8:13](https://ref.ly/Exod8:13), [Je 11:19](https://ref.ly/Jer11:19) עֵץ בְּלַחְמוֺ a tree *with* its sap; [1 K 19:19](https://ref.ly/1Kgs19:19) וְהוּא בִּשְׁנֵים הֶעָשָר and he *with* the 12th. **b.** often of what one takes or brings with one: [Gn 32:11](https://ref.ly/Gen32:11) בְּמַקְלִי *with* my staff I passed over Jordan, Ju [11:34](https://ref.ly/Judg11:34), [15:1](https://ref.ly/Judg15:1), [1 S 1:24](https://ref.ly/1Sam1:24), [Is 7:24](https://ref.ly/Isa7:24); [Mi 6:6](https://ref.ly/Mic6:6) al. קִדֵּם בְּ to go to meet *with;* בָּא בְּ to come *with* [Lv 16:3](https://ref.ly/Lev16:3), [Psalm 66:13](https://ref.ly/Ps66:13); [71:16](https://ref.ly/Ps71:16). (In Arabic this usage is developed more fully than in Hebrew, and اتَي بِ lit. *to come with,* دَهَبَ بِ lit. *to go away with,* are used idiomatically in the sense of *to bring,* and *to take away* respectively: WAG ii. § 56 b.) Hence בְּאֵין, בְּאֶפֶס, בִּבְלִי, בְּלאֹ \= *without.* **c.** of concomitant (or surrounding) conditions, as בְּחִפָּזוֺן *with* (or *in*) haste; בְּצֶדֶק *with* (or *in*) righteousness; בִּשְׁגָגָה *in* error; בִּתְרוּעָה וּבְקוֺל שׁוֺפָר [2 S 6:15](https://ref.ly/2Sam6:15); often in such phrases as בְּצִדְקָֽתְךָ [Psalm 31:2](https://ref.ly/Ps31:2) *in* thy righteousness; בְּחַסְדְּךָ *in* thy mercy [Ex 15:13](https://ref.ly/Exod15:13); —בְּאָשְׁרִי *with* my happiness! \= happy am I [Gn 30:13](https://ref.ly/Gen30:13); [Psalm 29:4](https://ref.ly/Ps29:4) the voice of ´י is בַּכֹּחַ *with* power \= is powerful; [Ex 32:12](https://ref.ly/Exod32:12) בְּרָעָה *with* evil purpose; [Psalm 73:8](https://ref.ly/Ps73:8) בְרָע *in* wickedness; [90:10](https://ref.ly/Ps90:10) בִּגְבוּרֹת *with* strength. **2\.** of the instrument or means: as **a.** בַּחֶרֶב *with* the sword [Ex 5:3](https://ref.ly/Exod5:3) etc.; בְּרַגְלַיִם *with* the feet [Is 28:3](https://ref.ly/Isa28:3); …בְּיַד *by* the hand of … (v. sub יָד); to stone בְּאֶבֶן or בָּֽאֲבָנִים *with* stones [Lv 20:2](https://ref.ly/Lev20:2), [Nu 14:10](https://ref.ly/Num14:10) etc.; שָׁתָה בְּ to drink *with* a cup [Gn 44:5](https://ref.ly/Gen44:5), [Am 6:6](https://ref.ly/Amos6:6) (cf. in Aram. [Dn 5:2](https://ref.ly/Dan5:2)); to cry בְּגָרוֺן *with* the throat [Is 58:1](https://ref.ly/Isa58:1); to burn בָּאֵשׁ *in* or *with* fire (oft.); to slay or to perish בָּרָעָב *through* hunger or בַּדֶּבֶר *through* pestilence [Ex 16:3](https://ref.ly/Exod16:3), [Je 21:9](https://ref.ly/Jer21:9) \+ oft. (cf. [Jb 27:15](https://ref.ly/Job27:15) בַּמָּוֶת יִקָּבֵָרוּ); to save *with* or *by* Ju [7:7](https://ref.ly/Judg7:7), [1 S 14:6](https://ref.ly/1Sam14:6). **b.** idiom., with certain verbs, as שָׁחַק בְּ to play *with* [Jb 40:29](https://ref.ly/Job40:29); עָבַד בְּ to labour *with* a person (as with an instrument), i.e. to use him as a slave [Ex 1:14](https://ref.ly/Exod1:14), [Je 22:13](https://ref.ly/Jer22:13), [27:7](https://ref.ly/Jer27:7) al.; עָשָה בְּ [Je 18:23](https://ref.ly/Jer18:23), [Ne 9:24](https://ref.ly/Neh9:24), [Dn 11:7](https://ref.ly/Dan11:7), [Est 1:15](https://ref.ly/Esth1:15), [2:11](https://ref.ly/Esth2:11), [3:11](https://ref.ly/Esth3:11), [6:6](https://ref.ly/Esth6:6); \& perh. דִּבֶּר בְּ (of God) to speak *with* one [Nu 12:2](https://ref.ly/Num12:2), [12:8a](https://ref.ly/Num12:8) al. (v. Ew§ 217 f. (3\); DrSm ii. 23, 2\). Further בַּבַּעַל, הִתְנַבֵּא ביהוה to prophesy *with* or *by* ´י or Baal, ´י or Baal being the inspirer; שָׁאַל בְּ, דָּרַשׁ to inquire or ask *by means of* a god (or oracle). **c.** ביהוה *through* ´י (\= by His aid) in many connexions, as [Psalm 18:30](https://ref.ly/Ps18:30), [44:6](https://ref.ly/Ps44:6), [56:5](https://ref.ly/Ps56:5), [56:11](https://ref.ly/Ps56:11), [60:14](https://ref.ly/Ps60:14), [Is 26:13](https://ref.ly/Isa26:13), [45:25](https://ref.ly/Isa45:25), [Ho 1:7](https://ref.ly/Hos1:7), [Zc 10:12](https://ref.ly/Zech10:12); with pass. verbs [Dt 33:29](https://ref.ly/Deut33:29), [Is 45:17](https://ref.ly/Isa45:17) (to be saved): and even of the immediate cause [Nu 36:2](https://ref.ly/Num36:2) to be commanded ביהוה by ´י, [Gn 9:6](https://ref.ly/Gen9:6) בָּאָדָם *by* man shall his blood be shed (both unusual), [Ho 14:4](https://ref.ly/Hos14:4). **d.** allied is the use of בְּ in such phrases as to bless, swear, speak, prophesy, etc., בְּשֵׁם *in* the name of … (i.e. the name being used or appealed to in the act) [Dt 6:13](https://ref.ly/Deut6:13), [Je 11:21](https://ref.ly/Jer11:21), etc. (so to swear ביהוה by ´י [Jos 2:12](https://ref.ly/Josh2:12) etc., בִּימִינוֺ [Is 62:8](https://ref.ly/Isa62:8); to bless בְּךָ *with thee* [Gn 48:20](https://ref.ly/Gen48:20), to swear *with me* [Psalm 102:9](https://ref.ly/Ps102:9), i.e. using my name in oath, [Je 29:22](https://ref.ly/Jer29:22)); almost \= in the authority and power of [1 S 17:45](https://ref.ly/1Sam17:45), [25:5](https://ref.ly/1Sam25:5), [1 K 21:8](https://ref.ly/1Kgs21:8), [Mi 4:5](https://ref.ly/Mic4:5), [Zc 10:12](https://ref.ly/Zech10:12), [Psalm 20:6](https://ref.ly/Ps20:6), [44:6](https://ref.ly/Ps44:6), [89:25](https://ref.ly/Ps89:25). **e.** בַּמָּה or בַּמֶּה *by means of what? how?* [Gn 15:8](https://ref.ly/Gen15:8), Ju [6:15](https://ref.ly/Judg6:15), [16:5](https://ref.ly/Judg16:5), [16:6](https://ref.ly/Judg16:6), [16:10](https://ref.ly/Judg16:10), [1 K 22:21](https://ref.ly/1Kgs22:21), [Mal 2:17](https://ref.ly/Mal2:17). בְּזאֹת *by means of this* [Gn 42:15](https://ref.ly/Gen42:15), [42:33](https://ref.ly/Gen42:33). [Ex 7:17](https://ref.ly/Exod7:17), [Nu 16:28](https://ref.ly/Num16:28); *with this* \= *on this condition* [Gn 34:15](https://ref.ly/Gen34:15), [34:22](https://ref.ly/Gen34:22), [1 S 11:2](https://ref.ly/1Sam11:2), [Is 27:9](https://ref.ly/Isa27:9). **3\.** of cost or price (the *Beth pretii*), the price, whether given or received, being treated as the instrumental means by which the act is accomplished, *with, for, at the cost of:* thus regularly **a.** with שָכַר *hire* [Gn 30:16](https://ref.ly/Gen30:16), פָּדָה *redeem* [Ex 34:20](https://ref.ly/Exod34:20), אֵרֵש *betroth* [2 S 3:14](https://ref.ly/2Sam3:14), קָנָה *buy* [2 S 24:24](https://ref.ly/2Sam24:24); [1 K 2:23](https://ref.ly/1Kgs2:23) בְּנַפְשׁוֺ *at the cost of* his life hath A. spoken this word, [2 S 23:17](https://ref.ly/2Sam23:17) who went בְּנַפְשוֺתָם *at peril of* their lives, [Pr 7:23](https://ref.ly/Prov7:23), [La 5:9](https://ref.ly/Lam5:9), [Jos 6:26](https://ref.ly/Josh6:26) בִּבְכֹרוֺ יְיַסְּדֶנָּה *at the price of* his firstborn shall he lay its foundations, [1 Ch 12:19](https://ref.ly/1Chr12:19) בְּרָאשֵׁינוּ *to the jeopardy of* our heads he will fall away, etc. **b.** with מָכַר *sell* [Dt 21:14](https://ref.ly/Deut21:14); עָבַד *serve* [Gn 29:18](https://ref.ly/Gen29:18), [29:20](https://ref.ly/Gen29:20), [Ho 12:13](https://ref.ly/Hos12:13); הֵמִיר *exchange* [Lv 27:10](https://ref.ly/Lev27:10), [Ho 4:7](https://ref.ly/Hos4:7) their glory I will exchange *for* ignominy, [Psalm 106:20](https://ref.ly/Ps106:20); נָתַן בְּנֶשֶׁךְ to give *for* interest [Lv 25:37](https://ref.ly/Lev25:37), [Psalm 15:5](https://ref.ly/Ps15:5); in other connexions [Gn 23:9](https://ref.ly/Gen23:9), [47:16](https://ref.ly/Gen47:16) f. [Is 45:13](https://ref.ly/Isa45:13), [La 1:11](https://ref.ly/Lam1:11), Ct [8:7](https://ref.ly/Song8:7), [8:11](https://ref.ly/Song8:11); [Dt 19:21](https://ref.ly/Deut19:21) נֶפֶשׁ בְּנֶפֶשׁ עַיִן בְּעַיִן life *for* life, eye *for* eye; [Is 2:22](https://ref.ly/Isa2:22) בַּמֶּה נֶחְשָׁב הוּא *at what* is he to be accounted? [7:23](https://ref.ly/Isa7:23) a thousand vines בְּאֶלֶף כָּסֶף *at* a thousand (shekels of) silver.—Hence (perhaps) the idiom. usages שָׁנָה בְשָׁנָה year *for* year, one year like another, annually [Dt 15:20](https://ref.ly/Deut15:20) al.; יוֺם בְּיוֺם (late), כְּיוֺם בְּיוֺם † [1 S 18:10](https://ref.ly/1Sam18:10); כְּפַעַם בְּפַעַם (v. יוֺם, פַּעַם); חֹדֶשׁ בְּחֹדֶשׁ † [1 Ch 27:1](https://ref.ly/1Chr27:1). **4\.** rather peculiarly, in certain cases where the object of an action may be treated as the *instrument* by which it is accomplished: as הֵנַיעַ בְּראֹשׁ to shake *with* the head [Je 18:6](https://ref.ly/Jer18:6), [Jb 16:4](https://ref.ly/Job16:4) (as well as הֵנִיעַ ראֹשׁ [Psalm 22:8](https://ref.ly/Ps22:8)); to open *with* the mouth [Jb 16:10](https://ref.ly/Job16:10), *with* the lips [Psalm 22:8](https://ref.ly/Ps22:8); to gnash *with* the teeth [Jb 16:9](https://ref.ly/Job16:9) (to gnash the teeth [Psalm 35:16](https://ref.ly/Ps35:16)); to wink *with* the eye [Pr 6:13](https://ref.ly/Prov6:13) (to wink the eye, *ib.* [10:10](https://ref.ly/Prov10:10)); נָתַן בְּקוֺל to utter *with* the voice [Psalm 46:7](https://ref.ly/Ps46:7), [68:34](https://ref.ly/Ps68:34), [Je 12:8](https://ref.ly/Jer12:8) (but נָתַן קוֺל is more common); to stretch out *with* the hand [La 1:17](https://ref.ly/Lam1:17); הֵרִים בַּמַּטֶּה (unusual) [Ex 7:20](https://ref.ly/Exod7:20). So קָרָא בְּשֵׁם to call *with* the name—in diff. senses, acc. to the context, viz. to *proclaim* [Ex 33:19](https://ref.ly/Exod33:19), [34:5](https://ref.ly/Exod34:5), [Is 44:5](https://ref.ly/Isa44:5), [Psalm 49:12](https://ref.ly/Ps49:12); to *invoke* [Gn 4:26](https://ref.ly/Gen4:26), [12:8](https://ref.ly/Gen12:8), [1 K 18:24–26](https://ref.ly/1Kgs18:24-1Kgs18:26), [Is 12:4](https://ref.ly/Isa12:4); to *name honourably* [Is 43:1](https://ref.ly/Isa43:1), [45:3](https://ref.ly/Isa45:3). Cf. Ew§ 282 d Ges§ 119\. 3 b R. WAG ii. § 56 b β De:[Jb 31:12](https://ref.ly/Job31:12); [Pr 20:30](https://ref.ly/Prov20:30). **5\.** with a causal force, *through, on account of:* [Gn 18:28](https://ref.ly/Gen18:28) הֲתַשְׁחִית בַּחֲמִשָּׁה wilt thou destroy *on account of* five the whole city? [Lv 26:39](https://ref.ly/Lev26:39), [Nu 16:26](https://ref.ly/Num16:26), [Dt 9:4](https://ref.ly/Deut9:4), [9:5](https://ref.ly/Deut9:5), [24:16](https://ref.ly/Deut24:16) :אִישׁ בְּחֶטְאוֺ יוּמָֽתוּ they shall be put to death, each *because of* his own sin (cf. [Je 31:30](https://ref.ly/Jer31:30), Ez [3:18](https://ref.ly/Ezek3:18), v. [18:17](https://ref.ly/Ezek18:17)) [2 S 3:27](https://ref.ly/2Sam3:27), [14:7](https://ref.ly/2Sam14:7) (cf. [Jon 1:14](https://ref.ly/Jonah1:14)) [Is 7:4](https://ref.ly/Isa7:4) (‖מִן) [28:7](https://ref.ly/Isa28:7) (‖מִן) [50:1](https://ref.ly/Isa50:1), [53:5](https://ref.ly/Isa53:5), [57:17](https://ref.ly/Isa57:17), [Je 51:46](https://ref.ly/Jer51:46), [Psalm 5:8](https://ref.ly/Ps5:8), [5:11](https://ref.ly/Ps5:11), [6:8](https://ref.ly/Ps6:8) (‖מִן) [31:10](https://ref.ly/Ps31:10), [32:3](https://ref.ly/Ps32:3), [42:10](https://ref.ly/Ps42:10), [90:9](https://ref.ly/Ps90:9), [94:23](https://ref.ly/Ps94:23). So in בַּאֲשֶׁר, בִּגְלַל (v. אֲשֶׁר, גָּלָל), \& (sts.) in בִּדְבַר *at, through,* the word of .… **6\.** of the material *with* which a work is wrought, both absol. עָשָׁה בַזָּהָב to work *with* gold [Ex 31:4](https://ref.ly/Exod31:4), [1 K 7:14](https://ref.ly/1Kgs7:14); and to make a thing *with* (in our idiom, *of*) gold [Ex 38:8](https://ref.ly/Exod38:8), Ez [7:20](https://ref.ly/Ezek7:20), [1 K 15:22](https://ref.ly/1Kgs15:22) (בָּנָה). Without a verb [Lv 13:52](https://ref.ly/Lev13:52), [2 Ch 9:18](https://ref.ly/2Chr9:18). **7\.** *with* for *although, in spite of* (cf. Germ. *bei* alle dem): [Lv 26:27](https://ref.ly/Lev26:27), [Nu 14:11](https://ref.ly/Num14:11) בְּכֹל הָאֹתוֺת *in spite of* all the signs that I have wrought, [Dt 1:32](https://ref.ly/Deut1:32), [Is 47:9](https://ref.ly/Isa47:9), [Psalm 27:3](https://ref.ly/Ps27:3); esp. in the phrase בְּכָל־זאת *for* all this [Is 5:25](https://ref.ly/Isa5:25), [9:11](https://ref.ly/Isa9:11), [9:16](https://ref.ly/Isa9:16), [9:20](https://ref.ly/Isa9:20), [10:4](https://ref.ly/Isa10:4), [Psalm 78:32](https://ref.ly/Ps78:32) al. (Cf. in Ar. بِمَاء Qor 9:25\.) **8\.** of a standard of measurement or computation, *with, by:* [Ex 12:4](https://ref.ly/Exod12:4) בְּמִכְסַת נְפָשֹׁת *by* the computation of souls; [Lv 5:25](https://ref.ly/Lev5:25) al. בְּעֶרְכְּךָ *by* thy reckoning; Ez [4:10](https://ref.ly/Ezek4:10); בְּמִסְפָּר *by* number [Dt 25:2](https://ref.ly/Deut25:2) \+;…בְּמִסְפַּר *by* the number of … [Lv 25:15](https://ref.ly/Lev25:15), [25:50](https://ref.ly/Lev25:50), [Nu 1:2](https://ref.ly/Num1:2), etc.; בְּשֶׁקֶל הַקֹּדֶשׁ [Ex 30:13](https://ref.ly/Exod30:13) al. (in P); בָּאַמָּה often (v. אַמָּה); [Dt 3:11](https://ref.ly/Deut3:11) בְּאַמַּת אִישׁ *by* the cubit of a man; [2 S 14:26](https://ref.ly/2Sam14:26) בְּאֶבֶן הַמֶּלֶךְ. Of a model, [Gn 1:26](https://ref.ly/Gen1:26) בְּצַלְמֵנוּ *in* our image, [5:1](https://ref.ly/Gen5:1), [5:3](https://ref.ly/Gen5:3), [Ex 25:40](https://ref.ly/Exod25:40); בְּדֶרֶךְ *with* the way (\= in the manner) of ... [Is 10:24](https://ref.ly/Isa10:24), [10:26](https://ref.ly/Isa10:26), [Am 4:10](https://ref.ly/Amos4:10). **IV.** בְּ is used also with certain classes of verbs, though the explanation of its use may be sometimes doubtful: viz. **a.** with verbs of *taking refuge, trusting, relying,* as הֶאֱמִין, בָּטַח, חָסָה, נִשְׁעַן. **b.** with verbs of *ruling, governing, restraining,* as מָלַךְ, מָשַׁל, נָגַש, עָצַר, רָדָה, שָׁלַט. **c.** with verbs of *rejoicing, feeling pleasure* or *satisfaction,* etc., as גִּיל, עָלַץ, שוּש, שָמֵחַ, חָפֵץ, רָחָה, שָבַע (but with this verb מִן is more common). \[Prob. a case of **III. 5\.**] **d.** with verbs expressive of sensible perception, to denote the pleasurable or attentive exercise of the faculty concerned, as שָׁמַע בְּ to listen *to,* הִבִּיט בְּ, רָאָה, חָזָה, to look *upon,* הֵרִיחַ בְּ to smell *at* (see these words). **e.** occasionally also with verbs of *speaking, thinking, mentioning, knowing,* to denote the object of the action, as דִּבֶּר בְּ to speak *about* [Dt 6:7](https://ref.ly/Deut6:7), [Psalm 87:3](https://ref.ly/Ps87:3) al. (v. sub דִּבֶּר); הִלֵּל בְּ, הָגָה, שִיחַ [Psalm 44:9](https://ref.ly/Ps44:9), [63:7](https://ref.ly/Ps63:7), [69:13](https://ref.ly/Ps69:13); יָדַע [Je 38:24](https://ref.ly/Jer38:24); זָכַר once [Je 3:16](https://ref.ly/Jer3:16), הִזְכִּיר oft.; [Psalm 71:6](https://ref.ly/Ps71:6) בְּךָ תְהִלָּתִי *of* thee is my praise. **V.** Followed by an inf. c., בְּ forms a periphrasis for the gerund, though in English it is commonly to be rendered by a verb and conj., viz.:—**1\.** as a *temporal* conj., as [Gn 2:4](https://ref.ly/Gen2:4) בְּהִבָּֽרְאָם *in* their *being created* \= *when* they *were* created, [4:8](https://ref.ly/Gen4:8) בִּהְיוֺתָם *in* their *being* (\= *when* they *were*) in the field; and constantly. Sometimes it has in appearance the force of *after that*, as [Gn 33:18](https://ref.ly/Gen33:18), [Ex 3:12](https://ref.ly/Exod3:12), [13:17](https://ref.ly/Exod13:17); but as a rule this is really due to the action denoted by the inf. being treated as extending over a period *within* which the action of the principal verb takes place: so esp. in the phrase בְּצֵאת יִשְרָאֵל מִמִּצְרַיִם, even of events at the close of the 40 years, [Dt 4:45](https://ref.ly/Deut4:45), [23:5](https://ref.ly/Deut23:5), [Jos 5:4](https://ref.ly/Josh5:4), the whole period being treated as that in which Egypt was left (comp. [2 K 2:1](https://ref.ly/2Kgs2:1), where the time included is future). Cases, however, occur in which this explanation will hardly apply, as [Dt 27:4](https://ref.ly/Deut27:4), [24:12](https://ref.ly/Deut24:12). **2\.** as a *causal* conj. (cf. above **III. 5**), as [Gn 19:16](https://ref.ly/Gen19:16) ´בְּחֶמְלַת י עָלָיו *through* J.’s having compassion upon him, [Ex 16:7](https://ref.ly/Exod16:7), [33:16](https://ref.ly/Exod33:16), [34:29](https://ref.ly/Exod34:29), [Dt 1:27](https://ref.ly/Deut1:27) אֹתָנוּ ´בְּשִנְאַת י *through* J.’s hating us, etc. ([9:28](https://ref.ly/Deut9:28) similarly מִן), [1 K 18:18](https://ref.ly/1Kgs18:18) (\= *in that* ye have …) Ez [9:8](https://ref.ly/Ezek9:8), [43:8](https://ref.ly/Ezek43:8), [44:7](https://ref.ly/Ezek44:7), [2 Ch 28:6](https://ref.ly/2Chr28:6). **3\.** as a *concessive* conj., *when* \= *though:* [Is 1:15](https://ref.ly/Isa1:15), [Psalm 46:3](https://ref.ly/Ps46:3) בְּהָמִיר אָרֶץ *though* the earth do change. *Note.*—[Ex 10:12](https://ref.ly/Exod10:12) בָּאַרְבֶּה can only be rendered ‘ *with* the locusts,’ the locusts being conceived as implicit in Moses' uplifted hand: but prob. לָאַרְבֶּה should be read. Thrice in late Heb. בְּ is used peculiarly: [1 Ch 7:23](https://ref.ly/1Chr7:23) for *with* misfortune was it in his house (בְּרָעָה chosen for the purpose of explaining בְּרִיעָה); [9:33](https://ref.ly/1Chr9:33) עֲלֵיהֶם בַּמְּלָאכָה it devolved upon them *with* the work; [Ezr 3:3](https://ref.ly/Ezra3:3) for *with* terror (was it) upon them from the peoples of the countries (the sentence without a verb as oft. in Chr.: DrIntr.. 504 f.): cf. [8:34](https://ref.ly/Ezra8:34). Comp. Ew§ 295 f. 

> \[Str1119] † בְּמוֺ poet. for בְּ (v. sub מוֺ: cf. Sab. בם, DHMZMG 1883, 344\) [Is 25:10](https://ref.ly/Isa25:10) Qr (\< Kt בְּמֵי) [43:2](https://ref.ly/Isa43:2), [44:16](https://ref.ly/Isa44:16), [44:19](https://ref.ly/Isa44:19), [Psalm 11:2](https://ref.ly/Ps11:2), [Jb 9:30](https://ref.ly/Job9:30) Kt (\> Qr בְמֵי) [16:4](https://ref.ly/Job16:4), [16:5](https://ref.ly/Job16:5), [19:16](https://ref.ly/Job19:16), [37:8](https://ref.ly/Job37:8).

> II. בְּ, perh. abbrev. in **n.pr.** for בֶּ־ן, בֵּית q.v.

> \[Str872] בִּאָה v. sub בּוֺא. p. 99

## באר (id: BDB00979)

\[Str874] † \[באר] **vb.** only **Pi.** make distinct, plain (so NH, Aram.; LagBN 58 prop. for Qal בָּאֵר, בָּאֹר, cf. deriv. infr.; Thes \& most comp. Ar. بَأَرَ *dig a pit* or *well*, but this prob. denom. v. Lagl. c.)—**Pi.** *Pf.* 3 ms. בֵּאֵר [Dt 1:5](https://ref.ly/Deut1:5), *Imv.* בָּאֵר Hb [2:2](https://ref.ly/Hab2:2); *Inf. abs.* בַּאֵר [Dt 27:8](https://ref.ly/Deut27:8) (cf. Bö§ 393, 4\)—*make distinct, plain*, of letters on tablets Hb [2:2](https://ref.ly/Hab2:2) כְּתֹב חָזוֺן וּבָאֵר עַ־ל הַלֻּחוֺת לְמַעַן יָרוּץ קוֺרֵא בוֺ, i.e. so that one may run past and (still) read; or, so that one may read swiftly; on stones [Dt 27:8](https://ref.ly/Deut27:8) וְכָתַבְתָּ עַ־ל בַּאֵר הֵיטֵב …. הָאֲבָנִים *and thou shalt write upon the* \[whitewashed] *stones* all the words of this law, *doing it plainly and well;* fig. *explain, expound* [Dt 1:5](https://ref.ly/Deut1:5) הוֺאִיל משֶׁה בֵּאֵר חַתּוֺרָה *Moses began* (and) *expounded the law.*

> \[Str875,876,879] † בְּאֵר **n.f.**:[Gn 26:21](https://ref.ly/Gen26:21) well, pit, mostly Hex, Gn 23 t. Ex 1 t. Nu 5 t., 37 t. in all; Ar. بِئْرٌ, Aram. בְּאֵרָא, בֵּיר, בֵּירָא, ܒܻܪܳܐ, Sab. באר DHMZMG. 1875, 608, As. *bêru*, LyonSargon 61 (connexion with above √ not clear; Lagl. c. *spring* of water, as *coming to light, appearing;* possible, although meaning in use rather *well*, than *spring;* v. however, [Gn 16:14](https://ref.ly/Gen16:14) cf. v:[7](https://ref.ly/Gen16:7), [26:19](https://ref.ly/Gen26:19), [Nu 21:17](https://ref.ly/Num21:17))—´בּ abs. [Gn 21:30](https://ref.ly/Gen21:30) \+; cstr. [21:19](https://ref.ly/Gen21:19) \+; sf. בְּאֵֶרָךָ [Pr 5:15](https://ref.ly/Prov5:15); Pl. abs. בְּאֵרֹת [Gn 26:15](https://ref.ly/Gen26:15); cstr. id. [26:18](https://ref.ly/Gen26:18); cf. בֶּאֱרֹת בֶּאֱרֹת חֵמָר [Gn 14:10](https://ref.ly/Gen14:10); —**1\.** *a well*, often as made by digging (חָפַר) [Gn 21:25](https://ref.ly/Gen21:25), [21:30](https://ref.ly/Gen21:30) (E), [26:15](https://ref.ly/Gen26:15), [26:18](https://ref.ly/Gen26:18), [26:19](https://ref.ly/Gen26:19), [26:21](https://ref.ly/Gen26:21), [26:22](https://ref.ly/Gen26:22), [26:32](https://ref.ly/Gen26:32) (all J), also poet. [Nu 21:18](https://ref.ly/Num21:18) (\+ כרה) vid. also v:[16](https://ref.ly/Num21:16), v:[17](https://ref.ly/Num21:17) (where the well addressed, in song, עֲלִי בְאֵר); also c. כרה in prose [Gn 26:25](https://ref.ly/Gen26:25); also with no ref. to its origin [Gn 16:14](https://ref.ly/Gen16:14) (J; ‖עַיִן v:[7](https://ref.ly/Gen16:7)), [29:2](https://ref.ly/Gen29:2), [Ex 2:15](https://ref.ly/Exod2:15) (J) [Nu 20:17](https://ref.ly/Num20:17), [21:22](https://ref.ly/Num21:22) (E) [2 S 17:18](https://ref.ly/2Sam17:18), [17:21](https://ref.ly/2Sam17:21); מַיִם(ה)בְּאֵר [Gn 21:19](https://ref.ly/Gen21:19) (E) [24:11](https://ref.ly/Gen24:11) (J; ‖עַיִן vv:[13](https://ref.ly/Gen24:13), vv:[16](https://ref.ly/Gen24:16), vv:[43](https://ref.ly/Gen24:43), vv:[45](https://ref.ly/Gen24:45)); (cf. also [21:25](https://ref.ly/Gen21:25), [26:18](https://ref.ly/Gen26:18) supr. \& esp. v:[19](https://ref.ly/Gen26:19) בְּאֵר מַיִם חַיִּים); water taken from it by drawing (שָׁאַב) [Gn 24:11](https://ref.ly/Gen24:11), [24:20](https://ref.ly/Gen24:20); flocks watered from it (הִשְׁקָה מִ־ן) [29:2](https://ref.ly/Gen29:2), [29:3](https://ref.ly/Gen29:3), [29:8](https://ref.ly/Gen29:8), [29:10](https://ref.ly/Gen29:10) (cf. esp. [Ex 2:16](https://ref.ly/Exod2:16) *they drew*, דלה, and filled the troughs); also מִתּוֺךְ בְּאֵרֶָךָ …. שְׁתֵה מַיִם [Pr 5:15](https://ref.ly/Prov5:15) (‖בּוֺר); the opening called פִּי הַבְּאֵר [Gn 29:2](https://ref.ly/Gen29:2), [29:3](https://ref.ly/Gen29:3), [29:3](https://ref.ly/Gen29:3), [29:8](https://ref.ly/Gen29:8), [29:10](https://ref.ly/Gen29:10); cf. ´פְּנֵי הַבּ [2 S 17:19](https://ref.ly/2Sam17:19) (rd. prob. פּי so Sam. Targum SyrVer Vulgate, cf. Dr); fig. of fresh delights of woman beloved Ct [4:15](https://ref.ly/Song4:15) מַעְיַן גַּנִּים בְּאֵר מַיִם חַיִּים וְנוֺזְלִים מִן־לְבָנוֺן. **2\.** *pit* (\= בּוֺר); pits of bitumen [Gn 14:10](https://ref.ly/Gen14:10) (cf. supr.); בְּאֵר שַׁחַת [Psalm 55:24](https://ref.ly/Ps55:24) *pit of (the) grave;* cf. [69:16](https://ref.ly/Ps69:16) וְאַל־תֶּאְטַר עָלַי בְּאֵר פִּיהָ *and let not (the) pit shut its mouth over me* (‖מְצוּלָה); fig. of strange woman בְּאֵר צָרָה *a narrow pit*, out of which rescue is difficult [Pr 23:27](https://ref.ly/Prov23:27) (‖שׁוּחָה עֲמֻקָּה). **3\.** as **n.pr.loc. a.** c. ה—ָ loc. בְּאֵ֫רָה a station of Isr. in desert [Nu 21:16](https://ref.ly/Num21:16), possibly \= בְּאֵר אֵילִים [Is 15:8](https://ref.ly/Isa15:8). **b.** same form Ju [9:21](https://ref.ly/Judg9:21), acc. to Euseb. LagOnom.238, 2nd ed. 250 8 miles north of Eleutheropolis; cf. RobBR i. 452 who comp. *el\-Bîreh*, near Beth\-shemesh.

> \[Str879] בְּאֵר אֵילִים v. בְּאֵר **3\. a.** above

> \[Str883] † בְּאֵר לַחַי רֹאִי **n.pr.loc.** (lit. *well* of the *living One that seeth me*) [Gn 16:14](https://ref.ly/Gen16:14) (where explan., from story of Hagar) [24:62](https://ref.ly/Gen24:62), [25:11](https://ref.ly/Gen25:11) (all J); perh. name of ancient shrine or holy place, cf. StaZAW ii. 347 \& Di [Gn 16:14](https://ref.ly/Gen16:14); W. of Kadesh, cf. Jer sub Barad, LagOnom. 101, 2nd ed. 135, v. Rowlands in WilliamsHoly City, 489 TrumbullKadesh\-Barnea 64\.
> 
> 91:b. בְּאֵר לַחַי רֹאִי : לַחַי perhaps orig. I. לְחִי *jaw(\-bone);* on n.pr.loc. cpd. with לחי, γνάφος, v. LXXJu [15:9](https://ref.ly/Judg15:9) WeProl. 344 NesAJSem. Lang. xill (1897\) 176\.

> \[Str884] † בְּאֵר שֶׁ֫בַע **n.pr.loc.** Beersheba (*well of seven,* explained [Gn 21:30](https://ref.ly/Gen21:30), [21:31](https://ref.ly/Gen21:31) as place of *swearing by seven* lambs, or, *well of oath,* v. שְׁבֻעָה; cf. same meaning otherwise derived [26:33](https://ref.ly/Gen26:33))—בְּאֵר שֶׁבַע [Gn 26:33](https://ref.ly/Gen26:33) \+ 13 t., בְּאֵר־שֶׁבַע [Jos 19:2](https://ref.ly/Josh19:2); בְּאֵר שָׁבַע [Gn 21:14](https://ref.ly/Gen21:14) \+ 18 t.; בְּאֵ֫רָה שֶׁבַע (ה—ָ loc.) [Gn 46:1](https://ref.ly/Gen46:1)—south from Hebron, acc. to Onom. c. 20 miles LagOnom. 103, 234, 2nd ed. 138, 248; mod. *Bîr\-es\-Seba*, 12 h. fr. Hebron RobBR i. 204 f. Surveyiii. 394 [Gn 21:14](https://ref.ly/Gen21:14), [21:31](https://ref.ly/Gen21:31), [21:32](https://ref.ly/Gen21:32), [21:33](https://ref.ly/Gen21:33), [22:19](https://ref.ly/Gen22:19), [22:19](https://ref.ly/Gen22:19), [26:23](https://ref.ly/Gen26:23), [26:33](https://ref.ly/Gen26:33), [28:10](https://ref.ly/Gen28:10), [46:1](https://ref.ly/Gen46:1), [46:5](https://ref.ly/Gen46:5), [Jos 15:28](https://ref.ly/Josh15:28), [19:2](https://ref.ly/Josh19:2), Ju [20:1](https://ref.ly/Judg20:1), [1 S 3:20](https://ref.ly/1Sam3:20), [8:2](https://ref.ly/1Sam8:2), [2 S 17:11](https://ref.ly/2Sam17:11), [24:7](https://ref.ly/2Sam24:7), [1 K 19:3](https://ref.ly/1Kgs19:3), [2 K 12:2](https://ref.ly/2Kgs12:2), [23:8](https://ref.ly/2Kgs23:8), [Am 5:5](https://ref.ly/Amos5:5), [1 Ch 4:28](https://ref.ly/1Chr4:28), [2 Ch 19:4](https://ref.ly/2Chr19:4), [24:1](https://ref.ly/2Chr24:1), [Ne 11:27](https://ref.ly/Neh11:27), [11:30](https://ref.ly/Neh11:30); in phrase ´מִדָּן וְעַד־בּ *from Dan to Beersheba* (i.e. all the territory of Israel, v. דָּן) [Am 8:14](https://ref.ly/Amos8:14), Ju [20:1](https://ref.ly/Judg20:1), [1 S 3:20](https://ref.ly/1Sam3:20), [2 S 3:10](https://ref.ly/2Sam3:10), [17:11](https://ref.ly/2Sam17:11), [24:2](https://ref.ly/2Sam24:2), [24:15](https://ref.ly/2Sam24:15), [1 K 5:5](https://ref.ly/1Kgs5:5), \& (only Ch) מִבְּאֵר שֶׁבַע וְעַד־דָּן *from Beersheba to Dan* [1 Ch 21:2](https://ref.ly/1Chr21:2), [2 Ch 30:5](https://ref.ly/2Chr30:5).

> \[Str878] † בְּאֵרָא **n.pr.m.** a man of Asher [1 Ch 7:37](https://ref.ly/1Chr7:37).

> \[Str880] † בְּאֵרָה **n.pr.m.** a Reubenite [1 Ch 5:6](https://ref.ly/1Chr5:6).

> \[Str881] † בְאֵרוֺת **n.pr.loc.** (but only [2 S 4:2](https://ref.ly/2Sam4:2) acc. to Masorah, v. BD:[Ezr 2:23](https://ref.ly/Ezra2:23) elsewh. בְּאֵרֹת), city of the Gibeonites [Jos 9:17](https://ref.ly/Josh9:17); assigned to Benjamin [2 S 4:2](https://ref.ly/2Sam4:2), [Jos 18:25](https://ref.ly/Josh18:25); cf. also [Ezr 2:25](https://ref.ly/Ezra2:25), [Ne 7:29](https://ref.ly/Neh7:29); mod. *el\-Bîreh* RobBR i. 452 BDPal 214 Surveyiii. 88\.

> \[Str886] † בְּאֵרֹתִי **adj.gent.** always c. art. [2 S 4:2](https://ref.ly/2Sam4:2), [4:5](https://ref.ly/2Sam4:5), [4:9](https://ref.ly/2Sam4:9), [23:37](https://ref.ly/2Sam23:37) \= הַבֵּרֹתִי [1 Ch 11:39](https://ref.ly/1Chr11:39); pl. הַבְּאֵרֹתִים [2 S 4:3](https://ref.ly/2Sam4:3).

> \[Str885] † בְּאֵרֹת בְּנֵי־יַעֲקָן **n.pr.loc.** [Dt 10:6](https://ref.ly/Deut10:6) (cf. [Nu 33:31](https://ref.ly/Num33:31), [33:32](https://ref.ly/Num33:32), where בני יעקן), a station of Isr. in desert, prob. in country of Horites, cf. Di.

> \[Str882] † בְאֵרִי **n.pr.m.** (*my well*). **1\.** a Hittite, Esau's father\-in\-law [Gn 26:34](https://ref.ly/Gen26:34). **2\.** Hosea's father [Ho 1:1](https://ref.ly/Hos1:1).
> 
> 92:a. בְּאֵרִי: \< *the man from Beer ?* Nesl. c..

> \[Str877] † \[בּאֹר] **n.m.**:[Je 2:13](https://ref.ly/Jer2:13) cistern, pit, well (for בְּאֹר cf. בּוֺר)—sg. Kt באר [2 S 23:15](https://ref.ly/2Sam23:15), [23:16](https://ref.ly/2Sam23:16), [23:20](https://ref.ly/2Sam23:20) (Qr בֹּר); appar. *well* v:[15](https://ref.ly/2Sam23:15), v:[16](https://ref.ly/2Sam23:16) (rd. perh. בְּאֵר), *pit* v:[20](https://ref.ly/2Sam23:20); pl. [Je 2:13](https://ref.ly/Jer2:13) *me they have forsaken, the fountain of living water*, לַחְצֹב לָהֶם בּאֹרוֺת בּאֹרֹת נִשְׁבָּרִים, *to hew out for themselves cisterns, broken cisterns*, etc.

[Gn 37:22](https://ref.ly/Gen37:22) \+; בֹּר [Ex 21:33](https://ref.ly/Exod21:33) ([2 S 23:20](https://ref.ly/2Sam23:20) Qr, v. באר supr.); cstr. בּוֺר [2 K 10:14](https://ref.ly/2Kgs10:14) \+ ([2 S 23:15](https://ref.ly/2Sam23:15), [23:16](https://ref.ly/2Sam23:16) Qr, v. באר supr.); בֹּ֫רָה (ה—ָ loc.) ## בָּאַשׁ (id: BDB00999)

\[Str807] † בָּאַשׁ **vb.** have a bad smell, stink (Ar. بِئْسَ *be evil*, Aram. בְּאֵשׁ, ܒܶܐܫ *be evil*, As. *bîšu* LotzTP 78\)—**Qal** *Pf.* וּבָאַשׁ consec. [Ex 7:18](https://ref.ly/Exod7:18); *Impf.* וַיִּבְאַשׁ v:[21](https://ref.ly/Exod7:21), ־אַָשׁ [16:20](https://ref.ly/Exod16:20); 3 fs. תִּבְאַשׁ [Is 50:2](https://ref.ly/Isa50:2); וַתִּבְאַשׁ [Ex 8:10](https://ref.ly/Exod8:10); —*stink*, of Nile, on account of dead fish [Ex 7:18](https://ref.ly/Exod7:18), [7:21](https://ref.ly/Exod7:21) (E); of land of Egypt, owing to dead frogs [8:10](https://ref.ly/Exod8:10) (J); of manna kept over [16:20](https://ref.ly/Exod16:20) (P ? or R) וַיָּרֻם תּוֺלָעִים וַיִּבְאַָשׁ *and it grew foul* (rotten, decayed) *with worms, and stank;* [Is 50:2](https://ref.ly/Isa50:2) תִּבְאַשׁ דְּגָתָם מֵאֵין מַיִם וְתָמֹת בַּצָּמָא *their fish stink for lack of water*, etc. (LXX Lo Di rd. תִּיבַשׁ, *dry up* and die, which suits ‖, but not the usage of יָבֵשׁ). **Niph.** *Pf.* נִבְאַשׁ [1 S 13:4](https://ref.ly/1Sam13:4); נִבְאֲשׁוּ [2 S 10:6](https://ref.ly/2Sam10:6); נִבְאַשְׁתָּ [2 S 16:2](https://ref.ly/2Sam16:2); —only fig. *make oneself odious, become odious* (cf. Eng. *be in bad odour*), sq. אֵת *with* \= *towards;* נִבְאַשְׁתָּ אֶת־אָבִיךָ [2 S 16:21](https://ref.ly/2Sam16:21) *thou hast become odious with thy father;* also sq. בְּ (rather strangely) [1 S 13:4](https://ref.ly/1Sam13:4) *Isr. made themselves odious to the Philistines;* [2 S 10:6](https://ref.ly/2Sam10:6) Ammonites to David. **Hiph.** *Pf.* הִבְאִישׁ [Ex 16:24](https://ref.ly/Exod16:24), [1 S 27:12](https://ref.ly/1Sam27:12); הִבְאִישׁוּ [Psalm 38:6](https://ref.ly/Ps38:6); הִבְאַשְׁתֶּם [Ex 5:21](https://ref.ly/Exod5:21); *Impf.* יַבְאִישׁ [Pr 13:5](https://ref.ly/Prov13:5), [Ec 10:1](https://ref.ly/Eccl10:1); *Inf. abs.* הַבְאֵשׁ [1 S 27:12](https://ref.ly/1Sam27:12); *cstr. sf.* לְהַבְאִישֵׁנִי [Gn 34:30](https://ref.ly/Gen34:30); —**1\.** *emit a stinking odour* [Ex 16:24](https://ref.ly/Exod16:24) of manna (cf. Qal v20); [Psalm 38:6](https://ref.ly/Ps38:6) הִבְאִישׁוּ נָמַקּוּ חַבּוּרֹתָי *my wounds have grown stinking, they have festered* (of chastisement for sin); [1 S 17:12](https://ref.ly/1Sam17:12) fig. of David הַבְאֵשׁ הִבְאִישׁ בְּעַמּוֺ *he hath become utterly abhorred among his people*. **2\.** *cause to stink*, זְבוּבֵי מָוֶת יַבְאִישׁ יַבִּיעַ שֶׁמֶן רוֺקֵָחַ *dead flies cause to stink* (and) *to ferment the oil of a perfumer;* usually fig. הִבְאַשְׁתֶּם אֶת־רֵיחֵנוּ [Ex 5:21](https://ref.ly/Exod5:21) (J), i.e. ye have made us odious, sq. בְּעֵינֵי, cf. (c. acc. Pers.) [Gn 34:30](https://ref.ly/Gen34:30) sq. בְ; also without obj. [Pr 13:5](https://ref.ly/Prov13:5) *a wicked man makes odious and shameful* (De Now Str; Be Ew Hi Zö *acts odiously and shamefully*). **Hithp.** *Pf.* הִתְבָּאֲשׁוּ [1 Ch 19:6](https://ref.ly/1Chr19:6) *they had made themselves odious* (\= Niph. in ‖ [2 S 10:6](https://ref.ly/2Sam10:6)), sq. עִם.

> \[Str893] † בֵּבַי **n.pr.m.** a chief of returning exiles בֵּכָי [Ne 10:16](https://ref.ly/Neh10:16); בְּנֵי בֵבַי [Ezr 8:11](https://ref.ly/Ezra8:11); בֶּן־בֵּבָי [Ezr 8:11](https://ref.ly/Ezra8:11); בְּנֵי בֵבָי [Ezr 2:11](https://ref.ly/Ezra2:11), [10:28](https://ref.ly/Ezra10:28), [Ne 7:16](https://ref.ly/Neh7:16).

## בָּגַד (id: BDB01007)

\[Str898] † \[בָּגַד] **vb.** act or deal treacherously—**Qal** *Pf.* בָּֽגְדָה [Je 3:20](https://ref.ly/Jer3:20) \+ 14 t.; *Impf.* יִבְגֹּד [Mal 2:15](https://ref.ly/Mal2:15) \+ 5 t.; נִבְגַּד [Mal 2:10](https://ref.ly/Mal2:10); *Inf. abs.* בָּגוֺד [Is 48:8](https://ref.ly/Isa48:8), [Je 5:11](https://ref.ly/Jer5:11); *cstr.* בְּגֹד [Is 33:1](https://ref.ly/Isa33:1); בִּגְדוֺ [Ex 21:8](https://ref.ly/Exod21:8); *Pt.* בֹּגֵד [Pr 22:12](https://ref.ly/Prov22:12) \+ 11 t.; בּוֺגֵד [Is 33:1](https://ref.ly/Isa33:1) \+ 10 t.;—*act* or *deal treacherously, faithlessly, deceitfully*, in the marriage relation, in matters of property or right, in covenants, in word and in general conduct. **a.** *abs.* [1 S 14:33](https://ref.ly/1Sam14:33), [Jb 6:15](https://ref.ly/Job6:15), [Psalm 78:57](https://ref.ly/Ps78:57), [Is 24:16](https://ref.ly/Isa24:16), [33:1](https://ref.ly/Isa33:1), [33:1](https://ref.ly/Isa33:1), [48:8](https://ref.ly/Isa48:8), [Mal 2:11](https://ref.ly/Mal2:11). Cf. phrases אחי בגדו כמו נחל *my brethren have dealt deceitfully as a brook* [Jb 6:15](https://ref.ly/Job6:15); בֹּגְדִים בָּ\+נדוּ וּבֶגֶד בּוֺגְדִים בָּ\+גדוּ *the treacherous have dealt treacherously, yea in treachery have the treacherous dealt treacherously* [Is 24:16](https://ref.ly/Isa24:16) (striking alliteration); בֹּגְדֵי אָוֶן *treacherous in wickedness* [Psalm 59:6](https://ref.ly/Ps59:6). **b.** with בְּ [Ex 21:8](https://ref.ly/Exod21:8) (E), Ju [9:23](https://ref.ly/Judg9:23), [Is 33:1](https://ref.ly/Isa33:1), [33:1](https://ref.ly/Isa33:1), [Je 3:20](https://ref.ly/Jer3:20), [5:11](https://ref.ly/Jer5:11), [12:6](https://ref.ly/Jer12:6), [La 1:2](https://ref.ly/Lam1:2), [Ho 5:7](https://ref.ly/Hos5:7), [6:7](https://ref.ly/Hos6:7), [Mal 2:10](https://ref.ly/Mal2:10), [2:14](https://ref.ly/Mal2:14), [2:15](https://ref.ly/Mal2:15), [2:16](https://ref.ly/Mal2:16). **c.** c. acc. [Psalm 73:15](https://ref.ly/Ps73:15). **d.** c. מִן pregnant, בָּֽגְדָה אִשָּׁה מֵרֵעָהּ *acteth treacherously (in departing) from her friend* [Je 3:20](https://ref.ly/Jer3:20). The ptcp. is used [Pr 2:22](https://ref.ly/Prov2:22) \+ 8 t., [Psalm 25:3](https://ref.ly/Ps25:3), [59:6](https://ref.ly/Ps59:6), [119:158](https://ref.ly/Ps119:158), [Is 21:2](https://ref.ly/Isa21:2), [24:16](https://ref.ly/Isa24:16), [24:16](https://ref.ly/Isa24:16), [33:1](https://ref.ly/Isa33:1), [Je 3:8](https://ref.ly/Jer3:8), [3:11](https://ref.ly/Jer3:11), [9:1](https://ref.ly/Jer9:1), Hb [1:13](https://ref.ly/Hab1:13), [2:5](https://ref.ly/Hab2:5); בֹּגְדֵי בָגֶד *dealers treacherously in treachery* (very *treacherously*) [Je 12:1](https://ref.ly/Jer12:1).

> \[Str899] † I. בֶּ֫גֶד **n.\[m.]** treachery, [Is 24:16](https://ref.ly/Isa24:16), [Je 12:1](https://ref.ly/Jer12:1).

Di in *loco* Köi. 182\) garment, covering—[Gn 28:2](https://ref.ly/Gen28:2) \+ 36 t.; *sf.* בִּגְדוֺ Ez [9:3](https://ref.ly/Ezek9:3) \+ (14 t. without *dag. lene* GesLgb. 94\); pl. בְּגָדִים [Lv 6:4](https://ref.ly/Lev6:4) \+ 32 t.; cstr. בִּגְדֵי [Gn 27:15](https://ref.ly/Gen27:15) \+ 39 t.; sf. בְּגָדֶיךָ [1 K 22:20](https://ref.ly/1Kgs22:20) \+ 81 t.; בִּגְדֹתֶיךָ [Psalm 45:9](https://ref.ly/Ps45:9); —**1\.** *garment, clothing, raiment, robe* of any kind, from the filthy clothing of the leper to the holy robes of the high priest, the simplest covering of the poor as well as the costly raiment of the rich and noble, used throughout Heb. ## I בָּדַד (id: BDB01020)

\[Str909] † I. \[בָּדַד] **vb.** be separate, isolated (Ar. بَدَّ *cause to withdraw;* II. *separate, disunite*, [Gn 11:8](https://ref.ly/Gen11:8) Saad.; IV. *divide into parts;* x. *go alone, act independently*), only *Pt.* בּוֺדֵד [Is 14:31](https://ref.ly/Isa14:31) (of straggler in army), [Ho 8:9](https://ref.ly/Hos8:9) פֶּרֶא בוֺדֵד לוֺ a wild\-ass (sim. of Ephr.) *going alone* for itself (i.e. willfully: v. sub לְ), [Psalm 102:8](https://ref.ly/Ps102:8) (of bird sitting solitarily).

> \[Str905] II. בַּד, בָּד **n.m.** separation, concr. part (بِدٌّ, بِدَّةٌ *portion*)—sf. (always with לְ) לְבַדִּי, לְבַדּוֺ etc., 3 f. pl. † [Gn 21:28](https://ref.ly/Gen21:28) לְבַדְּהֶן, v:[29](https://ref.ly/Gen21:29) לְבַדָּ֫נָה;—**1\.** with לְ, only in sg., לְבַד prop. *in a state of* (v. sub לְ) *separation, alone, by itself* (Fr. *à part*). **a.** [Ex 26:9](https://ref.ly/Exod26:9) (\= [36:16](https://ref.ly/Exod36:16)) five curtains לְבַד *by themselves*, and six curtains לְבַד *by themselves*, Ju [7:5](https://ref.ly/Judg7:5) him shalt thou set לבד *apart*, [Zc 12:12](https://ref.ly/Zech12:12)–14\. **b.** with sf. (89 t.) to express the idea of *by oneself, alone* (prop. *in his, thy, my separation*), [Gn 2:18](https://ref.ly/Gen2:18) it is not good for man to be לְבַדּוֺ *alone*, [21:28](https://ref.ly/Gen21:28) and A. set the seven lambs לְבַדְּהֶן *by themselves* (lit. in their separation), [32:17](https://ref.ly/Gen32:17), [43:32](https://ref.ly/Gen43:32), [2 S 10:8](https://ref.ly/2Sam10:8), [Is 5:8](https://ref.ly/Isa5:8); [Gn 42:38](https://ref.ly/Gen42:38) הוּא לְבַדּוֺ he *alone*, [Ex 18:14](https://ref.ly/Exod18:14) אַתָּה לְבַדְּךָ thou *alone*, [Nu 11:14](https://ref.ly/Num11:14) אָנֹכִי לְבַדִּי I *alone*, [1 K 19:10](https://ref.ly/1Kgs19:10), [19:14](https://ref.ly/1Kgs19:14); [Dt 8:3](https://ref.ly/Deut8:3) not upon bread *alone*, [29:13](https://ref.ly/Deut29:13), [2 S 13:32](https://ref.ly/2Sam13:32), [18:24](https://ref.ly/2Sam18:24), [Is 44:24](https://ref.ly/Isa44:24), [49:21](https://ref.ly/Isa49:21), [63:3](https://ref.ly/Isa63:3) \+; after an oblique case, as a dat. [Ex 22:19](https://ref.ly/Exod22:19), Ju [3:20](https://ref.ly/Judg3:20), [Psalm 51:6](https://ref.ly/Ps51:6) לְךָ לְבַדְּךָ against thee *alone* have I sinned; a genit. [71:16](https://ref.ly/Ps71:16) I will make mention of :צִדְקָֽתְךָ לְבַדֶּֽךָ the righteousness of thee *alone*. **c.** as adv. of limitation, † [Is 26:13](https://ref.ly/Isa26:13) *only* through thee do we celebrate thy name, [Ec 7:29](https://ref.ly/Eccl7:29). **d.** followed by מִן it becomes a prep., *apart from, besides*, [Ex 12:37](https://ref.ly/Exod12:37), [Nu 29:39](https://ref.ly/Num29:39), [Dt 3:5](https://ref.ly/Deut3:5), [18:8](https://ref.ly/Deut18:8) (rd. מִמְּכָרָיו with LXX SyrVer Targum Aq Ew Di) Ju [8:26](https://ref.ly/Judg8:26), [20:15](https://ref.ly/Judg20:15) al. (15 t.); once, [Ezr 1:6](https://ref.ly/Ezra1:6), with עַל instead of מִן. **e.** מִלְּבַד:33 (prob. inverted for לְבַד מִן *besides*) (chiefly P and late): [Gn 26:1](https://ref.ly/Gen26:1), [46:26](https://ref.ly/Gen46:26), [Lv 9:17](https://ref.ly/Lev9:17), [23:38](https://ref.ly/Lev23:38) (4 t.) [Nu 5:8](https://ref.ly/Num5:8), [6:21](https://ref.ly/Num6:21), [17:14](https://ref.ly/Num16:49) \+ 12 t. Nu 28–29; [Dt 28:69](https://ref.ly/Deut28:69), [Jos 22:29](https://ref.ly/Josh22:29), [1 K 10:13](https://ref.ly/1Kgs10:13), [1 Ch 3:9](https://ref.ly/1Chr3:9), [2 Ch 9:12](https://ref.ly/2Chr9:12), [17:19](https://ref.ly/2Chr17:19), [31:16](https://ref.ly/2Chr31:16), [Ezr 2:65](https://ref.ly/Ezra2:65) \= [Ne 7:67](https://ref.ly/Neh7:67), [Dn 11:4](https://ref.ly/Dan11:4). >  † **2\.** concr. *part* [Ex 30:34](https://ref.ly/Exod30:34) (P) בַּד בְּבַד יִהְיֶה *part* for (i.e. like; cf. בְּ **III. 3\.** end) *part* shall it be. † **3\.** בַּדִּים *parts*, spec. *extended from something*, i.e. (*a*) of a body, *members, limbs* [Jb 18:13](https://ref.ly/Job18:13) (of man), [41:4](https://ref.ly/Job41:4) (of crocodile); (*b*) of a vine, *rods* or *shoots* Ez [17:6](https://ref.ly/Ezek17:6), [19:14](https://ref.ly/Ezek19:14); (*c*) of *poles* or *staves* used for carrying the ark [Ex 25:13](https://ref.ly/Exod25:13), [25:14](https://ref.ly/Exod25:14), [25:15](https://ref.ly/Exod25:15), [35:12](https://ref.ly/Exod35:12), [37:4](https://ref.ly/Exod37:4), [37:5](https://ref.ly/Exod37:5), [39:35](https://ref.ly/Exod39:35), [40:20](https://ref.ly/Exod40:20), [Nu 4:6](https://ref.ly/Num4:6), [1 K 8:7](https://ref.ly/1Kgs8:7), [8:8](https://ref.ly/1Kgs8:8), [8:8](https://ref.ly/1Kgs8:8) (\= [2 Ch 5:8](https://ref.ly/2Chr5:8), [5:9](https://ref.ly/2Chr5:9), [5:9](https://ref.ly/2Chr5:9)), or table of shewbread [Ex 25:27](https://ref.ly/Exod25:27), [25:28](https://ref.ly/Exod25:28), [35:13](https://ref.ly/Exod35:13), [37:14](https://ref.ly/Exod37:14), [37:15](https://ref.ly/Exod37:15), [Nu 4:8](https://ref.ly/Num4:8), or altar of B. O. [Ex 27:6](https://ref.ly/Exod27:6), [27:6](https://ref.ly/Exod27:6), [27:7](https://ref.ly/Exod27:7), [27:7](https://ref.ly/Exod27:7), [35:16](https://ref.ly/Exod35:16), [38:5](https://ref.ly/Exod38:5), [38:6](https://ref.ly/Exod38:6), [38:7](https://ref.ly/Exod38:7), [39:39](https://ref.ly/Exod39:39), [Nu 4:14](https://ref.ly/Num4:14), or altar of incense [Ex 30:4](https://ref.ly/Exod30:4), [30:5](https://ref.ly/Exod30:5), [35:15](https://ref.ly/Exod35:15), [37:27](https://ref.ly/Exod37:27), [37:28](https://ref.ly/Exod37:28), [Nu 4:11](https://ref.ly/Num4:11); (*d*) more gen. *bars* (of fortress) [Ho 11:6](https://ref.ly/Hos11:6), (of a gate) [Jb 17:16](https://ref.ly/Job17:16) fig. בַּדֵּי שְׁאֹל (v. [Is 38:10](https://ref.ly/Isa38:10)).

> \[Str910] † בָּדָד **n.\[m.]** isolation, separation: [Is 27:10](https://ref.ly/Isa27:10) עִיר בְּצוּרָה בָּדָד the fenced city is *isolation*, i.e. is solitary (subst. for adj.: Dr§ 189\. 2\); more usu. as adv. accus., to signify *alone*, [Dt 32:12](https://ref.ly/Deut32:12); esp. with vbs. of dwelling, [Lv 13:46](https://ref.ly/Lev13:46) בָּדָד יֵשֵׁב he shall dwell *alone* (lit. *in isolation*), [Je 15:17](https://ref.ly/Jer15:17), [La 1:1](https://ref.ly/Lam1:1), [3:28](https://ref.ly/Lam3:28): fig. of freedom from attack, security [Dt 33:28](https://ref.ly/Deut33:28) (of Isr.), [Je 49:31](https://ref.ly/Jer49:31) (Kedar); so לְבָדָד [Nu 23:9](https://ref.ly/Num23:9) (Isr.) [Mi 7:14](https://ref.ly/Mic7:14), [Psalm 4:9](https://ref.ly/Ps4:9) for thou לְבָדָד לָבֶטַח תּוֺשִׁיבֵנִי makest me dwell *solitarily*, in safety (v. [Dt 33:28](https://ref.ly/Deut33:28)).

## II בדד (id: BDB01023)

 II. בדד (prob. i.q. בָּדָא q.v. *talk idly*).

> \[Str907] † III. \[בַּד] **n.m.** only *Pl.* בַּדִּים. **a.** *empty, idle talk* (Ph. CIS3, 6 listen not to בדנם \= Heb. בַּדֵּיהֶם; cf ܒܶܕܝܳܐ *vain talk*), esp. with collat. idea of imaginary pretensions or claims: [Jb 11:3](https://ref.ly/Job11:3) בַּדֶּיךָ מְתִים יַחֲרִישׁוּ thy *idle talk* brings men to silence (‖וַתִּלְעַג), [Is 16:6](https://ref.ly/Isa16:6) (of Moab) לאֹ כֵן בַּדָּיו his *boastings* are not right (unfounded), hence [Je 48:30](https://ref.ly/Jer48:30). **b.** concr. *empty talkers, praters* (cf. NH בַּדָּאָה, Syr. ܒܳܕܽܘܝܳܐ, *liar*), of false prophets, [Is 44:25](https://ref.ly/Isa44:25), [Je 50:36](https://ref.ly/Jer50:36).

> \[Str911] † בְּדַד **n.pr.m.** father of an Edom. ruler (הֲדַד) [Gn 36:35](https://ref.ly/Gen36:35) \= [1 Ch 1:46](https://ref.ly/1Chr1:46).

> \[Str1767] בְּדֵי v. דַּי

> \[Str912] † בֵּֽדְיָה **n.pr.m.** (\= עֲבֵדְיָה *servant of* ´י ? LXX Βαραια, LXXL Βαδαια) an Israelite [Ezr 10:35](https://ref.ly/Ezra10:35).

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 44:25; 1 Chronicles 1:46; Jeremiah 50:36; Jeremiah 48:30; Job 11:3; Isaiah 16:6; Genesis 36:35; Ezra 10:35
* **Associated Articles:** דַּי (ID: `BDB02101@BDBHebrewLexicon`)


## בָּדַל (id: BDB01028)

\[Str914] \[בָּדַל] **vb.** be divided, separate (Ar. بَدَلَ *change, substitute*, Mish. בָּדַל *divide*, Syr. ܒܕܰܠ Ithp. Ethp.)—**Hiph.** *divide, separate; Pf.* הִבְדִּיל [Nu 16:9](https://ref.ly/Num16:9), [Dt 10:8](https://ref.ly/Deut10:8); sf. וְהִבְדִּילוֺ consec. [Dt 29:20](https://ref.ly/Deut29:20); 3 fs. וְהִבְדִּילָה [Ex 26:33](https://ref.ly/Exod26:33); וְהִבְדַּלְתָּ [Nu 8:14](https://ref.ly/Num8:14); הִבְדִּילוּ Ez [22:26](https://ref.ly/Ezek22:26), etc. *Impf.* יַבְדִּיל [Lv 1:17](https://ref.ly/Lev1:17) \+; וַיַּבְדֵּל [Gn 1:4](https://ref.ly/Gen1:4), [1:7](https://ref.ly/Gen1:7), [1 Ch 25:1](https://ref.ly/1Chr25:1); sf. יַבְדִּילַנִי [Is 56:3](https://ref.ly/Isa56:3); וָאַבְדִּל [Lv 20:26](https://ref.ly/Lev20:26); וָאַבְדִּילָה [Ezr 8:24](https://ref.ly/Ezra8:24), etc.; *Inf. abs.* הַבְדֵּל [Is 56:3](https://ref.ly/Isa56:3); *cstr.* לְהַבְדִּיל [Gn 1:14](https://ref.ly/Gen1:14) \+; *Pt.* מַבְדִּיל [Gn 1:6](https://ref.ly/Gen1:6); מַבְדִּילִים [Is 59:2](https://ref.ly/Isa59:2); —**1\.** *divide, separate*, subj. God [Gn 1:4](https://ref.ly/Gen1:4) sq. וּבֵין…. בֵּין *between light and darkness*, cf. v:[7](https://ref.ly/Gen1:7); subj. heavenly bodies v:[14](https://ref.ly/Gen1:14), v:[18](https://ref.ly/Gen1:18); subj. firmament sq. לְ…בֵּין [Gn 1:6](https://ref.ly/Gen1:6) (all in P's source); [Ex 26:33](https://ref.ly/Exod26:33) of the פָּרֹכֶת in tab., sq. …וּבֵין…בֵּין לָכֶם; of iniquities, separating men from God לְבֵין…בֵּין [Is 59:2](https://ref.ly/Isa59:2). **2\.** *separate, set apart*, sq. מִן, of ´י, setting off Isr. from other peoples [Lv 20:24](https://ref.ly/Lev20:24) (H) [Nu 16:9](https://ref.ly/Num16:9) (P); [Lv 20:26](https://ref.ly/Lev20:26) sq. also לִהְיוֺת לִי; cf. [1 K 8:53](https://ref.ly/1Kgs8:53) לְךָ לְנַחֲלָה ´הִבְדּ *separate them to thyself as an inheritance;* of Moses, setting apart Levites, sq. מִתּוֺח [Nu 8:14](https://ref.ly/Num8:14); of separating an individual from the people, i.e. excluding him [Dt 29:20](https://ref.ly/Deut29:20), sq. also לְרָעָה; sq. מֵעַל [Is 56:3](https://ref.ly/Isa56:3), [56:3](https://ref.ly/Isa56:3), i.e. excluding him; cf. also [Ne 13:3](https://ref.ly/Neh13:3) sq. מִן; of setting apart (forbidding) beasts as unclean לָכֶם לְטַמֵּא [Lv 20:25](https://ref.ly/Lev20:25); of setting apart the tribe of Levi ´לָשֵאת וגו [Dt 10:8](https://ref.ly/Deut10:8) (subj. ´י); cf. also (hum. subj.) [1 Ch 25:1](https://ref.ly/1Chr25:1), sq. לַעֲבֹדָה, [2 Ch 25:10](https://ref.ly/2Chr25:10) sq. Inf.; similarly [Ezr 8:24](https://ref.ly/Ezra8:24); even sq. ptcp. Ez [39:14](https://ref.ly/Ezek39:14) they shall set apart men עֹבְרִים, i.e. *to pass through*, or, *men that shall pass through* (RV); so of setting apart cities, acc. [Dt 4:41](https://ref.ly/Deut4:41) cf. [19:2](https://ref.ly/Deut19:2), [19:7](https://ref.ly/Deut19:7). **3\.** *make a distinction* between clean and unclean, holy and profane, sq. וּבֵין…בֵּין [Lv 20:25](https://ref.ly/Lev20:25) (H); [10:10](https://ref.ly/Lev10:10), [11:47](https://ref.ly/Lev11:47) (both P); sq. לְ…בֵּין Ez [22:26](https://ref.ly/Ezek22:26); [42:20](https://ref.ly/Ezek42:20). **4\.** *divide into parts* [Lv 1:17](https://ref.ly/Lev1:17); [5:8](https://ref.ly/Lev5:8) (P), prohibited in case of fowls offered in sacrifice. **Niph.** *Pf.* נִבְדְּלוּ [1 Ch 12:8](https://ref.ly/1Chr12:8), [Ezr 9:1](https://ref.ly/Ezra9:1); *Impf.* יִבָּדֵל [Ezr 10:8](https://ref.ly/Ezra10:8); וַיִּבָּדֵל [1 Ch 23:13](https://ref.ly/1Chr23:13); וַיִּבָּֽדְלוּ [Ezr 10:16](https://ref.ly/Ezra10:16), [Ne 9:2](https://ref.ly/Neh9:2); *Imv.* הִבָּֽדְלוּ [Nu 16:21](https://ref.ly/Num16:21), [Ezr 10:11](https://ref.ly/Ezra10:11); *Pt.* נִבְדָּל [Ezr 6:21](https://ref.ly/Ezra6:21), [Ne 10:29](https://ref.ly/Neh10:29). **1\.** (reflex. of **Hiph. 2**) **a.** *separate oneself* from people of the land, heathen, and their practices, also from non\-Jewish wives, sq. מִן [Ezr 6:21](https://ref.ly/Ezra6:21), [9:1](https://ref.ly/Ezra9:1), [10:11](https://ref.ly/Ezra10:11), [Ne 9:2](https://ref.ly/Neh9:2); abs. in same sense [Ezr 10:16](https://ref.ly/Ezra10:16) (cf. StaG. ii. 199 \& 179 n. Wl: Pharisäer u. Sadd. 76\). **b.** *withdraw from* (Moses \& Aaron from Israel) [Nu 16:21](https://ref.ly/Num16:21) (P), sq. מִתּוֺח. **c.** *separate oneself* unto (אֶל) David [1 Ch 12:8](https://ref.ly/1Chr12:8). **d.** [Ne 10:29](https://ref.ly/Neh10:29) combines **a.** \& **c.** *separate oneself* מֵעַמֵּי הָֽאֲרָצוֺת אֶל־תּוֺרַת הָאֱלֽהִים. **2**: pass., *be separated*. **a.** *be excluded* from the people, sq. מִן [Ezr 10:8](https://ref.ly/Ezra10:8). ב: *be set apart*, of Aaron, sq. לְהַקְדִּישׁוֺ [1 Ch 23:13](https://ref.ly/1Chr23:13).—On הבדיל, \& distinction fr. הפריד v. DrJPh. xi. 219\.

> \[Str915] † \[בָּדָל] **n.\[m.]** piece, severed piece, cstr. בְּדַל־אֹזֶן [Am 3:12](https://ref.ly/Amos3:12).

> \[Str3995] † \[מִבְדָּלָה] **n.f.** separate place, הַמִּבְדָּלוֺת [Jos 16:9](https://ref.ly/Josh16:9) (appos. הֶעָרִים); but rd. prob. הַמֻּבְדָּלוֺת **Hoph.** *Pt.* fr. בדל, or הַנִּבְדָּלוֺת **Niph.** *Pt.*, cf. Di.

> \[Str913] † בְּדִיל **n.\[m.]** alloy, tin, dross (orig. *that which is separated* from precious metal; cf. Plin.:Hist. Nat. xxiv. 16, xxxiii. 9\)—´בּ abs. [Nu 31:22](https://ref.ly/Num31:22) \+ 4 t.; Pl. sf. בְּדִילָיִח [Is 1:25](https://ref.ly/Isa1:25); —**1\.** *alloy*, [Is 1:25](https://ref.ly/Isa1:25) fig. of evil of Jerusalem, which ´י will remove (הֵסִיר; ‖סִגָיִח). **2\.** *tin* (*plumbum album*) [Nu 31:22](https://ref.ly/Num31:22) (P; \+ זָהָב, כֶּסֶף, נְחשֶׁת, בַּרְזֶל, עֹפָרֶת); fig. of Isr. Ez [22:18](https://ref.ly/Ezek22:18) (\+ נְחשֶׁת, בַּרְזֶל, עוֺפֶרֶת); in simile v:[20](https://ref.ly/Ezek22:20) (\+ id. \+ כֶּסֶף); as article of commerce brought to Tyre from Tarshish Ez [27:12](https://ref.ly/Ezek27:12) (\+ כֶּסֶף, בַּרְזֶל, עוֺפֶרֶת). **3\.** *plummet*, הָאֶבֶן הַבְּדִיל (appos.) [Zc 4:10](https://ref.ly/Zech4:10).

> \[Str916] † בְּדֹ֫לַח **n.\[m.]** prob. bdellium (SyrVer ܒܶܪܽܘܠܚܳܐ; Targum בְּדוּלְחָא; etym. dub.; LagGes. Abh. 20 prop. Skr. *udûkhala*) c. art. ´הַבְּ, apparently therefore well known; one of the products of the land Havilah [Gn 2:12](https://ref.ly/Gen2:12); used in simile of colour of manna וְעֵינוֺ כְּעֵין הַבְּדֹלַח [Nu 11:7](https://ref.ly/Num11:7).—Meaning somewhat uncertain; LXX [Gn 2:12](https://ref.ly/Gen2:12) ἄνθραξ, [Nu 11:7](https://ref.ly/Num11:7) κρύ σταλλος; Saad. AW Ki Bo al. *pearls*, cf. Lag Or. ii. 44; JosAnt. iii. 1\. 6 Aq Theod Symm Targum most *bdellium*, an odoriferous transparent gum, of yellowish colour. (Cf. SmithDict. Bible SigismundAromata, 18\.)

> \[Str917] † בְּדָן **n.pr.m.** (\= עַבְדָּן ?). **1\.** a judge of Israel [1 S 12:11](https://ref.ly/1Sam12:11); but rd prob. בָּרָק, so LXX SyrVer We, cf. Dr (v. Ju [4:6](https://ref.ly/Judg4:6) f.). **2\.** a Manassite [1 Ch 7:17](https://ref.ly/1Chr7:17).

## בדק (id: BDB01034)

בדק (*penetrate, split* ? cf. Aram. בְּדַק, ܒܕܰܩ *explore;* but these perh. denom. cf. LagGN 1882, 400\).

> \[Str919] † בֶּ֫דֶק **n.m.**:[2 K 12:6](https://ref.ly/2Kgs12:6) fissure, rent, breach (Aram. בִּידְקָא, ܒܕܩܐ (Nasar.))—´בּ abs. [2 K 12:6](https://ref.ly/2Kgs12:6); cstr. *ib.* \+ 6 t.; sf. בִּדְקֵח Ez [27:9](https://ref.ly/Ezek27:9), [27:27](https://ref.ly/Ezek27:27); —in as building; temple הַבַּיִת ´ב [2 K 12:6](https://ref.ly/2Kgs12:6), [12:7](https://ref.ly/2Kgs12:7), [12:8](https://ref.ly/2Kgs12:8), [12:8](https://ref.ly/2Kgs12:8), [12:9](https://ref.ly/2Kgs12:9), [22:5](https://ref.ly/2Kgs22:5), cf. [12:13](https://ref.ly/2Kgs12:13) (´בֵּית י ´ב); v:[6](https://ref.ly/2Kgs12:6) abs.; alw. c. חִזֵּק *repair;* cf. מַחֲזִיקֵי ´ב *repairers of thy fissures* Ez [27:9](https://ref.ly/Ezek27:9), [27:27](https://ref.ly/Ezek27:27) (of Tyre).

> \[Str918] † \[בָּדַק] **vb. denom.** mend, repair, only **Qal** *Inf. cstr.* לִבְדוֺק וּלְחַזֵּק הַבָּיִת [2 Ch 34:10](https://ref.ly/2Chr34:10).

> \[Str920] † בִּדְקַר **n.pr.m.** Isr. officer [2 K 9:25](https://ref.ly/2Kgs9:25) (v. בֵּן).
> 
> 96:a. Before בִּדְקֵר, ins.: ‘† \[בָּדַק] **vb. denom.** mend fissures (of);—**Qal** *Inf. cstr.* לִדְבּוֺק [2 Ch 34:10](https://ref.ly/2Chr34:10) (\+ וּלְחַוֵּק; acc. הַבָּיִת).’

* **Associated Articles:** בֵּן (ID: `BDB01286@BDBHebrewLexicon`)


## בהה (id: BDB01038)

בהה (cf. Ar. بَهِىَ *be empty;* on a possible connexion in As. cf. HptBAS i. 18\).

> \[Str922] † בֹּ֫הוּ **n.\[m.]** emptiness (on form v. Ges§ 84 a, 1 b Sta§ 95, 198 a, on usage cf. LagOr. ii. 60 f.) alw. c. תֹּהוּ q.v.;—תֹּהוּ וָבֹהוּ [Gn 1:2](https://ref.ly/Gen1:2) of primæval earth; [Je 4:23](https://ref.ly/Jer4:23) of earth under judgment of ´י; קַו־תַהֹוּ וְאַבְנֵי בֹהוּ [Is 34:11](https://ref.ly/Isa34:11), *the line of wasteness and the stones of emptiness*, i.e. plummets, employed, not as usual for building, but for destroying walls; cf. Di \& v. sub אבן **6\.**

> \[Str923] † בַּ֫הַט **n.\[m.]** a costly stone, perh. porphyry (cf. Egypt. *behiti, behet, behat* BrugschDict. v. 438 DümichenGesch. 167 f. WendelAltäg. Bau\- u. Edelsteine (1888\) 77 f.; LXX has σμαραγδίτης), [Est 1:6](https://ref.ly/Esth1:6) עַל ־וָשֵׁשׁ וְדַר וְסֹחָרֶת´רִֽצֲפַת ב *upon a pavement of porphyry and marble*, etc.

## בָּהַל (id: BDB01041)

\[Str926] † \[בָּהַל] **vb.** (NH id. Pt. pass. בהול *disquieted;* Pi. *disquiet;* Targum Pa. בַּהֵיל *hasten, be precipitate*, also *dismay:* but ܒܗܶܠ is *be quiet*)—**Niph.** *Pf.* נִבְהַל [1 S 28:21](https://ref.ly/1Sam28:21); נִבְהֲלָה [Psalm 6:4](https://ref.ly/Ps6:4); נִבְהַלְתִּי [Is 21:3](https://ref.ly/Isa21:3); וְנִבְהָלְתִּי consec. [Jb 21:6](https://ref.ly/Job21:6); נִבְהֲלוּ [Gn 45:3](https://ref.ly/Gen45:3) \+, etc.; *Impf.* וַיִּבָּהֵל Ju [20:41](https://ref.ly/Judg20:41); 2 ms. תִּבָּהֵל [Ec 8:3](https://ref.ly/Eccl8:3); יִבָּהֵל\+וּן [Psalm 104:29](https://ref.ly/Ps104:29), etc.; *Pt.* נִבְהָל [Psalm 30:8](https://ref.ly/Ps30:8); נִֽבֳהָל [Pr 28:22](https://ref.ly/Prov28:22); f. נִבְהָלָה [Zp 1:18](https://ref.ly/Zeph1:18); —**1\.** *be disturbed, dismayed, terrified,* [Gn 45:3](https://ref.ly/Gen45:3) (E) Ju [20:41](https://ref.ly/Judg20:41), [1 S 28:21](https://ref.ly/1Sam28:21), [2 S 4:1](https://ref.ly/2Sam4:1) (‖וַיִּרְפּוּ יָדָיו, cf. Ez [7:27](https://ref.ly/Ezek7:27) supr.); of bones of sufferer [Psalm 6:3](https://ref.ly/Ps6:3) (‖ cf. infr. v:[4](https://ref.ly/Ps6:4)); of hands of dismayed people Ez [7:27](https://ref.ly/Ezek7:27); esp. at chastisements \& judgments of ´י [Ex 15:15](https://ref.ly/Exod15:15) (song, in E) [Psalm 6:4](https://ref.ly/Ps6:4) (subj. נֶפֶשׁ, ‖ cf. supr. v:[3](https://ref.ly/Ps6:3)) v:[11](https://ref.ly/Ps6:10) (‖בושׁ) [30:8](https://ref.ly/Ps30:8); cf. [104:29](https://ref.ly/Ps104:29); [83:18](https://ref.ly/Ps83:18) (‖בושׁ) [90:7](https://ref.ly/Ps90:7), [Is 13:8](https://ref.ly/Isa13:8), [21:3](https://ref.ly/Isa21:3) (sq. מִן of occasion of fear) [Jb 23:15](https://ref.ly/Job23:15) (*id.*) [Je 51:32](https://ref.ly/Jer51:32), Ez [26:18](https://ref.ly/Ezek26:18) (del. LXX Co); [Jb 4:5](https://ref.ly/Job4:5) (‖לאה), cf. also [21:6](https://ref.ly/Job21:6) as adj. *terrible* [Zp 1:18](https://ref.ly/Zeph1:18) כָּלָה אַח־נִבְהָלָה. **2\.** *be in haste, hasty* (late, cf. Aram. above): [Ec 8:3](https://ref.ly/Eccl8:3) מִפָּנָיו תֵּלֵח ´אַל־תִּבּ *be not hasty (to) go from him;* [Pr 28:22](https://ref.ly/Prov28:22) נִבֳהָל לַהוֺן *hastening after riches.* **Pi.** *Impf.* וַיְבַהֵל [Est 2:9](https://ref.ly/Esth2:9); sf. יְבַהֲלֻהוּ [Dn 11:44](https://ref.ly/Dan11:44); וִיבַהֶלְךָ [Jb 22:10](https://ref.ly/Job22:10); יְבַהֲלֵמוֺ [Psalm 2:5](https://ref.ly/Ps2:5); 2 ms. תְּבַהֲלֵם [Psalm 83:16](https://ref.ly/Ps83:16); אַל־תְּבַהֵל [Ex 5:1](https://ref.ly/Exod5:1), [7:9](https://ref.ly/Exod7:9); *Inf. sf.* לְבַהֲלֵנִי [2 Ch 35:21](https://ref.ly/2Chr35:21); ם—ָ [32:18](https://ref.ly/2Chr32:18); *Part.* מבלהים Kt, מְבַהֲלִים Qr [Ezr 4:4](https://ref.ly/Ezra4:4) (BeRy pref. Kt, v. בלהּ);—**1\.** *dismay, terrify,* sq. sf. [2 Ch 32:18](https://ref.ly/2Chr32:18) (‖ירא), [Dn 11:44](https://ref.ly/Dan11:44), [Jb 22:10](https://ref.ly/Job22:10) (subj. פַּחַד), [Psalm 2:5](https://ref.ly/Ps2:5) (subj. ´י) [83:16](https://ref.ly/Ps83:16) (‖רדף; subj. ´י); cf. also [Ezr 4:4](https://ref.ly/Ezra4:4) (v. sub בלהּ). **2\.** *hasten, make haste, act hastily* (late), [2 Ch 35:21](https://ref.ly/2Chr35:21) וֵאלֽהִים אָמַר לְבַהֲלֵנִי *God hath given command to speed me* (RVm); sq. inf. *make haste* [Est 2:9](https://ref.ly/Esth2:9); of hasty speech [Ec 5:1](https://ref.ly/Eccl5:1) ´אַל־תְּב עַל־פִּיךָ (‖וְלִבְּךָ אַל־יְמַהֵר לְהוֺצִיא דָבָר); of anger [Ec 7:9](https://ref.ly/Eccl7:9) בְּרוּחֲךָ לִכְעוֺס ´אַל־תְּב. **Pu.** *Pt.* pl. מְבֹהָלִים [Est 8:14](https://ref.ly/Esth8:14), cf. מְבֹהֶלֶת Qr [Pr 20:21](https://ref.ly/Prov20:21) (so rd. with Vrss Now Str; AV RV); Kt מבחלת v. בחל;—*hastened* [Est 8:14](https://ref.ly/Esth8:14) of royal posts (‖דְּחוּפִים); *hastily gained* ´נַחֲלָה מְב [Pr 20:21](https://ref.ly/Prov20:21). **Hiph.** *Pf.* sf. הִבְהִילָנִי [Jb 23:16](https://ref.ly/Job23:16); *Impf.* sf. וַיַּבְהִלוּהוּ [2 Ch 26:20](https://ref.ly/2Chr26:20); 3 mpl. וַיַּבְהִלוּ [Est 6:14](https://ref.ly/Esth6:14); —**1\.** *dismay, terrify,* sq. sf. [Jb 23:16](https://ref.ly/Job23:16) (subj. שַׁדַּי; ‖הֵרַח לִבִּי). **2\.** (late) *hasten, hurry* (trans.), [2 Ch 26:20](https://ref.ly/2Chr26:20) וַיַּבְהִלוּהוּ מִשָּׁם *and they hurried him thence* (‖נִדְחַף); *make haste,* sq. inf. [Est 6:14](https://ref.ly/Esth6:14) לְהָבִיא ´וַיַּב *and they make haste to bring* Haman.

> \[Str928] † בֶּֽהָלָה **n.f.** dismay, sudden terror or ruin (cf. As. *bêltu, terror,* DlPr 32\)—´בּ abs. [Lv 26:16](https://ref.ly/Lev26:16) \+ 2 t.; pl. וּבֶֽהָלוֺת [Je 15:8](https://ref.ly/Jer15:8); —*sudden terror* [Lv 26:16](https://ref.ly/Lev26:16) ´וְהִפְקַדְתִּ֫י עֲלֵיכֶם בּ (appositives follow); cf. [Je 15:8](https://ref.ly/Jer15:8); [Is 65:23](https://ref.ly/Isa65:23) לאֹ יֵֽלְדוּ לַבֶּהָלָה, [Psalm 78:33](https://ref.ly/Ps78:33) וַיְכַל־בַּהֶבֶל יְמֵיהֶָם וּשְׁנוֺתָם בַּבֶּהָלֶה *and he ended their days in a breath, and their years in sudden terror.*

## בהם (id: BDB01043)

בהם (Ar. بهم, IV. *shut,* x. *impeded in speech, tongue\-tied;* Eth. ብህመ፡ *be dumb*).

> \[Str929] בְּהֵמָה:187 **n.f.** beast, animal, cattle (Ar. بَهِيمَةٌ)—´בּ [Gn 1:24](https://ref.ly/Gen1:24) \+ 137 t.; cstr. בֶּהֱמַת [Nu 3:41](https://ref.ly/Num3:41), [3:41](https://ref.ly/Num3:41) \+ 10 t.; sf. בְּהֶמְתְּךָ [Lv 19:19](https://ref.ly/Lev19:19) \+ 4 t.; בְּהֶמְתֶָּךָ [Ex 20:10](https://ref.ly/Exod20:10) \+ 4 t.; בְּהֶמְתּוֺ [Gn 36:6](https://ref.ly/Gen36:6), [Pr 12:10](https://ref.ly/Prov12:10); pl. abs. בְּהֵמוֺת [Dt 32:24](https://ref.ly/Deut32:24) \+ 6 t. \+ [Psalm 73:22](https://ref.ly/Ps73:22) (v. infr.); cstr. בַּהֲמוֺת [Psalm 8:8](https://ref.ly/Ps8:8) \+ 5 t.;—*beast,* \& coll. *beasts* ([Gn 8:1](https://ref.ly/Gen8:1) \+ very oft.) **1\.** of living creatures other than man (אָדָם) [Gn 8:1](https://ref.ly/Gen8:1), [Ex 8:13](https://ref.ly/Exod8:13), [8:14](https://ref.ly/Exod8:14), [9:9](https://ref.ly/Exod9:9), [9:10](https://ref.ly/Exod9:10), [9:22](https://ref.ly/Exod9:22), [22:18](https://ref.ly/Exod22:18), [Lv 18:23](https://ref.ly/Lev18:23), [18:23](https://ref.ly/Lev18:23) (where ´בּ is male, so [20:16](https://ref.ly/Lev20:16)) [Dt 27:21](https://ref.ly/Deut27:21), [Psalm 36:7](https://ref.ly/Ps36:7) etc.; ´מֵאָדָם וְעַד בּ (´בּ including all the larger animals) [Ex 9:25](https://ref.ly/Exod9:25), [12:12](https://ref.ly/Exod12:12), [Psalm 135:8](https://ref.ly/Ps135:8), [Je 50:3](https://ref.ly/Jer50:3) etc.; as inferior to man [Jb 18:3](https://ref.ly/Job18:3), [Psalm 49:13](https://ref.ly/Ps49:13), [49:21](https://ref.ly/Ps49:20), so also [Ec 3:18](https://ref.ly/Eccl3:18), [3:19](https://ref.ly/Eccl3:19), [3:19](https://ref.ly/Eccl3:19), [3:21](https://ref.ly/Eccl3:21); opp. also birds \& reptiles [Gn 6:7](https://ref.ly/Gen6:7), [7:23](https://ref.ly/Gen7:23), [8:17](https://ref.ly/Gen8:17) cf. Ez [44:31](https://ref.ly/Ezek44:31) etc.; also to fishes [1 K 5:13](https://ref.ly/1Kgs5:13), [Jb 12:7](https://ref.ly/Job12:7) cf. v:[8](https://ref.ly/Job12:8), [Psalm 8:9](https://ref.ly/Ps8:9), [Zp 1:3](https://ref.ly/Zeph1:3). **2\.** opp. also to wild beasts חַיַּת הָאָרֶץ, הַשָּדֶה ´ח [Gn 1:24](https://ref.ly/Gen1:24), [1:25](https://ref.ly/Gen1:25), [1:26](https://ref.ly/Gen1:26), [2:20](https://ref.ly/Gen2:20), [3:14](https://ref.ly/Gen3:14), [7:14](https://ref.ly/Gen7:14), [7:21](https://ref.ly/Gen7:21), [9:10](https://ref.ly/Gen9:10), [Psalm 50:10](https://ref.ly/Ps50:10), [148:10](https://ref.ly/Ps148:10), [Is 46:1](https://ref.ly/Isa46:1) etc.; esp. therefore *cattle*, as owned and used by man, [Gn 47:18](https://ref.ly/Gen47:18) (´מִקְנֵה הַבּ) [Ex 20:10](https://ref.ly/Exod20:10) \= [Dt 5:14](https://ref.ly/Deut5:14), [Lv 19:19](https://ref.ly/Lev19:19), [26:22](https://ref.ly/Lev26:22), [Nu 3:41](https://ref.ly/Num3:41), [3:41](https://ref.ly/Num3:41), [3:45](https://ref.ly/Num3:45), [3:45](https://ref.ly/Num3:45), [Dt 2:35](https://ref.ly/Deut2:35), [11:15](https://ref.ly/Deut11:15), [Jon 4:11](https://ref.ly/Jonah4:11), [Zc 2:8](https://ref.ly/Zech2:8), [8:10](https://ref.ly/Zech8:10), [14:15](https://ref.ly/Zech14:15), [Ezr 1:4](https://ref.ly/Ezra1:4), [1:6](https://ref.ly/Ezra1:6), [Ne 9:37](https://ref.ly/Neh9:37), [10:37](https://ref.ly/Neh10:37) etc.; in one (late) passage of animal for riding (horse ? mule ?) [Ne 2:12](https://ref.ly/Neh2:12), [2:12](https://ref.ly/Neh2:12), [2:14](https://ref.ly/Neh2:14). **3\.** rarely of *wild beasts*, esp. carnivora, יַעַר ´בּ [Mi 5:7](https://ref.ly/Mic5:7); הָאָרֶץ ´בּ [Dt 28:26](https://ref.ly/Deut28:26), [Is 18:6](https://ref.ly/Isa18:6), [18:6](https://ref.ly/Isa18:6), [Je 7:33](https://ref.ly/Jer7:33), [15:3](https://ref.ly/Jer15:3), [16:4](https://ref.ly/Jer16:4), [19:7](https://ref.ly/Jer19:7), [34:20](https://ref.ly/Jer34:20); הַשָּדֶה ´בּ [1 S 17:44](https://ref.ly/1Sam17:44); alone, [Pr 30:30](https://ref.ly/Prov30:30); שֶׁן־בְּהֵמוֺת [Dt 32:24](https://ref.ly/Deut32:24); ´שֹׁד בּ Hb [2:17](https://ref.ly/Hab2:17).—On בְּהֵמוֺת [Psalm 73:22](https://ref.ly/Ps73:22) cf. infr.

> \[Str930] † בְּהֵמוֺת **n.m.** behemoth, i.e. hippopotamus (appar. pl. intens. of foreg.; acc. to Di [Jb 40:15](https://ref.ly/Job40:15) cf. De [Is 30:6](https://ref.ly/Isa30:6) fr. an (assumed) Egyptain *p\-ehemau, ox of the water*) [Jb 40:15](https://ref.ly/Job40:15) (on identity, cf. further BoHieroz. iii. 705\); prob. also [Psalm 73:22](https://ref.ly/Ps73:22) בְּהֵמוֺת הָיִיתִי עִמָּח *a behemoth was I with* (*toward*) *thee* (so Hi De; Che *beasts*); acc. to De Or also in בַּהֲמוֺת [Is 30:6](https://ref.ly/Isa30:6) *the burden of the behemoth of the south* (supposed to be a designation of Egypt; but this unlikely, cf. Che Di, rd. therefore) rather *beasts of the south*, viz. of Judah.

## בהן (id: BDB01046)

בהן (cf. Ar. بَهَمَ IV. *shut, cover* (v. foregoing) whence also በርሀ፡ \= בֹּהֶן; as closing and covering the hand, cf. Lane).

Sam. for MT בֹּהֶן);—*thumb* (יָדָם) יָדוֺ ´בּ, \& *great toe* (דַגְלָם) רַגְלוֺ ´בּ (always named together) [Ex 29:20](https://ref.ly/Exod29:20), [29:20](https://ref.ly/Exod29:20), [Lv 8:23](https://ref.ly/Lev8:23), [8:23](https://ref.ly/Lev8:23), [8:24](https://ref.ly/Lev8:24), [8:24](https://ref.ly/Lev8:24), [14:14](https://ref.ly/Lev14:14), [14:14](https://ref.ly/Lev14:14), [14:17](https://ref.ly/Lev14:17), [14:17](https://ref.ly/Lev14:17), [14:25](https://ref.ly/Lev14:25), [14:25](https://ref.ly/Lev14:25), [14:28](https://ref.ly/Lev14:28), [14:28](https://ref.ly/Lev14:28); ´וְר ´בְּהֹנוֺת י Ju [1:6](https://ref.ly/Judg1:6), [1:7](https://ref.ly/Judg1:7).

> \[Str932] † בֹּ֫הַן **n.pr.m.** (*closing, covering ?*) ´אֶבֶן בּ בֶּן־רְאוּבֵן [Jos 15:6](https://ref.ly/Josh15:6), [18:17](https://ref.ly/Josh18:17), a mark of division between Judah \& Benjamin.

## בהק (id: BDB01049)

בהק (NH בָּהַק *shine,* Aram. בְּהַק, بَاهِمٌ, Aph. *id.*; hence בַּהֲקִיתָא, بِهَامٌ, an eruption, v. sq.)

## בהר (id: BDB01051)

בהר (NH Hiph. *be bright, shine;* cf. Aram. בְּהַר, ܒܗܩܝܬܐ (not Pe.) \& deriv.; Eth. بَهَرَ Ar. ܒܗܪ *surpass*, esp. in brightness, *shine brightly*).

> \[Str934] † בַּהֶ֫רֶת **n.f.** brightness, bright spot, of eruption on skin (NH *id.*, Aram. בַּהְרָא)—´בּ abs. [Lv 13:2](https://ref.ly/Lev13:2) \+ 7 t.; בֶּהָרֶת [Lv 14:56](https://ref.ly/Lev14:56); pl. abs. בֶּהָרוֺת [Lv 13:38](https://ref.ly/Lev13:38), [13:39](https://ref.ly/Lev13:39); —*bright spot* (sore, scar, etc.), clean or unclean [Lv 14:56](https://ref.ly/Lev14:56); following a burn (from fire) [Lv 13:24](https://ref.ly/Lev13:24), [13:25](https://ref.ly/Lev13:25), [13:26](https://ref.ly/Lev13:26), [13:28](https://ref.ly/Lev13:28), possible beginning of leprosy [Lv 13:2](https://ref.ly/Lev13:2), [13:4](https://ref.ly/Lev13:4), [13:19](https://ref.ly/Lev13:19), [13:23](https://ref.ly/Lev13:23); but possibly a (passing) eruption מִסְפַּחַת v:[6](https://ref.ly/Lev13:6); or due to a boil שְׁחִין v:[19](https://ref.ly/Lev13:19); v:[23](https://ref.ly/Lev13:23); —due to בֹּהַק (q. v.) [Lv 13:38](https://ref.ly/Lev13:38), [13:39](https://ref.ly/Lev13:39).

> \[Str925] † בָּהִירִ **adj.** bright, brilliant, of light; only [Jb 37:21](https://ref.ly/Job37:21) הוּא בַּשְּׁחָקִים ´לאֹ רָאוּ אוֺר בּ.

* **Associated Passages:** Leviticus 13:38; Job 37:21; Leviticus 13:19; Leviticus 13:4; Leviticus 13:6; Leviticus 14:56; Leviticus 13:23; Leviticus 13:24; Leviticus 13:2; Leviticus 13:28; Leviticus 13:26; Leviticus 13:39; Leviticus 13:25


## בּוֺא (id: BDB01054)

\[Str935] בּוֺא:2569 **vb.** come in, come, go in, go (As. *bâ’u* HptKAT 499, Eth. ቦአ፡ Ar. بَآءَ *return*)—**Qal** *Pf.* בָּא [Gn 6:13](https://ref.ly/Gen6:13) \+; 3 fs. בָּ֫אָה [Gn 15:17](https://ref.ly/Gen15:17) \+; sf. בָּאַתְנוּ [Psalm 44:18](https://ref.ly/Ps44:18); 2 ms. בָּ֫אתָ [Jos 13:1](https://ref.ly/Josh13:1) \+, בָּ֫אתָה [2 S 3:7](https://ref.ly/2Sam3:7); 2 fs. בָּאתְ [Gn 16:8](https://ref.ly/Gen16:8), [Ru 2:12](https://ref.ly/Ruth2:12), וּבָאתְ consec.
