[Lv 16:27](https://ref.ly/Lev16:27), Ez [30:11](https://ref.ly/Ezek30:11); sq. מִן [Je 10:9](https://ref.ly/Jer10:9). **c.** *be introduced, put*, sq. בְּ, staves into rings [Ex 27:7](https://ref.ly/Exod27:7); vessel into water [Lv 11:32](https://ref.ly/Lev11:32).

> \[Str872] † בִּאָה **n.f.** entrance, entry, בַּבִּאָה Ez [8:5](https://ref.ly/Ezek8:5), i.e. of temple (but del. B Co).

> \[Str3996,3997] † מָבוֺא **n.m.**:[Je 38:14](https://ref.ly/Jer38:14) entrance, a coming in, entering;—´מ abs. [Je 38:14](https://ref.ly/Jer38:14) \+ 3 t. \+ Ez [42:9](https://ref.ly/Ezek42:9) Kt (Qr מֵבִיא wrong, cf. Co); cstr. מְבוֺא [Dt 11:30](https://ref.ly/Deut11:30) \+ 12 t.; sf. מְבוֺאוֺ [Psalm 104:19](https://ref.ly/Ps104:19) \+ 2 t.; מְבֹאוֺ [Psalm 50:1](https://ref.ly/Ps50:1); מבואך [2 S 3:25](https://ref.ly/2Sam3:25) Kt (Qr מוֺבָאֶָךָ inexplicable cf. Dr); pl. cstr. מְבוֺאֵי Ez [26:10](https://ref.ly/Ezek26:10), מְבוֺאֹת Ez [27:3](https://ref.ly/Ezek27:3); —**1\.** *entrance,* i.e. place or way of entrance, into a city Ju [1:24](https://ref.ly/Judg1:24), [1:25](https://ref.ly/Judg1:25), [1 Ch 4:39](https://ref.ly/1Chr4:39), [Pr 8:3](https://ref.ly/Prov8:3) (פְּתָחִים ´מ); into buildings [2 K 11:16](https://ref.ly/2Kgs11:16), [16:18](https://ref.ly/2Kgs16:18), [1 Ch 9:19](https://ref.ly/1Chr9:19) (שֹׁמְרֵי הַמָּבוֺא), [2 Ch 23:13](https://ref.ly/2Chr23:13), [23:15](https://ref.ly/2Chr23:15), [Je 38:14](https://ref.ly/Jer38:14), Ez [42:9](https://ref.ly/Ezek42:9), [46:19](https://ref.ly/Ezek46:19); v. also מְבוֺאֹת יָם *entrance of (the) sea, gate of the sea*, of situation of Tyre Ez [27:3](https://ref.ly/Ezek27:3). **2\.** *entering,* act of entrance, by violence, in storm of city Ez [26:10](https://ref.ly/Ezek26:10); *coming in* or together, making a crowd, כִּמְבוֺא עָם Ez [33:31](https://ref.ly/Ezek33:31) sim. of eagerness to hear Yahweh's word; in phrase אֶת־מוֺצָאֲךָ וְאֶת־מבואך [2 S 3:25](https://ref.ly/2Sam3:25) *thy going out and thy coming in, mode of entering temple,* or *the people who enter* Ez [44:5](https://ref.ly/Ezek44:5); particularly of *sunset* מְכוֺא הַשֶּׁמֶשׁ [Psalm 104:19](https://ref.ly/Ps104:19); \= *west* [Dt 11:30](https://ref.ly/Deut11:30); [Jos 1:4](https://ref.ly/Josh1:4), [23:4](https://ref.ly/Josh23:4) הַיָּם הַגָּרוֺל מְבוֺא הַשֶּׁמֶשׁ \= Mediterranean, [Zc 8:7](https://ref.ly/Zech8:7) אֶרֶץ מְבוֺא הַשֶּׁמֶשׁ (‖אֶרֶץ מִזְרָח), [Mal 1:11](https://ref.ly/Mal1:11), [Psalm 50:1](https://ref.ly/Ps50:1), [113:3](https://ref.ly/Ps113:3) *sunset* \= *west*, opp. sunrise \= east; in phr. *from E. to W.*, i.e. over the whole earth, everywhere on earth.

> \[Str4126] † \[מוֺבָא] **n.\[m.]** in \- coming, entrance, מוֺבָאֶָךָ Qr [2 S 3:25](https://ref.ly/2Sam3:25), cf. Kt sub מבוא; וּמוֺבָאָיו Ez [43:11](https://ref.ly/Ezek43:11) *and its entrances* (‖וּמוֺצָאָיו), del. B Co. In both, ungramm. form for assonance with מוצא.

> \[Str8393] † תְּבוּאָה **n.f.** *proventus*, product, revenue—´תּ abs. [Psalm 107:37](https://ref.ly/Ps107:37) \+ 3 t.; cstr. תְּבוּאַת [Lv 23:39](https://ref.ly/Lev23:39) \+ 11 t.; sf. תְּבוּאָתִי [Jb 31:12](https://ref.ly/Job31:12), [Pr 8:19](https://ref.ly/Prov8:19); תְּבוּאָתְּךָ [Dt 14:28](https://ref.ly/Deut14:28) \+ 2 t.; תְּבוּאָתֶָךָ [Pr 3:9](https://ref.ly/Prov3:9); תְּכוּאָתוֺ [Lv 19:25](https://ref.ly/Lev19:25) \+ Ez [48:18](https://ref.ly/Ezek48:18) Qr (Kt תבואתה), תְּבוּאָתֹה [Je 2:3](https://ref.ly/Jer2:3); תְּבוּאָתָהּ [Ex 23:10](https://ref.ly/Exod23:10) \+ 7 t. \+ Ez [48:18](https://ref.ly/Ezek48:18) Kt (so Co); תְּבוּאָתֵנוּ [Lv 25:20](https://ref.ly/Lev25:20); pl. תְּבוּאֹת [Lv 25:15](https://ref.ly/Lev25:15) \+ 4 t.; תְּכוּאוֺת [Pr 14:4](https://ref.ly/Prov14:4), [16:8](https://ref.ly/Prov16:8); תֵּבוּאֹתֵיכֶם [Je 12:13](https://ref.ly/Jer12:13); —**1\.** *product, yield*, usually of earth (\= crops, etc.) הָאָרֶץ ´תּ [Ex 23:10](https://ref.ly/Exod23:10), [Lv 19:25](https://ref.ly/Lev19:25), [23:29](https://ref.ly/Lev23:29), [25:3](https://ref.ly/Lev25:3), [25:7](https://ref.ly/Lev25:7) (used as food for man \& beast, cf. v:[22](https://ref.ly/Lev25:22)) [Jos 5:12](https://ref.ly/Josh5:12), cf. [Ne 9:37](https://ref.ly/Neh9:37), [Lv 25:15](https://ref.ly/Lev25:15), [25:16](https://ref.ly/Lev25:16), also Ez [48:18](https://ref.ly/Ezek48:18); in [Gn 47:24](https://ref.ly/Gen47:24) ´בַּתּ must \= *of the crops* (בּ partitive, cf. בְּ **I. 2\. b**; LXX del. בְּ); שָדֶה´ת [2 K 8:6](https://ref.ly/2Kgs8:6), [2 Ch 31:5](https://ref.ly/2Chr31:5); כֶּרֶם ´תּ [Dt 22:9](https://ref.ly/Deut22:9) cf. [Psalm 107:37](https://ref.ly/Ps107:37); ´לָחֶם תּ חָאֲדָמָה [Is 30:23](https://ref.ly/Isa30:23); גֹּרֶן ´תּ \& ָ\+יקֶב ´תּ [Nu 18:30](https://ref.ly/Num18:30); זֶרַע ´תּ [Dt 14:22](https://ref.ly/Deut14:22); דָּגָן ´תּ [2 Ch 32:38](https://ref.ly/2Chr32:38); as property of husbandmen, or people [Lv 25:20](https://ref.ly/Lev25:20), [Dt 14:28](https://ref.ly/Deut14:28), [16:25](https://ref.ly/Deut16:25), [26:12](https://ref.ly/Deut26:12), [Pr 3:9](https://ref.ly/Prov3:9); crops as determined by season, שָׁמֶשׁ ´תּ [Dt 33:14](https://ref.ly/Deut33:14) (‖גֶרֶשׁ יְרָחִים); *yield* of a year שָׁנָה [Lv 25:12](https://ref.ly/Lev25:12), [25:22](https://ref.ly/Lev25:22); cf. v:[21](https://ref.ly/Lev25:21) ´עָשָה תּ, subj. שָׁנָה. **2\. a.** *income, revenue*, in general [Jb 31:12](https://ref.ly/Job31:12) (almost \= possessions) [Pr 10:16](https://ref.ly/Prov10:16), [14:4](https://ref.ly/Prov14:4), [15:6](https://ref.ly/Prov15:6), [16:8](https://ref.ly/Prov16:8), [Ec 5:9](https://ref.ly/Eccl5:9) cf. [Is 23:3](https://ref.ly/Isa23:3) (revenue of Tyre from trade with Egypt in bread stuffs). **b.** fig. *gain* of wisdom חָכְמָה ´תּ [Pr 3:14](https://ref.ly/Prov3:14), [8:19](https://ref.ly/Prov8:19); *product of lips* (שְפָתָיו ´תּ) [Pr 18:20](https://ref.ly/Prov18:20), i.e. results of his speech (‖פְּרִי פִי־אִישׁ); of Isr. as Yahweh's product [Je 2:3](https://ref.ly/Jer2:3); of Israel's wickedness [Je 12:13](https://ref.ly/Jer12:13).

## I בּוּז (id: BDB01059)

\[Str936] † I. \[בּוּז] **vb.** despise (NH *id.*)—**Qal** *Pf.* 3 ms. בַּז [Zc 4:10](https://ref.ly/Zech4:10) (cf. Köi. 439\); 3 pl. בָּזוּ [Pr 1:7](https://ref.ly/Prov1:7); *Impf.* יָבוּז [Pr 23:9](https://ref.ly/Prov23:9); 3 fs. וְתָבֻז [Pr 30:17](https://ref.ly/Prov30:17); 2 ms. תָּבוּז (Juss.) [Pr 23:22](https://ref.ly/Prov23:22); pl. יָבוּזוּ [Pr 6:30](https://ref.ly/Prov6:30), Ct [8:7](https://ref.ly/Song8:7); יָבֻזוּ Ct [8:1](https://ref.ly/Song8:1); *Inf. abs.* בּוֺז Ct [8:7](https://ref.ly/Song8:7); *Pt.* [Pr 11:12](https://ref.ly/Prov11:12) \+ 2 t.;—*despise, shew despite toward* (WisdLt \& poet.) c. acc. [Pr 1:7](https://ref.ly/Prov1:7); c. Inf. \+ ל [Pr 30:17](https://ref.ly/Prov30:17); elsewh. sq. ל \+ noun, [Pr 6:30](https://ref.ly/Prov6:30), [11:12](https://ref.ly/Prov11:12), [13:13](https://ref.ly/Prov13:13), [14:21](https://ref.ly/Prov14:21), [23:9](https://ref.ly/Prov23:9), [23:22](https://ref.ly/Prov23:22), Ct [8:1](https://ref.ly/Song8:1), [8:7](https://ref.ly/Song8:7), [8:7](https://ref.ly/Song8:7), [Zc 4:10](https://ref.ly/Zech4:10).

> \[Str937] † II. בּוּז **n.m.**:[Pr 18:3](https://ref.ly/Prov18:3) contempt—´בּ abs. [Gn 38:23](https://ref.ly/Gen38:23) \+ 9 t.; cstr. [Jb 31:34](https://ref.ly/Job31:34); —(WisdLt \& poet., exc. [Gn 38:23](https://ref.ly/Gen38:23) J). **1\.** *contempt* springing from pride and wickedness [Jb 31:34](https://ref.ly/Job31:34), [Psalm 123:3](https://ref.ly/Ps123:3), [123:4](https://ref.ly/Ps123:4) (‖לַעַג); joined in one adverb. phr. with גאוה [Psalm 31:19](https://ref.ly/Ps31:19) ´וָב ´בְּג; springing from prosperity and ease [Jb 12:5](https://ref.ly/Job12:5); \= object of contempt [Gn 38:23](https://ref.ly/Gen38:23), [Pr 12:8](https://ref.ly/Prov12:8); ‖חֶרְפָּה [Psalm 119:22](https://ref.ly/Ps119:22), [Pr 18:3](https://ref.ly/Prov18:3). **2\.** judicial, poured out (שׁפך) by God, in judgment [Jb 12:21](https://ref.ly/Job12:21) \= [Psalm 107:40](https://ref.ly/Ps107:40).

> \[Str939] † בּוּזָה **n.f.** contempt [Ne 3:36](https://ref.ly/Neh3:36) ´הָיִינוּ בּ *we have become* (an object of) *contempt*.

> \[Str938] † III. בּוּז **n.pr.m.** (cf. As. n.pr.loc. *Bâzu* DlPa 307; ZK 1885, 93 f.) **1\.** 2nd son of Nahor, Abraham's brother [Gn 22:21](https://ref.ly/Gen22:21) J; \= tribe named with דְּדָן \& תֵּימָא [Je 25:23](https://ref.ly/Jer25:23). **2\.** a Gadite [1 Ch 5:14](https://ref.ly/1Chr5:14).

> \[Str940] † I. בּוּזִי **adj. gent.** (\= בעזי fr. בֹּעַז acc. to J. DerenbourgRÉJ i. 6\) of Elihu's father [Jb 32:2](https://ref.ly/Job32:2), [32:6](https://ref.ly/Job32:6).

> \[Str941] † II. בוּזִי **n.pr.m.** father of Ezekiel Ez [1:3](https://ref.ly/Ezek1:3).

> \[Str942] † בַּוַּי **n.pr.m.** a Jew of Nehem.'s time [Ne 3:18](https://ref.ly/Neh3:18) \= בִּנּוּי v:[24](https://ref.ly/Neh3:24) which prob. rd. also v:[18](https://ref.ly/Neh3:18) (LXX Βεδει, Βενει, Βεζερ); cf, further SmListen 12\.

## בּוּךְ (id: BDB01066)

\[Str943] † בּוּךְ **vb.** perplex, confuse (? Ar. بَاكَ *stir up* (water of spring) then *be disturbed, confused;* cf. As. *bâku, lead captive*, orig. *lead astray*? COT [Ezr 4:2](https://ref.ly/Ezra4:2))—only **Niph.** *Pf.* 3 fs. נָב֫וֺכָה [Est 3:15](https://ref.ly/Esth3:15); 3 mpl. נָבֹ֫כוּ [Jo 1:18](https://ref.ly/Joel1:18); *Pt.* pl. נְבֻכִים [Ex 14:3](https://ref.ly/Exod14:3); —*be confused, in confusion* (of a city) [Est 3:15](https://ref.ly/Esth3:15); \= wander aimlessly (of Isr.) [Ex 14:3](https://ref.ly/Exod14:3); so of cattle [Jo 1:18](https://ref.ly/Joel1:18) (Merx prop. בָּכוּ, after LXX ἔκλαυσαν, but בכה not elsewhere of cattle).

> \[Str3998] † מְבוּכָה **n.f.** confusion, confounding, [Is 22:5](https://ref.ly/Isa22:5) יוֺם מְהוּמָה וּמְבוּסָה וּמְבוּכָה *a day of consternation and down\-treading and confusion*; [Mi 7:4](https://ref.ly/Mic7:4) עַתָּה תִּהְיֶה מְבוּכָתָם *now shall be their confusion*.

> \[Str945] † I. בּוּל **n.\[m.]** Bul, 8th month \= *Marcheswan*, As. *Araḫ samna*, [1 K 6:38](https://ref.ly/1Kgs6:38) בְּיֶרַח בּוּל הוּא הַחֹדֶשׁ הַשְּׁמִינִי; (Canaanitish; Ph. ירח בל; a Palm. god is בול (in n.pr. עבדבול, Vog93 זבדבול etc.) \& ירחבול (Vog93, moon\-god), which DHMBer. Wien. Ak. cviii. 977 connects with Heb. בּוּל; BaeRel 87 f. (q.v.) thinks \= בעל, cf. Thesp. 560\).

> \[Str944] II. בּוּל **n.m.** *product, produce*, v. יבול

> \[Str946] בּוּנָה **n.pr.m** v. בּין

> \[Str1138] בּוּנִּי **n.pr.m.** v. בנה

## בּוּס (id: BDB01072)

\[Str947] † \[בּוּס] **vb.** tread down, trample—**Qal** *Impf.* יָבוּס [Psalm 60:14](https://ref.ly/Ps60:14) \= [108:14](https://ref.ly/Ps108:13); 3 *fs.* תָּבוּס [Pr 27:7](https://ref.ly/Prov27:7), אָבוּס [Is 63:6](https://ref.ly/Isa63:6); *sf.* 3 s. אֲבוּסֶנּוּ [Is 14:25](https://ref.ly/Isa14:25); 1 *pl.* נָבוּס [Psalm 44:6](https://ref.ly/Ps44:6); —*tread down, trample* (of warriors), no obj. expr. [Zc 10:5](https://ref.ly/Zech10:5); *id.*, fig. c. acc. (subj. ´י, as warrior) [Is 14:25](https://ref.ly/Isa14:25) cf. [Psalm 60:14](https://ref.ly/Ps60:14) \= [108:14](https://ref.ly/Ps108:13), [Is 63:6](https://ref.ly/Isa63:6); (subj. men, with God's help) [Psalm 44:6](https://ref.ly/Ps44:6); fig. \= *reject, loathe* [Pr 27:7](https://ref.ly/Prov27:7). **Pol.** *Pf.* בּוֺסְסוּ [Is 63:18](https://ref.ly/Isa63:18); בֹּסְסוּ Je 12; 10—*tread down* in bad sense, subj. enemies of ´י, obj. acc. [Je 12:10](https://ref.ly/Jer12:10) (in metaph.); \= *desecrate* (obj. מקדשׁ) [Is 63:18](https://ref.ly/Isa63:18). **Hithpol.** *Pt. f.* מִתְבּוֺסֶסֶת Ez [16:6](https://ref.ly/Ezek16:6), [16:22](https://ref.ly/Ezek16:22)—of the blind movements of infant's limbs, *kick out* (this way and that), fig. of Jerusalem. **Hoph.** *Pt.* מוּבָס [Is 14:19](https://ref.ly/Isa14:19)—pass. *trodden down* [Is 14:19](https://ref.ly/Isa14:19), of corpse, sim. of king of Babylon.

> \[Str2982] † יְבוּס **n.pr.loc.** Jebus, name of Jerusalem acc. to Ju [19:10](https://ref.ly/Judg19:10) יְבוּס הִיא יְרוּשָׁלַםִ, cf. also v:[11](https://ref.ly/Judg19:11), [1 Ch 11:4](https://ref.ly/1Chr11:4) יְרוּשָׁלַםִ הִיא יְבוּס, cf. v:[5](https://ref.ly/1Chr11:5).

> \[Str2983] † יְבוּסִי, יְבֻסִי **1\. adj. gent.** Jebusite, Jebusites, sg. אורנה הַיְבֻסִי [2 S 24:16](https://ref.ly/2Sam24:16), [24:18](https://ref.ly/2Sam24:18) cf. [1 Ch 21:15](https://ref.ly/1Chr21:15), [21:18](https://ref.ly/1Chr21:18), [21:28](https://ref.ly/1Chr21:28), [2 Ch 3:1](https://ref.ly/2Chr3:1); as subst. *a Jebusite* [Zc 9:7](https://ref.ly/Zech9:7); usually c. art. ´הַי coll. *the Jebusites*, in hist. statement [Gn 10:16](https://ref.ly/Gen10:16) (J) \= [1 Ch 1:14](https://ref.ly/1Chr1:14), [Nu 13:29](https://ref.ly/Num13:29) (E) [Jos 9:1](https://ref.ly/Josh9:1), [11:3](https://ref.ly/Josh11:3), [12:8](https://ref.ly/Josh12:8), [24:11](https://ref.ly/Josh24:11) (all D) Ju [3:5](https://ref.ly/Judg3:5), [Ne 9:8](https://ref.ly/Neh9:8), all in list of Canaanitish peoples; so also [1 K 9:20](https://ref.ly/1Kgs9:20) \= [2 Ch 8:7](https://ref.ly/2Chr8:7), where remnant of these peoples referred to; also [Ezr 9:1](https://ref.ly/Ezra9:1), which seems to shew that this list had become a standing expression for early inhabitants; cf. further in promises (JED) [Gn 15:21](https://ref.ly/Gen15:21), [Ex 3:8](https://ref.ly/Exod3:8), [3:17](https://ref.ly/Exod3:17), [13:5](https://ref.ly/Exod13:5), [23:23](https://ref.ly/Exod23:23), [33:2](https://ref.ly/Exod33:2), [34:11](https://ref.ly/Exod34:11), [Dt 7:1](https://ref.ly/Deut7:1), [20:17](https://ref.ly/Deut20:17), [Jos 3:10](https://ref.ly/Josh3:10); specif. defined as inhabitants of Jebus\-Jerusalem [Jos 15:63](https://ref.ly/Josh15:63), [15:63](https://ref.ly/Josh15:63) (J) Ju [1:21](https://ref.ly/Judg1:21), [1:21](https://ref.ly/Judg1:21), [2 S 5:6](https://ref.ly/2Sam5:6), [5:8](https://ref.ly/2Sam5:8) (on which cf. Dr) [1 Ch 11:4](https://ref.ly/1Chr11:4) (called also יוֺשֵׁב הָאָרֶץ [2 S 5:6](https://ref.ly/2Sam5:6) \& ישְׁבֵי הָאָרֶץ [1 Ch 11:4](https://ref.ly/1Chr11:4)); v. also עִיר הַיְבוּסִי הַזּאֹת Ju [19:11](https://ref.ly/Judg19:11); whence **2\.** *Jerusalem*, Hex only P, [Jos 15:8](https://ref.ly/Josh15:8) גֵּי בֶן־הִנֹּם אֶל־כֶּתֶף היבוסי מִנֶּגֶב הִיא יְרוּשָׁלָםִ, cf. [18:16](https://ref.ly/Josh18:16), [18:28](https://ref.ly/Josh18:28) וְהַיְבוּסִי הִיא יְרוּשָׁלִַםִ.—Cf. כנען.

> \[Str4001] † מְבוּסָה **n.f.** down\-treading, subjugation, גּוֺי קַו־קַו וּמְבוּסָה [Is 18:2](https://ref.ly/Isa18:2), [18:7](https://ref.ly/Isa18:7) *a nation of might and of down\-treading* (Che *all\-subduing*); יוֺם מְהוּמָה וּמְבוּכָה ´וּמ [Is 22:5](https://ref.ly/Isa22:5), cf. מְבוּכָה sub בוך.

> \[Str8395] † \[תְּבוּסָה] **n.f.** down\-treading \= ruin, downfall, cstr. תְּבוּסַת אחזיהו [2 Ch 22:7](https://ref.ly/2Chr22:7) *the downfall of Ahaziah*.

## בוץ (id: BDB01079)

[Is 10:14](https://ref.ly/Isa10:14) (in simil.); (of ostrich רננים) [Jb 39:14](https://ref.ly/Job39:14); (of great viper צפעוני) [Is 59:5](https://ref.ly/Isa59:5), [59:5](https://ref.ly/Isa59:5) (metaph.)

> \[Str948] † בּוּץ, בֻּץ **n.\[m.]** byssus (late), (Ph. בוץ, Aram. בּוּץ, ܒܘܽܨ; √ dub.; Birch, WilkinsonEgyptians ii. 158 f. fr. Egypt. *hbos, clothe;* v. Say. Herodotii. 86; Thes fr. √ בוץ, but NöZMG 1875, 650; Armen., acc. to LagSem. i. 72 al.) a fine white Egyptian linen, and cloth made of it, מִשְׁפְּחוֺת בֵּית־עֲבֹדַת הַבֻּץ [1 Ch 4:21](https://ref.ly/1Chr4:21) *families of the house of byssus\-working;* cf. [2 Ch 2:13](https://ref.ly/2Chr2:13); מְכֻרְבָּל בִּמְעִיל בּוּץ [1 Ch 15:27](https://ref.ly/1Chr15:27) *clothed in a robe of byssus* (but rd. rather as ‖ [2 S 6:14](https://ref.ly/2Sam6:14) מְכַרְכֵּר בְּכָל־עֹז *was dancing with all his might*); cf. ´תַּכְרִיח בּ [Est 8:15](https://ref.ly/Esth8:15); מְלֻבָּשִׁים בּוּץ [2 Ch 5:12](https://ref.ly/2Chr5:12); חַבְלֵי בוּץ [Est 1:6](https://ref.ly/Esth1:6), [2 Ch 3:14](https://ref.ly/2Chr3:14) material of פָּרֹכֶת; article of trade Ez [27:16](https://ref.ly/Ezek27:16) (del. LXX Co). Cf. also שֵׁשׁ.

## בּוֺשׁ (id: BDB01088)

\[Str954] בּוֺשׁ:109 **vb.** be ashamed (Sab. בוס; מהבאס \= *evil\-doer* DHMZMG 1883, 375, ‖ Aram. בְּהַת, Syr. ܒܗܶܬ; Ar. بَاثَ, بَثَّ mean *disperse*)—**Qal** *Pf.* [Je 48:39](https://ref.ly/Jer48:39); בּשׁ [Je 48:13](https://ref.ly/Jer48:13); בּוֺשָׁה [Je 15:9](https://ref.ly/Jer15:9); בּשְׁתִּי [Je 31:19](https://ref.ly/Jer31:19) \+ 25 t.; *Impf.* יֵבוֺשׁ [Is 29:22](https://ref.ly/Isa29:22) \+ 57 t.; *Inf.* abs. בּוֺשׁ [Je 6:15](https://ref.ly/Jer6:15), [8:12](https://ref.ly/Jer8:12); *Imv.* בּוֺשִׁי [Is 23:4](https://ref.ly/Isa23:4), Ez [16:52](https://ref.ly/Ezek16:52), בּוֺשׁוּ Ez [36:32](https://ref.ly/Ezek36:32); *Pt.* pl. בּוֺשִׁים Ez [32:30](https://ref.ly/Ezek32:30); —**1\.** abs. *feel shame* [Je 6:15](https://ref.ly/Jer6:15) \+ 16 t., [Is 19:9](https://ref.ly/Isa19:9), [23:4](https://ref.ly/Isa23:4), [37:27](https://ref.ly/Isa37:27), Is:2 [45:16](https://ref.ly/Isa45:16) \+ 14 t., Ez [16:63](https://ref.ly/Ezek16:63), [Mi 7:16](https://ref.ly/Mic7:16), [Jo 2:26](https://ref.ly/Joel2:26), [2:27](https://ref.ly/Joel2:27), [2 K 19:26](https://ref.ly/2Kgs19:26), Ez [9:6](https://ref.ly/Ezek9:6), [Jb 6:20](https://ref.ly/Job6:20), [Psalm 6:11](https://ref.ly/Ps6:11) (\+ 27 t., chiefly in late Psalms). **2\.** sq. מִן, *be ashamed of*, i.e. *disconcerted, disappointed by reason of* [Je 2:36](https://ref.ly/Jer2:36), [2:36](https://ref.ly/Jer2:36), [12:13](https://ref.ly/Jer12:13), [48:13](https://ref.ly/Jer48:13), [Is 1:29](https://ref.ly/Isa1:29), [20:5](https://ref.ly/Isa20:5), Ez [32:30](https://ref.ly/Ezek32:30), [36:32](https://ref.ly/Ezek36:32). **3\.** with obj. בּשְׁתִּי לִשְׁאוֺל *I am ashamed to ask* Ez 8:22; לאֹ תֵבשׁוּ תַּהְכְּרוּ לִי *ye are not ashamed to deal hardly with me* (impf. subj.) [Jb 19:3](https://ref.ly/Job19:3). בּושׁ often ‖נכלם \& חפר; בּשׁוּ וְהָכְלְמוּ *they are ashamed and confounded* [Je 14:3](https://ref.ly/Jer14:3), [22:22](https://ref.ly/Jer22:22), Is:2 [41:11](https://ref.ly/Isa41:11), [45:16](https://ref.ly/Isa45:16), [45:17](https://ref.ly/Isa45:17), Ez [16:52](https://ref.ly/Ezek16:52), [36:32](https://ref.ly/Ezek36:32), [Psalm 35:4](https://ref.ly/Ps35:4), [69:7](https://ref.ly/Ps69:7), Ez [9:6](https://ref.ly/Ezek9:6); וּבשׁוּ הַחֹזִים וְחָֽפְרוּ הַקֹּסְמִים *and the seers shall be ashamed and the diviners confounded* [Mi 3:7](https://ref.ly/Mic3:7), cf. [Je 15:9](https://ref.ly/Jer15:9), [Jb 6:20](https://ref.ly/Job6:20), [Psalm 35:26](https://ref.ly/Ps35:26), [40:15](https://ref.ly/Ps40:15), [70:3](https://ref.ly/Ps70:3), [71:24](https://ref.ly/Ps71:24); יֵבוֺשׁ מְקוֺרוֺ [Ho 13:15](https://ref.ly/Hos13:15) (rd. יִיבַשׁ *be dry*); עַד־בּוֺשׁ † Ju [3:25](https://ref.ly/Judg3:25), [2 K 2:17](https://ref.ly/2Kgs2:17), [8:11](https://ref.ly/2Kgs8:11). **Polel** *Pf.* בּשֵׁשׁ *delay* (in shame), בּשֵׁשׁ משֶׁה לָרֶדֶת *Moses delayed to descend* [Ex 32:1](https://ref.ly/Exod32:1) (J), מַדּוּעַ בּשֵׁשׁ רִכְבּוֺ לָבוֺא *why delay his chariots to come ?* Ju [5:28](https://ref.ly/Judg5:28). **Hiph. 1\.** הֱבִישׁוֺתָ [Psalm 44:8](https://ref.ly/Ps44:8); הֱבִישֹׁתָה [Psalm 53:6](https://ref.ly/Ps53:6); *Impf.* 2 ms. sf. תְּבִישֵׁנִי [Psalm 119:31](https://ref.ly/Ps119:31), [119:116](https://ref.ly/Ps119:116); תָּבִישׁוּ [Psalm 14:6](https://ref.ly/Ps14:6); *put to shame, Pt.* מֵבִישׁ [Pr 10:5](https://ref.ly/Prov10:5), [14:35](https://ref.ly/Prov14:35), [19:26](https://ref.ly/Prov19:26), [29:15](https://ref.ly/Prov29:15); *one causing shame* or *acting shamefully*, ‖מִשְכִּיל; עֶבֶד מַשְכִּיל יִמְשֹׁל בְּבֵן מֵבִישׁ *a servant acting wisely will rule over a son causing shame* [Pr 17:2](https://ref.ly/Prov17:2); מְבִישָׁה ‖אֵשֶׁת חַיִל [Pr 12:4](https://ref.ly/Prov12:4). **2\.** הוֺבִישׁ [2 S 19:6](https://ref.ly/2Sam19:6), [Je 2:26](https://ref.ly/Jer2:26), [6:15](https://ref.ly/Jer6:15), [Jo 1:10](https://ref.ly/Joel1:10), [1:12](https://ref.ly/Joel1:12); הֹבִישׁ [Je 10:14](https://ref.ly/Jer10:14) \+ 7 t., [Ho 2:7](https://ref.ly/Hos2:7), [Jo 1:12](https://ref.ly/Joel1:12), [1:17](https://ref.ly/Joel1:17), [Is 30:5](https://ref.ly/Isa30:5) (but הבאיש Kt); הֹבִשׁוּ [Je 8:9](https://ref.ly/Jer8:9); [8:12](https://ref.ly/Jer8:12); *Imv.* הֹבִישׁוּ [Jo 1:11](https://ref.ly/Joel1:11) (all the forms in Joel derived √ יָבֵשׁ by ancient versions and some moderns, vid. ## בזק (id: BDB01103)

בזק (Aram. בְּזַק, ܒܙܰܩ *scatter;* Ar. بَزَقَ *rise* (of sun) is prob. erroneous v. Lane199).

> \[Str965] † בָּזָק **n.\[m.]** lightning flash ? Ez [1:14](https://ref.ly/Ezek1:14) Hi Co del.; verse om. in old MSS. of LXX; sense uncertain, possibly error for ברק.

> \[Str966] † בֶּ֫זֶקִ **n.pr.loc.** [1 S 11:8](https://ref.ly/1Sam11:8) (בְּבָזֶק) rendezvous of Israel under Saul and Sam.; Ju [1:4](https://ref.ly/Judg1:4), [1:5](https://ref.ly/Judg1:5) home of Adoni\-bezek; on loc. cf. Euseb. LagOnom. 105, 2nd ed. 139; see also Stu. Ju [1:4](https://ref.ly/Judg1:4); 17th (mile\-)stone fr. Neapolis toward Scythopolis; mod. *Ḫirbet Ibzîḳ*, 14 Eng. m. fr. Nâblus, Surveyii. 231\.

* **Associated Passages:** Judges 1:4; Ezekiel 1:14; 1 Samuel 11:8; Judges 1:5


## בָּזַר (id: BDB01106)

\[Str967] † \[בָּזַר] **vb.** scatter (Ar. بَذَرَ, Aram. בְּדַר)—**Qal** *Impf.* [Dn 11:24](https://ref.ly/Dan11:24) בִּזָּה וְשָׁלָל וּרְכוּשׁ לָהֶם יִבְ\+זוֺר *plunder and spoil and possessions he will scatter among them* (subj. Antiochus Epiph.) **Pi.** *Pf.* בִּזַּר, *id.*, [Psalm 68:31](https://ref.ly/Ps68:31), but rd. *Imv.* בַּזֵּר (בַּזַּר) (LXX SyrVer Vulgate so most; De follows MT), subj. ref. to ´י, obj. עַמִּים.

> \[Str968] † בִּזְּתָא **n.pr.m.** a eunuch of Ahasuerus, [Est 1:10](https://ref.ly/Esth1:10) (Thes comp. Pers. *beste, ligatus* sc. *membro*, e.g. *spado*, cf. VullersDict. Pers. sub بَستَه).

* **Associated Passages:** Esther 1:10; Daniel 11:24; Psalms 68:31

— I. \[בָּחֵל]: NöZAW xvii (1897\), 188 disproves Syr. ܒܲܚܸܠ, and adopts the view of GeiUrschrift 270 (בחל euphem. for a √ II. בעל \= *loathe,* c. בְּ, assumed (Thes Buhl) for [Je 3:14](https://ref.ly/Jer3:14), [31:32](https://ref.ly/Jer31:32); v. infr. on p. 127a).

## בָּחַן (id: BDB01110)

\[Str974] † \[בָּחַן] **vb.** examine, try (cf. Aram. בְּחַן, Syr. ܒܰܚܶܢ, *try, examine;* Ar. محن I. VIII. *test*, cf. WSG 65\)—**Qal** *Pf.* sf. בְּחָנַ֫נִי [Jb 23:10](https://ref.ly/Job23:10), בָּחַנְתָּ [Psalm 17:3](https://ref.ly/Ps17:3) \+ 7 t.; *Impf.* יִבְחָן [Psalm 11:5](https://ref.ly/Ps11:5) \+ 5 t.; *Imv.* sf. בְּחָנֵנִי [Psalm 26:2](https://ref.ly/Ps26:2) \+ 2 t.; *Inf.* בְּחֹן [Zc 13:9](https://ref.ly/Zech13:9); *Pt.* בֹּחֵן [Je 11:20](https://ref.ly/Jer11:20) \+ 5 t. (‖צרף, נסה);—**1\.** *examine, scrutinize, try* [Psalm 11:5](https://ref.ly/Ps11:5), [139:23](https://ref.ly/Ps139:23), [Jb 7:18](https://ref.ly/Job7:18); עַפְעַפָּיו יִבְחֲנוּ בְנֵי אָדָם *His eyelids try the children of men* (search them through and through) [Psalm 11:4](https://ref.ly/Ps11:4). **2\.** *prove, test, try*. **a.** with the metaphor of gold [Jb 23:10](https://ref.ly/Job23:10); וּבְחַנְתִּים כִּבְחֹן אֶת־הַזָּהָב *and I will try them as one tries gold* [Zc 13:9](https://ref.ly/Zech13:9). **b.** without metaphor, of God testing persons [Psalm 26:2](https://ref.ly/Ps26:2), [66:10](https://ref.ly/Ps66:10), [81:8](https://ref.ly/Ps81:8), [Je 9:6](https://ref.ly/Jer9:6); their ways [Je 6:27](https://ref.ly/Jer6:27); the לֵב [Psalm 17:3](https://ref.ly/Ps17:3), [Je 12:3](https://ref.ly/Jer12:3), [Pr 17:3](https://ref.ly/Prov17:3), [1 Ch 29:17](https://ref.ly/1Chr29:17); reins [Je 17:10](https://ref.ly/Jer17:10); heart and reins [Psalm 7:10](https://ref.ly/Ps7:10), [Je 11:20](https://ref.ly/Jer11:20) (\= [20:12](https://ref.ly/Jer20:12)). **c.** of man testing or tempting God [Psalm 95:9](https://ref.ly/Ps95:9), [Mal 3:10](https://ref.ly/Mal3:10), [3:15](https://ref.ly/Mal3:15). **d.** the ear trying words [Jb 12:11](https://ref.ly/Job12:11) \= [34:3](https://ref.ly/Job34:3). **Niph.** *Impf.* יִבָּחֵן [Jb 34:36](https://ref.ly/Job34:36), [Gn 42:15](https://ref.ly/Gen42:15), [42:16](https://ref.ly/Gen42:16), *to be tried, proved*. **Pu.** בֹּחַן Ez [21:18](https://ref.ly/Ezek21:18) *the trial has been made* (LXX SyrVer Vulgate Ges MV Ew Ke, but noun בֹּ֫חַן MT Symm Haev.; rd. בְּחֵן *with grace, favour*, Hi Co).

> \[Str976] † בֹּ֫חַן **n.\[m.]** testing, [Is 28:16](https://ref.ly/Isa28:16) אֶבֶן בֹּחַן *a tested, tried stone*, i.e. approved for use as a foundation\-stone. On Ez [21:18](https://ref.ly/Ezek21:18) MT v. foreg. *ad fin.*

> \[Str975] † בַּ֫חַן **n.\[m.]** watch\-tower, [Is 32:14](https://ref.ly/Isa32:14).

> \[Str971] † \[בחון] sf. בַּחוּנָיו (dag. f. implic. Qr; בחיניו Kt) [Is 23:13](https://ref.ly/Isa23:13) *their siege\-towers*.

> \[Str969] † בָּחוֺן **n.\[m.]** assayer, one who tries metals [Je 6:27](https://ref.ly/Jer6:27) (Ges§ 84, 3 Ew§ 152 b).

> \[Str971] בחיניו [Is 23:13](https://ref.ly/Isa23:13) Kt; v. \[בחון] supr.

## בָּטָה (id: BDB01126)

\[Str981] † \[בָּטָה, בָּטָא] **vb.** speak rashly, thoughtlessly (NH *id.*, בטי)—**Qal** *Pt.* בּוֺטֶה [Pr 12:18](https://ref.ly/Prov12:18) *one that babbleth* (opp. לְשׁוֺן חֲכָמִים). **Pi.** *Impf.* יְבַטֵּא בִשְׁבוּעָה [Lv 5:4](https://ref.ly/Lev5:4) *speak rashly, unadvisedly;* so וַיְבַטֵּא בִשְפָתָיו (of Moses) [Psalm 106:33](https://ref.ly/Ps106:33); *Inf.* לְבַטֵּא בִשְפָתַיִם [Lv 5:4](https://ref.ly/Lev5:4) (P).

> \[Str4008] † מִבְטָא **n.\[m.]** rash utterance, מִבְטָא שפתיה [Nu 30:7](https://ref.ly/Num30:7), [30:9](https://ref.ly/Num30:9).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 106:33; Numbers 30:9; Numbers 30:7; Leviticus 5:4; Proverbs 12:18

## II בטח (id: BDB01135)

II. בטח (√ assumed for following).

> \[Str20] † אֲבַטִּחִים **n.\[m.] pl.** water\-melons (Mish. אבטיח, Sam. ܒܛܝܚ; cf. LöwNo. 297; Ar. بِطِّيخٌ etc.; perh. loan\-word in Heb. cf. Sta§ 258; mod. Egypt. *baṭṭich, biṭṭich* cf. reff. in Di [Nu 11:5](https://ref.ly/Num11:5); on formation cf. LagBN 10, who comp. Eth. *aqṭala*)—Egyptian fruit, ´הָא [Nu 11:5](https://ref.ly/Num11:5) (‖הִקִּשֻּׁאִים, הֶֽחָצִיר, הַבְּצָלִים, הַשּׁוּמִים); LXX τοὺς πέπονας.

* **Associated Passages:** Numbers 11:5


## בָּטַל (id: BDB01137)

\[Str988] † \[בָּטַל] **vb.** cease (NH בָּטֵל, Ar. بَطَلَ, Eth. በጠለ፡ both *be futile, vain*, but As. *baṭâlu, cease* LotzTP 68, so Aram. בְּטֵיל, بطل)—**Qal** *Pf.* וּבָֽטְליּ consec. [Ec 12:3](https://ref.ly/Eccl12:3) *and* the grinders *cease*.

* **Associated Passages:** Ecclesiastes 12:3


## I בטן (id: BDB01138)

I. בטן (meaning dub.; √ of following).

[3:10](https://ref.ly/Job3:10) (Ges De), i.e. my brother or *men of my clan* RSK 33 f., others *my sons* (Ew), *my grandsons* (Di); also in addressing Isr. As a whole (masc. sf.) [Dt 7:13](https://ref.ly/Deut7:13), [28:4](https://ref.ly/Deut28:4), [28:11](https://ref.ly/Deut28:11), [28:18](https://ref.ly/Deut28:18), [28:53](https://ref.ly/Deut28:53), [30:9](https://ref.ly/Deut30:9), cf. מַחֲמַדֵּי בִטְנָם [Ho 9:16](https://ref.ly/Hos9:16) *beloved ones of their womb;* מִן־הַבֶּטֶן (מִבֶּטֶן) \= *from birth* Ju [13:5](https://ref.ly/Judg13:5), [13:7](https://ref.ly/Judg13:7), [Psalm 58:4](https://ref.ly/Ps58:4), [71:6](https://ref.ly/Ps71:6), cf. also [Is 44:2](https://ref.ly/Isa44:2), [44:24](https://ref.ly/Isa44:24), [46:3](https://ref.ly/Isa46:3) (‖רָחַָם) [48:8](https://ref.ly/Isa48:8), [49:1](https://ref.ly/Isa49:1), [49:5](https://ref.ly/Isa49:5); מִבֶּטֶן נִמִּי *id.* Ju [16:17](https://ref.ly/Judg16:17), [Jb 31:18](https://ref.ly/Job31:18), [Psalm 22:11](https://ref.ly/Ps22:11) (‖רֶחֶם). **4\.** architectural word of some rounded projection connected with the two pillars Jachin \& Boaz [1 K 7:20](https://ref.ly/1Kgs7:20) (Klo rds. הבית).

## II בטן (id: BDB01141)

II. בטן (asumed as √ of follwing; mng. unknown).

> \[Str992] † בָּטְנִים **n.\[m.]pl.** pistachio, an oval nut (cf. As. *buṭnu* COTGloss \& SchrMBAK 1881, 419, Aram. בּוּטְנָא, ܒܶܛܡܬܳܐ, Ar. بُطْمٌ; on Punic Βουτνούμ BlauZMG 1873, 527\) \= *pistacia terebinthus* RobBR i. 208, ii. 222, PostPEF Oct. 1888, 218, No. 214 f; Tr. Vict. Inst. xxii. 271 etc. *pist. vera* LöwNo. 44\); [Gn 43:11](https://ref.ly/Gen43:11) one of the articles carried from Canaan to Egypt by sons of Jacob as present to Joseph; still a delicacy in Egypt and Syria, cf.Wetzst in Löwp.420\.

> \[Str993] † בְּטֹנִים **n.pr.loc.** city of Gad, E. of Jordan [Jos 13:26](https://ref.ly/Josh13:26), mod. *Baṭne*, W. of *Es\-Salṭ* (v. d. VeldeMemoir 298\) \= Βοτνία cf. LagOnom. 294, 2nd ed. 247\.

* **Associated Passages:** Joshua 13:26; Genesis 43:11


## בָּיַי (id: BDB01144)

\[Str15] † \[בָּיַי, בַּי] **vb.** entreat (Ar. بَيَّ *come as suppliant, entreat*, still current in the Ḥauran: v. Wetzst in De:[Jb 34:36](https://ref.ly/Job34:36)), of which (prob.) אָבִי [Jb 34:36](https://ref.ly/Job34:36) is 1 s. impf. (used dialectically): אָבִי יִבְּחֵן אִיּוֺב *would that* (lit. *I entreat that*) Job were tried!

> \[Str994] † בִּי **part. of entreaty**, craving permission to address a superior, always foll. By אֲדֹנִי (or אֲדֹנָי), and always (exc. [Jos 7:8](https://ref.ly/Josh7:8)) at the beginning of a speech, **I pray, excuse** me—(not improb. from √ ביי; so that בִּי אֲדֹנִי will be literally *a supplication of* (i.e. *to* *my lord*! cf. Wetzst l.c., who compares the Ar. دَخْلُ سَيِّدَى lit. *a prayer to my lord*! a standing formula \= *Pray, excuse me*, used exactly as בֹּי אֲדֹנִי. Acc. to other contr. From בְּעִי, from בָּעָה *to ask*, and so lit. *a petition*! cf. Aram. בְּבָעוּ, ܒܒܳܥܽܘ (e.g. [Gn 19:18](https://ref.ly/Gen19:18) Targum, [Nu 12:13](https://ref.ly/Num12:13) SyrVer); but ע is not often elided in Heb.)—[Gn 44:18](https://ref.ly/Gen44:18) בִּי אֲדֹנִי יְדַבֶּר־נָא *I pray*, my lord, let thy servant speak, etc.; so [Nu 12:11](https://ref.ly/Num12:11), Ju [6:13](https://ref.ly/Judg6:13), [1 S 1:26](https://ref.ly/1Sam1:26), [1 K 3:17](https://ref.ly/1Kgs3:17), [3:26](https://ref.ly/1Kgs3:26), and foll. by a pl. subj. [Gn 43:20](https://ref.ly/Gen43:20) יָרֹד יָרַדְנוּ ´בִּי א *Oh*, my lord, we came down, etc.; בִּי אֲדֹנָי (to God) [Ex 4:10](https://ref.ly/Exod4:10), [4:13](https://ref.ly/Exod4:13), [Jos 7:8](https://ref.ly/Josh7:8), Ju [6:15](https://ref.ly/Judg6:15), [13:8](https://ref.ly/Judg13:8) (LXX in Pent. and Jos. δέομαι, δεόμεθα: in other books absurdly ἐν ἐμοί).

* **Associated Passages:** Job 34:36; Exodus 4:13; 1 Kings 3:26; Numbers 12:13; Genesis 19:18; Numbers 12:11; Genesis 44:18; 1 Samuel 1:26; Genesis 43:20; Judges 6:15; Judges 13:8; Exodus 4:10; Judges 6:13; 1 Kings 3:17; Joshua 7:8

> \[Str996,996,1143] \[בַּ֫יִן] **subst**. prop. interval, space between (بَيْنٌ *id.*)—cstr. בֵּין once [Is 44:4](https://ref.ly/Isa44:4) (Baer) בֵּן, בֵּינִי, בֵּֽינְךָ, בֵינֵח, בֵּינוֺ (בֵּינָיו † [Jos 3:4](https://ref.ly/Josh3:4), [8:11](https://ref.ly/Josh8:11) Qr); with pl. sf. in pl. form בֵּינֵינוּ (בֵּנֵינוּ), etc.; also בֵּינוֺת † Ez [10:2](https://ref.ly/Ezek10:2), [10:2](https://ref.ly/Ezek10:2), [10:6](https://ref.ly/Ezek10:6), [10:6](https://ref.ly/Ezek10:6), [10:7](https://ref.ly/Ezek10:7), [10:7](https://ref.ly/Ezek10:7) (\+ [1:13](https://ref.ly/Ezek1:13) LXX Hi Ew etc. for דְּמוּת), בֵּינוֺתֵינוּ † [Gn 26:28](https://ref.ly/Gen26:28), [Jos 22:34](https://ref.ly/Josh22:34), Ju [11:10](https://ref.ly/Judg11:10), בֵּינוֺתָם † [Gn 42:23](https://ref.ly/Gen42:23), [2 S 21:7](https://ref.ly/2Sam21:7), [Je 25:16](https://ref.ly/Jer25:16); dual בֵּנַיִם (v. infr.);—**1\.** always (exc. dual) as **prep.** in the interval of, between (so Aram. בֵּין, \& pl. בֵּינֵי, ܒܰܝܢܰܝ, ܒܰܝܢܳܬ; በቢይኖት፡), as [Gn 15:17](https://ref.ly/Gen15:17) בֵּין הַגְּזָרִים *between* the pieces, [Ex 13:9](https://ref.ly/Exod13:9) al. בֵּין עֵינֶיךָ *between* thy eyes (v. עַיִן), [Is 22:11](https://ref.ly/Isa22:11) al. *between* the two walls (v. חוֺמָה), [Jb 24:11](https://ref.ly/Job24:11), [30:7](https://ref.ly/Job30:7), [40:30](https://ref.ly/Job41:6) part him *between* merchants; rather more gen. *among* [Ho 13:15](https://ref.ly/Hos13:15), Ct [2:2](https://ref.ly/Song2:2), [2:3](https://ref.ly/Song2:3), Ez [19:2](https://ref.ly/Ezek19:2), [31:3](https://ref.ly/Ezek31:3), [Jb 34:37](https://ref.ly/Job34:37); less exactly *within* [Pr 26:13](https://ref.ly/Prov26:13) a lion is בֵּין הָֽרְחֹבוֺת *within* the board places (cf. [23:13](https://ref.ly/Prov23:13) בְּתוֺח): once with a sing. (unusual) [Dn 8:16](https://ref.ly/Dan8:16) בֵּין אוּלַי *between* the Ulai, i.e. between its banks. When the space separating two distinct objects is to be indicated, this is done **a.** most commonly by repeating בין, as [Gn 13:3](https://ref.ly/Gen13:3) בֵּין בֵּיתְאֵל וּבֵין הָעָי lit. *in the interval of* Bethel, and *in the interval of* ‘Ai, i.e. *between* Bethel *and* ‘Ai, [16:14](https://ref.ly/Gen16:14), [17:7](https://ref.ly/Gen17:7), [31:50](https://ref.ly/Gen31:50), [31:51](https://ref.ly/Gen31:51) \+ oft. **b.** more rarely by …בֵּין לְ, [Gn 1:6](https://ref.ly/Gen1:6) dividing בֵּין מַיִם לָמָיִם lit. *in the interval of* waters *with reference to* waters, i.e. *between* the waters *and* the waters, [Lv 20:25](https://ref.ly/Lev20:25), [27:33](https://ref.ly/Lev27:33), [Dt 17:8](https://ref.ly/Deut17:8), [17:8](https://ref.ly/Deut17:8), [17:8](https://ref.ly/Deut17:8), [2 S 19:36](https://ref.ly/2Sam19:36), [1 K 3:9](https://ref.ly/1Kgs3:9), Ez [41:18](https://ref.ly/Ezek41:18), [42:20](https://ref.ly/Ezek42:20), [44:23](https://ref.ly/Ezek44:23), [44:23](https://ref.ly/Ezek44:23), [Jon 4:11](https://ref.ly/Jonah4:11), [Mal 3:18](https://ref.ly/Mal3:18), [3:18](https://ref.ly/Mal3:18), [2 Ch 14:10](https://ref.ly/2Chr14:10). **c.** by וּלְ…בֵּין † [Jo 2:17](https://ref.ly/Joel2:17). **d.** by לְבֵין…בֵּין † [Is 59:2](https://ref.ly/Isa59:2). בֵּין is used not only of actual locality, but also with verbs of dividing (fig.) [Gn 1:14](https://ref.ly/Gen1:14), [Lv 10:10](https://ref.ly/Lev10:10), and of judging, knowing, teaching, etc., if the idea of *distinguishing* be involved, as [Gn 16:5](https://ref.ly/Gen16:5), [2 S 19:36](https://ref.ly/2Sam19:36) הַאֵדַע בֵּין טוֺב לְרָע can I discern *between* good and evil ? [1 K 3:9](https://ref.ly/1Kgs3:9), [Jon 4:11](https://ref.ly/Jonah4:11); [Gn 31:49](https://ref.ly/Gen31:49) (watch *between*), Ju [11:10](https://ref.ly/Judg11:10) (hear), [Jos 22:27](https://ref.ly/Josh22:27) (witness), [Is 2:4](https://ref.ly/Isa2:4) and he shall judge (arbitrate) *between* the nations, [5:3](https://ref.ly/Isa5:3); [Lv 27:33](https://ref.ly/Lev27:33), Ez [44:23](https://ref.ly/Ezek44:23); [Mal 3:18](https://ref.ly/Mal3:18), [2 Ch 14:10](https://ref.ly/2Chr14:10) (see RV); and in other metaph. applications, as of a covenant or sign *between* two contracting parties, [Gn 9:12](https://ref.ly/Gen9:12), [9:15](https://ref.ly/Gen9:15), [Ex 31:13](https://ref.ly/Exod31:13) \+; or an oath [Gn 26:28](https://ref.ly/Gen26:28), [2 S 21:7](https://ref.ly/2Sam21:7); enmity or strife [Gn 3:15](https://ref.ly/Gen3:15), [13:7](https://ref.ly/Gen13:7), [Dt 25:1](https://ref.ly/Deut25:1), [Pr 6:19](https://ref.ly/Prov6:19); peace [1 K 5:26](https://ref.ly/1Kgs5:26); good\-will [Pr 14:9](https://ref.ly/Prov14:9).—It is used of *time* in the phrase of P בֵּין הָעַרְבַּיִם (v. sub עֶרֶב), \& [Ne 5:18](https://ref.ly/Neh5:18) בֵּין עֲשֶרֶת יָמִים *during the interval of* ten days, i.e. every ten days (unusual).
> 
>  With other prepositions:—**a.** † אֶל־בֵּין, after a verb implying motion, *in between, in among* Ez [31:10](https://ref.ly/Ezek31:10), [31:14](https://ref.ly/Ezek31:14). So אֶל־בֵּינוֺת לְ † ib. [10:2](https://ref.ly/Ezek10:2). **b.** † בְּבֵן [Is 44:4](https://ref.ly/Isa44:4) *in the midst of, amongst* (LXX Ew Di Che כְּבֵין מַיִם חָצִיר). **c.** † עַל־בֵּין nearly as אֶל־בֵּין Ez [19:11](https://ref.ly/Ezek19:11) **d.** † מִבֵּין *from between:* [Gn 49:10](https://ref.ly/Gen49:10) nor the ruler's staff מִבֵּין רַגְלָיו *from between* his feet (where, as the king sits in state, he holds it), [Ex 25:22](https://ref.ly/Exod25:22) *from between* the cherubim (so [Nu 7:89](https://ref.ly/Num7:89)), [Ho 2:4](https://ref.ly/Hos2:4), [Zc 6:1](https://ref.ly/Zech6:1), [9:7](https://ref.ly/Zech9:7), [Dt 28:57](https://ref.ly/Deut28:57) the after\-birth הַיּוֺצֵת מִבֵּין רַגְלֶיהָ that cometh forth *from between* her feet, i.e. from her womb (cf. Il. 19\. 10 πεσεῖν μετὰ ποσσὶ γυναικός) Repeated [2 K 16:14](https://ref.ly/2Kgs16:14) to specify the two objects *from between* which a thing is moved. Ez [47:18](https://ref.ly/Ezek47:18) is difficult and uncertain: v. Comm. Less precisely *from, the midst of:* [Nu 17:2](https://ref.ly/Num17:2) מִבֵּין הַשְּרֵפָה *out of the midst of* the burning, [Psalm 104:12](https://ref.ly/Ps104:12) מִבֵּין עֳפָאִים *from amongst* the branches they utter their song, [Je 48:45](https://ref.ly/Jer48:45) and a flame מִבֵּין סִיחוֺן  *from the midst of* Sihon (Sihon representing his people: but expression is singular; rd. perh. ´מִבֵּית ס, cf. [Nu 21:28](https://ref.ly/Num21:28) ´מִקִּרְיַת ס: ‖ in both passages מֵחֶשְׁבּוֺן) , Ez [37:21](https://ref.ly/Ezek37:21). **e.** † מִבֵּינוֺת לְ *from between* Ez [10:2](https://ref.ly/Ezek10:2), [10:6](https://ref.ly/Ezek10:6), [10:6](https://ref.ly/Ezek10:6), [10:7](https://ref.ly/Ezek10:7).

> \[Str1002] † בִּירָה **n.f.** castle, palace (late \& prob. loan\-word; cf. As *bîrtu, fortress* DlHA 22; Pers. *bâru* \= Skr. *bura, bari*, v. Ry [Ne 2:8](https://ref.ly/Neh2:8))—**1\.** of *temple* at Jerusalem [1 Ch 29:1](https://ref.ly/1Chr29:1), [29:19](https://ref.ly/1Chr29:19) הַבִּירָת; of for\-tress near temple [Ne 2:8](https://ref.ly/Neh2:8), [7:2](https://ref.ly/Neh7:2) (cf. βᾶρις LXXL [Ne 1:1](https://ref.ly/Neh1:1), [2:8](https://ref.ly/Neh2:8), [7:2](https://ref.ly/Neh7:2)—B ἁβιρά, βειρά—and later βᾶρις JosAnt. xv. 11, 4\). **2\.** בְּשׁוּשַׁן הַבִּירָה [Ne 1:1](https://ref.ly/Neh1:1), [Est 1:2](https://ref.ly/Esth1:2), [1:5](https://ref.ly/Esth1:5), [2:3](https://ref.ly/Esth2:3), [2:5](https://ref.ly/Esth2:5), [2:8](https://ref.ly/Esth2:8), [3:15](https://ref.ly/Esth3:15), [8:14](https://ref.ly/Esth8:14), [9:6](https://ref.ly/Esth9:6), [9:11](https://ref.ly/Esth9:11), [9:12](https://ref.ly/Esth9:12), [Dn 8:2](https://ref.ly/Dan8:2); in these passages it appar. means a fortress in the city bearing the same name (cf. esp. [Est 3:15](https://ref.ly/Esth3:15), [8:14](https://ref.ly/Esth8:14), [8:15](https://ref.ly/Esth8:15), [9:6](https://ref.ly/Esth9:6), [9:12](https://ref.ly/Esth9:12), [9:15](https://ref.ly/Esth9:15), [9:18](https://ref.ly/Esth9:18)).

> \[Str1003] † \[בִּירָנִית] **n.f.** fortress, fortified place (late); only pl. abs. בִּירָנִיּוֺת וְעָרֵי מִסְכְּנוֺת [2 Ch 17:12](https://ref.ly/2Chr17:12) built by Jehoshaphat; וּמִגְדָּלִים ´בּ [27:4](https://ref.ly/2Chr27:4) built by Jotham.

## בַּ֫יִת (id: BDB01159)

\[Str1004] בַּ֫יִת:2034 **n.m.**:[Dt 8:12](https://ref.ly/Deut8:12) house (Ph. בת, MI. בת, sf. ביתה, Ar.بَيْتٌ, Aram. בַּיְתָא, ܒܰܝܬܳܐ As. *bîtu*, COTGloss, Sab. בית, בת, *fortress* DHMZMG 1883, 387; *temple* Hal257 DHMZMG 1876, 697, Eth. ቤት፡ Palm. in בת עלמא, בת מקברתא *sepulchre* Vog32, 64 al.; etym. dub.; Thes √ בות, Aram. בּוּת, ܒܳܬ *spend the night,* Ar. بَاتَ Eth. ቤተ፡ but this perh. denom., \& בית fr. √ ר“ל c. ת afform. cf. Sta:§187 a)—abs. בַּיִת [Ex 12:30](https://ref.ly/Exod12:30) \+; בָּיִת [Gn 33:17](https://ref.ly/Gen33:17) \+; בַּ֫יְתָה (ה—ָ loc.) [Psalm 68:7](https://ref.ly/Ps68:7) \+; cstr. בֵּית [Gn 12:15](https://ref.ly/Gen12:15) \+; sf. בֵּיתִי [Gn 15:2](https://ref.ly/Gen15:2) \+; בֵּיתְךָ [Gn 7:1](https://ref.ly/Gen7:1) \+ , etc.; הַבַּ֫יְתָה, בַּיְתָה (*in*) *to the house, homeward,* [Gn 19:10](https://ref.ly/Gen19:10), [Psalm 68:7](https://ref.ly/Ps68:7) \+; also בֵּ֫יתָה (*in*)*to the house of …*; pl. בָּֽתִּים [Ex 1:21](https://ref.ly/Exod1:21) \+ (i.e. *bâttîm*, v. Nö in MeArch. i. 456 f., cf. Ges§ 96\); cstr. בָּֽתֵּי [Ex 8:17](https://ref.ly/Exod8:17) \+; sf. בָּתֶּיךָ [Ex 10:6](https://ref.ly/Exod10:6); בָּֽתָּיו [1 Ch 28:11](https://ref.ly/1Chr28:11); בָּֽתֵּינוּ [Ex 12:27](https://ref.ly/Exod12:27) \+; בָּֽתֵּיכֶם [Gn 42:19](https://ref.ly/Gen42:19); בָּתֵּיהֶם [Nu 16:32](https://ref.ly/Num16:32) \+; (בָּֽתֵּ֫ימוֺ [Psalm 49:12](https://ref.ly/Ps49:12)), etc.;—**1\.** *house*, **a.** as dwelling habitation [Gn 19:2](https://ref.ly/Gen19:2), [19:3](https://ref.ly/Gen19:3), [19:4](https://ref.ly/Gen19:4), [19:11](https://ref.ly/Gen19:11), [27:15](https://ref.ly/Gen27:15), [33:17](https://ref.ly/Gen33:17) (obj. of בָּנָה) [Ex 12:7](https://ref.ly/Exod12:7) \+ oft.; מוֺשַׁב ´בּ [Lv 25:29](https://ref.ly/Lev25:29) (cf. בַּיִת לְשִׁבְתִּי [2 S 7:5](https://ref.ly/2Sam7:5)); בֵּית \= *in the house of*, when modifying word follows, cf. DrSm 29, n. 2 (after st. cstr. מוֺלֶדֶת בַּיִת אוֺ מוֺלֶדֶת חוּץ [Lv 19:9](https://ref.ly/Lev19:9)); e.g. אָבִיךָ ´בּ [Gn 24:23](https://ref.ly/Gen24:23); אִישָׁהּ ´בּ [Nu 30:11](https://ref.ly/Num30:11) יהוה ´בּ [2 K 23:24](https://ref.ly/2Kgs23:24); so in n.pr. ביתאל [Am 7:13](https://ref.ly/Amos7:13); בַּ֫יְתָה(ה) *into the house, home* [Ex 9:19](https://ref.ly/Exod9:19) (of man \& beast) cf., Ju [19:18](https://ref.ly/Judg19:18), [1 S 6:7](https://ref.ly/1Sam6:7) (cf. v:[10](https://ref.ly/1Sam6:10)) [1 K 13:7](https://ref.ly/1Kgs13:7), [13:15](https://ref.ly/1Kgs13:15), [Psalm 68:7](https://ref.ly/Ps68:7), [Is 14:17](https://ref.ly/Isa14:17) etc.; cf. sub **7** infr.; partic (α) in J occasionally of tent [Gn 27:15](https://ref.ly/Gen27:15) cf. [33:17](https://ref.ly/Gen33:17) (c. בנה; here of nomad's hut); usually (β ) house of solid materials, with doorposts, etc. [Ex 12:7](https://ref.ly/Exod12:7), [Dt 6:9](https://ref.ly/Deut6:9), [11:20](https://ref.ly/Deut11:20), walls [Lv 14:37](https://ref.ly/Lev14:37), [14:39](https://ref.ly/Lev14:39), of stones, timber \& mortar v:[45](https://ref.ly/Lev14:45) (cf. v:[40](https://ref.ly/Lev14:40), v:[42](https://ref.ly/Lev14:42), v:[43](https://ref.ly/Lev14:43)); so also [Jos 2:15](https://ref.ly/Josh2:15); supported by pillars Ju [16:26](https://ref.ly/Judg16:26) f.; with roof on which one could walk [2 S 11:12](https://ref.ly/2Sam11:12) etc.; v. esp. temple and king's house in Jerusalem 1 K 5–7 etc.; cf. Ct [1:7](https://ref.ly/Song1:7); [Je 22:14](https://ref.ly/Jer22:14); of Ezekiel's temple Ez 4o f.; בָּֽתֵּכֶם סְפוּנִים of luxurious houses [Hg 1:4](https://ref.ly/Hag1:4); בנה בָּֽתִּים טֹבִים [Dt 8:12](https://ref.ly/Deut8:12); בּנה בַּיִת־חָדָשׁ [Dt 20:5](https://ref.ly/Deut20:5) cf. [22:8](https://ref.ly/Deut22:8); also c. בנה [Dt 28:30](https://ref.ly/Deut28:30) \+ oft.; (γ) cstr. before word of material, אֲרָזִים ´בּ [2 S 7:2](https://ref.ly/2Sam7:2), [7:7](https://ref.ly/2Sam7:7) \= [1 Ch 17:1](https://ref.ly/1Chr17:1) (´הַא ´בּ) v:[6](https://ref.ly/1Chr17:6); הַשֵּׁן ´בּ [1 K 22:39](https://ref.ly/1Kgs22:39) cf. [Am 3:15](https://ref.ly/Amos3:15); גָּזִית ´בּ  *house of hewn* stone [Am 5:11](https://ref.ly/Amos5:11); cf. בֵּית יַעַר הַלְּבָנוֺן [1 K 7:2](https://ref.ly/1Kgs7:2), [2 Ch 9:20](https://ref.ly/2Chr9:20), בֵּית הַיַּעַר [Is 22:8](https://ref.ly/Isa22:8); (δ) also before word of quality or characteristic, בֵּית אֵבֶל [Ec 7:2](https://ref.ly/Eccl7:2) ‖מִשְׁתֶּה ´בּ v:[4](https://ref.ly/Eccl7:4) ‖שִמְחָה ´בּ; מַרְזֵחַ ´בּ [Je 16:5](https://ref.ly/Jer16:5); מִשְׁתֶּה ´בּ [Je 16:8](https://ref.ly/Jer16:8); בָּֽתֵּי חֶמְדָּתֵךְ Ez [26:12](https://ref.ly/Ezek26:12); בֵּית תַּעֲנֻגֶ\+יהָ [Mi 2:9](https://ref.ly/Mic2:9); cf. מְרִי ´בּ *rebellious house* sub **5\. d** infr.; (ε) in combinations, of structures for various purposes:—(1\) בֵּית הַמֶּלֶךְ \= *palace* [1 K 9:1](https://ref.ly/1Kgs9:1), [9:10](https://ref.ly/1Kgs9:10), [10:12](https://ref.ly/1Kgs10:12) \+, etc.; בֵּית מַמְלָכָה [Am 7:13](https://ref.ly/Amos7:13) cf. sub **2\.** infr.; הַמַּלְכוּת ´בּ [Est 5:1](https://ref.ly/Esth5:1) cf. [2:16](https://ref.ly/Esth2:16); לְמַלְכוּתוֺ ´בּ [2 Ch 1:18](https://ref.ly/2Chr1:18); (2\) בֵּית הַסֹּהַר \=  *the prison* [Gn 39:20](https://ref.ly/Gen39:20), [39:20](https://ref.ly/Gen39:20), [39:21](https://ref.ly/Gen39:21), [39:22](https://ref.ly/Gen39:22), [39](https://ref.ly/Gen39:39); [22](https://ref.ly/Gen39:22), [39:23](https://ref.ly/Gen39:23), [40:3](https://ref.ly/Gen40:3), [40:5](https://ref.ly/Gen40:5); cf. מִשְׁמַרְכֶם ´בּ [Gn 42:19](https://ref.ly/Gen42:19); מִשְׁמֶרֶת ´בּ [2 S 20:3](https://ref.ly/2Sam20:3); ´בּ הָאָסוּר [Je 37:15](https://ref.ly/Jer37:15); הָאֲסיּרים ´בּ Ju [16:21](https://ref.ly/Judg16:21); [16:25](https://ref.ly/Judg16:25); הָסַוּרִים ´בּ [Ec 4:14](https://ref.ly/Eccl4:14); הַכֶּלֶא ´בּ [1 K 22:27](https://ref.ly/1Kgs22:27), [2 K 17:4](https://ref.ly/2Kgs17:4), [2 Ch 18:26](https://ref.ly/2Chr18:26), [Je 37:15](https://ref.ly/Jer37:15), [37:18](https://ref.ly/Jer37:18) cf. [Is 42:7](https://ref.ly/Isa42:7); כֶּלֶא ´בּ [2 K 25:27](https://ref.ly/2Kgs25:27); הַמַּהְפֶּכֶת ´בּ [2 Ch 16:10](https://ref.ly/2Chr16:10); הַכְּליּ֯א ´בּ [Je 37:4](https://ref.ly/Jer37:4) [52:31](https://ref.ly/Jer52:31); בָּֽתֵּי כְלָאִים [Is 42:22](https://ref.ly/Isa42:22); בֵּית־הַבּוֺר [Je 37:16](https://ref.ly/Jer37:16); הַפְּקֻדּוֺת ´בּ [Je 52:31](https://ref.ly/Jer52:31); (3\) בֵּית הַנָּשִׁים *house of the women, harem* [Est 2:9](https://ref.ly/Esth2:9), [2:11](https://ref.ly/Esth2:11), [2:13](https://ref.ly/Esth2:13), [2:14](https://ref.ly/Esth2:14); (4\) מִשְׁתֵּה הַיַּיִן ´בּ [Est 7:8](https://ref.ly/Esth7:8); הַָ\+יּיִן ´בּ Ct [2:4](https://ref.ly/Song2:4); cf. בָּֽתֵּי מָשוֺש [Is 32:13](https://ref.ly/Isa32:13); (5\) בֵּית הַחֹרֶף [Am 3:15](https://ref.ly/Amos3:15), [Je 36:22](https://ref.ly/Jer36:22); ´הַקָּיִץ בּ [Am 3:15](https://ref.ly/Amos3:15); (6\) בֵּית נְכֹתֹה \& בֵּית כֵּלָיו [2 K 20:13](https://ref.ly/2Kgs20:13) \= [Is 39:2](https://ref.ly/Isa39:2); (7\) בֵּית הַגָּן *garden\-h.* (or n. pr.? cf. p. MI בת במת) v. also [13:32](https://ref.ly/1Kgs13:32), [2 K 17:29](https://ref.ly/2Kgs17:29), [17:32](https://ref.ly/2Kgs17:32); בֵּית אוֺצַר אֱלֽהָיו [Dn 1:2](https://ref.ly/Dan1:2) etc.; made of woven material [2 K 23:7](https://ref.ly/2Kgs23:7); אֲשֶׁר הַנָּשִׁים אֹרְגוֺת שָׁם בָּֽתִּים לָאֲשֵׁרָה perh. \= *tent\-shrines* but txt. dub. LXX χεττιεῖν (כתנות ?); LXXL στολάς; cf. EwH iii.718 \& Klo; (ζ) of portion of larger building (late), so pl. בָּֽתָּיו [1 Ch 28:11](https://ref.ly/1Chr28:11) i.e. of the temple (‖גַּנְזַכָּיו, עֲלִיֹּתָיו, חֲדָרָיו); cf. הַכַּפֹּרֶת ´בּ *ib.*; הַמְַבַשְּׁלִים ´בּ Ez [46:24](https://ref.ly/Ezek46:24). **b.** as shelter or abode of animals [1 S 6:7](https://ref.ly/1Sam6:7), [6:10](https://ref.ly/1Sam6:10) cf. [Ex 9:19](https://ref.ly/Exod9:19); בֵּית עַכָּבִישׁ [Jb 8:14](https://ref.ly/Job8:14) *spider's web* (cf. בָּנָה כָעָשׁ בֵּיתוֺ [27:18](https://ref.ly/Job27:18)); of wild ass [Jb 39:6](https://ref.ly/Job39:6) שַמְתִּי עֲרָבָה בֵיתוֺ; צִפּוֺר מָֽצְאָה בַּיִת [Psalm 84:4](https://ref.ly/Ps84:4) (‖קֵן); חֲסִידָה בְּרוֺשִׁים בֵּיתָהּ [Psalm 104:17](https://ref.ly/Ps104:17) (cf. ‖ \& Che); [Pr 30:26](https://ref.ly/Prov30:26) (of the שְׁפַנִּים ‘ conies ’). **c.** fig. of human bodies בָּֽתֵּי־חֹמֶר [Jb 4:19](https://ref.ly/Job4:19) *houses of clay*, cf. in phr. שֹׁמְרֵי הַבַּיִת [Ec 12:3](https://ref.ly/Eccl12:3)  *keepers of the house*, i.e. the arms; v. further בֵּית מְגוּרָי [Psalm 119:54](https://ref.ly/Ps119:54). **d.** of shₑ’ôl, שְׁאוֺל בֵּיתִי [Jb 17:13](https://ref.ly/Job17:13), cf. בֵּית מוֺעֵד לְכָל־חָי [30:23](https://ref.ly/Job30:23) (‖מָוֶת); בֵּית עוֺלָמוֺ [Ec 12:5](https://ref.ly/Eccl12:5) (perh. \= tomb, v. De). **e.** of abode of light \& darkness [Jb 38:20](https://ref.ly/Job38:20). **f.** of land of Ephraim as יהוה ´בּ [Ho 8:1](https://ref.ly/Hos8:1), [9:15](https://ref.ly/Hos9:15) (cf. ´אֶרֶץ י [9:3](https://ref.ly/Hos9:3)). **2\.** *place*, of Jerusalem בֵּית קִבְרוֺת אֲבֹתַי [Ne 2:3](https://ref.ly/Neh2:3) (‖עִיר v:[5](https://ref.ly/Neh2:5)), cf. also n.pr. cpd. with בֵּית, infr., \& בֵּית מַמְלָכָה [Am 7:13](https://ref.ly/Amos7:13) (pred. of Bethel); בֵּית נְתִיבוֺת [Pr 8:2](https://ref.ly/Prov8:2) i.e. where paths meet RV; אֲבָנִים ´בּ [Jb 8:17](https://ref.ly/Job8:17); on both these v. בֵּית sub \[בַּיִן] בֵּין p. 108\. † **3\.** *receptacle*, תְּעָלָה כְּבֵית סָאתַיִם [1 K 18:32](https://ref.ly/1Kgs18:32) *a trench like a receptacle of two seahs;* בָּֽתֵּי נֶפֶשׁ [Is 3:20](https://ref.ly/Isa3:20) i.e. vials of perfume; esp. בָּֽתִּים לְבַדִּים [Ex 25:27](https://ref.ly/Exod25:27) *holders for the staves*, i.e. rings, טַבָּעֹת *ib.;* so [37:14](https://ref.ly/Exod37:14), [37:27](https://ref.ly/Exod37:27), [38:5](https://ref.ly/Exod38:5); cf. בָּֽתִּים לַבְּרִיחִם [26:29](https://ref.ly/Exod26:29), [36:34](https://ref.ly/Exod36:34) *holders for the bars* (all P, \& all expl. by טבעת); בֵּית־לָהּ סָבִיב Ez [1:27](https://ref.ly/Ezek1:27) (si vera l.) *its* \[cstr. as [Psalm 58:5](https://ref.ly/Ps58:5)] *house* (\= enclosing cage) *was round about* (del. BHiCo). **4\.** of house as containing a family, hence in phr. of slaves belonging to household יְלִידֵי בֵיתוֺ [Gn 14:14](https://ref.ly/Gen14:14) cf. [17:12](https://ref.ly/Gen17:12) (opp. מִקְנַת־כֶּסֶף) v:[13](https://ref.ly/Gen17:13) (all ‖ *id.*) fig. of Israel [Je 2:14](https://ref.ly/Jer2:14) (‖עֶבֶד); cf. בֶּן־בֵּיתִי [Gn 15:3](https://ref.ly/Gen15:3); בְּנֵי־בַיִת הָיָה לִי [Ec 2:7](https://ref.ly/Eccl2:7) (as token of wealth \& prosperity); also of one's sister מוֺלֶדֶת בַּיִת [Lv 18:19](https://ref.ly/Lev18:19) (‖חוּץ ´מ cf. infr.); אַנְשֵׁי בֵיתוֺ [Gn 17:27](https://ref.ly/Gen17:27); ´נַפְשׁוֺת בּ [Gn 36:6](https://ref.ly/Gen36:6); כָּל־הַנֶּפֶשׁ לְבֵית־יַעֲקֹב [Gn 46:27](https://ref.ly/Gen46:27). [1 K 12:23](https://ref.ly/1Kgs12:23) supr.; (ι) † יִשָּשכָר ´בּ [1 K 15:27](https://ref.ly/1Kgs15:27); (κ) † לֵוִי ´בּ [Ex 2:1](https://ref.ly/Exod2:1) (E) [Nu 17:23](https://ref.ly/Num17:23) (P) [Zc 12:13](https://ref.ly/Zech12:13) (´ל ´משׁפחת ב), [Psalm 135:20](https://ref.ly/Ps135:20); \& (λ) אַהֲרֹן ´בּ [Psalm 115:10](https://ref.ly/Ps115:10), [115:12](https://ref.ly/Ps115:12), [118:3](https://ref.ly/Ps118:3), [135:19](https://ref.ly/Ps135:19). **e.** technically, yet with some looseness of usage, בֵּית אָב *father's house,* of family or clan, pl. בֵּית אָבוֺת *father\-houses, families* (e.g. [Nu 1:2](https://ref.ly/Num1:2)) (79 t.; only P \& Chr) [Ex 6:14](https://ref.ly/Exod6:14), [12:3](https://ref.ly/Exod12:3), [Nu 1:2](https://ref.ly/Num1:2), [1:4](https://ref.ly/Num1:4), [1:18](https://ref.ly/Num1:18) \+ 41 t. Nu; [Jos 22:14](https://ref.ly/Josh22:14), [22:14](https://ref.ly/Josh22:14), [1 Ch 4:38](https://ref.ly/1Chr4:38), [5:13](https://ref.ly/1Chr5:13) \+ 25 t. Ch; [Ezr 2:59](https://ref.ly/Ezra2:59), [10:16](https://ref.ly/Ezra10:16), [Ne 7:61](https://ref.ly/Neh7:61), [10:35](https://ref.ly/Neh10:35); \= tribe [Nu 17:17](https://ref.ly/Num17:17), [17:17](https://ref.ly/Num17:17), [17:18](https://ref.ly/Num17:3), [17:21](https://ref.ly/Num17:6); \= main division of tribe [Nu 3:20](https://ref.ly/Num3:20), [3:24](https://ref.ly/Num3:24) \+, cf. [34:14](https://ref.ly/Num34:14), [34:14](https://ref.ly/Num34:14) etc.; futher sub\-division [Ex 12:3](https://ref.ly/Exod12:3), [1 Ch 7:2](https://ref.ly/1Chr7:2), [7:7](https://ref.ly/1Chr7:7) \+; cf. esp. [1 Ch 23:11](https://ref.ly/1Chr23:11) *Jeush and Beriah had not many sons, therefore they became* לְבֵית אָב לִפְקֻדָּה אֶחָת (cf. ראשׁי אבות, sub אב). **6\.** house, including *household affairs,* persons, property, etc. וַיַּפְקִדֵהוּ עַל־בֵּיתוֺ [Gn 39:4](https://ref.ly/Gen39:4) cf. בְּבֵיתוֺ v:[5](https://ref.ly/Gen39:5); אֲשֶׁר עַל־בֵּיתוֺ [Gn 44:1](https://ref.ly/Gen44:1), [44:4](https://ref.ly/Gen44:4); hence אשׁר על הבית as title of governor of the palace (EwH iii. 269\) [Is 22:15](https://ref.ly/Isa22:15), [36:3](https://ref.ly/Isa36:3) cf. [1 K 4:6](https://ref.ly/1Kgs4:6), [2 K 15:5](https://ref.ly/2Kgs15:5); in Israel [1 K 16:9](https://ref.ly/1Kgs16:9), [18:3](https://ref.ly/1Kgs18:3), [2 K 10:5](https://ref.ly/2Kgs10:5): further [2 S 17:23](https://ref.ly/2Sam17:23), [2 K 20:1](https://ref.ly/2Kgs20:1) \= [Is 38:1](https://ref.ly/Isa38:1); hence of personal property [1 K 13:8](https://ref.ly/1Kgs13:8) אִם־תִּתֶּן־לִי אֶת־חֲצִי בֵּיתֶָךָ; family and property (everything on which one depends) [Jb 8:15](https://ref.ly/Job8:15). **7\.** בַּ֫יְתָה, בָּיְתָה, lit. *housewards,* hence metaph. *inwards,* † [Ex 28:26](https://ref.ly/Exod28:26) \= [39:19](https://ref.ly/Exod39:19) (sc. of the ephod), [1 K 7:25](https://ref.ly/1Kgs7:25) \= [2 Ch 4:4](https://ref.ly/2Chr4:4) (sc. of the circle of oxen supporting the molten sea); [2 S 5:9](https://ref.ly/2Sam5:9) מִן־הַמִּלּוֺא וָבָיְתָה from the Millo and *inward*, Ez [44:17](https://ref.ly/Ezek44:17). **8\.** מִבַּיִת **a.** adv. *on the inside* (of a building, chest, etc.: opp. מִחוּץ) † [Gn 6:14](https://ref.ly/Gen6:14), [Ex 25:11](https://ref.ly/Exod25:11) \= [37:2](https://ref.ly/Exod37:2), [Lv 14:41](https://ref.ly/Lev14:41), [1 K 6:15](https://ref.ly/1Kgs6:15), [6:16](https://ref.ly/1Kgs6:16), [7:9](https://ref.ly/1Kgs7:9), [2 K 6:30](https://ref.ly/2Kgs6:30) (of a person's clothes), Ez [7:15](https://ref.ly/Ezek7:15); so with ה *loc.* מִבַּ֫יְתָה † [1 K 6:15](https://ref.ly/1Kgs6:15) **b.** † מִבֵּית לְ (cstr. Ges§ 130, 1 n.) prep. *within* (opp. מִחוּץ לְ), מִבֵּית לַפָּרֹכֶת *within* the veil [Ex 26:33](https://ref.ly/Exod26:33), [Lv 16:2](https://ref.ly/Lev16:2), [16:12](https://ref.ly/Lev16:12), [Nu 18:7](https://ref.ly/Num18:7) לְכָל־דְּבַר הַמִּזִבֵּחַ וּלְמִבֵּית לַפָּרֹכֶת for everything of the altar, and for (that) *within* the veil; [1 K 7:8](https://ref.ly/1Kgs7:8), [7:31](https://ref.ly/1Kgs7:31); (rd. לַכְּתֵפֹת; see VB \& StaZAW 1883, 165\); אֶל־מִבֵּית לְ (after a verb of motion) *in within* [Lv 16:15](https://ref.ly/Lev16:15), [2 K 11:15](https://ref.ly/2Kgs11:15) \= [2 Ch 23:14](https://ref.ly/2Chr23:14).

 Note.—בית perh. occurs abbrev. into בּ in n.pr. בְּעֶשְׁתְּרָה q.v. so Thes al.; cf. also Aram. בא \= בית in like usage LagArmen. Stud. § 339; Se i. 51; GGA 1884, 276\. 

> \[Str1006] † בַּ֫יִת c. art. הַבַּיִת [Is 15:2](https://ref.ly/Isa15:2) acc. to Ew Brd Di al. **n.pr.loc** but abbrev.; perh. for בֵּית דִּבְלָתַיִם [Je 48:22](https://ref.ly/Jer48:22) (so Ew al.) or בֵּית בָּמוֺת cf. MI27 (cf. De Di); others (Ges De Che Or) take ´הַבּ here \= *the house*, i.e. temple or shrine.
> 
> 110:b. בית ad fin.: on בְּ as abbrev. בית v. WetzestHauran 110 KampffmeyerZPV xv (1892\). 111\.

> \[Str1007] † בֵּית אָ֫וֶן **n.pr.loc.** (*house of iniquity* or *idolatry*? hardly likely unless as alteration of orig. בֵּית אוֺן, *house of wealth* or *strength*) eastward from Bethel [Jos 7:2](https://ref.ly/Josh7:2), [18:12](https://ref.ly/Josh18:12), [1 S 14:23](https://ref.ly/1Sam14:23), [1 S 13:5](https://ref.ly/1Sam13:5) ´בֵּית־א (on [Ho 4:15](https://ref.ly/Hos4:15), [5:8](https://ref.ly/Hos5:8), [10:5](https://ref.ly/Hos10:5) v. בֵּֽיתְאֵל infr.); site unknown.

> \[Str1008] בֵּֽיתְאֵל:71 **n.pr.loc.** Bethel (so read, not בֵּית־אֵל as [Jos 7:2](https://ref.ly/Josh7:2) \+ acc. to v. d. H; cf. Baer [Gn 12:8](https://ref.ly/Gen12:8); *house of God*, or *house of Ēl*)—**1\.** ancient place and seat of worship in Ephraim on border of Benjamin, identif. with *Luz* (former name) [Gn 28:19](https://ref.ly/Gen28:19), [35:6](https://ref.ly/Gen35:6), [Jos 18:13](https://ref.ly/Josh18:13), Ju [1:23](https://ref.ly/Judg1:23); appar. distinguished from Luz [Jos 16:2](https://ref.ly/Josh16:2) (yet cf. Di); name connected with vision of Jacob when journeying to Paddan\-Aram (JE) [Gn 28:19](https://ref.ly/Gen28:19), [35:7](https://ref.ly/Gen35:7) (where אֵל בֵּֽיתְאֵל, but LXX SyrVer Vulgate Ol del. אֵל; Di maintains); cf. [Ho 12:5](https://ref.ly/Hos12:5); when journeying *from* P.\-A. [35:15](https://ref.ly/Gen35:15) (P); name appar. given first to a stone ([Gn 28:18](https://ref.ly/Gen28:18), [35:14](https://ref.ly/Gen35:14)) cf. WeSkizzen iii. 70; הַר בֵּֽיתְאֵל [1 S 13:2](https://ref.ly/1Sam13:2); later important place of worship [1 S 10:3](https://ref.ly/1Sam10:3) עָלָה אֶ־ל הָאֱלֽהִים בֵּיתְאֵל; abode of prophet [1 K 13:11](https://ref.ly/1Kgs13:11), [2 K 2:3](https://ref.ly/2Kgs2:3); Jeroboam set up one of the golden calves at Bethel [1 K 12:29](https://ref.ly/1Kgs12:29) f. cf. also [13:1](https://ref.ly/1Kgs13:1) f. [2 K 10:29](https://ref.ly/2Kgs10:29), [23:15](https://ref.ly/2Kgs23:15), [Je 48:13](https://ref.ly/Jer48:13) v. further [2 K 17:28](https://ref.ly/2Kgs17:28), [2 K 23:15](https://ref.ly/2Kgs23:15) Th prop. on internal grounds, בֵּית אַל *house of nothingness,* or אָלָה ´בּ *house of execration;* אָלָה ´בּ also RenanHist. iii. 185; in proph. [Am 3:14](https://ref.ly/Amos3:14), [4:4](https://ref.ly/Amos4:4), [5:5](https://ref.ly/Amos5:5), [5:5](https://ref.ly/Amos5:5), [5:6](https://ref.ly/Amos5:6), [7:10](https://ref.ly/Amos7:10), [7:13](https://ref.ly/Amos7:13), [Ho 10:15](https://ref.ly/Hos10:15); note esp. [Am 5:5](https://ref.ly/Amos5:5) ביתאל יִהְיֶה לְאָוֶן, \& בֵּית אָרֶן (*house of iniquity*) as substit. for ביתאל [Ho 4:15](https://ref.ly/Hos4:15), [5:8](https://ref.ly/Hos5:8), [10:5](https://ref.ly/Hos10:5) (עֶגְלוֺת בית און);—mod. *Beitîn* RobBR i. 448 f. GuêrinJudêe iii. 14–27 cf. BDPal 215 Surveyii. 305\. **2\.** place in south country of Judah, not far from Beersheba \& Ziklag [1 S 30:27](https://ref.ly/1Sam30:27) cf. We Dr; \= MT כסיל [Jos 15:30](https://ref.ly/Josh15:30) (txt. err.; LXX Βαιθηλ); also בְּתוּל [Jos 19:4](https://ref.ly/Josh19:4), בְּתוּאֵל [1 Ch 4:30](https://ref.ly/1Chr4:30).

DrSm lxviii); city assigned to Reuben [Jos 13:17](https://ref.ly/Josh13:17), [Nu 32:3](https://ref.ly/Num32:3), [32:38](https://ref.ly/Num32:38), [1 Ch 5:8](https://ref.ly/1Chr5:8); possessed by Moab [Je 48:23](https://ref.ly/Jer48:23), Ez [25:9](https://ref.ly/Ezek25:9);—mod. *Ma‘în* TristrMoab 303 f. > \[Str1032,358] † בֵּית חוֺרֹן **n.pr.loc.** [Jos 10:10](https://ref.ly/Josh10:10), [10:11](https://ref.ly/Josh10:11), also חֹרוֺן ´בּ, חֹרֹן ´בּ, \& in Ch חוֺרוֺן ´בּ, two cities in Ephraim, lower \& upper B.H. ## בָּכָה (id: BDB01214)

\[Str1058] † בָּכָה:114 **vb.** weep, bewail (Ar. بَكَى, Aram. בְּכָא, بكْء, As. *bakû* ZimBP 23, 56, Eth. በከየ፡)—**Qal** *Pf.* ´בּ [Gn 43:14](https://ref.ly/Gen43:14), [Ho 12:5](https://ref.ly/Hos12:5); וּבָֽכְתָה consec. [Dt 21:13](https://ref.ly/Deut21:13); בָּכִ֫יתִּי [Jb 30:25](https://ref.ly/Job30:25), etc.; *Impf.* וַיֵּבְח [Gn 37:35](https://ref.ly/Gen37:35) \+ 16 t. (וַיֵּבְךְּ [Gn 27:28](https://ref.ly/Gen27:28) etc.); 3 fs. תִּבְכֶּה [1 S 1:10](https://ref.ly/1Sam1:10) \+ 2 t.; וַתִּבְכֶּה [1 S 1:7](https://ref.ly/1Sam1:7), וַתֵּבְךְּ [Gn 21:16](https://ref.ly/Gen21:16) (where LXX masc. cf. Di) \+ 4 t.; 2 ms. תִּבְכֶּה [Is 30:19](https://ref.ly/Isa30:19), Ez [24:16](https://ref.ly/Ezek24:16); וַתִּבְכֶּה [2 K 22:19](https://ref.ly/2Kgs22:19), וַתֵּבְךְּ [2 S 12:21](https://ref.ly/2Sam12:21), [2 Ch 34:27](https://ref.ly/2Chr34:27); 2 fs. תִּבְכִּי [1 S 1:8](https://ref.ly/1Sam1:8); אֶבְכֶּה Ju [11:37](https://ref.ly/Judg11:37) \+ 4 t.; וָאֶבְכֶּה [2 S 12:22](https://ref.ly/2Sam12:22) \+ 2 t.; יִבְכּוּ [Lv 10:6](https://ref.ly/Lev10:6) \+ 3 t.; יִבְכָּ\+יוּן [Jb 31:38](https://ref.ly/Job31:38), [Is 33:7](https://ref.ly/Isa33:7); 3 fpl. תִּבְכֶּינָה [Jb 27:15](https://ref.ly/Job27:15), [Psalm 78:64](https://ref.ly/Ps78:64); ´וַתִּב [Ru 1:9](https://ref.ly/Ruth1:9), [1:14](https://ref.ly/Ruth1:14), etc.; *Imv.* pl. בְּכוּ [Je 22:10](https://ref.ly/Jer22:10), [Jo 1:5](https://ref.ly/Joel1:5); בְּכֶינָה [2 S 1:24](https://ref.ly/2Sam1:24); *Inf. abs.* בָּכוֺ [Is 30:19](https://ref.ly/Isa30:19) \+ 3 t. \+ [Mi 1:10](https://ref.ly/Mic1:10) (where rd. בְּעַכּוֺ, v. עכו); בָּכֹה [1 S 1:10](https://ref.ly/1Sam1:10) \+ 3 t.; *cstr.* לִבְכּוֺת [Gn 43:30](https://ref.ly/Gen43:30) \+ 3 t.; לִבְכֹּתָהּ [Gn 23:2](https://ref.ly/Gen23:2); *Pt.* בֹּכֶה (בּוֺכֶה) [Ex 2:6](https://ref.ly/Exod2:6) \+ 6 t.; fs. בּוֺכִיָּה [La 1:16](https://ref.ly/Lam1:16); pl. בֹּכִים (´בּו) [Ezr 3:12](https://ref.ly/Ezra3:12) \+, etc.;—**1\.** *weep* (in grief, humiliation, or joy), abs. [Gn 42:24](https://ref.ly/Gen42:24), [43:30](https://ref.ly/Gen43:30), [43:30](https://ref.ly/Gen43:30), [Ex 2:6](https://ref.ly/Exod2:6), [Nu 11:4](https://ref.ly/Num11:4), [11:10](https://ref.ly/Num11:10), [14:1](https://ref.ly/Num14:1), [1 S 1:7](https://ref.ly/1Sam1:7), [1:8](https://ref.ly/1Sam1:8), [1:10](https://ref.ly/1Sam1:10), [11:5](https://ref.ly/1Sam11:5), [30:4](https://ref.ly/1Sam30:4), [30:4](https://ref.ly/1Sam30:4), [2 S 1:12](https://ref.ly/2Sam1:12), [3:16](https://ref.ly/2Sam3:16), [13:36](https://ref.ly/2Sam13:36), [15:30](https://ref.ly/2Sam15:30), [15:30](https://ref.ly/2Sam15:30), [19:1](https://ref.ly/2Sam19:1), [19:2](https://ref.ly/2Sam19:2), [2 K 8:11](https://ref.ly/2Kgs8:11), [8:12](https://ref.ly/2Kgs8:12), [Is 30:19](https://ref.ly/Isa30:19), [30:19](https://ref.ly/Isa30:19), [Je 41:6](https://ref.ly/Jer41:6), [50:4](https://ref.ly/Jer50:4), Ez [24:16](https://ref.ly/Ezek24:16), [24:23](https://ref.ly/Ezek24:23), [La 1:2](https://ref.ly/Lam1:2), [Zc 7:3](https://ref.ly/Zech7:3), [Jo 1:5](https://ref.ly/Joel1:5), [2:17](https://ref.ly/Joel2:17), [Psalm 78:64](https://ref.ly/Ps78:64), [126:6](https://ref.ly/Ps126:6) cf. [69:11](https://ref.ly/Ps69:11) (but on text v. Che) [Jb 27:15](https://ref.ly/Job27:15), [Ec 3:4](https://ref.ly/Eccl3:4) (opp. *laugh* שחק) [Ezr 10:1](https://ref.ly/Ezra10:1), [Ne 8:9](https://ref.ly/Neh8:9), [Est 8:3](https://ref.ly/Esth8:3); once c. inanim. subj. [Jb 31:38](https://ref.ly/Job31:38) ´תְּלָמֶיהָ יִב *its furrows weep;* on [Ne 1:4](https://ref.ly/Neh1:4) יָשַׁבְתִּי וָאֶבְכֶּה cf. As.*attašab abakî*, Flood130 ed. Dl Gu§ 77 HptBAS i. 145; of loud weeping נָשָא ´הַקּוֺל וַיִּב [Gn 21:16](https://ref.ly/Gen21:16), [27:38](https://ref.ly/Gen27:38), [29:11](https://ref.ly/Gen29:11), Ju [2:4](https://ref.ly/Judg2:4), [21:2](https://ref.ly/Judg21:2), [1 S 11:4](https://ref.ly/1Sam11:4), [24:17](https://ref.ly/1Sam24:17), [2 S 3:32](https://ref.ly/2Sam3:32), [13:36](https://ref.ly/2Sam13:36), [Jb 2:12](https://ref.ly/Job2:12), [Ru 1:9](https://ref.ly/Ruth1:9), [1:14](https://ref.ly/Ruth1:14), cf. ´בְּאָזְנֵי י ´בּ [Nu 11:18](https://ref.ly/Num11:18), also [Jb 30:31](https://ref.ly/Job30:31). **2\.** c. acc. cogn. בְּכִי Ju [21:2](https://ref.ly/Judg21:2), [2 S 13:36](https://ref.ly/2Sam13:36), [2 K 20:3](https://ref.ly/2Kgs20:3) \= [Is 38:3](https://ref.ly/Isa38:3), cf. בִּבְכִי [Is 16:9](https://ref.ly/Isa16:9), מִבְּכִי [Je 48:32](https://ref.ly/Jer48:32), \& חַרְבֶּה בֶּכֶה ´בּ [Ezr 10:1](https://ref.ly/Ezra10:1); sq. קוֺל גָּדוֺל [2 S 15:33](https://ref.ly/2Sam15:33), cf. בקול גדול ´בּ [Ezr 3:12](https://ref.ly/Ezra3:12); *weep bitterly* ´מַר יִב [Is 33:7](https://ref.ly/Isa33:7); also c. inf. abs. *weep intensely, grievously* [1 S 1:10](https://ref.ly/1Sam1:10), [Je 22:10](https://ref.ly/Jer22:10), [La 1:2](https://ref.ly/Lam1:2), cf. [Is 30:19](https://ref.ly/Isa30:19) \& [Mi 1:10](https://ref.ly/Mic1:10) (but on text v. supr.) **3\.** sq. עַל *weep upon*, i.e. embrace and weep, עַל־צואריו [Gn 45:14](https://ref.ly/Gen45:14), [46:29](https://ref.ly/Gen46:29) cf. [45:15](https://ref.ly/Gen45:15), [50:1](https://ref.ly/Gen50:1), also עַל־פָּנָיו ´בּ [2 K 13:14](https://ref.ly/2Kgs13:14); v. further [Gn 33:4](https://ref.ly/Gen33:4), [45:14](https://ref.ly/Gen45:14) \& וַיִּבְכּוּ אִישׁ אֶת־רֵעֵהוּ [1 S 20:41](https://ref.ly/1Sam20:41); also sq. עַל *weep over, for* Ju [11:37](https://ref.ly/Judg11:37), [11:38](https://ref.ly/Judg11:38), [2 S 3:34](https://ref.ly/2Sam3:34), [La 1:16](https://ref.ly/Lam1:16); sq. אֶ־לֹ [2 S 1:24](https://ref.ly/2Sam1:24), [3:32](https://ref.ly/2Sam3:32), Ez [27:31](https://ref.ly/Ezek27:31); sq. לְ [Je 22:10](https://ref.ly/Jer22:10), [48:32](https://ref.ly/Jer48:32), [Jb 30:25](https://ref.ly/Job30:25); sq. מִפְּנִי *because of* [Je 13:17](https://ref.ly/Jer13:17); sq. temporal clause (of occasion of weeping) [Gn 50:17](https://ref.ly/Gen50:17), [Psalm 137:1](https://ref.ly/Ps137:1), [Ne 8:9](https://ref.ly/Neh8:9). **4\.** sq. acc. *bewail* [Gn 23:2](https://ref.ly/Gen23:2), [37:35](https://ref.ly/Gen37:35), [50:3](https://ref.ly/Gen50:3), [Lv 10:6](https://ref.ly/Lev10:6), [Nu 20:29](https://ref.ly/Num20:29), [Dt 21:13](https://ref.ly/Deut21:13), [34:8](https://ref.ly/Deut34:8), [Je 8:23](https://ref.ly/Jer8:23), cf. [Is 16:9](https://ref.ly/Isa16:9). **5\.** sq. עַל in sense of burden, annoy with weeping [Nu 11:13](https://ref.ly/Num11:13), Ju [14:16](https://ref.ly/Judg14:16), [14:17](https://ref.ly/Judg14:17) cf. לִפְנֵי [Nu 11:20](https://ref.ly/Num11:20). **6\.** ´לִפְנֵי י ´בּ of penitent weeping Ju [20:23](https://ref.ly/Judg20:23) (cf. Be; v. also [21:2](https://ref.ly/Judg21:2)) [Dt 1:45](https://ref.ly/Deut1:45), [2 K 22:19](https://ref.ly/2Kgs22:19), [2 Ch 34:27](https://ref.ly/2Chr34:27), cf. also [Nu 25:6](https://ref.ly/Num25:6); joined with fasting Ju [20:26](https://ref.ly/Judg20:26), [2 S 12:21](https://ref.ly/2Sam12:21), [12:22](https://ref.ly/2Sam12:22), cf. [Ezr 10:1](https://ref.ly/Ezra10:1); so of weeping in anxious entreaty [Ho 4:5](https://ref.ly/Hos4:5); on pt. as n.pr. Ju [2:1](https://ref.ly/Judg2:1), [2:5](https://ref.ly/Judg2:5) v. בֹּכִים infr. **Pi.** *Pt.* fs. מְבַכָּה *lament* [Je 31:25](https://ref.ly/Jer31:25) sq. עַל; *bewail*, pl. מְבַכּוֺת sq. acc. Ez [8:14](https://ref.ly/Ezek8:14).

> \[Str1059] † בֶּ֫כֶה **n.\[m.]** *a weeping*, only [Ezr 10:1](https://ref.ly/Ezra10:1) בכו ´העם הַרְבֵּה בּ.

> \[Str1065] † בְּכִי **n.m.**:Ju [21:2](https://ref.ly/Judg21:2) weeping—בְּכִי Ju [21:2](https://ref.ly/Judg21:2) \+ 20 t. (also cstr. [Dt 34:8](https://ref.ly/Deut34:8), etc.); בִּבְכִָי [Gn 45:2](https://ref.ly/Gen45:2); בֶָּכִי [Is 15:2](https://ref.ly/Isa15:2) \+ 6 t.; sf. בִּכְִי\+י [Psalm 6:9](https://ref.ly/Ps6:9); —*weeping* [Psalm 30:6](https://ref.ly/Ps30:6), [Is 15:2](https://ref.ly/Isa15:2), [15:5](https://ref.ly/Isa15:5); as acc. cogn. (c. בכה) Ju [21:2](https://ref.ly/Judg21:2), [2 S 13:36](https://ref.ly/2Sam13:36), [2 K 20:3](https://ref.ly/2Kgs20:3) \= [Is 38:3](https://ref.ly/Isa38:3) cf. [Is 16:9](https://ref.ly/Isa16:9) (בבכי) [Je 48:32](https://ref.ly/Jer48:32) (מבכי); audible ´קול ב [Psalm 6:9](https://ref.ly/Ps6:9), [Is 65:19](https://ref.ly/Isa65:19), [Ezr 3:13](https://ref.ly/Ezra3:13) cf. [Je 3:21](https://ref.ly/Jer3:21), [31:16](https://ref.ly/Jer31:16), [Is 15:3](https://ref.ly/Isa15:3) (ייליל יֹרֵד בבכי v. [Je 9:17](https://ref.ly/Jer9:17)); so also [Gn 45:2](https://ref.ly/Gen45:2) (´ויתן את־קלו בב) \& [Je 48:5](https://ref.ly/Jer48:5) (בבכי יעלה בכי i.e. the sound of it shall ascend)—but text here suspicious, cf. [Is 15:5](https://ref.ly/Isa15:5); as disfiguring [Jb 16:16](https://ref.ly/Job16:16); ‖ words of mourning [Est 4:3](https://ref.ly/Esth4:3) (אֵבֶל, צוֺם, מִסְפֵּד), [Je 31:9](https://ref.ly/Jer31:9) (תחנונים cf. [3:21](https://ref.ly/Jer3:21)), [9:10](https://ref.ly/Jer9:10), [31:15](https://ref.ly/Jer31:15) (נהי), cf. בכי אבל משׁה[Dt 34:8](https://ref.ly/Deut34:8) \& Di *ad loc.;* cotrition (humiliation) [Jo 2:12](https://ref.ly/Joel2:12) (צום, מִסְפֵּד), [Is 22:12](https://ref.ly/Isa22:12) (מספד, קרחה, חגור שק), cf. [Mal 2:13](https://ref.ly/Mal2:13) (אנקה, דמעה); of bitter weeping [Is 22:4](https://ref.ly/Isa22:4) אֲמָרֵר בַּבָּכִי, cf. [Je 31:15](https://ref.ly/Jer31:15) (\& [Is 33:7](https://ref.ly/Isa33:7)); בכי יעזר [Is 16:9](https://ref.ly/Isa16:9), [Je 48:32](https://ref.ly/Jer48:32) i.e. YaÔzer in Moab, cf. יעזר n.pr.; [Psalm 102:10](https://ref.ly/Ps102:10) (מסכתי ´שׁקוי בב cf. [42:4](https://ref.ly/Ps42:4), [80:6](https://ref.ly/Ps80:6) \& Bab. *dîmtu maštîtî, tears* (*were*) *my drink* ZimBP 34, 42\). Trop., of trickling streams (נהרות) in mines—hindrance to miners [Jb 28:11](https://ref.ly/Job28:11).

> \[Str439] † בָּכוּת **n.f. weeping**. Only in אַלּוֺן בָּכוּת [Gn 35:8](https://ref.ly/Gen35:8) i.e. *mourning oak,* cf. אלון, p. 47\.

> \[Str1068] † \[בְּכִית] **n.f. weeping** [Gn 50:4](https://ref.ly/Gen50:4) ימי בְכִיתוֺ, i.e. the appointed time of mourning for him.

> \[Str1066] † בֹּכִים **n.pr.loc.** near Bethel, אל־הַבֹּכִים Ju [2:1](https://ref.ly/Judg2:1), בֹּכִים v:[5](https://ref.ly/Judg2:5) (cf. v:[4](https://ref.ly/Judg2:4)); LXX [2:1](https://ref.ly/Judg2:1) τὸν Κλαυθμῶνα καὶ ἐπὶ Βαιθηλ; v:[5](https://ref.ly/Judg2:5) Κλαυθμῶνες i.e. בְּכָּ יִם (LXXL Κλαυθμών);—on poss. connex. with אַלּוֺן בָּכוּת [Gn 35:8](https://ref.ly/Gen35:8) cf. Stu \& WeBleek's Einl. ed. 4, 183, Comp. Hex. 1889, 215; but perh. rd. ביתאל instead of בכים in v:[1](https://ref.ly/Gen35:1), cf. We BuRS 20\.

## בָּכַר (id: BDB01220)

\[Str1069] † \[בָּכַר] **vb.** (NH בְּכֵּר, Aram. בַּכֵּר, ܒܰܟܰܪ; cf. Ar. بَكَرَ *rise early, do anything early;* بِكْرٌ, *virgin, woman having her first child;* Eth. በኵር፡ *primogenitus;* As. *bukru, first\-born*, Dl:§ 65, 5\)—**Pi.** *Impf.* יְבַכֵּר Ez [47:12](https://ref.ly/Ezek47:12); *Inf.* לְבַכֵּר [Dt 21:16](https://ref.ly/Deut21:16); —**1\.** *bear early, new fruit* Ez [47:12](https://ref.ly/Ezek47:12). **2\.** *make* or *constitute as first\-born* [Dt 21:16](https://ref.ly/Deut21:16) (den. of בכיר). **Pu.** *Impf.* יְבֻכַּר [Lv 27:26](https://ref.ly/Lev27:26) *born* or *made a firstling*. **Hiph.** *Pt. f.* מַבְכִּירָה [Je 4:31](https://ref.ly/Jer4:31) *one bearing her first child*.

> \[Str1060] בְּכוֺר:122 **n.m.** first\-born—[Gn 35:23](https://ref.ly/Gen35:23) \+ 78 t.; בְּכֹר [Gn 25:13](https://ref.ly/Gen25:13) \+ 14 t.; sf. בְּכֹּרִי [Gn 49:3](https://ref.ly/Gen49:3) \+ 14 t.; בְּכוֺרוֺ [Gn 38:6](https://ref.ly/Gen38:6) \+ 3 t.; pl. cstr. בְּכוֺרֵי [Ne 10:37](https://ref.ly/Neh10:37) \+ 2 t.; בְּכוֺרֵיהֶם [Psalm 136:10](https://ref.ly/Ps136:10); pl. f. בְּכֹרוֺת [Gn 4:4](https://ref.ly/Gen4:4) [Ne 10:37](https://ref.ly/Neh10:37); בְּכֹרֹת [Dt 12:6](https://ref.ly/Deut12:6), [12:17](https://ref.ly/Deut12:17), [14:23](https://ref.ly/Deut14:23); —**1\.** *men and women:* **a.** *individuals* [Gn 25:13](https://ref.ly/Gen25:13) \+ 69 t. **b.** coll. [Nu 3:46](https://ref.ly/Num3:46); [3:50](https://ref.ly/Num3:50), [8:16](https://ref.ly/Num8:16), [18:15](https://ref.ly/Num18:15). **c.** pl. [Ne 10:37](https://ref.ly/Neh10:37), [Psalm 135:8](https://ref.ly/Ps135:8), [136:10](https://ref.ly/Ps136:10). **d.** כל בכור [Ex 11:5](https://ref.ly/Exod11:5), [12:12](https://ref.ly/Exod12:12), [12:29](https://ref.ly/Exod12:29), [13:2](https://ref.ly/Exod13:2), [13:13](https://ref.ly/Exod13:13), [13:15](https://ref.ly/Exod13:15), [13:15](https://ref.ly/Exod13:15), [22:28](https://ref.ly/Exod22:28), [34:2](https://ref.ly/Exod34:2), [Nu 3:12](https://ref.ly/Num3:12), [3:12](https://ref.ly/Num3:12), [3:12](https://ref.ly/Num3:12), [3:13](https://ref.ly/Num3:13), [3:40](https://ref.ly/Num3:40), [3:42](https://ref.ly/Num3:42), [3:43](https://ref.ly/Num3:43), [3:45](https://ref.ly/Num3:45), [8:17](https://ref.ly/Num8:17), [8:17](https://ref.ly/Num8:17), [8:18](https://ref.ly/Num8:18), [33:4](https://ref.ly/Num33:4), [Psalm 78:51](https://ref.ly/Ps78:51), [105:36](https://ref.ly/Ps105:36). **2\.** *animals:* **a.** *individuals* [Lv 27:26](https://ref.ly/Lev27:26), [Nu 18:17](https://ref.ly/Num18:17), [Dt 15:19](https://ref.ly/Deut15:19), [15:19](https://ref.ly/Deut15:19), [33:17](https://ref.ly/Deut33:17). **b.** coll. בכור בהמה [Ex 11:5](https://ref.ly/Exod11:5), [12:29](https://ref.ly/Exod12:29), [13:15](https://ref.ly/Exod13:15), [Nu 3:41](https://ref.ly/Num3:41), [18:15](https://ref.ly/Num18:15); כל הבכור [Dt 15:19](https://ref.ly/Deut15:19). **c.** pl. בכרות [Gn 4:4](https://ref.ly/Gen4:4), [Dt 12:6](https://ref.ly/Deut12:6), [12:17](https://ref.ly/Deut12:17), [14:23](https://ref.ly/Deut14:23), [Ne 10:37](https://ref.ly/Neh10:37). **3\.** *figurative,* n. of relation בכור מות *first\-born of death* [Jb 18:13](https://ref.ly/Job18:13) (deadly disease); בְּכוֺרֵי דַלִּים *first\-born of the poor* (the poorest) [Is 14:30](https://ref.ly/Isa14:30); Israel is the first\-born of Yahweh among the nations [Ex 4:22](https://ref.ly/Exod4:22) cf. [Je 31:9](https://ref.ly/Jer31:9); and the seed of David among dynasties [Psalm 89:28](https://ref.ly/Ps89:28).

> \[Str1071] † בֶּ֫כֶר **n.pr.m** (*young camel*, Ar. بَكْرٌ, As. *bakru* AsrbAnnals ix. 65\)—**1\.** son of Ephraim [Nu. 26:35](https://ref.ly/Num26:35) \= בֶּרֶד [1 Ch 7:20](https://ref.ly/1Chr7:20). **2\.** son of Benjamin [Gn 46:21](https://ref.ly/Gen46:21), [1 Ch 7:6](https://ref.ly/1Chr7:6), [7:8](https://ref.ly/1Chr7:8) (בָּכֶר).

> \[Str1074] † בֹּֽכְרוּ **n.pr.m.** a Benjamite (on form cf. גַּשְׁמוּ, מְלִיכוּ, \& Ol:§ 107 ad fin.; on Nab. n. pr. in ו v. EutNab 24, 25, etc. \& Nöib. 73 ff.) [1 Ch 8:38](https://ref.ly/1Chr8:38) \= [9:44](https://ref.ly/1Chr9:44).

> \[Str1075] † בִּכְּרִי **n.pr.m.** (*youthful*)—**1\.** a Benjamite [2 S 20:1](https://ref.ly/2Sam20:1), [20:2](https://ref.ly/2Sam20:2), [20:6](https://ref.ly/2Sam20:6), [20:7](https://ref.ly/2Sam20:7), [20](https://ref.ly/2Sam20:20); [10](https://ref.ly/2Sam20:10), [20:13](https://ref.ly/2Sam20:13), [20:21](https://ref.ly/2Sam20:21), [20:22](https://ref.ly/2Sam20:22); only in phr. שֶׁבַע בֶּן־בִּכְּרִי. **2\.** perh. **adj.gent.** pl. c. art. הַבִּכְּרִים [2 S 20:14](https://ref.ly/2Sam20:14) *the Bichrites* (i.e. family of Sheba‘), MT הַבֵּרִים; cf. LXX ἐν Χαρρει \= כרי(ב); so Klo Dr.

> \[Str1072,1070] † בִּכְרָה **n.f.** young camel, dromedary (Ar. بَكْرَهٌ *young she\-camel*) [Je 2:23](https://ref.ly/Jer2:23); pl. cstr. בִּכְרֵי [Is 60:6](https://ref.ly/Isa60:6).

> \[Str1062] † בְּכֹרָה **n.f.** right of first\-born [Gn 25:32](https://ref.ly/Gen25:32), [25:34](https://ref.ly/Gen25:34) (J) [Dt 21:17](https://ref.ly/Deut21:17), [1 Ch 5:1](https://ref.ly/1Chr5:1), [5:2](https://ref.ly/1Chr5:2); sf. בְּכֹרָתִי [Gn 27:36](https://ref.ly/Gen27:36) (JE); בְּכֹרָתְךָ [Gn 25:31](https://ref.ly/Gen25:31) (J); בְּכֹרָתוֺ [Gn 25:33](https://ref.ly/Gen25:33), [43:33](https://ref.ly/Gen43:33) (J) [1 Ch 5:1](https://ref.ly/1Chr5:1).

> \[Str1064] † בְּכוֺרַת **n.pr.m.** (*first\-born*) Benjamite [1 S 9:1](https://ref.ly/1Sam9:1).

> \[Str1067] † בְּכִירָה **n.f.** first\-born, always of women [Gn 19:31](https://ref.ly/Gen19:31), [9:33](https://ref.ly/Gen9:33), [19:34](https://ref.ly/Gen19:34), [19:37](https://ref.ly/Gen19:37), [29:26](https://ref.ly/Gen29:26) (JE) [1 S 14:49](https://ref.ly/1Sam14:49).

> \[Str1063,1073] † בִּכּוּרָה **n.f.** first ripe fig, early fig (regarded as a delicacy) (Löw391; cf. Ar. بَاكُورَة, Span. *albacora*, Moorish *bokkôre*) [Mi 7:1](https://ref.ly/Mic7:1), [Ho 9:10](https://ref.ly/Hos9:10); sf. בִּכּוּרָהּ \= בִּכּוּרָתָהּ (rd. בִּכּוּרָה Di), [Is 28:4](https://ref.ly/Isa28:4); pl. בַּכֻּרוֺת [Je 24:2](https://ref.ly/Jer24:2).
> 
> 114:b. בִּכּוּרָה: בַּכֻּרוֺת [Je 24:2](https://ref.ly/Jer24:2) prob. a diff. word, pl. abstr. \= *early ripeness,* cf. Du.

> \[Str1061] † בִּכּוּרִים **n.m.** first\-fruits—[Lv 2:14](https://ref.ly/Lev2:14), [23:17](https://ref.ly/Lev23:17), [Nu 28:26](https://ref.ly/Num28:26) (P) [2 K 4:42](https://ref.ly/2Kgs4:42), [Ne 3:12](https://ref.ly/Neh3:12), [13:31](https://ref.ly/Neh13:31); בִּכֻּרִים [Lv 23:20](https://ref.ly/Lev23:20) (P); cstr. בִּכּוּרֵי [Ex 23:16](https://ref.ly/Exod23:16), [23:19](https://ref.ly/Exod23:19) (E) [34:22](https://ref.ly/Exod34:22), [34:26](https://ref.ly/Exod34:26) (J) [Nu 13:20](https://ref.ly/Num13:20) (E) [18:13](https://ref.ly/Num18:13) (P) [Ne 10:36](https://ref.ly/Neh10:36), [10:36](https://ref.ly/Neh10:36), Ez [44:30](https://ref.ly/Ezek44:30); sf. בִּכּוּרֶיךָ [Lv 2:14](https://ref.ly/Lev2:14); —the first of grain and ftuit that ripened and was gathered and offered to God according to the ritual; לֶחֶם הַבִּכּוּרִים bread made of the new grain offered at Pentecost [Lv 23:20](https://ref.ly/Lev23:20); יום הבכורים *day* of the first\-fruits (Pentecost) [Nu 28:26](https://ref.ly/Num28:26).

> \[Str1077] בַּל v. sub בלה. p. 115

> \[Str1078] בֵּל, בֵּלְאשַׁצַּר v. sub בעל, p. 128\.

Merodach\-baladan* \= *Marduk\-abal\-iddina, Marduk hath given a son;* cf. *Esarhaddon,* v. אֵסַרְחַדֹּן).
