## בָּלַג (id: BDB01235)

\[Str1082] † \[בָּלַג] **vb.** gleam, smile (Ar. بَلِجَ)—only **Hiph.** *Impf.* 1 S. c. וְ subord. וְאַבְ֫לִיגָה [Jb 9:27](https://ref.ly/Job9:27) \+ 2 t.; *Pt.* מַבְלִיג [Am 5:9](https://ref.ly/Amos5:9); —**1\.** *shew a smile, look cheerful* [Psalm 39:14](https://ref.ly/Ps39:14), [Jb 9:27](https://ref.ly/Job9:27), [10:20](https://ref.ly/Job10:20). **2\.** *cause to burst* or *flash* שֹׁד על־עָז ´המב [Am 5:9](https://ref.ly/Amos5:9) (cf. Ew St).

> \[Str1083] † בִּלְגָּה **n.pr.m.** (*cheerfulness*)—**1\.** priest of 15th course (David's time) [1 Ch 24:14](https://ref.ly/1Chr24:14). **2\.** priest that went up with Zerubbabel [Ne 12:5](https://ref.ly/Neh12:5), [12:18](https://ref.ly/Neh12:18).

> \[Str1084] † בִּלְגַּי **n.prm.** (*id.*) priest with Neh. [Ne 10:9](https://ref.ly/Neh10:9).

> \[Str4010] † \[מַבְלִיגִית] **n.f.** smiling, cheerfulness, source of brightening—מַבְלִיגִיתִי עֲלֵי יָגוֺן [Je 8:18](https://ref.ly/Jer8:18) *a source of brightening to me in sorrow;* but text dub. cf. VB Che.

> \[Str1085] † בִּלְדַּד **n.pr.m.** (LXX Βαλδαδ; NöZMG 1888, 479 *Bel has loved,* cf. אֶלְדָּד; DlPa 298; ZK ii. 177 comp. cuneif. *Bir\-Dadda*, cf. HptHbr 1885, 224\) 2nd friend of Job (הַשֻּׁחִי) הַשּׁוּחִי ´בּ [Jb 2:11](https://ref.ly/Job2:11), [8:1](https://ref.ly/Job8:1), [18:1](https://ref.ly/Job18:1), [25:1](https://ref.ly/Job25:1), [42:9](https://ref.ly/Job42:9).

> \[Str1088] † בָּלָה **n.pr.loc.** in Simeon [Jos 19:3](https://ref.ly/Josh19:3).

* **Associated Passages:** Job 42:9; Joshua 19:3; Job 2:11; Job 18:1; Job 9:27; Psalms 39:14; Amos 5:9; Job 8:1; Nehemiah 10:9; 1 Chronicles 24:14; Job 25:1; Nehemiah 12:18; Jeremiah 8:18; Nehemiah 12:5; Job 10:20


## בָּלָה (id: BDB01241)

\[Str1086] † \[בָּלָה] **vb.** become old and worn out (Ar. بَلِىَ, Aram. בְּלֵי, ܒܠܴܐ, Eth. በልየ፡ *id.*)—**Qal** *Pf.* בָּלְֽתָה [Dt 8:4](https://ref.ly/Deut8:4), בָּלוּ [29:4](https://ref.ly/Deut29:4) \+; *Impf.* יִבְלֶה [Jb 13:28](https://ref.ly/Job13:28) etc.; *Inf.* c. sf. בְּלֽתִי [Gn 18:12](https://ref.ly/Gen18:12); —*wear out* (intr.), esp. of garments [Dt 8:4](https://ref.ly/Deut8:4), [29:4](https://ref.ly/Deut29:4), [29:4](https://ref.ly/Deut29:4), all c. מֵעַל pregn. *wear out* (and fall) *from upon...* (hence [Ne 9:21](https://ref.ly/Neh9:21)), [Jos 9:13](https://ref.ly/Josh9:13); fig. of the heavens (with sim. of garment) [Is 50:9](https://ref.ly/Isa50:9), [Psalm 102:27](https://ref.ly/Ps102:27) כַּבֶּגֶד יִבְלוּ, the earth [Is 51:6](https://ref.ly/Isa51:6) כַּבֶּגֶד תִּבְלֶה; the bones (through suffering) [Psalm 32:3](https://ref.ly/Ps32:3); afflicted man [Jb 13:28](https://ref.ly/Job13:28) וְהוּא כְּרָקָב יִבְלֶה (‖כְּבֶגֶד אֲכָלוֺ עָשׁ); of an aged and decrepit woman [Gn 18:12](https://ref.ly/Gen18:12) (J) אַחֲרֵי בְלֽתִי after *I am worn out.* **Pi.** caus. of Qal. **a.** *wear out* (trans.), fig. [La 3:4](https://ref.ly/Lam3:4) בִּלָּה בְשָרִי וְעוֺרִי, [Psalm 49:15](https://ref.ly/Ps49:15) and their form לְבַלּוֺת שְּׁאוֺל is for She’ôl *to consume away* (others rd. לִבְלוֺת is for *wasting away* \[Dr§ 204], connecting ´שׁ with foll.), [1 Ch 17:9](https://ref.ly/1Chr17:9) לְבַלּֽתוֺ *to wear* it (Isr.) *out* (altered fr. לְעַנּוֺתוֺ [2 S 7:10](https://ref.ly/2Sam7:10)), cf. [Dn 7:25](https://ref.ly/Dan7:25) Aram. **b.** *wear out by use, use to the full,* [Is 65:22](https://ref.ly/Isa65:22) and the work of their hands יְבַלּוּ they shall *use to the full, enjoy,* [Jb 21:13](https://ref.ly/Job21:13) they *wear out* their days in prosperity (Qr here יְכַלּוּ *complete,* which perh. is the true reading in both passages; cf. [Ex 5:13](https://ref.ly/Exod5:13), [Jb 36:11](https://ref.ly/Job36:11)).—On בַּ֝לּוֺתֹּי [Psalm 92:11](https://ref.ly/Ps92:11), v. sub בָּלַל.

> \[Str1087] † \[בָּלֶה] **adj.** worn out; f. בָּלָה Ez [23:43](https://ref.ly/Ezek23:43) (of a woman, cf. [Gn 18:12](https://ref.ly/Gen18:12) supr.); pl. בָּלִים [Jos 9:4](https://ref.ly/Josh9:4) (sacks), v:[4](https://ref.ly/Josh9:4) (wine\-skins), בָּלוֺת v:[5](https://ref.ly/Josh9:5) (sandals), v:[5](https://ref.ly/Josh9:5) (garments).

> \[Str1094] † \[בְּלוֺא] **n.\[m.]** worn out things, rags (Syr. ܒ̈ܠܳܝܶܐ *id.*) pl. cstr. בְּלֽויֵ [Je 38:11](https://ref.ly/Jer38:11), [38:11](https://ref.ly/Jer38:11), בְּלוֺאֵי v:[12](https://ref.ly/Jer38:12).

> \[Str8399] † \[תַּבְלִית] **n.f.** destruction: c. sf. [Is 10:25](https://ref.ly/Isa10:25) וְאַפִּי עַל־תַּבְלִיתָם and mine anger for *their destruction.*

> \[Str1077] בַּל:69 **adv.** not (Ph. *id.:* e.g. CISi. 165, 15 בל יכן לכהן shall *not* be for the priest; :3, 3 בל עתי \= before my time) a poet. syn. of לאֹ, of comparatively rare occurrence, [Ho 7:2](https://ref.ly/Hos7:2), [9:16](https://ref.ly/Hos9:16) (Qr) [Is 14:21](https://ref.ly/Isa14:21), [35:9](https://ref.ly/Isa35:9), [43:17](https://ref.ly/Isa43:17), [Pr 9:13](https://ref.ly/Prov9:13), [14:7](https://ref.ly/Prov14:7), [19:23](https://ref.ly/Prov19:23), [22:29](https://ref.ly/Prov22:29), [23:7](https://ref.ly/Prov23:7), [23:35](https://ref.ly/Prov23:35), [23:35](https://ref.ly/Prov23:35), [24:23](https://ref.ly/Prov24:23), [1 Ch 16:30](https://ref.ly/1Chr16:30) ( \= [Psalm 96:10](https://ref.ly/Ps96:10)), only besides, except in the passages cited, in other Psalms: often repeated in the same context, as [Is 26:10](https://ref.ly/Isa26:10), [26:10](https://ref.ly/Isa26:10), [26:11](https://ref.ly/Isa26:11), [26:14](https://ref.ly/Isa26:14), [26:14](https://ref.ly/Isa26:14), [26:18](https://ref.ly/Isa26:18), [26:18](https://ref.ly/Isa26:18), [33:20](https://ref.ly/Isa33:20), [33:20](https://ref.ly/Isa33:20), [33:21](https://ref.ly/Isa33:21), [33:23](https://ref.ly/Isa33:23), [33:23](https://ref.ly/Isa33:23), [33:24](https://ref.ly/Isa33:24), [44:8](https://ref.ly/Isa44:8), [44:9](https://ref.ly/Isa44:9), [44:9](https://ref.ly/Isa44:9), [44:9](https://ref.ly/Isa44:9), [Psalm 10:4](https://ref.ly/Ps10:4), [10:6](https://ref.ly/Ps10:6), [10:11](https://ref.ly/Ps10:11), [10:15](https://ref.ly/Ps10:15), [10:18](https://ref.ly/Ps10:18), [16:2](https://ref.ly/Ps16:2), [16:4](https://ref.ly/Ps16:4), [16:4](https://ref.ly/Ps16:4), [16:8](https://ref.ly/Ps16:8), [17:3](https://ref.ly/Ps17:3), [17:3](https://ref.ly/Ps17:3), [17:5](https://ref.ly/Ps17:5), [21:3](https://ref.ly/Ps21:3), [21:8](https://ref.ly/Ps21:8), [21:12](https://ref.ly/Ps21:12); also used oft. with אֶמּוֺט, יִמּוֺט, תִּמּוֺט [Psalm 10:6](https://ref.ly/Ps10:6), [16:8](https://ref.ly/Ps16:8), [21:8](https://ref.ly/Ps21:8), [30:7](https://ref.ly/Ps30:7), [46:6](https://ref.ly/Ps46:6), [93:1](https://ref.ly/Ps93:1), [96:10](https://ref.ly/Ps96:10), [104:5](https://ref.ly/Ps104:5), [Pr 10:30](https://ref.ly/Prov10:30), [12:3](https://ref.ly/Prov12:3), [Jb 41:15](https://ref.ly/Job41:15). In [Is 40:24](https://ref.ly/Isa40:24) it is prob. that it acquires from the context the sense of *hardly:* yea, *hardly* are they planted, yea, *hardly* are they sown ..., when he even bloweth upon them, and they wither; cf. לאֹ [2 K 20:4](https://ref.ly/2Kgs20:4). Joined anomalously with an infin., [Psalm 32:9](https://ref.ly/Ps32:9) בַּל קְרוֺב אֵלֶיךָ (else) there is *not* coming nigh thee (i.e. else they will not approach thee).

> \[Str1097] † בְּלִי **subst.** wearing out (بِلَني, ܒܠܳܝ *wearing out* of a garment), hence **1\.** fig. *destruction* [Is 38:17](https://ref.ly/Isa38:17) שַׁחַת בְּלִי pit of destruction (of She’ôl). **2\.** *defect, failure,* hence **adv. of negation** (cf. אֶפֶס), chiefly poet. for לאֹ, אֵין:—**a.** with finite vb. rare and only once in prose, [Gn 31:20](https://ref.ly/Gen31:20) (E) עַל־בְּלִי הִגִּיד לוֺ because he told him not, [Is 14:6](https://ref.ly/Isa14:6), [32:10](https://ref.ly/Isa32:10), [Ho 8:7](https://ref.ly/Hos8:7), [9:16](https://ref.ly/Hos9:16) (Kt) [Jb 41:18](https://ref.ly/Job41:18). **b.** used to negative an adj. or ptcp. [2 S 1:21](https://ref.ly/2Sam1:21) בְּלִי מָשִׁיחַ *not* anointed, [Psalm 19:4](https://ref.ly/Ps19:4) [Ho 7:8](https://ref.ly/Hos7:8): more freq., esp. in Job, joined with a subst. in sense of *without,* [Jb 8:11](https://ref.ly/Job8:11) will the reed\-grass grow בְּלִי־מָיִם *without* water? [24:10](https://ref.ly/Job24:10) they go about naked בְּלִי לְבוּשׁ *without* clothing, [31:39](https://ref.ly/Job31:39), [33:9](https://ref.ly/Job33:9), [34:6](https://ref.ly/Job34:6), [38:2](https://ref.ly/Job38:2) words בְּלִי־דַעַת *without* knowledge, [39:16](https://ref.ly/Job39:16), [42:3](https://ref.ly/Job42:3), [Psalm 59:5](https://ref.ly/Ps59:5) בְּלִי־עָוֺן יְרוּצוּן *without* (my) iniquity they run (against me), cf. v:[4](https://ref.ly/Ps59:4) לאֹ, [63:2](https://ref.ly/Ps63:2) a dry land בְּלִי מָיִם *without* water, [Is 28:8](https://ref.ly/Isa28:8), [Jb 30:8](https://ref.ly/Job30:8) בְּנֵי בְלִי־שֵׁם children of (men of) *no* name.
> 
>  With preps. **a.** † בִּבְלִי, in בִּבְלִי דַעַת [Dt 4:42](https://ref.ly/Deut4:42), [19:4](https://ref.ly/Deut19:4), [Jos 20:3](https://ref.ly/Josh20:3), [20:5](https://ref.ly/Josh20:5) D (\= *unawares:* all in D's law of homicide); *without knowledge* [Jb 35:16](https://ref.ly/Job35:16), [36:12](https://ref.ly/Job36:12). **b.** † לִבְלִי *in a state of* (v. sub לְ) *no*..., i.e. *without,*[Is 5:14](https://ref.ly/Isa5:14) לִבְלִי־חֹק *regardless of, without* measure, [Jb 38:41](https://ref.ly/Job38:41), [41:25](https://ref.ly/Job41:25) that is made לִבְלִי־חֵת (to be) *in a state of no* fear, i.e. to be *fearless*. **c.** † מִבְּלִי (*a*) *from want of*, followed by a subst. or infin., מִן expressing *causation,* [Dt 9:28](https://ref.ly/Deut9:28) ´מִבְּלִי יְכֹלֶת י on account of Jehovah's *not* being able... (in [Nu 14:16](https://ref.ly/Num14:16) מִבִּלְתִּי), [28:55](https://ref.ly/Deut28:55) (Ew238 d; but also RSJPh xvi. 72\), [Is 5:13](https://ref.ly/Isa5:13) *for want of* knowledge, [Ho 4:6](https://ref.ly/Hos4:6), Ez [34:5](https://ref.ly/Ezek34:5), [La 1:4](https://ref.ly/Lam1:4) מִבְּלִי בָּאֵי מוֺעֵד *for lack of* comers to the stated feast. Followed by a pleon. אֵין in the phrase …הֲמִבְּלִי אֵין is it *on account of* there being *no*...? (lit. is it *from the deficiency of no*...? cf. ܡܢ ܒܠܝ ܕܠܝܬ in Syr.; PS528), [Ex 14:11](https://ref.ly/Exod14:11), [2 K 1:3](https://ref.ly/2Kgs1:3), [1:6](https://ref.ly/2Kgs1:6), [1:16](https://ref.ly/2Kgs1:16). (β) *so that there is no*... (lit. *away from* there being *no*..., מִן expressing *negation,* and בְּלִי being pleon., as in מֵאֵין, v. sub אַיִן **6 d** β); [Je 2:15](https://ref.ly/Jer2:15) its cities are burnt מִבְּלִי יוֺשֵׁב *so that there is no* inhabitant, [9:9–11](https://ref.ly/Jer9:9-Jer9:11), Ez [14:15](https://ref.ly/Ezek14:15), [Zp 3:6](https://ref.ly/Zeph3:6). Once as a conj. מִבְּלִי אֲשֶׁר, with pleon. לאֹ, *so that not*... [Ec 3:11](https://ref.ly/Eccl3:11). In Job מִבְּלִי is used more freely \= *without,* the connexion with a preceding verb being no longer distinctly felt: [4:20](https://ref.ly/Job4:20) *without* any heeding, they perish for ever, [6:6](https://ref.ly/Job6:6), [24:7](https://ref.ly/Job24:7), [24:8](https://ref.ly/Job24:8); prob. also [4:11](https://ref.ly/Job4:11), [31:19](https://ref.ly/Job31:19) (though here the sense (α) would be admissible). (γ) in [Jb 18:15](https://ref.ly/Job18:15) תִּשְׁכּוֺן בְּאָהֳלוֺ מִבְּלִי־לוֺ, מִן is prob. *partitive* (so Hi): there shall dwell in his tent *what is naught* of his: Ew§ 323a De less probably *even naught,* cf. sub אַיִן **6 d** γ; Ges ‘ terror (supplied from v:[14](https://ref.ly/Job18:14)) shall dwell in his tent *so that it is no more* his. ’ **d.** † עַד־בְּלִי *till* there be *no*... [Psalm 72:7](https://ref.ly/Ps72:7), [Mal 3:10](https://ref.ly/Mal3:10).

> \[Str1099,1097] † בְּלִימָה **n.\[m.] nothingness** (from בְּלִי \& מָה, lit. *not\-aught*) [Jb 26:7](https://ref.ly/Job26:7) who hangeth the earth on ´בְּ.

> \[Str1100] † בְּלִיַּ֫עַל:27 **n.\[m.] worthlessness** (cpd. בְּלִי *not, without* and יַעַל *worth, use, profit*)—´בּ [Dt 13:14](https://ref.ly/Deut13:14) \+ 20 t.; בְּלִָ\+יּעַל [Psalm 101:3](https://ref.ly/Ps101:3) \+ 5 t.;—the quality of *being useless, good for nothing.* **1\.** abstr. אִישׁ בליעל(ה), אַנְשֵׁי הבליעל, *worthless, good\-for\-nothing, base fellows* [1 S 25:25](https://ref.ly/1Sam25:25), [2 S 16:7](https://ref.ly/2Sam16:7), [20:1](https://ref.ly/2Sam20:1), [1 K 21:13](https://ref.ly/1Kgs21:13), [Pr 16:27](https://ref.ly/Prov16:27); \= ´בֶּן־ב [1 S 25:17](https://ref.ly/1Sam25:17), ´בְּנֵי ב [Dt 13:14](https://ref.ly/Deut13:14), Ju [19:22](https://ref.ly/Judg19:22), [20:13](https://ref.ly/Judg20:13), [1 S 2:12](https://ref.ly/1Sam2:12), [10:27](https://ref.ly/1Sam10:27), [1 K 21:10](https://ref.ly/1Kgs21:10), [21:13](https://ref.ly/1Kgs21:13), [2 Ch 13:7](https://ref.ly/2Chr13:7); ´בַּת ב [1 S 1:16](https://ref.ly/1Sam1:16) (drunken woman); ´עֵד ב *base witness* [Pr 19:28](https://ref.ly/Prov19:28); ´דְּבַר ב *base, wicked thing* [Psalm 41:9](https://ref.ly/Ps41:9) (yet cf. **3** infr.), [101:3](https://ref.ly/Ps101:3) (add prob. also [1 S 29:10](https://ref.ly/1Sam29:10), so LXX We Dr); ´ב…דָּבָר (elliptical and in apposition) [Dt 15:9](https://ref.ly/Deut15:9). **2\.** concr. elliptical of ´אישׁ ב [2 S 23:6](https://ref.ly/2Sam23:6), [Jb 34:18](https://ref.ly/Job34:18); כָּל־אִישׁ רַע וּבְלִיַּעַל [1 S 30:22](https://ref.ly/1Sam30:22); אָדָם בְּלִיַּעַל [Pr 6:12](https://ref.ly/Prov6:12). **3\.** *ruin, destruction:* so [Psalm 41:9](https://ref.ly/Ps41:9) acc. to De Che al., but v. supr.; ´יֹעֵץ ב *counsellor of ruin* [Na 1:11](https://ref.ly/Nah1:11); ´ב alone *a man of ruin, destroyer* [Na 2:1](https://ref.ly/Nah2:1); ´נַחֲלֵי ב *floods of destruction* (‖שְׁאוֺל) [2 S 22:5](https://ref.ly/2Sam22:5) \= [Psalm 18:5](https://ref.ly/Ps18:5).

> \[Str1107] † בִּלְעֲדֵי (perh. from בַּל and עַד, עֲדֵי *unto;* Syr. ܒܶܠܥܳܕ \= ἄνευ, χωρίς; Nab. בלעד *except,* EutNab 3:9\)—sf. בִּלְעָדַי (3 t.), בַּלְעָדַי (4 t.), בִּלְעָדֶיךָ (once)—prop. *not unto,* hence apart from, except, without: **a.** [Jb 34:32](https://ref.ly/Job34:32) בִּלְעֲדֵי אֶחֱזֶה *except, apart from* (what) I see myself, do thou instruct me. With sf. [Gn 41:44](https://ref.ly/Gen41:44) בִּלְעָדֶיךָ *apart from* thee, *without* thee, no one shall lift up the head, [Is 45:6](https://ref.ly/Isa45:6) for there is none בִּלְעָדָי *except* me. Also with sf., as a particle of deprecation, [Gn 14:24](https://ref.ly/Gen14:24) בִּלְעָדַי *not to me !* i.e. I claim nothing, (in our idiom) *not at all !* [41:16](https://ref.ly/Gen41:16). **b.** with מִן, מִבַּלְעֲדֵי (so ܡܶܢ ܒܶܠܥܳܕ *without*). (α) *apart from,* esp. with the collat. idea of without the knowledge and consent, [Nu 5:20](https://ref.ly/Num5:20), [2 K 18:25](https://ref.ly/2Kgs18:25) ( \= [Is 36:10](https://ref.ly/Isa36:10)) am I now come up *apart from, without* ´י against this place to destroy it? [Je 44:19](https://ref.ly/Jer44:19) (cf. בִלְעָדֶיךָ [Gn 41:44](https://ref.ly/Gen41:44)). (β) *apart from, besides, except,* [Jos 22:19](https://ref.ly/Josh22:19), [2 S 22:32a](https://ref.ly/2Sam22:32) ( \= [Psalm 18:32a](https://ref.ly/Ps18:32)) for who is God *except* ´י? v:[32b](https://ref.ly/Ps18:32); similarly [Is 43:11](https://ref.ly/Isa43:11) וְאֵין מִבַּלְעָדַי מוֺשִׁיעַ, [44:6](https://ref.ly/Isa44:6), [44:8](https://ref.ly/Isa44:8), [45:21](https://ref.ly/Isa45:21).

> \[Str1115] \[בֵּ֫לֶת] **subst.** (from בָּלָה, of the form דֶּלֶת Ol:§ 146 b) prop. *failure,* hence used as **particle of negation,** not, except (cf. בְּלִי, אֶפֶס), twice with sf. (v. infr.), elsewhere always בִּלְתִּי (with binding vowel י—ִ, as mark of cstr. state: Sta§ 343 Ges§ 90, 3\), (Ph. בלת *only:* Tabnith\-Inscr.:5\)—†**1\. adv.** not, with an adj. [1 S 20:26](https://ref.ly/1Sam20:26) בִּלְתִּי טָהוֺר *not* clean, with a subst. [Is 14:6](https://ref.ly/Isa14:6) מַכַּת בִּלְתִּי סָרָה a stroke of *non*\-cessation, i.e. a never\-ceasing stroke, with a finite vb. (si vera l.) Ez [13:3](https://ref.ly/Ezek13:3) (RVmbut v. Dr§ 41 Obs.). † **2\.** after a preceding negation, *not* \= except (syn. זוּלָתִי), [Gn 21:26](https://ref.ly/Gen21:26) I have not heard בִּלְתִּי הַיּוֺם *except* to\-day, [Ex 22:19](https://ref.ly/Exod22:19) he that sacrificeth ´בִּלְתִּי לי *except* unto ´י, [Nu 32:12](https://ref.ly/Num32:12), [Jos 11:19](https://ref.ly/Josh11:19): so בִּלְתִּי אִם [Gn 47:18](https://ref.ly/Gen47:18), Ju [7:14](https://ref.ly/Judg7:14) (cf. כִּי אִם [Gn 28:17](https://ref.ly/Gen28:17), [Ne 2:2](https://ref.ly/Neh2:2)). With sf. (attached to the ground\-form בֵּלֶת) בִּלְתִּי *except* me †[Ho 13:4](https://ref.ly/Hos13:4), בִּלְתֶָּךָ *expect* thee † [1 S 2:2](https://ref.ly/1Sam2:2). †**3\. conj.** (likewise after a neg., expressed or implied) [Gn 43:3](https://ref.ly/Gen43:3) בִּלְתִּי אֲחִיכֶם אִתְּכֶם *except* your brother (be) with you, v:[5](https://ref.ly/Gen43:5) [Nu 11:6](https://ref.ly/Num11:6) our soul is dry, there is nothing at all; *save that* our eyes are toward the manna, [Is 10:4](https://ref.ly/Isa10:4) (and where will ye leave your glory ?) *save that* they bow down under the prisoners, and fall under the slain! i.e. (iron.) their only refuge will be among the corpses of a battle\-field. So בִּלְתִּי אִם [Am 3:3](https://ref.ly/Amos3:3), [3:4](https://ref.ly/Amos3:4).—[Dn 11:18](https://ref.ly/Dan11:18), where no neg. precedes, it is difficult to extract a sense consistent with the gen. usage of בִּלְתִּי: Ges *besides that* his reproach he will return unto him, Ew *only, nothing but,* Hi *certainly,* Drechsler (on [Is 10:4](https://ref.ly/Isa10:4)) *nay, even* (cf. RV).
> 
> **4\.** With preps. **a.** לְבִלְתִּי:86 *so as not*..., *in order not*... negation of לְ sq. inf.), usually sq. inf. cstr., as [Gn 4:15](https://ref.ly/Gen4:15) gave a sign to Cain לְבִלְתִּי הַכּוֺת־אֹתוֺ *in order* that any finding him should *not* smite him, [19:21](https://ref.ly/Gen19:21), [38:9](https://ref.ly/Gen38:9), [Ex 8:18](https://ref.ly/Exod8:18), [8:25](https://ref.ly/Exod8:25), [9:17](https://ref.ly/Exod9:17), [Lv 18:30](https://ref.ly/Lev18:30), [20:4](https://ref.ly/Lev20:4), [26:15](https://ref.ly/Lev26:15), [Dt 8:11](https://ref.ly/Deut8:11), [17:12](https://ref.ly/Deut17:12) the man that doeth presumptuously לְבִלְתִּי שְׁמֹעַ *so as not* to hearken etc. (cf. [Je 16:12](https://ref.ly/Jer16:12), [17:23](https://ref.ly/Jer17:23), [18:10](https://ref.ly/Jer18:10), [42:13](https://ref.ly/Jer42:13), [Dn 9:11](https://ref.ly/Dan9:11)) v:[20](https://ref.ly/Dan9:20), Ju [2:23](https://ref.ly/Judg2:23) \+; לְבִלְתִּי הוֺעִיל *in order not to* profit (the result represented forcibly as the design; cf. sub לְמַעַן) [Is 44:10](https://ref.ly/Isa44:10), [Je 7:8](https://ref.ly/Jer7:8); after vbs. of commanding [Gn 3:11](https://ref.ly/Gen3:11) which I commanded thee לְבִלְתִּי אֲכָל־מִמֶּנּוּ *not to* eat thereof, [2 K 17:15](https://ref.ly/2Kgs17:15), [Je 35:8](https://ref.ly/Jer35:8) f.; [35:14](https://ref.ly/Jer35:14), [Ru 2:9](https://ref.ly/Ruth2:9), swearing [Dt 4:21](https://ref.ly/Deut4:21), [Jos 5:6](https://ref.ly/Josh5:6), Ju [21:7](https://ref.ly/Judg21:7), Ez [20:15](https://ref.ly/Ezek20:15), agreeing [2 K 12:9](https://ref.ly/2Kgs12:9), interceding [Je 36:25](https://ref.ly/Jer36:25). Once לְבִלְתִּי לְ [2 K 23:10](https://ref.ly/2Kgs23:10) (cf. לְמַעַן לְ Ez [21:20](https://ref.ly/Ezek21:20), בַּעֲבוּר לְ [1 Ch 19:3](https://ref.ly/1Chr19:3)). Twice as conj. with the impf., [Ex 20:20](https://ref.ly/Exod20:20), [2 S 14:14](https://ref.ly/2Sam14:14) (cf. בַּעֲבוּר, \& מִן [Dt 33:11](https://ref.ly/Deut33:11)). In [Je 23:14](https://ref.ly/Jer23:14), [27:18](https://ref.ly/Jer27:18) sq. perf., which is inconsistent with the nature of a final conj.: rd. either יָשׁוּבוּ, יָבאֹוּ, or שׁוּב, בוֺא (cf. Dr§ 41 Obs.). On Ez [13:3](https://ref.ly/Ezek13:3) v. supr. †**b.** מִבִּלְתִּי *an account of not*... (negation of מִן sq. inf.): sq. inf. [Nu 14:16](https://ref.ly/Num14:16) ´מִבִּלְתִּי יְכֹלֶת י; a verbal noun Ez [16:28](https://ref.ly/Ezek16:28) מִבִּלְתִּי שָבְעָתֵח. †**c.** עַד־בִּלְתִּי *until not*..., sq. a perf. (Ges; RSJPh xvi. 72\), or an inf. (Ew§ 238 d), in the phrase (לָהֶם) עַד־בִּלְתִּי הִשְׁאִיר־לוֺ שָרִיד *until* one left him (them) *not* a remnant, [Nu 21:35](https://ref.ly/Num21:35), [Dt 3:3](https://ref.ly/Deut3:3), [Jos 8:22](https://ref.ly/Josh8:22), [10:33](https://ref.ly/Josh10:33), [11:8](https://ref.ly/Josh11:8), [2 K 10:11](https://ref.ly/2Kgs10:11).—[Jb 14:12](https://ref.ly/Job14:12) עַד־בִּלְתִּי שָׁמַיִם *till* there be *no* heaven (cf. עַד־בְּלִי [Psalm 72:7](https://ref.ly/Ps72:7)).

## בָּלַהּ (id: BDB01251)

\[Str1089] † \[בָּלַהּ] **vb.** trouble (Aram. ܒܰܠܗܺܝ, cf. Ar. بَلِهَ *be weak in intellect;* v. also בהל—only **Pi.** *Pt.* מְבַלֲהִים אותם לִבְנוֺת Kt (Qr מְבַהֲלִים needless) [Ezr 4:4](https://ref.ly/Ezra4:4) *troubled them in building.*

> \[Str1091] †בַּלָּהָה **n.f.** terror, dreadful event, calamity, destruction—בַּלָּהָה [Is 17:14](https://ref.ly/Isa17:14); pl. בַּלָּהוֺת [Jb 18:11](https://ref.ly/Job18:11) \+ 7 t.; cstr. בַּלְהוֺת [Jb 24:17](https://ref.ly/Job24:17); —**1\.** only pl. *terrors* [Jb 18:11](https://ref.ly/Job18:11), [27:20](https://ref.ly/Job27:20), [30:15](https://ref.ly/Job30:15); ´מֶלֶח בּ [Jb 18:14](https://ref.ly/Job18:14) \= death, cf. צַלְמָוֶת ´בּ [Jb 24:17](https://ref.ly/Job24:17). **2\.** *calamity* [Is 17:14](https://ref.ly/Isa17:14), pl. [Psalm 73:19](https://ref.ly/Ps73:19); *calamity, destruction* Ez [26:21](https://ref.ly/Ezek26:21), [27:36](https://ref.ly/Ezek27:36), [28:19](https://ref.ly/Ezek28:19).

> \[Str1090] †I. בִּלְהָה **n.pr.f.** (etym. dub.) handmaid of Rachel, concubine of Jacob [Gn 29:29](https://ref.ly/Gen29:29), [30:3](https://ref.ly/Gen30:3), [30:4](https://ref.ly/Gen30:4), [30:5](https://ref.ly/Gen30:5), [30:7](https://ref.ly/Gen30:7), [35:22](https://ref.ly/Gen35:22), [35:25](https://ref.ly/Gen35:25) (sons Dan \& Naphtali) [37:2](https://ref.ly/Gen37:2) (prob. gloss, Ol) [46:25](https://ref.ly/Gen46:25), [1 Ch 7:13](https://ref.ly/1Chr7:13).

> †II. בִּלהָה **n.pr.loc.** a city of Simeon [1 Ch 4:29](https://ref.ly/1Chr4:29), prob. \= בַּעֲלָה [Jos 15:29](https://ref.ly/Josh15:29), בָּלָה [19:3](https://ref.ly/Josh19:3) cf. Di; site dub.

> \[Str1092] †בִּלְהָן **n.pr.m. 1\.** descendant of Esau [Gn 36:27](https://ref.ly/Gen36:27) \= [1 Ch 1:42](https://ref.ly/1Chr1:42). **2\.** a Benjamite [1 Ch 7:10](https://ref.ly/1Chr7:10), [7:10](https://ref.ly/1Chr7:10).

> בְּלוֺא v. sub בלה.

> \[Str1095] †בֵּלְטְשַׁאצַּר **n.pr.m.** [Dn 1:7](https://ref.ly/Dan1:7), בֵּלְטְאשַׁצַָּר [Dn 10:1](https://ref.ly/Dan10:1) (prob. \= Bab. *balâṭsu\-uṣur, protect his life!* COT [Dn 1:7](https://ref.ly/Dan1:7) Dl in BDDn. p. ix; HoffmZA 1887, 56 conj. אצר \[ר]בלט שׁ *Balaṭ* ( \= good Saturn?) *protect the king!*—[Dn 4:5](https://ref.ly/Dan4:5) conn. with *Bel*, but name then inexplicable), name given to Daniel by Neb.

> בְּלִי, בְּלִימָה, בְּלִיַּעַל v. sub בלה.

## I בָּלַל (id: BDB01259)

\[Str1101] †I. בָּלַל **vb.** mingle, mix, confuse, confound (Ar. بَلَّ *moisten* (with water), cf. بَلَلٌ *moisture*, As. *balâlu*, DlPr 70; cf. Ph. בלל name of a sacrifice, \& NH בִּלְבֵּל; Aram. בַּלְבֵּל, ܒܰܠܒܶܠ)—**Qal** *Pf.* בָּלַל [Gn 11:9](https://ref.ly/Gen11:9), בַּ֝לּֽתִֹּי [Psalm 92:11](https://ref.ly/Ps92:11) (but cf. infr.); *Impf.* 1 pl. נָֽבְלָה \= נָכֹ֫לָּה [Gn 11:7](https://ref.ly/Gen11:7), cf. Köi. 325; *Pt.* pass. בָּלוּל [Ex 29:40](https://ref.ly/Exod29:40) \+ 3 t., בְּלוּלָה [Lv 2:5](https://ref.ly/Lev2:5) \+ 28 t., בְּלוּלֽת [Ex 29:2](https://ref.ly/Exod29:2) \+ 4 t.;—**1\.** *mingle, confuse* (obj. שָפָה \= speech, language, q.v.) [Gn 11:7](https://ref.ly/Gen11:7), [11:9](https://ref.ly/Gen11:9) (J). **2\.** *mix* (cakes or flour, etc. always with oil) term. techn. sacrif., only P (H [Lv 23:13](https://ref.ly/Lev23:13)), cf. Di on [Lv 2:4](https://ref.ly/Lev2:4); usually as מנחה, ´בַּשֶּׁמֶן ´סֹלֶת בּ [Ex 29:40](https://ref.ly/Exod29:40), [Lv 2:5](https://ref.ly/Lev2:5), [14:10](https://ref.ly/Lev14:10), [14:21](https://ref.ly/Lev14:21), [23:13](https://ref.ly/Lev23:13), [Nu 7:13](https://ref.ly/Num7:13), [7:19](https://ref.ly/Num7:19), [7:25](https://ref.ly/Num7:25), [7:31](https://ref.ly/Num7:31), [7:37](https://ref.ly/Num7:37), [7:43](https://ref.ly/Num7:43), [7:49](https://ref.ly/Num7:49), [7:55](https://ref.ly/Num7:55), [7:61](https://ref.ly/Num7:61), [7:67](https://ref.ly/Num7:67), [7:73](https://ref.ly/Num7:73), [7:79](https://ref.ly/Num7:79), [Nu 8:8](https://ref.ly/Num8:8), [15:4](https://ref.ly/Num15:4), [15:6](https://ref.ly/Num15:6), [15:9](https://ref.ly/Num15:9), [28:5](https://ref.ly/Num28:5), [28:9](https://ref.ly/Num28:9), [28:12](https://ref.ly/Num28:12), [28:12](https://ref.ly/Num28:12), [28:13](https://ref.ly/Num28:13), [28:20](https://ref.ly/Num28:20), [28:28](https://ref.ly/Num28:28), [29:3](https://ref.ly/Num29:3), [23:9](https://ref.ly/Num23:9), [23:14](https://ref.ly/Num23:14), ´בשׁ ´מנחה ב [Lv 7:10](https://ref.ly/Lev7:10) (opp. חֲרֵבָה) [9:4](https://ref.ly/Lev9:4), ´בשׁ ´חַלּוֺת מַצּוֺת ב [Ex 29:2](https://ref.ly/Exod29:2), [Lv 7:12](https://ref.ly/Lev7:12), [7:12](https://ref.ly/Lev7:12), i.e. made by mixing with oil; סלת ´בשׁ ´חלות מצות ב [Lv 2:4](https://ref.ly/Lev2:4), ´בשׁ ´סלת חלות ב [Nu 6:15](https://ref.ly/Num6:15), i.e. fine flour (in the form) of cakes so made. בַּ֝לּֽתִֹּי בְּשֶׁמֶן רַעֲנָן [Psalm 92:11](https://ref.ly/Ps92:11) *I shall be* (*am*) *anointed with fresh oil* AV RV; vb. not elsewhere in this sense; LXX Vulgate Hup Che rd. בְּלֽתִי fr. בלה, inf. cstr. sf., abstr. for concrete, *my wasting* \= *my wasting strength,* of declining age; Israel under figure of old man; this however is not favoured by context. The passage is therefore doubtful. **Hithpo.** *Impf.* יִתְבּוֺלָל [Ho 7:8](https://ref.ly/Hos7:8); ´אפרים בעמים הוא י *Ephraim, among the peoples doth he mix himself;* but Ew Now derive here fr. בלל \= בלה (or נבל) *waste away*, cf. Hiph. **Hiph.** *Impf.* וַנָּ֫בֶל [Is 64:5](https://ref.ly/Isa64:5) *and we faded away*, but rd. perh. וַנִּכֹּל fr. נבל cf. Di (De, less probably, derives from בלל—cf. Ew Now [Ho 7:8](https://ref.ly/Hos7:8)—or בול \= נבל).

> \[Str1098] † בְּלִיל **n.m.**:[Is 30:24](https://ref.ly/Isa30:24) fodder (Aram. ܒܠܝܺܠ; cf. Ar. بُلَّةٌ *moisture of fresh pasture*)—בְּלִיל [Is 30:24](https://ref.ly/Isa30:24); sf. בְּלִילוֺ [Jb 6:5](https://ref.ly/Job6:5), [24:6](https://ref.ly/Job24:6)—*fodder* (strictly, *mixed fodder, farrago*) [Jb 6:5](https://ref.ly/Job6:5); as growing in field [24:6](https://ref.ly/Job24:6) cf. [Is 30:24](https://ref.ly/Isa30:24).

> \[Str1101] †II. \[בָּלַל] **vb. denom.** to give provender—**Qal** *Impf.* וַָ\+יּבָל Ju [19:21](https://ref.ly/Judg19:21) Qr (Kt ויבול) sq. לְ *give provender to* the asses.

> \[Str7642] † שַׁבְּלוּל **n.m.** snail (Shaph. form, *causing moisture,* from notion of moisture or fluid in בלל);—name due to slimy trail [Psalm 58:9](https://ref.ly/Ps58:9), שַׁבְּלוּל תֶּמֶס יַהֲלֽךְ.

> \[Str8397] † תֶּ֫בֶל **n.\[m.]** confusion, violation of nature, or the divine order—[Lv 18:23](https://ref.ly/Lev18:23), [20:12](https://ref.ly/Lev20:12) (H) cf. Di on [18:15](https://ref.ly/Lev18:15).

> \[Str8400] † תְּבַלֻּל **n.\[m.]** confusion, obscurity (on form cf. Ar. conj. v. Inf. Sta§ 267\) [Lv 21:20](https://ref.ly/Lev21:20) (H) בעינו ´ת i.e. defective sight? cf. Di. (‖שֶׁבֶר, גִּבֵּן, דַּק, מְרוֺחַ אָשֶׁח, etc.)

## בָּלַם (id: BDB01265)

## בלס (id: BDB01266)

בלס (cf. Eth. በለስ፡ *fig*, Ar. بَلَسٌ a kind of *fig*, cf. LagM. i. 59 f. 68 f.; hence following).

> \[Str1103] † \[בָּלַס] **vb. denom.** gather figs, tend fig\-trees, sycamores (LXX κνίζων, Vulgate *vellicans,* prob. properly to *nip* the sycamore fruit to fit it for eating, v. TristrNat. Hist. Bib. 399 Boii. cap. 39, p. 383 f.; Theodoret ap. FIHexapl. *ad loc.* Theophrastiv. 2\)—**Qal** *Pt.* בּוֺלֵס, [Am 7:14](https://ref.ly/Amos7:14) שִׁקְמִים ´בּ.

* **Associated Passages:** Amos 7:14

[Jb 7:19](https://ref.ly/Job7:19), [Is 28:4](https://ref.ly/Isa28:4), subj. דָּג [Jon 2:1](https://ref.ly/Jonah2:1); subj. שִׁבֳּלִים [Gn 41:7](https://ref.ly/Gen41:7), [41:24](https://ref.ly/Gen41:24); מַטֶּה [Ex 7:12](https://ref.ly/Exod7:12). **2\.** *swallow up, engulf,* subj. אֶרֶץ [Ex 15:12](https://ref.ly/Exod15:12), [Nu 16:30](https://ref.ly/Num16:30), [16:32](https://ref.ly/Num16:32), [16:34](https://ref.ly/Num16:34), [26:10](https://ref.ly/Num26:10), [Dt 11:6](https://ref.ly/Deut11:6), [Psalm 106:17](https://ref.ly/Ps106:17); fig. of greed [Jb 20:15](https://ref.ly/Job20:15) (obj. חַיִל; opp. קיא *vomit*); of violence, extortion [Pr 1:12](https://ref.ly/Prov1:12) (כִּשְׁאוֺל); of devastation by enemy [Ho 8:7](https://ref.ly/Hos8:7), [Je 51:34](https://ref.ly/Jer51:34), [Psalm 124:3](https://ref.ly/Ps124:3); over\-whelming by calamity [Psalm 69:16](https://ref.ly/Ps69:16) (subj. מצולה); of full enjoyment, profit [Jb 20:18](https://ref.ly/Job20:18) (no obj.) **Niph.** *Pf.* נִבְלַע [Ho 8:8](https://ref.ly/Hos8:8), נִבְלְעוּ [Is 28:7](https://ref.ly/Isa28:7); —*swallowed up*, i.e. devastated [Ho 8:8](https://ref.ly/Hos8:8); engulfed by wine (yet cf. **Pi.** [Is 3:12](https://ref.ly/Isa3:12)) [Is 28:7](https://ref.ly/Isa28:7) (מן־היין ´נ, ‖שָׁגוּ בַשֵּׁכָר). **Pi.** *Pf.* בִּלַּע [Is 25:8](https://ref.ly/Isa25:8) \+ 3 t.; וּבִלַּע consec. [Is 25:7](https://ref.ly/Isa25:7); 3 pl. בִּלֵָּעוּ [Is 3:12](https://ref.ly/Isa3:12); 1 pl. בִּלָּעְנוּ [La 2:16](https://ref.ly/Lam2:16), sf. בִּלַּעֲנוּהוּ [Psalm 35:25](https://ref.ly/Ps35:25); *Impf.* יְבַלַּ־ע [Pr 19:28](https://ref.ly/Prov19:28), sf. 3 ms. יְבַלְּעֶנּוּ [Jb 8:18](https://ref.ly/Job8:18) [Pr 21:20](https://ref.ly/Prov21:20); יְבַלְּעֵם [Psalm 21:10](https://ref.ly/Ps21:10); 3 fs. sf. 3 ms. תְּבַלְּעֶנּוּ [Ec 10:12](https://ref.ly/Eccl10:12); 2 ms. תְּבַלַּע [2 S 20:19](https://ref.ly/2Sam20:19), sf. וַתְּבַלְּעֵנִי [Jb 10:8](https://ref.ly/Job10:8); 1 s. אֲבַלַּע [2 S 20:20](https://ref.ly/2Sam20:20), אֲבַלֵָּעַ [Is 19:3](https://ref.ly/Isa19:3); *Imv.* בַּלַּע [Psalm 55:10](https://ref.ly/Ps55:10); *Inf.* בַּלַּע [Nu 4:20](https://ref.ly/Num4:20), Hb [1:13](https://ref.ly/Hab1:13), בַּלֵָּעַ [La 2:8](https://ref.ly/Lam2:8), sf. בַּלְּעוֺ [Jb 2:3](https://ref.ly/Job2:3); *Pt.* sf. מְבַלְּעָיִח [Is 49:19](https://ref.ly/Isa49:19); —**1\.** *swallow* [Nu 4:20](https://ref.ly/Num4:20) (כְּבַלַּע *as a swallowing* \= for an instant); elsewhere **2\.** *swallow up, engulf,* usually c. acc., **a.** fig. of destruction, ruin, [Is 3:12](https://ref.ly/Isa3:12) (obj. דֶּרֶח); (Ba from a √ II. בלע *confound,* cf. Di; v. also [9:15](https://ref.ly/Isa9:15), [19:3](https://ref.ly/Isa19:3), [28:7](https://ref.ly/Isa28:7), [Psalm 55:10](https://ref.ly/Ps55:10), [107:27](https://ref.ly/Ps107:27)); subj. ´י [La 2:2](https://ref.ly/Lam2:2), [2:5](https://ref.ly/Lam2:5), [2:5](https://ref.ly/Lam2:5), [2:8](https://ref.ly/Lam2:8), [Jb 2:3](https://ref.ly/Job2:3), [10:8](https://ref.ly/Job10:8), [Psalm 21:10](https://ref.ly/Ps21:10) (‖אכל), [Is 19:3](https://ref.ly/Isa19:3) (obj. עֵצָה), i.e. *confuse, confound;* so [Psalm 55:10](https://ref.ly/Ps55:10) בַּלַּע אֲדֹנָי פַּלַּג לְשׁוֺנָם *confuse, Lord, divide their speech* (cf. בלל [Gn 11:7](https://ref.ly/Gen11:7), [11:9](https://ref.ly/Gen11:9) \& v. ## בָּלַק (id: BDB01276)

\[Str1110] † \[בָּלַק] **vb.** waste, lay waste (As. *balâḳu*, Pi. *destroy,* LyonSargon 61; cf. Ar. بَلُّوقَةٌ *a desert*)—**Po.** *Pt.* without מ, sf. בּוֺלְקָהּ [Is 24:1](https://ref.ly/Isa24:1) (‖בּוֺקֵק, subj. ´י, obj. הארץ);—on form (which might also be Qal Pt.) cf. Ol:§ 254 LagGN 1882, 403; also בקק Po. [Je 51:2](https://ref.ly/Jer51:2), and intensive use of Inf. abs. Niph. [Is 24:3](https://ref.ly/Isa24:3); further Pu. (i.e. intens.) Pt. [Na 2:11](https://ref.ly/Nah2:11) (this however perhaps largely influenced by assonance). **Pu.** *Pt.* מְבֻלָּקָה [Na 2:11](https://ref.ly/Nah2:11) (´בּוּקָה וּמְבוּקָה ומב), *devastated,* or as subst. *a devastated city.*

> \[Str1111] בָּלָק **n.pr.m.** (*devastator*) king of Moab, בֶּן צִפּוֺר ´בּ [Nu 22:2](https://ref.ly/Num22:2), [22:4](https://ref.ly/Num22:4), [22:7](https://ref.ly/Num22:7) \+ 37 t. Nu 22 – 24 (all JE) \+ [Jos 24:9](https://ref.ly/Josh24:9) (E) Ju [11:25](https://ref.ly/Judg11:25), [Mi 6:5](https://ref.ly/Mic6:5).

> בֵּלְשַׁאצַּר, בֵּלְאשַׁצַּר v. בֵּל sub בעל.

> \[Str1114] † בִּלְשָׁן **n.pr.m.** (\= *inquirer*? NH \& Aram. בלשׁ; cf. sub בן) an Israelite who returned with Zerubbabel [Ezr 2:2](https://ref.ly/Ezra2:2) \= [Ne 7:7](https://ref.ly/Neh7:7).

> \[בֵּ֫לֶת], בִּלְתִּי v. sub בלה. p. 115

> \[Str1116] † בָּמָה:104 **n.f.** high place (√appar. בום on account of firm —ָ; cf. As. *bâmâtê* ZimBP 48, Moab. במת MI3:27\)—[Je 48:35](https://ref.ly/Jer48:35) \+ 18 t.; הַבָּמָ֫תָה [1 S 9:13](https://ref.ly/1Sam9:13); pl. בָּמוֺת [Nu 21:19](https://ref.ly/Num21:19) \+ 62 t.; cstr. בָּֽמֳתֵי [Jb 9:8](https://ref.ly/Job9:8), [Is 14:14](https://ref.ly/Isa14:14), [Am 4:13](https://ref.ly/Amos4:13); בָּֽמֳו֯תֵי [Dt 32:13](https://ref.ly/Deut32:13), [Is 58:14](https://ref.ly/Isa58:14), [Mi 1:3](https://ref.ly/Mic1:3) (Ew§ 211 d Ges§ 87, 5 archaic fem. cstr. with retracted accent before monosyl. in poetry, *bâmŏ\-thê* not *bŏm*); sf. בָּמֹתַי [2 S 22:34](https://ref.ly/2Sam22:34) \+ 3 t.; בָּמוֺתַי [Psalm 18:34](https://ref.ly/Ps18:34) \+ 10 t.;—**1\.** *high place, mountain:* בָּמוֺת יָעַר *forest mountains* [Mi 3:12](https://ref.ly/Mic3:12) \= [Je 26:18](https://ref.ly/Jer26:18); במות עולם *ancient mountains* Ez [36:2](https://ref.ly/Ezek36:2); במות ארנן [Nu 21:28](https://ref.ly/Num21:28) (E poet.) **2\.** *high places, battle\-fields,* the chief places of the land giving possession, victory, dominion: על במותיך *on thy high places* (Gilboa, the battle\-field) [2 S 1:19](https://ref.ly/2Sam1:19), [1:25](https://ref.ly/2Sam1:25) (in v:[19](https://ref.ly/2Sam1:19) LXX has a doublet מֵתֶיךָ *thy dead,* v. We Dr). **a.** *of Israel:* רכב על במתי ארץ *ride upon the high places of the land* [Dt 32:13](https://ref.ly/Deut32:13) \& [Is 58:14](https://ref.ly/Isa58:14) cf. [Dt 33:29](https://ref.ly/Deut33:29), [Psalm 18:34](https://ref.ly/Ps18:34) \= [2 S 22:34](https://ref.ly/2Sam22:34), Hb [3:19](https://ref.ly/Hab3:19). **b.** *of God:* דרך על במתי ארץ *tread upon the high places of the earth* [Am 4:13](https://ref.ly/Amos4:13) cf. [Mi 1:3](https://ref.ly/Mic1:3); במתי ים [Jb 9:8](https://ref.ly/Job9:8); עלה על במתי עב (aspiration of the king of Babylon) [Is 14:14](https://ref.ly/Isa14:14). **3\.** *high places*, as places of worship, at first on hills and mountains, later on artificial mounds or platforms, under green trees, and in cities; still later for the chapels erected thereon, and once apparently for a portable sanctuary (decked with diverse colours) Ez [16:16](https://ref.ly/Ezek16:16). The ancient worship of Israel was conducted on these high places. In the times of Samuel and David they ascended to them, descended from them, and offered sacrifices on them, [1 S 9:12–25](https://ref.ly/1Sam9:12-1Sam9:25), [10:5](https://ref.ly/1Sam10:5), [10:13](https://ref.ly/1Sam10:13) (הבמה for הביתה We Dr). The custom continued in the reign of Solomon, but Gibeon was הבמה הגדולה [1 K 3:2–4](https://ref.ly/1Kgs3:2-1Kgs3:4) cf. [1 Ch 16:39](https://ref.ly/1Chr16:39), [21:29](https://ref.ly/1Chr21:29), [2 Ch 1:3](https://ref.ly/2Chr1:3), [1:13](https://ref.ly/2Chr1:13). High places of Baal were also used [Nu 22:41](https://ref.ly/Num22:41) (E) [Je 19:5](https://ref.ly/Jer19:5), [32:35](https://ref.ly/Jer32:35); of Moab [Is 15:2](https://ref.ly/Isa15:2), [16:12](https://ref.ly/Isa16:12), [Je 48:35](https://ref.ly/Jer48:35) (cf. MI27); these must be demolished [Nu 33:52](https://ref.ly/Num33:52) (J). Solomon built במות (platforms or chapels) to Chemosh and Milkom on the Mt. of Evil Counsel opposite Jerusalem [1 K 11:7](https://ref.ly/1Kgs11:7): Jeroboam made temples on the ancient high places of Dan and Bethel [1 K 12:31](https://ref.ly/1Kgs12:31), [12:32](https://ref.ly/1Kgs12:32), [2 Ch 11:15](https://ref.ly/2Chr11:15); they are called במות און [Ho 10:8](https://ref.ly/Hos10:8), במות ישחק [Am 7:9](https://ref.ly/Amos7:9): the kings of Israel built במות and בתי הבמות in all their cities [2 K 17:9](https://ref.ly/2Kgs17:9), and the people worshipped there [2 K 17:11](https://ref.ly/2Kgs17:11); these were also used by the mixed population after the exile of Israel [2 K 17:29](https://ref.ly/2Kgs17:29), [17:32](https://ref.ly/2Kgs17:32), [17:32](https://ref.ly/2Kgs17:32): these various idolatrous high places were first destroyed by Josiah [1 K 13:2](https://ref.ly/1Kgs13:2), [13:32](https://ref.ly/1Kgs13:32), [13:38](https://ref.ly/1Kgs13:38), [2 K 23:5–20](https://ref.ly/2Kgs23:5-2Kgs23:20), [2 Ch 34:3](https://ref.ly/2Chr34:3). The worship of Yahweh on high places continued in Judah until the exile [1 K 22:44](https://ref.ly/1Kgs22:44), [2 K 15:35](https://ref.ly/2Kgs15:35); the sanctity code predicts that Yahweh will destroy them [Lv 26:30](https://ref.ly/Lev26:30); they were regarded as the reason for the rejection of Shiloh [Psalm 78:58](https://ref.ly/Ps78:58). The compiler of Kings, writing from the point of view of the Deut. code, complains רַק הַבָּמוֺת לאֹ סָרוּ [2 K 12:4](https://ref.ly/2Kgs12:4), [14:4](https://ref.ly/2Kgs14:4), [15:4](https://ref.ly/2Kgs15:4), [15:35](https://ref.ly/2Kgs15:35) cf. [2 Ch 15:17](https://ref.ly/2Chr15:17), [20:33](https://ref.ly/2Chr20:33), and praises the few pious kings who destroyed them. **a.** Rehoboam built במות with מצבות \& אשׁרים on every high hill and under every green tree [1 K 14:23](https://ref.ly/1Kgs14:23). **b.** Asa did not remove the high places [1 K 15:14](https://ref.ly/1Kgs15:14) ([2 Ch 14:2](https://ref.ly/2Chr14:2), [14:4](https://ref.ly/2Chr14:4) is incorrect unless במות בעל). **c.** Jehoshaphat in his reform on the basis of the covenant code did not remove them [1 K 22:44](https://ref.ly/1Kgs22:44) (עוֺד הֵסִיר אֶת־הַבָּמוֺת [2 Ch 17:16](https://ref.ly/2Chr17:16) is doubtless incorrect, possibly rd. מצבות); Jehoram, his son, made high places in the cities of Judah [2 Ch 21:11](https://ref.ly/2Chr21:11) (LXX Vulgate; not *mountains* HCT); and Ahaz sacrificed on high places on the hills and under every green tree and in every city of Judah [2 K 16:4](https://ref.ly/2Kgs16:4), [2 Ch 28:4](https://ref.ly/2Chr28:4), [28:25](https://ref.ly/2Chr28:25); cf. [Mi 1:5](https://ref.ly/Mic1:5) (rd. חטאת? so LXX SyrVer Targum Che al.; yet cf. JBL1890, 73 f.) **d.** Hezekiah removed them [2 K 18:4](https://ref.ly/2Kgs18:4), [18:22](https://ref.ly/2Kgs18:22), [2 Ch 31:1](https://ref.ly/2Chr31:1), [32:12](https://ref.ly/2Chr32:12), [Is 36:7](https://ref.ly/Isa36:7); but Manasseh rebuilt them [2 K 21:3](https://ref.ly/2Kgs21:3), [2 Ch 33:3](https://ref.ly/2Chr33:3), [33:19](https://ref.ly/2Chr33:19), and the people continued to sacrifice thereon to Yahweh [2 Ch 33:17](https://ref.ly/2Chr33:17). **e.** Josiah, in his reform, based on the Deut. code, defiled them and brake them down from Geba to Beersheba [2 K 23:5](https://ref.ly/2Kgs23:5), [23:8](https://ref.ly/2Kgs23:8), [23:9](https://ref.ly/2Kgs23:9); but subsequently there were במות התפת in the valley of Ben Hinnom [Je 7:31](https://ref.ly/Jer7:31), and במות throughout Judah [Je 17:3](https://ref.ly/Jer17:3) cf. Ez [6:3](https://ref.ly/Ezek6:3), [6:6](https://ref.ly/Ezek6:6), [20:29](https://ref.ly/Ezek20:29) (questioned by Ew \& Co). **4\.** *funereal mound* (?) Ez [43:7](https://ref.ly/Ezek43:7) (Thes, but *in their high places* AV RV; *in their death* Targum Theod Ew Hi RVm), [Is 53:9](https://ref.ly/Isa53:9) (Lowth Ew Bö Rodwell Orelli; but *in his death* AV RV, or *martyr death* De Che Br).

> \[Str1120,1117] † בָּמוֺת **n.pr.loc.** (*high place* or *great high place*) place in Moab [Nu 21:19](https://ref.ly/Num21:19), [21:20](https://ref.ly/Num21:20) \= בָּמוֺת בַּעַל [Nu 22:41](https://ref.ly/Num22:41), [Jos 13:17](https://ref.ly/Josh13:17) possibly on Mt. *‘ Aṭṭarûs* cf. Di.

> \[Str1118] † בִּמְהָל **n.pr.m.** (\= ´בֶּן־מ *son of circumcision*? cf. sub בן) descendant of Asher [1 Ch 7:33](https://ref.ly/1Chr7:33).

> \[Str1119] בְּמוֺ v. sub בּ, p. 91\.

> \[Str1120] בָּמוֺת v. sub במה. above

## בֵּן (id: BDB01286)

\[Str1121] בֵּן:4870 **n.m.** son (MI Ph. בן; so Sab. CISiv. No. 2, cf. בני DHMSem. Sprachforsch. 6; Ar. اِبْنٌ; As. *bin*(*u*), LyonSargon 9, 1\. 57; esp. in *bin\-bin, grandson* COTGloss, cf. Dl infr.; Aram. בַּר, ܒܰܪ, pl. בְּנִין, ܒܢܺܝܢ; cf. Palm., esp. VogNo. 21\. 31\. 36a al.; possibly orig. connected with בנה *build,* so Thes, cf. As. *bânu, begetter* (DlPr 104 \& cf. (29 t. in combination בִּן־נוּן (ישׁוע, הושׁע) יהושׁע); sf. בְּנִי [Gn 21:10](https://ref.ly/Gen21:10) \+; בִּנְךָ [Ex 20:10](https://ref.ly/Exod20:10) \+; לִבֱ\+נךָ [Dt 7:3](https://ref.ly/Deut7:3), [1 K 11:13](https://ref.ly/1Kgs11:13); בְּנֵךְ [Gn 30:14](https://ref.ly/Gen30:14) \+; בְּנוֺ [Gn 4:17](https://ref.ly/Gen4:17) \+; בְּנָהּ [Gn 21:10](https://ref.ly/Gen21:10) \+; pl. בָּנִים [Gn 3:16](https://ref.ly/Gen3:16) \+; cstr. בְּנֵי [Gn 6:2](https://ref.ly/Gen6:2) \+; sf. בָּנַי [Gn 31:43](https://ref.ly/Gen31:43) \+; בָּנֵינוּ [Jos 22:25](https://ref.ly/Josh22:25) \+; בְּנֵיכֶם [Ex 3:22](https://ref.ly/Exod3:22) \+, etc.;—**1\.** *son,* male child, born of a woman [Gn 4:25](https://ref.ly/Gen4:25), [16:11](https://ref.ly/Gen16:11), [16:15](https://ref.ly/Gen16:15), [17:19](https://ref.ly/Gen17:19) cf. v:[16](https://ref.ly/Gen17:16), [18:10](https://ref.ly/Gen18:10), [18:14](https://ref.ly/Gen18:14), [19:37](https://ref.ly/Gen19:37), [19:38](https://ref.ly/Gen19:38) \+ oft., cf. בֶּן־בִּטְנָהּ [Is 49:15](https://ref.ly/Isa49:15); begotten by a man [Gn 5:4](https://ref.ly/Gen5:4) f; [5:28](https://ref.ly/Gen5:28), [6:10](https://ref.ly/Gen6:10), [11:11](https://ref.ly/Gen11:11) f. \+ oft.; ‖בַּת (בָּנוֺת) *daughter* [Gn 5:4](https://ref.ly/Gen5:4), [5:7](https://ref.ly/Gen5:7), [5:10](https://ref.ly/Gen5:10) f., [11:11](https://ref.ly/Gen11:11), [11:13](https://ref.ly/Gen11:13), [11:15](https://ref.ly/Gen11:15) f., [Ex 20:10](https://ref.ly/Exod20:10), [Dt 5:14](https://ref.ly/Deut5:14), [16:11](https://ref.ly/Deut16:11), [16:14](https://ref.ly/Deut16:14), [1 S 30:3](https://ref.ly/1Sam30:3), [30:6](https://ref.ly/1Sam30:6), [Jb 1:2](https://ref.ly/Job1:2), [42:13](https://ref.ly/Job42:13) \+; of son as desired [Gn 30:2](https://ref.ly/Gen30:2) (cf. [15:2](https://ref.ly/Gen15:2), [16:2](https://ref.ly/Gen16:2), [17:17](https://ref.ly/Gen17:17), [18:10](https://ref.ly/Gen18:10) f., [1 S 1:5–11](https://ref.ly/1Sam1:5-1Sam1:11)) [2 K 4:14](https://ref.ly/2Kgs4:14), [4:28](https://ref.ly/2Kgs4:28), [Psalm 127:3](https://ref.ly/Ps127:3) \+; rejoiced in [Gn 30:6](https://ref.ly/Gen30:6) \+; beloved [Ex 21:5](https://ref.ly/Exod21:5), [2 S 19:1](https://ref.ly/2Sam19:1), [19:3](https://ref.ly/2Sam19:3), [19:5](https://ref.ly/2Sam19:5), [1 K 3:26](https://ref.ly/1Kgs3:26); cared for [Dt 1:31](https://ref.ly/Deut1:31); spared [Mal 3:17](https://ref.ly/Mal3:17); disciplined \& trained [Dt 8:5](https://ref.ly/Deut8:5), [Pr 3:12](https://ref.ly/Prov3:12), [13:24](https://ref.ly/Prov13:24), [19:18](https://ref.ly/Prov19:18), [29:17](https://ref.ly/Prov29:17); owing reverence, obedience, etc. to parents [Pr 6:20](https://ref.ly/Prov6:20), [10:1](https://ref.ly/Prov10:1), [13:1](https://ref.ly/Prov13:1); בִּנְךָ בְּכוֺרְךָ *thy first\-born son* [Gn 27:32](https://ref.ly/Gen27:32); הַבֵּן הַבְּכֹר [Dt 21:15](https://ref.ly/Deut21:15) cf. [1 S 8:2](https://ref.ly/1Sam8:2); בְּנָהּ הַגָּדֹל *her elder son* [Gn 27:15](https://ref.ly/Gen27:15), [27:42](https://ref.ly/Gen27:42); בְּנוֺ הַגָּדֹל [27:1](https://ref.ly/Gen27:1); בְּנָהּ הַקָּטָן *her younger son* [Gn 27:15](https://ref.ly/Gen27:15), [27:42](https://ref.ly/Gen27:42). In partic. **a.** בֶּן־אִמּוֺ *son of his mother,* i.e. own (uterine) brother [Gn 43:29](https://ref.ly/Gen43:29), cf. [27:29](https://ref.ly/Gen27:29), Ju [8:19](https://ref.ly/Judg8:19), [Psalm 50:20](https://ref.ly/Ps50:20), [69:9](https://ref.ly/Ps69:9), \& v. אֵם; בְּנֵי אָבִיךָ *sons of thy father* \= brethren [Gn 49:8](https://ref.ly/Gen49:8) (poet.) †**b.** בְּנֵי דֹדֵיהֶן \= *cousins* [Nu 36:11](https://ref.ly/Num36:11). **c.** בְּנִי *my son,* as term of kindliness or endearment, used by Eli to Samuel [1 S 3:6](https://ref.ly/1Sam3:6), [3:16](https://ref.ly/1Sam3:16) cf. [4:16](https://ref.ly/1Sam4:16), [24:17](https://ref.ly/1Sam24:17), [26:17](https://ref.ly/1Sam26:17), [26:21](https://ref.ly/1Sam26:21), [26:25](https://ref.ly/1Sam26:25), v. also [Pr 1:8](https://ref.ly/Prov1:8), [1:10](https://ref.ly/Prov1:10), [2:1](https://ref.ly/Prov2:1) \+; cf. בִּנְךָ, used by Benhadad of himself to Elisha [2 K 8:9](https://ref.ly/2Kgs8:9); by Ahaz to Tiglath\-pileser [16:7](https://ref.ly/2Kgs16:7); esp. to express intimate and gracious relation with God: ´י calls Israel בְּנִי בְכֹרִי [Ex 4:22](https://ref.ly/Exod4:22) cf. v:[23](https://ref.ly/Exod4:23), [Ho 11:1](https://ref.ly/Hos11:1), v. also [Psalm 80:16](https://ref.ly/Ps80:16) (but cf. Che); בָּנִם אַתֶּם ליהוה אלהיכם [Dt 14:1](https://ref.ly/Deut14:1); בְּנֵי עֶלְיוֺן [Psalm 82:6](https://ref.ly/Ps82:6) (‖אלהים); בְּנֵי אֵל־חָי [Ho 2:1](https://ref.ly/Hos2:1); cf. further [Dt 32:5](https://ref.ly/Deut32:5) (pl.) v:[20](https://ref.ly/Deut32:20) (pl.) [Is 1:2](https://ref.ly/Isa1:2), [1:4](https://ref.ly/Isa1:4), [30:1](https://ref.ly/Isa30:1), [30:9](https://ref.ly/Isa30:9), [Je 3:14](https://ref.ly/Jer3:14), [3:22](https://ref.ly/Jer3:22), [4:22](https://ref.ly/Jer4:22), [31:20](https://ref.ly/Jer31:20); of future Davidic king [2 S 7:14](https://ref.ly/2Sam7:14) \= [1 Ch 17:13](https://ref.ly/1Chr17:13) cf. [Psalm 2:7](https://ref.ly/Ps2:7); expressly referred to Solomon [1 Ch 22:10](https://ref.ly/1Chr22:10), [28:6](https://ref.ly/1Chr28:6); also of children (offered in fire) Ez [16:21](https://ref.ly/Ezek16:21). **d.** בְּנֵי האלהים applied to supernatural beings [Gn 6:2](https://ref.ly/Gen6:2), [6:4](https://ref.ly/Gen6:4), [Jb 1:6](https://ref.ly/Job1:6), [2:1](https://ref.ly/Job2:1); בְּנֵי אלהים [Jb 38:7](https://ref.ly/Job38:7); בְּנֵי אֵלִים [Psalm 29:1](https://ref.ly/Ps29:1) (on which cf. Che's note) [89:7](https://ref.ly/Ps89:7). **e.** בֶּן־אָדָם *son of man*, cf. ´בְּנֵי א, v. אָדָם; † בְּנֵי אִישׁ [Psalm 4:3](https://ref.ly/Ps4:3) \& (‖בני אדם) [49:3](https://ref.ly/Ps49:3), [62:10](https://ref.ly/Ps62:10). † **f.** בֶּן־בִּנְךָ \= *thy grandson* [Ex 10:2](https://ref.ly/Exod10:2), [Dt 6:2](https://ref.ly/Deut6:2), Ju [8:22](https://ref.ly/Judg8:22) cf., [Je 27:7](https://ref.ly/Jer27:7); pl. [Ex 34:7](https://ref.ly/Exod34:7), [Dt 4:9](https://ref.ly/Deut4:9), [4:25](https://ref.ly/Deut4:25), Ju [12:14](https://ref.ly/Judg12:14), [2 K 17:41](https://ref.ly/2Kgs17:41), [2 Ch 8:40](https://ref.ly/2Chr8:40), [Jb 42:16](https://ref.ly/Job42:16), [Psalm 128:6](https://ref.ly/Ps128:6), [Pr 13:22](https://ref.ly/Prov13:22), [17:6](https://ref.ly/Prov17:6), Ez [37:25](https://ref.ly/Ezek37:25); also בֵּן alone with similar reference [Gn 29:5](https://ref.ly/Gen29:5) (Laban son of Nahor); Laban calls his daughters' children his own sons [Gn 31:28](https://ref.ly/Gen31:28), [31:43](https://ref.ly/Gen31:43) cf. [32:1](https://ref.ly/Gen32:1); so of Naomi [Ru 4:17](https://ref.ly/Ruth4:17); בְּנֵי רְבִעִים [2 K 10:30](https://ref.ly/2Kgs10:30) *sons of the fourth generation,* and, in general, descendants [Jos 22:24](https://ref.ly/Josh22:24), [22:25](https://ref.ly/Josh22:25), [22:27](https://ref.ly/Josh22:27) \+; v. also sub **i.** infr. **g.** constantly, as more precise designation, added to personal name כָּלֵב בֶּן־יְפֻנֶּה [Nu 14:30](https://ref.ly/Num14:30), [32:12](https://ref.ly/Num32:12), [34:19](https://ref.ly/Num34:19) \+; יְהוֺשֻׁעַ בִּן־נוּן [Nu 11:28](https://ref.ly/Num11:28), [14:30](https://ref.ly/Num14:30), [32:12](https://ref.ly/Num32:12), [32:28](https://ref.ly/Num32:28), [34:17](https://ref.ly/Num34:17) \+; יָרָבְעָם בֶּן־נְבָט [1 K 12:2](https://ref.ly/1Kgs12:2), [12:15](https://ref.ly/1Kgs12:15) \+, etc.; also without personal name (often with implication of contempt) בֶּן־קִישׁ [1 S 10:11](https://ref.ly/1Sam10:11); בֶּן־יִשַׁי [1 S 20:27](https://ref.ly/1Sam20:27), [20:30](https://ref.ly/1Sam20:30), [20:31](https://ref.ly/1Sam20:31), [22:7](https://ref.ly/1Sam22:7), [22:8](https://ref.ly/1Sam22:8), [22:9](https://ref.ly/1Sam22:9), [22:13](https://ref.ly/1Sam22:13), [25:10](https://ref.ly/1Sam25:10), [2 S 20:1](https://ref.ly/2Sam20:1); בְּנֵי צְרוּיָה [2 S 16:10](https://ref.ly/2Sam16:10); בֶּן־רְמַלְיָהוּ [Is 7:4](https://ref.ly/Isa7:4), [7:5](https://ref.ly/Isa7:5), [7:9](https://ref.ly/Isa7:9), [8:16](https://ref.ly/Isa8:16); בֶּן־טָֽבְאַָל [Is 7:6](https://ref.ly/Isa7:6); cf. also בְּנֵי לֵוִי [Nu 16:7](https://ref.ly/Num16:7), [16:8](https://ref.ly/Num16:8). **h.** designated as בֶּן־זְקֻנִים i.e. born in old age of father [Gn 37:3](https://ref.ly/Gen37:3); opp. בְּנֵי הַנְּעוּרִים *sons of one's youth* [Psalm 127:4](https://ref.ly/Ps127:4); also בֶּן־בֵּיתִי *one born in my house* [Gn 15:3](https://ref.ly/Gen15:3) (i.e. slave) so בְּנֵי בַיִת [Ec 2:7](https://ref.ly/Eccl2:7). **i.** in various combinations: (α) as expression of contumely, בֶּ־ן נַעֲוַת הַמַּרְדּוּת [1 S 20:30](https://ref.ly/1Sam20:30); בֶּן־הַמְּרַצֵּחַ הַזֶּה [2 K 6:32](https://ref.ly/2Kgs6:32) *this son of a murderer;* cf. בְּנֵי־נָבָל [Jb 30:8](https://ref.ly/Job30:8); בְּנֵי בְלִי־שֵׁם *ib.;* בְּנֵי עֹנֲַנָה [Is 57:3](https://ref.ly/Isa57:3) (‖זֶרַע מְנָאֵף); cf. בֶּן־אִשָּׁה אַחֶרֶת Ju [11:2](https://ref.ly/Judg11:2) (cf. v:[1](https://ref.ly/Judg11:1)); (β) as term of respect, dignity, בֶּן־חוֺרִים *son of nobles* [Ec 10:17](https://ref.ly/Eccl10:17) (in Aram. \= *free born*); בֶּן־חֲכָמִים [Is 19:11](https://ref.ly/Isa19:11); בֶּן־מַלְכֵי־קֶדֶם *ib.;* cf. בֶּן־מֶלֶךְ [Psalm 72:1](https://ref.ly/Ps72:1) (‖מֶלֶךְ); בֶּן־אֲמָתֶָךָ [Psalm 86:16](https://ref.ly/Ps86:16) in addressing ´י (‖עַבְדֶָּךָ) \& בְּנֵי־עֲבָדֶיךָ [Psalm 102:29](https://ref.ly/Ps102:29); of noble appearance בְּנֵי הַמֶּלֶךְ Ju [8:18](https://ref.ly/Judg8:18). **j.** oft. pl. with name of ancestor, people, land, or city, to denote descendants, inhabitants, membership in a nation or family, etc.: (α) e.g. בְּנֵי־עֵבֶר [Gn 10:21](https://ref.ly/Gen10:21); As. *mâr šipri (šiprâtum), son of a messenger \= messenger,* and explains from the son's succeeding to father's calling) \& בֶּן־נָבִיא [Am 7:14](https://ref.ly/Amos7:14); prob. also † בְּנֵי הַכֹּהֲנִים [1 Ch 9:30](https://ref.ly/1Chr9:30), [Ezr 2:61](https://ref.ly/Ezra2:61), [10:18](https://ref.ly/Ezra10:18); † בְּנֵי הַשֹּׁעֲרִים [Ezr 2:42](https://ref.ly/Ezra2:42); cf. בְּנֵי הַגְּדוּד [2 Ch 25:13](https://ref.ly/2Chr25:13) *men of the troop,* v. RV Di al.; † (κ) כְּנֵי הַיִּצְהָר [Zc 4:14](https://ref.ly/Zech4:14) i.e. anointed ones; † (λ) בֶּן־מֶשֶׁק [Gn 15:2](https://ref.ly/Gen15:2) *son of possession,* i.e. heir; † (μ) הֵילֵל בֶּן־שָׁחַר [Is 14:12](https://ref.ly/Isa14:12) *son of dawn;* † (ν) of animals בְּנֵיִ שָׁהַץ i.e. proud beasts [Jb 28:8](https://ref.ly/Job28:8), [41:26](https://ref.ly/Job41:26); (ξ) of Jonah's gourd בִּן־לַיְלָה [Jon 4:10](https://ref.ly/Jonah4:10), [4:10](https://ref.ly/Jonah4:10); † (ο) of a fertile hill קֶרֶן בֶּן־שֶׁמֶן [Is 5:1](https://ref.ly/Isa5:1). **9\.** n. relat. of age: **a.** of men, וַיְהִי נֹחַ בֶּן־הֲמֵשׁ מֵאוֺת שָׁנָה [Gn 5:32](https://ref.ly/Gen5:32) cf.  Note.—בן appears perh. abbrev. as בּ in a few cpd. n.pr.; v. בִּדְקַר (\= בֶּן־דקר?), בִּלְשָׁן, בִּמְהָל, בַּעֲלִיס, בַּעֲנָה, בִּרְשַׁע, בִּשְׁלָם (so MV after Schol. Hamâsa3 ed. Freytag; Röde libr, hist, interpr, Arab, 20, 21; but this is very uncertain, cf. O1:§ 227 b, p. 613\).—On Lag.'s explan. of אבי in some n.pr. as for אבן \= בן cf. LagBN 75 \& v. אבינר, etc., but this is dub. 

> \[Str1122] † בֵּן **n.pr.m.** a Levite [1 Ch 15:18](https://ref.ly/1Chr15:18), but del. LXX Be Öt cf. v:[20](https://ref.ly/1Chr15:20).

> \[Str1125] † בֶּן־אֲבִינָדָב **n.pr.m.** (?) (*son of Abinadab*) an officer of Solomon [1 K 4:11](https://ref.ly/1Kgs4:11); but cf. אבינדב p. 4\.

> \[Str1126,206] † בֶּן־אוִֺ\+ני **n.pr.m.** (*son of my sorrow*) Rachel's name for Benjamin (cf. infr.) [Gn 35:18](https://ref.ly/Gen35:18).

> \[Str1127] † בֶּן־גֶּ֫בֶר **n.pr.m.** (*son of a man*, or *of Geber*) an officer of Solomon [1 K 4:13](https://ref.ly/1Kgs4:13).

> \[Str1128] † בֶּן־דֶּ֫קֶר **n.pr.m.** (?) (*son of Deker*) an officer of Solomon [1 K 4:9](https://ref.ly/1Kgs4:9).

> \[Str1130] † בֶּן־הֲדַד **n.pr.m.** (appar. *son of (god) Hadad* \= Aram. ܒܰܪ ܗܰܕܰܕ, SyrVer ܒܰܪ ܗܳܕܳܕ PS cf. [1 K 20:34](https://ref.ly/1Kgs20:34)) As. *Dad\-idri* COT (*Bir\-idri* Dl1. c.) [1 K 20:1](https://ref.ly/1Kgs20:1), [20:3](https://ref.ly/1Kgs20:3), [20:5](https://ref.ly/1Kgs20:5), [20:9](https://ref.ly/1Kgs20:9), [20:10](https://ref.ly/1Kgs20:10), [20:16](https://ref.ly/1Kgs20:16), [20:17](https://ref.ly/1Kgs20:17), [20:20](https://ref.ly/1Kgs20:20), [20:26](https://ref.ly/1Kgs20:26), [20:30](https://ref.ly/1Kgs20:30), [20:32](https://ref.ly/1Kgs20:32), [20:33](https://ref.ly/1Kgs20:33), [20:33](https://ref.ly/1Kgs20:33), [2 K 6:24](https://ref.ly/2Kgs6:24), [8:7](https://ref.ly/2Kgs8:7), [8:9](https://ref.ly/2Kgs8:9). **3\.** son of Hazael [2 K 13:3](https://ref.ly/2Kgs13:3), [13:24](https://ref.ly/2Kgs13:24), [13:25](https://ref.ly/2Kgs13:25) cf. > \[Str1134] † בֶּן־חַ֫יִל **n.pr.m.** (*son (man) of might*) a prince of Jehoshaphat [2 Ch 17:7](https://ref.ly/2Chr17:7).

> \[Str1135] † בֶּן־חָנָן **n.pr.m.** one of tribe of Judah [1 Ch 4:20](https://ref.ly/1Chr4:20).

> \[Str1142] † בְּנֵי יַעֲָקָן **n.pr.loc.** a station of Israel in wilderness [Nu 33:31](https://ref.ly/Num33:31), [33:32](https://ref.ly/Num33:32) (\= בְּאֵרוֺת בני יעקן [Dt 10:6](https://ref.ly/Deut10:6) q.v., p. 92; cf. also יַעֲקָן [1 Ch 1:42](https://ref.ly/1Chr1:42) \= וַעֲקָן [Gn 36:27](https://ref.ly/Gen36:27), \& Di [Nu 33:31](https://ref.ly/Num33:31)).

Ch \+ 6 t. [Ne 11:25–31](https://ref.ly/Neh11:25-Neh11:31). On [1 Ch 18:1](https://ref.ly/1Chr18:1) \& its variation from ‖ [2 S 8:1](https://ref.ly/2Sam8:1) vid. We Dr. **5\.** in phrases denoting character, quality, etc., בַּת־אֵל נֵכָר *daughter of a strange god,* i.e. idolatrous (woman or people) [Mal 2:11](https://ref.ly/Mal2:11); בַּת־גְּדוּר *daughter of a troop,* i.e. war\-like city [Mi 4:14](https://ref.ly/Mic4:14); בְּנוֺת הַשִּׁיר [Ec 12:4](https://ref.ly/Eccl12:4) *the daughters of song*, i.e. songs, melodious notes; בַּת־בְּלִיַּעַל [1 S 1:16](https://ref.ly/1Sam1:16) v. בְּלִיַּעַל sub בלה. † **6\.** בַּת יַעֲנָה \= *ostrich* [Lv 11:16](https://ref.ly/Lev11:16) \= [Dt 14:15](https://ref.ly/Deut14:15); pl. בְּנוֺת יַעֲנָה [Jb 30:29](https://ref.ly/Job30:29), [Mi 1:8](https://ref.ly/Mic1:8), [Is 13:21](https://ref.ly/Isa13:21), [34:13](https://ref.ly/Isa34:13), [43:20](https://ref.ly/Isa43:20), [Je 50:39](https://ref.ly/Jer50:39) (v. יַעֲנָה); בַּת־עַיִן *pupil of the eye* [La 2:18](https://ref.ly/Lam2:18) cf. Eth. ብንተ ዐይን፡ v. also אישׁוֺן † **7\.** fig. לַעֲלוּקָה שְׁתֵּי בָנוֺת [Pr 30:15](https://ref.ly/Prov30:15) *two daughters* (i.e. Shₑ’ôl \& the barren womb, cf. Comm.) † **8\.** of vine \= *branch* בָּנוֺת צָֽעֲרָה עֲלֵי־שׁוּר [Gn 49:22](https://ref.ly/Gen49:22) cf. Di \& v: sub בֵּן. † **9\.** as n. relat. (all P), of age of woman בַּת־תִּשְׁעִים שָׁנָה [Gn 17:17](https://ref.ly/Gen17:17); of ewe\-lamb בַּת־שְׁנָתָהּ [Lv 14:10](https://ref.ly/Lev14:10), [Nu 6:14](https://ref.ly/Num6:14); of she\-goat *id.* [Nu 15:27](https://ref.ly/Num15:27). Cf. בֵּן **9\.**—II. בַּת v. sub בתת p. 144\.

> \[Str1337] † בַּת־רַבִּים **n.pr.loc.** (*daughter of multitudes*) appellation of populous city of Heshbon, or of its gate, Ct [7:5](https://ref.ly/Song7:5) (‖חֶשְׁבּוֺן).

> \[Str1339] † בַּת־שֶׁ֫בַע **n.pr.f.** (*daughter of oath ?* cf. אֱלִישֶׁ֫בַע) wife of Uriah [2 S 11:3](https://ref.ly/2Sam11:3), [Psalm 51:2](https://ref.ly/Ps51:2); afterwards of David, \& mother Solomon [12:24](https://ref.ly/2Sam12:24), [1 K 1:11](https://ref.ly/1Kgs1:11), [1:15](https://ref.ly/1Kgs1:15), [1:16](https://ref.ly/1Kgs1:16), [1:31](https://ref.ly/1Kgs1:31), [2:13](https://ref.ly/1Kgs2:13), [2:18](https://ref.ly/1Kgs2:18), [2:19](https://ref.ly/1Kgs2:19); בַּת־שָׁבֵע [1 K 1:28](https://ref.ly/1Kgs1:28); cf. also foll.

> \[Str1340] † בַּת־שׁוּעַ **n.pr.f.** (?) (*daughter of opulence*?)—**1\.** wife of David, mother of Solomon, etc. [1 Ch 3:5](https://ref.ly/1Chr3:5) \= בַּת־שֶׁ֫בַּע q.v.; We Klo בַּת־שֶׁ֫רַע cf. Be; but prob. text. error. v. Dr on [2 S 11:3](https://ref.ly/2Sam11:3). **2\.** wife of Judah [1 Ch 2:3](https://ref.ly/1Chr2:3) הַכְּנַעֲנִית ´־שׁ´ב, RV *Bathshua* but in ‖ [Gn 38:2](https://ref.ly/Gen38:2), [38:12](https://ref.ly/Gen38:12) not a n.pr. (cf. v:[2](https://ref.ly/Gen38:2)).

> \[Str1332] † בִּתְיָה **n.pr.f.** (\= בַּת יָהּ i.e. *worshipper of Yah*? cf. Ph. n.pr.f. בתּבעל) [1 Ch 4:18](https://ref.ly/1Chr4:18) wife of Mered of Judah, called בַּת־פַּרְעֹה.

[Je 12:16](https://ref.ly/Jer12:16), Ez [36:33](https://ref.ly/Ezek36:33); *Impf.* יִבָּנֶה [Jb 12:14](https://ref.ly/Job12:14) \+ 4 t.; 3 fs. תִּבָּנֶה [Nu 21:27](https://ref.ly/Num21:27) \+ 3 t.; 2 ms. תִּבָּנֶה [Jb 22:33](https://ref.ly/Job22:33), אִבָּנֶה [Gn 16:2](https://ref.ly/Gen16:2), וְאִבָּנֶה [Gn 30:3](https://ref.ly/Gen30:3); 3 fpl. תִּבָּנֶינָה Ez [36:10](https://ref.ly/Ezek36:10); 2 fpl. תִּבָּנֶינָה [Is 44:26](https://ref.ly/Isa44:26); *Inf.* לְהִבָּנוֺת [Hg 1:2](https://ref.ly/Hag1:2) [Zc 8:9](https://ref.ly/Zech8:9), בְּהִבָּנֹתוֺ [1 K 6:7](https://ref.ly/1Kgs6:7), [6:7](https://ref.ly/1Kgs6:7); *Pt.* נִבְנֶה [1 Ch 22:19](https://ref.ly/1Chr22:19); —**1\. a.** *be built,* (lit.) of city [Nu 13:22](https://ref.ly/Num13:22); of temple [1 K 3:2](https://ref.ly/1Kgs3:2) (´לְשֵׁם י) [1 K 6:7](https://ref.ly/1Kgs6:7), [6:7](https://ref.ly/1Kgs6:7), [6:7](https://ref.ly/1Kgs6:7); (c. acc. mater.); Pt. \= *to be built* (gerundive) [1 Ch 22:19](https://ref.ly/1Chr22:19). **b.** *be rebuilt:* of city of Sihon [Nu 21:27](https://ref.ly/Num21:27) cf. [Is 25:2](https://ref.ly/Isa25:2), [44:26](https://ref.ly/Isa44:26), of wall of Jerusalem [Ne 7:1](https://ref.ly/Neh7:1), of Jerusalem [Je 30:18](https://ref.ly/Jer30:18), [31:4](https://ref.ly/Jer31:4), [31:38](https://ref.ly/Jer31:38), [Is 44:28](https://ref.ly/Isa44:28) cf. [Dn 9:25](https://ref.ly/Dan9:25) תָּשׁוּב וְנִבְנְתָה, \& Ez [26:14](https://ref.ly/Ezek26:14) (c. עוֺד), of ruinous places (הֶחֳרָבוֺת) Ez [36:10](https://ref.ly/Ezek36:10), [36:33](https://ref.ly/Ezek36:33), of a devoted city [Dt 13:17](https://ref.ly/Deut13:17) (c. עוֺד), of temple [Hg 1:2](https://ref.ly/Hag1:2), [Zc 1:16](https://ref.ly/Zech1:16), [8:9](https://ref.ly/Zech8:9), indef. subj. [Jb 12:14](https://ref.ly/Job12:14). **2\. a.** (fig.) of restored exiles, \= *established* [Je 12:16](https://ref.ly/Jer12:16), of prosperous wicked [Mal 3:15](https://ref.ly/Mal3:15), of repentant offender [Jb 22:23](https://ref.ly/Job22:23). **b.** *established,* made permanent, subj. חֶסֶד [Psalm 89:3](https://ref.ly/Ps89:3) (c. עוֺלָם), subj. בַּיִת [Pr 24:3](https://ref.ly/Prov24:3) (by wisdom). **c.** of childless wife, by means of concubine אִבָּנֶה מִמֶּגָּה [Gn 16:2](https://ref.ly/Gen16:2), [30:3](https://ref.ly/Gen30:3) *I shall be built up,* i.e. become the mother of a family, *from* or *through her;* cf. **Qal 2 a.**

> \[Str1138] בּוּנִּי v. בֻּנִּי infr. below

> \[Str1131] † בִּנּוּי**n.pr.m. 1\.** head of a family that returned with Zerubbabel [Ne 7:15](https://ref.ly/Neh7:15) (\= בָּנִי ‖ [Ezr 2:10](https://ref.ly/Ezra2:10) \& perh. [Ezr 10:34](https://ref.ly/Ezra10:34) cf. SmListen 14; but BeRy prop. here בִּגְוַי). **2\.** a Levite of Ezra's time [Ezr 8:33](https://ref.ly/Ezra8:33), [Ne 10:10](https://ref.ly/Neh10:10) (prop. \= בָּנִי [8:7](https://ref.ly/Neh8:7), בֻּנִּי [9:4](https://ref.ly/Neh9:4)) [12:8](https://ref.ly/Neh12:8) cf. [3:24](https://ref.ly/Neh3:24) (בַּוַי, text. err. v:[18](https://ref.ly/Neh3:18)) v. BeRy; also [Ezr 2:40](https://ref.ly/Ezra2:40) \= [Ne 7:43](https://ref.ly/Neh7:43) לִבְּנֵי rd. perh. לְבִנֻּי cf. SmListen 16 but BeRy otherwise; cf. further GrGesch.ii.2, 389\. **3\.** Israelites of Ezra's time, **a.** [Ezr 10:30](https://ref.ly/Ezra10:30); **b.** [Ezr 10:38](https://ref.ly/Ezra10:38).

> \[Str1137] † בָּנִי **n.pr.m. 1\.** one of David's heroes [2 S 23:36](https://ref.ly/2Sam23:36) בָּנִי הַגָּדִי (‖ [1 Ch 11:38](https://ref.ly/1Chr11:38) has בֶּן־הַגְרִי, but v. DrSm). **2\.** Levites, **a.** [1 Ch 6:31](https://ref.ly/1Chr6:31); **b.** [Ne 3:17](https://ref.ly/Neh3:17) cf. [8:7](https://ref.ly/Neh8:7) (\= בִּנּוּי [10:10](https://ref.ly/Neh10:10)) [9:4](https://ref.ly/Neh9:4), [9:4](https://ref.ly/Neh9:4) (repeated prob. by error, cf. BeRy) v:[5](https://ref.ly/Neh9:5), [10:14](https://ref.ly/Neh10:14), [11:22](https://ref.ly/Neh11:22). On [Ezr 2:40](https://ref.ly/Ezra2:40) \= [Ne 7:43](https://ref.ly/Neh7:43) cf. GrGesch. ii. 2, 389; he reads קדמיאל בני for קדמיאל לבני, taking בני as n.pr., as 3 [Ezr 5:26](https://ref.ly/Ezra5:26) Καδμιηλου καὶ Βαννου. **3\.** a man of Judah [1 Ch 9:4](https://ref.ly/1Chr9:4) (מִן) בָּנִי Qr ( \> Kt בנימן). **4\.** [Ezr 2:10](https://ref.ly/Ezra2:10) rd. בִּנּוּי cf. ‖ [Ne 7:15](https://ref.ly/Neh7:15). **5\.** heads of families of Isr. **a.** [Ezr 10:29](https://ref.ly/Ezra10:29); **b.** [Ne 10:15](https://ref.ly/Neh10:15); **c.** [Ezr 10:34](https://ref.ly/Ezra10:34) but perh. rd. בִּנּוּי or בִּגְּוַי cf. supr. **6\.** an Israelite [Ezr 10:38](https://ref.ly/Ezra10:38).—(בני as n.pr. Palm VogNo. 94\.)

> \[Str1138] † בֻּנִּי **n.pr.m. 1\.** Levites, **a.** [Ne 9:4](https://ref.ly/Neh9:4), but rd. perh. בִּנּוּי cf. BeRy \& vid. [10:10](https://ref.ly/Neh10:10), [12:8](https://ref.ly/Neh12:8), [Ezr 8:33](https://ref.ly/Ezra8:33); **b.** בּוּנִּי (בֶּן) [Ne 11:15](https://ref.ly/Neh11:15), but rd. perh. מִן בְּנֵי etc. cf. BeRy. **2\.** a chief of people [Ne 10:16](https://ref.ly/Neh10:16), perh. repetit. of בָּנִי v:[15](https://ref.ly/Neh10:15) cf. BeRy.

> \[Str1140] † בִּנְיָה **n.f.** structure, building Ez [41:13](https://ref.ly/Ezek41:13), cf. also בִּנְיָן.

> \[Str1141] † בְּנָיָ֫הוּ, בְּנָיָה **n.pr.m.** (*Yah hath built up,* cf. יבנאל, Sab. בנאל DHMZMG 1883, 15\)—**1\.** one of David's captains and heroes, son of Jehoiada, בניהו [2 S 8:18](https://ref.ly/2Sam8:18), [23:20](https://ref.ly/2Sam23:20), [23:22](https://ref.ly/2Sam23:22), [1 K 1:8](https://ref.ly/1Kgs1:8), [1:10](https://ref.ly/1Kgs1:10), [1:26](https://ref.ly/1Kgs1:26), [1:32](https://ref.ly/1Kgs1:32), [1:36](https://ref.ly/1Kgs1:36), [1:38](https://ref.ly/1Kgs1:38), [1:44](https://ref.ly/1Kgs1:44), [2:25](https://ref.ly/1Kgs2:25), [2:29](https://ref.ly/1Kgs2:29), [2:30](https://ref.ly/1Kgs2:30), [2:30](https://ref.ly/1Kgs2:30), [2:34](https://ref.ly/1Kgs2:34), [2:35](https://ref.ly/1Kgs2:35), [2:46](https://ref.ly/1Kgs2:46), [4:4](https://ref.ly/1Kgs4:4), [1 Ch 11:24](https://ref.ly/1Chr11:24), [18:17](https://ref.ly/1Chr18:17), [27:5](https://ref.ly/1Chr27:5), [27:6](https://ref.ly/1Chr27:6) cf. v:[34](https://ref.ly/1Chr27:34) (v. Be); \= בניה [2 S 20:23](https://ref.ly/2Sam20:23), [1 Ch 11:22](https://ref.ly/1Chr11:22). **2\.** one of David's thirty, בניהו [2 S 23:30](https://ref.ly/2Sam23:30) \= בניה [1 Ch 11:31](https://ref.ly/1Chr11:31), [27:14](https://ref.ly/1Chr27:14). **3\.** a Simeonite, בניה [1 Ch 4:36](https://ref.ly/1Chr4:36). **4\.** Levites, **a.** בניהו [1 Ch 15:18](https://ref.ly/1Chr15:18), [15:20](https://ref.ly/1Chr15:20), [15:24](https://ref.ly/1Chr15:24); **b.** (*id.*) [2 Ch 31:13](https://ref.ly/2Chr31:13); **c.** בניה [2 Ch 20:14](https://ref.ly/2Chr20:14). **5\.** Israelites, בניה **a.** [Ezr 10:25](https://ref.ly/Ezra10:25); **b.** v:[30](https://ref.ly/Ezra10:30); **c.** v:[35](https://ref.ly/Ezra10:35); **d.** v:[43](https://ref.ly/Ezra10:43); **e.** Ez [11:13](https://ref.ly/Ezek11:13) \= בניהו v:[1](https://ref.ly/Ezek11:1).

> \[Str1146] † בִּנְיָן **n.m.** structure (loan\-word \= ܒܢܝܢ acc. to LagBN 205\) applied **a.** to enclosing wall of Ezekiel's temple Ez [40:5](https://ref.ly/Ezek40:5); **b.** to rear\-building of same [41:12](https://ref.ly/Ezek41:12), [41:12](https://ref.ly/Ezek41:12), [41:15](https://ref.ly/Ezek41:15), but rd. prob. בִּנְיָה q.v.; so Sm Co; **c.** appar. to whole temple [42:1](https://ref.ly/Ezek42:1), [42:10](https://ref.ly/Ezek42:10) (Co rds. הבית); cf. v:[5](https://ref.ly/Ezek42:5) (del. Co);—Sm refers v:[1](https://ref.ly/Ezek42:1) to enclosing wall, vid. **a.**

[2 Ch 26:6](https://ref.ly/2Chr26:6); \= Gk. *Iamnia,* mod. YebnaBdPal 161\. **2\.** town in Naphtali [Jos 19:33](https://ref.ly/Josh19:33).

> \[Str2996] † יַבְנֶה **n.pr.loc.** (*he causeth to build*) a Philistine city [2 Ch 26:6](https://ref.ly/2Chr26:6) LXXL Ἰαβνη (Β̓Αβεννηρ) cf. Ἰεμναθ LXXL [Jos 15:46](https://ref.ly/Josh15:46) (A Ἰεμναι); \= יַבְנְאֵל **1\.** q.v.

> \[Str2997] † I. יִבְנְיָה **n.pr.m.** (*Yah buildeth up,* cf. בניהו) a Benjamite [1 Ch 9:8a](https://ref.ly/1Chr9:8) LXX Βανααμ, LXXL Ἰεβναα.
> 
>  \[Str2998] † II. יִבְנְיָה **n.pr.m.** (*id.;* al. יִבְנִיָּה but v. Baer's n.) a Benjamite [1 Ch 9:8b](https://ref.ly/1Chr9:8) LXX Βαναια, but LXXL Ἰεχονιου.

> \[Str4011] † \[מִבְנֶה] **n.m.** structure, cstr. כְּמִבְנֵה־עִיר Ez [40:2](https://ref.ly/Ezek40:2) *like the structure of city*.

> \[Str4012] † מְבֻנַּי **n.pr.m.** one of the David's heroes [2 S 23:27](https://ref.ly/2Sam23:27) rd. prob. סִבְּכַי ‖ [1 Ch 11:29](https://ref.ly/1Chr11:29) cf. DrSm.

> \[Str8403] † תַּבְנִית **n.f.** construction, pattern, figure —´תּ abs. [1 Ch 28:19](https://ref.ly/1Chr28:19); cstr. [Ex 25:9](https://ref.ly/Exod25:9), [25:9](https://ref.ly/Exod25:9) \+ 14 t. \+ Ez [8:10](https://ref.ly/Ezek8:10) (del. Co); תַּבְנִיתוֺ [2 K 16:10](https://ref.ly/2Kgs16:10), תַבְנִיתָם [Ex 25:40](https://ref.ly/Exod25:40)—**1\.** app. originally *construction, structure,* yet only P \& late: ´תַּבְנִית מִזְבַּה י [Jos 22:28](https://ref.ly/Josh22:28); cf. [Psalm 144:12](https://ref.ly/Ps144:12) in sim. הֵיכָל ´בְּנוֺתֵינוּ כְזְוִיּוֺת מְחֻטָּבִים תּ *carved acc. to the construction of a palace, palace\-fashion.* **2\.** *pattern,* acc. to which anything is to be constructed (P \& late), of tabernacle [Ex 25:9](https://ref.ly/Exod25:9), utensils of tab. v:[9](https://ref.ly/Exod25:9), v:[40](https://ref.ly/Exod25:40); an altar [2 K 16:10](https://ref.ly/2Kgs16:10) תַּבְנִיתוֺ לְכָ־ל מַעֲשֵהוּ (disting. fr. דְּמוּת); temple [1 Ch 28:11](https://ref.ly/1Chr28:11) cf. v:[12](https://ref.ly/1Chr28:12) chariot, i.e. cherubim [1 Ch 28:18](https://ref.ly/1Chr28:18); ´מַלְאֲכוֺת הַתּ [1 Ch 28:19](https://ref.ly/1Chr28:19) i.e. objects of which the patters is given. **3\.** *figure, image,* Hex only D, of idols in form of animals [Dt 4:16](https://ref.ly/Deut4:16), [4:17](https://ref.ly/Deut4:17), [4:17](https://ref.ly/Deut4:17), [4:18](https://ref.ly/Deut4:18), [4:18](https://ref.ly/Deut4:18); elsewhere late [Is 44:13](https://ref.ly/Isa44:13), Ez [8:10](https://ref.ly/Ezek8:10) (del. B Co) [Psalm 106:20](https://ref.ly/Ps106:20); cf. יָד ´תּ Ez [8:3](https://ref.ly/Ezek8:3), [10:8](https://ref.ly/Ezek10:8) i.e. something like a hand.

## בנט (id: BDB01324)

בנט (assumed as √ of אַבְנֵט, Thes Sta§ 257\).

> \[Str73] † אַבְנֵט **n.\[m.]** girdle —´א abs. [Ex 28:4](https://ref.ly/Exod28:4) \+; אַבְנֵֽטְךָ֙ [Is 22:21](https://ref.ly/Isa22:21); cstr. [Ex 39:29](https://ref.ly/Exod39:29); אַבְנֵטִים [Ex 28:40](https://ref.ly/Exod28:40); —*girdle,* of high priest [Ex 28:4](https://ref.ly/Exod28:4), [28:39](https://ref.ly/Exod28:39), [39:29](https://ref.ly/Exod39:29), [Lv 8:7](https://ref.ly/Lev8:7), [16:4](https://ref.ly/Lev16:4); of priests [Ex 28:40](https://ref.ly/Exod28:40), [29:9](https://ref.ly/Exod29:9), [Lv 8:13](https://ref.ly/Lev8:13) (all P); of high official [Is 22:21](https://ref.ly/Isa22:21).—Josephusiii. 7\. 2 ἀβανήθ; cf. further LagGes. Abh. 39\.
> 
> 126:a. אַבְנֵט: described JosAnt. iii. 7\. 2 as a sash wound round breast several times, and falling to the feet; in Targum SyrVer הֶמְיָן (Pers.) a money\-bag wound round body StaThLz. 1894, 236\.

## בעד (id: BDB01332)

בעד (Ar. بَعِدَ, بَعُدَ *be remote, distant,* Qor 9:42, بَعِيدٌ *distant;* Eth. በዐደ፡ *to change,* Psalm 33 title, ብዑድ፡ *different, distinct*, ብኣዕድ፡ *another*).

> \[Str1157,1157] בַּ֫עַד:105 **subst.** prop. separation, with a gen., *in separation from,* in usage a **prep. away** from, behind, about, on behalf of (Ar. بَعْدُ, of time, *after*)—abs. † Ct [4:1](https://ref.ly/Song4:1), [4:3](https://ref.ly/Song4:3), [6:7](https://ref.ly/Song6:7), elsewhere cstr. בְּעַר, with sf. בַּעֲדִי [Ex 8:24](https://ref.ly/Exod8:24) \+, בַּעֲדֵנִי † [Psalm 139:11](https://ref.ly/Ps139:11); בַּעַדְךָ [Gn 20:7](https://ref.ly/Gen20:7) \+; בַּעֲדוֺ etc.; 1 pl. בַּעֲדֵנוּ, † [Am 9:10](https://ref.ly/Amos9:10) בַּעֲדֵינוּ; בַּעַדְכֶם [1 S 7:5](https://ref.ly/1Sam7:5) \+; בַּעֲדָם [Lv 9:7](https://ref.ly/Lev9:7) \+;—**1\.** lit. **a.** with vbs. of falling, letting down, leaning forward so as to look out, *through* (lit. *away from*) a window, etc.: [Jos 2:15](https://ref.ly/Josh2:15) and she let him down by a cord בְּעַד הַחַלּוֺן *away from* the window, i.e. *out through* it, [1 S 19:12](https://ref.ly/1Sam19:12), [2 S 20:21](https://ref.ly/2Sam20:21), [2 K 1:2](https://ref.ly/2Kgs1:2) and A. fell בְּעַד הַשְּבָּכָה *out through* the lattice; [Gn 26:8](https://ref.ly/Gen26:8), Ju [5:28](https://ref.ly/Judg5:28) *out through* the window נִשְׁקְפָה she *looked forth* (lit. *leant forward*), [2 S 6:26](https://ref.ly/2Sam6:26) \+; pregn. [Jb 22:13](https://ref.ly/Job22:13) will he judge בְּעַד עֲרָפֶל (looking) *out through* the thick clouds ? Conversely [Jo 2:9](https://ref.ly/Joel2:9) *in through* the windows they come (the locusts) as a thief. **b.** idiom. with vbs. of *shutting,* esp. סָגַר בְּעַד to shut *behind* or *upon*—whether oneself, Ju [9:51](https://ref.ly/Judg9:51) and they entered the tower וַיִּסְגְּרוּ בַּעֲדָם and shut (the doors) *upon* themselves (sc. from the inside), [2 K 4:4](https://ref.ly/2Kgs4:4), [4:5](https://ref.ly/2Kgs4:5), [4:33](https://ref.ly/2Kgs4:33), [Is 26:20](https://ref.ly/Isa26:20); or another, [Gn 7:16](https://ref.ly/Gen7:16) בַּעֲדוֺ ´וַיִּסְגֹּר י and ´י shut *behind* or *upon* him (sc. from the outside), i.e. shut him in, Ju [3:23](https://ref.ly/Judg3:23) (the sf. in בַּעֲדוֺ referrring to Eglon), [2 K 4:21](https://ref.ly/2Kgs4:21) (but N. B. persons leaving a room shut the door אַחֲרֵי themselves [Gn 19:6](https://ref.ly/Gen19:6), [2 S 13:17](https://ref.ly/2Sam13:17), [13:18](https://ref.ly/2Sam13:18)): see also Ju [3:22](https://ref.ly/Judg3:22), [1 S 1:6](https://ref.ly/1Sam1:6) בְּעַד רַחְמָהּ ´סָגַּר י (cf. [Gn 20:18](https://ref.ly/Gen20:18) עָצַר בְּעַד רֶחֶם), [Jb 9:7](https://ref.ly/Job9:7) חָתַם בְּעַד to seal *up,* [1:10](https://ref.ly/Job1:10), [3:23](https://ref.ly/Job3:23) בְּעַד (הֵסֵח)שוּח to make a hedge *about*, [La 3:7](https://ref.ly/Lam3:7) גָּדַר בְּעַד to fence *about.* Somewhat peculiarly Am [9:10](https://ref.ly/Amos9:10) (who say,) Evil will not draw near, or come in front בַּעֲדֵינוּ *so as to be about us* (but Gr עָדֵינוּ *unto* us), [Jo 2:8](https://ref.ly/Joel2:8) (of the locusts) בְּעַד הַשֶּׁלַח יִפָֹּלוּ (prob.) *in among* the weapons they throw themselves (i.e. they pass about and between them without being injured or having their course impeded), [1 S 4:18](https://ref.ly/1Sam4:18) and Eli fell backward בְּעַד יַד הַשַּׁעַר, i.e. (si vera 1\.) *about* the side of the gate: but text dub.; v. Dr. Without a vb. [Jon 2:7](https://ref.ly/Jonah2:7) the earth, בְּרִיחֶיהָ בַעֲדִי her bars were *upon* me (or *about* me) for ever, [Psalm 139:11](https://ref.ly/Ps139:11) night shall be the light *about* me, [Psalm 3:4](https://ref.ly/Ps3:4) thou מָגֵּן בַּעֲדִי art a shield *about* me. Hence **c.** after a vb. of protecting, † [Zc 12:8](https://ref.ly/Zech12:8) in that day בְּעַד יושׁב ´יָגֵן י ירושׁלם will ´י give protection *about*, etc. **2\.** metaph. *on behalf of* (ὑπέρ); very freq. after הִתְפַּלֵּל [Gn 20:7](https://ref.ly/Gen20:7), [Nu 21:7](https://ref.ly/Num21:7), [1 S 7:5](https://ref.ly/1Sam7:5), [Psalm 72:15](https://ref.ly/Ps72:15) \+; also with other vbs. of entreating [Ex 8:24](https://ref.ly/Exod8:24), [1 S 7:9](https://ref.ly/1Sam7:9), or consulting (דָּרַשׁ) [Is 8:19](https://ref.ly/Isa8:19), [2 K 22:13](https://ref.ly/2Kgs22:13), [Je 21:2](https://ref.ly/Jer21:2); with כִּפֶּר *atone* [Ex 32:30](https://ref.ly/Exod32:30), [Lv 9:7](https://ref.ly/Lev9:7), [11:6](https://ref.ly/Lev11:6), [11:11](https://ref.ly/Lev11:11) \+, עָשָה (\= offer) † Ez [45:22](https://ref.ly/Ezek45:22): see also [2 S 10:12](https://ref.ly/2Sam10:12), [12:16](https://ref.ly/2Sam12:16), [Is 37:4](https://ref.ly/Isa37:4), [Je 7:16](https://ref.ly/Jer7:16), [11:4](https://ref.ly/Jer11:4), Ez [22:30](https://ref.ly/Ezek22:30), [Pr 20:16](https://ref.ly/Prov20:16), [27:13](https://ref.ly/Prov27:13), [Psalm 138:8](https://ref.ly/Ps138:8), [Jb 2:4](https://ref.ly/Job2:4) עוֺר בְּעַד עוֺר (v. עוֺר), [6:22](https://ref.ly/Job6:22). Almost \= *for the sake of, an account of* [Pr 6:26](https://ref.ly/Prov6:26), [Je 11:14](https://ref.ly/Jer11:14) (but LXX. SyrVer Vulgate Targum and many MSS. בְּעֵת רָעָתָם, cf. v:[12](https://ref.ly/Jer11:12), [15:11](https://ref.ly/Jer15:11)). [Is 32:14](https://ref.ly/Isa32:14) hill and watch\-tower הָיָה בְעַר מְעָרוֺת are come to be *on behalf of* (i.e. take the place of, serve as) caves for ever: but use is singular, and בְּעַד is prob. only dittogr. from מער in מערות (so Gr).—With מִן, ´מִבַּעַד לְ (cf. ´מִמַּעַל לְ, ´מִתַּחַת לְ): † Ct [4:1](https://ref.ly/Song4:1), [4:3](https://ref.ly/Song4:3), [6:7](https://ref.ly/Song6:7) thine eyes are doves מִבַּעַד לְחַמָּהֵךְ *from behind* thy veil. Cf. on בַּעַד GrätzMonatsschrift, 1879, 49 ff.

## בָּעָה (id: BDB01334)

\[Str1158] † \[בָּעָה] **vb.** inquire, cause to swell or boil up (NH *id;* Ar. بَغَى *seek, suppurate, swell;* Aram. בְּעָא, ܒܥܳܐ *seek*)—**Qal** *Impf.* 3 fs. תִּבְעֶה [Is 64:1](https://ref.ly/Isa64:1); 2 mpl. תִּבְעָיוּן [Is 21:12](https://ref.ly/Isa21:12); *Imv.* בְּעָיוּ [Is 21:12](https://ref.ly/Isa21:12); —**a.** of rising desire, *seek, inquire,* abs., of inquiring of prophet [Is 21:12](https://ref.ly/Isa21:12), [21:12](https://ref.ly/Isa21:12). **2\.** *cause to boil up,* ־אֵשׁ´מַיִם תִּב [Is 64:1](https://ref.ly/Isa64:1) (but gloss Che). **Niph.** *Pf.* נִבְעוּ Ob:[6](https://ref.ly/Obad1:6) *searched out* (‖נֶחְפְּשוּ); *Pt.* נִבְעֶה [Is 30:13](https://ref.ly/Isa30:13) *swelling, swelling out* (of decaying wall; Di *swelling, enlarging,* of crack in wall).

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 64:1; Isaiah 21:12; Isaiah 30:13


## בעז (id: BDB01335)

בעז (*quick ?* cf. Ar. بَغْزٌ *swiftness* (of horse)).

> \[Str1162] † בֹּ֫עַז **n.pr.m.** (*quickness ?*)—**1\.** kinsman of Naomi, who married Ruth [Ru 2:1](https://ref.ly/Ruth2:1), [2:3](https://ref.ly/Ruth2:3), [2:4](https://ref.ly/Ruth2:4), [2:5](https://ref.ly/Ruth2:5), [2:8](https://ref.ly/Ruth2:8), [2:11](https://ref.ly/Ruth2:11), [2:14](https://ref.ly/Ruth2:14), [2:15](https://ref.ly/Ruth2:15), [2:19](https://ref.ly/Ruth2:19), [2:23](https://ref.ly/Ruth2:23), [3:2](https://ref.ly/Ruth3:2), [3:7](https://ref.ly/Ruth3:7), [4:1](https://ref.ly/Ruth4:1), [4:1](https://ref.ly/Ruth4:1), [4:5](https://ref.ly/Ruth4:5), [4:8](https://ref.ly/Ruth4:8), [4:9](https://ref.ly/Ruth4:9), [4:13](https://ref.ly/Ruth4:13), also v:[21](https://ref.ly/Ruth4:21), v:[21](https://ref.ly/Ruth4:21), [1 Ch 2:11](https://ref.ly/1Chr2:11), [2:12](https://ref.ly/1Chr2:12) (LXX Βοος, Βοοζ). **2\.** name of the left hand of two pillars set up before temple (cf. also יָכִין sub כון) [1 K 7:21](https://ref.ly/1Kgs7:21) \= [2 Ch 3:17](https://ref.ly/2Chr3:17); (mng. obscure; MT appar. ref. to **1**, cf. Targum [2 Ch 3:17](https://ref.ly/2Chr3:17); Thes supposes name of architect or donor; Ew perh. sons of Solomon, etc.; rd. possibly בְּעֹז *in strength*, LXX [2 Ch 3:17](https://ref.ly/2Chr3:17) ἰσχύς; Th thinks יכין בעז a sentence, one word being engraved on each pillar, *he* (God) *establisheth in strength;* against him, however, Ke Be; Öt thinks an exclamation, *in strength !* expressing satisfaction of architect; Klo prop. for בעז, בַּעַל עֹז (cf. B [1 K 7:21](https://ref.ly/1Kgs7:21) Βαλαζ)).

or well\-grouped sayings; or collectors (of wise sentences) [Ec 12:11](https://ref.ly/Eccl12:11).—On [2 S 6:2](https://ref.ly/2Sam6:2) v. II. בַּעֲלָה. (בַּעַל in Hex not J or P; בעל [Lv 21:4](https://ref.ly/Lev21:4) LXX ἐζάπινα \= כבלּע [Nu 4:20](https://ref.ly/Num4:20): Di בְּאֵבֶל). Esp. II. *lord,* specif. as divine name, † Baal. **1\.** *without article:* במות בעל [Nu 22:41](https://ref.ly/Num22:41) (poet. Balaam); בעל פעור [Nu 25:3](https://ref.ly/Num25:3), [25:5](https://ref.ly/Num25:5) (E) [Dt 4:3](https://ref.ly/Deut4:3) (vid. below). This divine name is not used elsewhere in Hex. It probably originated from the sense of divine ownership, rather than sovereignty (RS Sem 92\). It seems to have been used in Northern Israel \= אדון in the South. It was the special name of the God of the Canaanites, Philistines, etc., \= Babylonian בֵּל, cf. SchrSK 1874, 335 ff. In later times scribes substituted בּשֶׁת, in n.pr. (ירבשׁת \= ירבעל, אשׁבשׁת \= אשׁבעל, vid. בּשֶׁת, GeiZMG 1862, 728 ff.), \& also in the text for בעל [Ho 9:10](https://ref.ly/Hos9:10), [Je 11:13](https://ref.ly/Jer11:13) (hence ἡ βάαλ [Je 2:23](https://ref.ly/Jer2:23), [7:9](https://ref.ly/Jer7:9), [11:13](https://ref.ly/Jer11:13), [11:17](https://ref.ly/Jer11:17), [19:5](https://ref.ly/Jer19:5), [Ho 2:10](https://ref.ly/Hos2:10), [13:1](https://ref.ly/Hos13:1) \+, [Rom 11:4](https://ref.ly/Rom11:4), see DIBaal mit d. weib. Hermon; either mod. *Bâniâs*, Gk. Paneas, NT Caesarea Philippi, where a grotto of Pan took the place of the ancient worship of Gad, RobBR iii. 410 TristrTpg 271; or *Ḥâsbêyâ* BDPal 297 Di; possibly \= בעל חרמון cf. Thes RobBR iii. 409\.

> \[Str1195] † בַּעֲנָא **n.pr.m.** (? בֶּן־עָנָה *son of distress*)—**1\.** name of two officers of Solomon, **a.** [1 K 4:12](https://ref.ly/1Kgs4:12); **b.** v:[16](https://ref.ly/1Kgs4:16). **2\.** father (ancestor) of an Israelite of Nehemiah's time [Ne 3:4](https://ref.ly/Neh3:4) cf. foll.

> \[Str1196] † בַּעֲנָה **n.pr.m.** (? *id.*)—**1\.** a Benjamite, one of the murderers of Ishbosheth [2 S 4:2](https://ref.ly/2Sam4:2), [4:5](https://ref.ly/2Sam4:5), [4:6](https://ref.ly/2Sam4:6), [4:9](https://ref.ly/2Sam4:9). **2\.** father of one of David's heroes [2 S 23:29](https://ref.ly/2Sam23:29) \= [1 Ch 11:30](https://ref.ly/1Chr11:30). **3\.** head of a family of returning exiles [Ezr 2:2](https://ref.ly/Ezra2:2) \= [Ne 7:7](https://ref.ly/Neh7:7); perh. also \= בַּעֲנָא [Ne 3:4](https://ref.ly/Neh3:4). **4\.** a chief of the people [Ne 10:28](https://ref.ly/Neh10:28).
