## I בָּעַר (id: BDB01366)

\[Str1197] † I. \[בָּעַר] **vb.** burn, consume (Targum בְּעַר *burn;* ܒܥܰܪ *seek out, collect, glean;* this apparently earlier mng.)—**Qal** *Pf.* 3 fs. בָּֽעֲרָה [Nu 11:3](https://ref.ly/Num11:3) \+ 2 t., וּבָֽעֲרָה consec. [Is 10:17](https://ref.ly/Isa10:17) \+ 3 t., בָּֽעֲרוּ Ju [15:14](https://ref.ly/Judg15:14) \+ 2 t. \+ [2 S 22:13](https://ref.ly/2Sam22:13) (but cf. De on Psalm 18), וּבָֽעֲרוּ consec. [Is 1:31](https://ref.ly/Isa1:31); *Impf.* יִבְעַר [Ex 3:3](https://ref.ly/Exod3:3), [Psalm 2:12](https://ref.ly/Ps2:12), etc.; *Pt.* בֹּעֵר [Ex 3:2](https://ref.ly/Exod3:2) \+ 6 t., בֹּעֵרָה [Is 34:9](https://ref.ly/Isa34:9), בּוֺעֵ֫רָה [Ho 7:4](https://ref.ly/Hos7:4), בֹּעֲרָ֫ה [Is 30:33](https://ref.ly/Isa30:33), בֹּעֶ֫רֶת [Je 20:9](https://ref.ly/Jer20:9), בֹּעֲרוֺת Ez [1:13](https://ref.ly/Ezek1:13); —*burn,* **1\.** (intr.) specif. *begin to burn, be kindled* [Psalm 18:8](https://ref.ly/Ps18:8) sq. מִן (subj. גֶּחָלִים) \= [2 S 22:9](https://ref.ly/2Sam22:9) (in v:[13](https://ref.ly/2Sam22:13) text. error cf. supr.), [Psalm 106:18](https://ref.ly/Ps106:18) (subj. אֵשׁ); fig. [Psalm 2:12](https://ref.ly/Ps2:12) (subj. אַפּוֺ) cf. [Je 44:6](https://ref.ly/Jer44:6) (subj. חֵמָה); [Is 30:33](https://ref.ly/Isa30:33) (c. בְּ, subj. ´נִשְׁמַת י), [Psalm 39:4](https://ref.ly/Ps39:4) (subj. אֵשׁ fig. of grief, distress) cf. [Je 20:9](https://ref.ly/Jer20:9). **2\.** *be burning, burn,* Ju [15:14](https://ref.ly/Judg15:14) (subj. פִּשְׁתִּים, c. בָּאֵשׁ), [Ex 3:2](https://ref.ly/Exod3:2) (הסנה, c. בָּאֵשׁ), v:[3](https://ref.ly/Exod3:3) (subj. *id.*); [Dt 4:11](https://ref.ly/Deut4:11), [5:20](https://ref.ly/Deut5:20), [9:15](https://ref.ly/Deut9:15) (all subj. הר, c. בָּאֵשׁ), cf. [Is 34:9](https://ref.ly/Isa34:9) (זֶפֶת בערה), fig. of destruction [Is 1:31](https://ref.ly/Isa1:31) (subj. חָסֹן \& פֹּעֲלוֺ); of torch לַפִּיד [Is 62:1](https://ref.ly/Isa62:1); of oven תַּנּוּר [Ho 7:4](https://ref.ly/Hos7:4), [7:6](https://ref.ly/Hos7:6) (i.e. heated by fire within it). **3\.** trans. *burn, consume* (subj. אשׁ, להבה etc., sq. בְּ) [Nu 11:1](https://ref.ly/Num11:1), [11:3](https://ref.ly/Num11:3) (´אשׁ י), [Jb 1:16](https://ref.ly/Job1:16) (אשׁ אלהים); in smile Ez 1:33 (´גחלי אשׁ י), [Psalm 83:15](https://ref.ly/Ps83:15) (only here trans. c. acc.; should תבער be pointed as Pi. ?); fig. (subj. wrath of ´י) [Is 42:25](https://ref.ly/Isa42:25) cf. [La 2:5](https://ref.ly/Lam2:5) (subj. fire \= fiery trial) [Is 43:2](https://ref.ly/Isa43:2). **4\.** act. but abs., fig., subj. wrath of ´י [Je 4:4](https://ref.ly/Jer4:4), [7:20](https://ref.ly/Jer7:20), [21:12](https://ref.ly/Jer21:12), [Psalm 89:47](https://ref.ly/Ps89:47) cf. [Is 10:17](https://ref.ly/Isa10:17), [Psalm 79:5](https://ref.ly/Ps79:5), [Is 30:27](https://ref.ly/Isa30:27) (בֹּעֵר אַפּוֺ… ´שֵׁם י) [Mal 3:19](https://ref.ly/Mal3:19) (היום בא); of human anger [Est 1:12](https://ref.ly/Esth1:12); subj. wickedness [Is 9:17](https://ref.ly/Isa9:17). **Pi.** *Pf.* בִּעֵר [1 K 22:47](https://ref.ly/1Kgs22:47), [2 K 23:24](https://ref.ly/2Kgs23:24), וּבִעֵר consec. [Lv 6:5](https://ref.ly/Lev6:5), בִּעַ֫רְתָּ [2 Ch 19:3](https://ref.ly/2Chr19:3), וּבִַ ערְתָּ֫ [Dt 13:6](https://ref.ly/Deut13:6) \+ 9 t. in Dt; וּבִעֲרוּ cons. Ez [39:9](https://ref.ly/Ezek39:9), [39:9](https://ref.ly/Ezek39:9) (9:a del. Co after Vrss), etc.; *Impf.* יְבַעֵר [1 K 14:10](https://ref.ly/1Kgs14:10); 2 ms. תְּבַעֵר [Dt 21:9](https://ref.ly/Deut21:9), יְבַעֲרוּ Ez [39:10](https://ref.ly/Ezek39:10); 2 mpl. תְּבַעֲרוּ [Ex 35:3](https://ref.ly/Exod35:3), וּנְבַעֲרָ֫ה subord. Ju [20:13](https://ref.ly/Judg20:13); *Pt.* מְבַעֲרִים [Je 7:18](https://ref.ly/Jer7:18); —**1\.** *kindle*, lit. c. acc. אשׁ [Ex 35:3](https://ref.ly/Exod35:3), [Je 7:18](https://ref.ly/Jer7:18) cf. Ez [39:9](https://ref.ly/Ezek39:9) (v. supr.) v:[10](https://ref.ly/Ezek39:10); fig. of ´י sending destruction Ez [21:4](https://ref.ly/Ezek21:4) cf. of human schemes [Is 50:11](https://ref.ly/Isa50:11); *light*, obj. lamps in temple [2 Ch 4:20](https://ref.ly/2Chr4:20) cf. [13:11](https://ref.ly/2Chr13:11). **2\.** *burn*, lit. c. acc. עֵצִים [Lv 6:5](https://ref.ly/Lev6:5), גָּלָל *dung* [1 K 14:10](https://ref.ly/1Kgs14:10); abs. [Is 44:15](https://ref.ly/Isa44:15) cf. [40:16](https://ref.ly/Isa40:16), [Ne 10:35](https://ref.ly/Neh10:35). **3\.** fig. *consume*, utterly remove, partic. of evil and guilt, c. acc., esp. in Deuteronomic phrase (מישראל)ובערת הרע מקרבך [Dt 13:6](https://ref.ly/Deut13:6), [17:7](https://ref.ly/Deut17:7), [17:12](https://ref.ly/Deut17:12), [19:13](https://ref.ly/Deut19:13), [19:19](https://ref.ly/Deut19:19), [21:21](https://ref.ly/Deut21:21) cf. v:[9](https://ref.ly/Deut21:9), [22:21](https://ref.ly/Deut22:21), [22:22](https://ref.ly/Deut22:22), [22:24](https://ref.ly/Deut22:24), [24:7](https://ref.ly/Deut24:7), v. also Ju [20:13](https://ref.ly/Judg20:13); further, [1 K 22:47](https://ref.ly/1Kgs22:47), [2 K 23:24](https://ref.ly/2Kgs23:24), [2 Ch 19:3](https://ref.ly/2Chr19:3); also of devoted (tabooed) things [Dt 26:13](https://ref.ly/Deut26:13), [26:14](https://ref.ly/Deut26:14); of persons (*exterminate*) [2 S 4:11](https://ref.ly/2Sam4:11); sq. אחרי pregn. [1 K 14:10](https://ref.ly/1Kgs14:10), [21:21](https://ref.ly/1Kgs21:21); \= devour, devastate, greedily enjoy the fruits of, [Is 3:14](https://ref.ly/Isa3:14); abs. היה לְבָעֵר *be for destruction, be destroyed* [Nu 24:22](https://ref.ly/Num24:22), [Is 5:5](https://ref.ly/Isa5:5), [6:13](https://ref.ly/Isa6:13); cf. רוּחַ בָּעֵר [Is 4:4](https://ref.ly/Isa4:4). **Pu.** *Pt.* מְבֹעָרֶת [Je 36:22](https://ref.ly/Jer36:22); —*burn* (i.e. be supplied with fire), of fire\-jar, הָאָח. **Hiph.** *Pf.* וְהִבְעַרְתִּ֫י [Na 2:14](https://ref.ly/Nah2:14); *Impf.* וַיַּבְעֵר Ju [15:5](https://ref.ly/Judg15:5), [2 Ch 28:3](https://ref.ly/2Chr28:3), וַיַּבְעֶ־ר Ju [15:5](https://ref.ly/Judg15:5); תַּבְעִיר Ez [5:2](https://ref.ly/Ezek5:2); *Pt.* מַבְעִיר [1 K 16:3](https://ref.ly/1Kgs16:3), מַבְעִר [Ex 22:5](https://ref.ly/Exod22:5); —**1\.** *kindle* (c. acc. cogn.) [Ex 22:5](https://ref.ly/Exod22:5), cf. Ju [15:5](https://ref.ly/Judg15:5) אשׁ בלפידים ´ויב *caused fire to burn among the brands.* **2\.** *burn up*, c. acc. Ju [15:5](https://ref.ly/Judg15:5), [2 Ch 28:3](https://ref.ly/2Chr28:3) (sacrifice of children בָּאֵשׁ) Ez [5:2](https://ref.ly/Ezek5:2) (בָּאוּר but cf. Co) [Na 2:14](https://ref.ly/Nah2:14) (בעשׁן). **3\.** *consume* \= *destroy* (cf. Pi.) [1 K 16:3](https://ref.ly/1Kgs16:3) (sq. אַחֲרֵי).

> \[Str1200] † בְּעֵרָה **n.f.** burning, only ´הַבּ as acc. cogn. with הִבְעִיר [Ex 22:5](https://ref.ly/Exod22:5).

> \[Str8407] † תַּבְעֵרָה **n.pr.loc.** in the wilderness (*burning*, cf. [Nu 11:3](https://ref.ly/Num11:3)) [Nu 11:3](https://ref.ly/Num11:3), [Dt 9:22](https://ref.ly/Deut9:22).

> \[Str1165] † \[בְּעִיר] **n.m.**:[Ex 22:4](https://ref.ly/Exod22:4) beasts, cattle, coll. (NH *id.*, Aram. *id.*, ܒܥܺܝܪܳܐ, Eth. ብዕርኣዊ፡ etc., Sab. בער DHMZMG 1876, 674; 1883, 329; Ar. بَعِيرٌ of *camel;* also *ass*, etc., cf. Lane227 a; connexion with above √ obscure)—sf. בְּעִירֹה [Ex 22:4](https://ref.ly/Exod22:4), בְּעִירֵנוּ [Nu 20:4](https://ref.ly/Num20:4), בְּעִירְכָם [Gn 45:17](https://ref.ly/Gen45:17), כְּ עִירָם [Nu 20:8](https://ref.ly/Num20:8) \+ 2 t.;—*beasts* of burden [Gn 45:17](https://ref.ly/Gen45:17) (i.e. asses [44:3](https://ref.ly/Gen44:3), [44:13](https://ref.ly/Gen44:13)); elsewhere general, *cattle* [Ex 22:4](https://ref.ly/Exod22:4), [Nu 20:4](https://ref.ly/Num20:4), [20:8](https://ref.ly/Num20:8), [20:11](https://ref.ly/Num20:11), [Psalm 78:48](https://ref.ly/Ps78:48)

> \[Str1197] † II. \[בָּעַר] **vb. denom.** be brutish—**Qal** *Impf.* יִבְעֲרוּ [Je 10:8](https://ref.ly/Jer10:8) (‖יכסלו) *be stupid, dull\-hearted, unreceptive;* cf. Pt. pl. בֹּעֲרִים [Psalm 94:8](https://ref.ly/Ps94:8) (‖כסיליס); of *inhuman, cruel, barbarous* men Ez [21:36](https://ref.ly/Ezek21:32). **Niph.** *Pf.* נִבְעַר [Je 10:14](https://ref.ly/Jer10:14), [51:17](https://ref.ly/Jer51:17), נִבְעֲרוּ [Je 10:21](https://ref.ly/Jer10:21); *Pt.* נִבְעָרָה [Is 19:11](https://ref.ly/Isa19:11); —*brutish, stupid* [Is 19:11](https://ref.ly/Isa19:11) (עצה, ‖אויל); *dull\-hearted*, ignorant of God [Je 10:14](https://ref.ly/Jer10:14), [10:21](https://ref.ly/Jer10:21), [51:17](https://ref.ly/Jer51:17). **Pi.** *Pf.* וּבִעֵר consec. [Ex 22:4](https://ref.ly/Exod22:4) *feed, graze* (בשדה אחר). **Hiph.** *Impf.* יַבְעֶ־ר [Ex 22:4](https://ref.ly/Exod22:4) *cause to be grazed over*, sq. שדה.
> 
> 129:b. II. \[בָּעַר] : **Niph.** [Je 10:14](https://ref.ly/Jer10:14) \= [51:17](https://ref.ly/Jer51:17) rather *stupefied* (by spectacle [10:13](https://ref.ly/Jer10:13) \= [51:16](https://ref.ly/Jer51:16)).

> \[Str1198] † בַּ֫עַר **n.m.** brutishness (only poet.)—abs. ´ב [Psalm 49:11](https://ref.ly/Ps49:11) \+ 3 t., בָּעַר [Pr 12:1](https://ref.ly/Prov12:1); —combination, ´אִישׁ־ב *brutish man* [Psalm 92:7](https://ref.ly/Ps92:7) (‖כסיל); elsewhere ´ב alone in same sense (concrete) [Psalm 49:11](https://ref.ly/Ps49:11) (‖ *id.*), \& as pred. \= adj., [Psalm 73:22](https://ref.ly/Ps73:22), [Pr 12:1](https://ref.ly/Prov12:1), [30:2](https://ref.ly/Prov30:2).

> \[Str1199] † בַּעֲרָא **n.pr.f.** wife of a Benjamite [1 Ch 8:8](https://ref.ly/1Chr8:8).

> \[Str1160] † בְּעוֺר **n.pr.m.** (*a burning;* Targum *torch*)—**1\.** father of Balaam [Nu 22:5](https://ref.ly/Num22:5), [31:8](https://ref.ly/Num31:8), [Dt 23:5](https://ref.ly/Deut23:5), [Jos 13:22](https://ref.ly/Josh13:22), [24:9](https://ref.ly/Josh24:9), [Mi 6:5](https://ref.ly/Mic6:5); בְּעֹר [Nu 24:3](https://ref.ly/Num24:3), [24:15](https://ref.ly/Num24:15) (´בנו ב). **2\.** father of בֶּלַע, a king of Edom [Gn 36:32](https://ref.ly/Gen36:32) \= [1 Ch 1:43](https://ref.ly/1Chr1:43).

> \[Str1202] † בַּעֲשֵיָה **n.pr.m.** (? \= מַעֲשֵיָה; so Thes; cf. DrSm. lxviii) a Levite [1 Ch 6:25](https://ref.ly/1Chr6:25); —cf. (וּ)מַעֲשֵיָה sub עשה.

> \[Str1201] בַּעְשָׁא **n.pr.m.** a king of Israel [1 K 15:16](https://ref.ly/1Kgs15:16), [15:17](https://ref.ly/1Kgs15:17), [15:19](https://ref.ly/1Kgs15:19) \+ 18 t. 1 K 15–16 \+ [21:22](https://ref.ly/1Kgs21:22), [2 K 9:9](https://ref.ly/2Kgs9:9), [2 Ch 16:1](https://ref.ly/2Chr16:1), [16:3](https://ref.ly/2Chr16:3), [16:5](https://ref.ly/2Chr16:5), [16:6](https://ref.ly/2Chr16:6), [Je 41:9](https://ref.ly/Jer41:9).

> \[Str1203] † בְּעֶשְׁתְּרָה **n.pr.loc.** (? \= בֵּית עשׁתרה \= *house of Ashtoreth*, cf. sub בית) a Levitical city in Manasseh [Jos 21:27](https://ref.ly/Josh21:27); \= עַשְׁתָּרוֺת [1 Ch 6:56](https://ref.ly/1Chr6:56)

## בָּעַת (id: BDB01377)

\[Str1204] † \[בָּעַת] **vb.** fall upon, startle, terrify (Ar. بَغَتَ *come* or *happen suddenly*, NH בָּעִת Hiph. *startle;* so Aram. בְּעֵת Pa. ܒܥܶܬ Aph.)—**Niph.** (late prose) *Pf.* נִבְעַת [1 Ch 21:30](https://ref.ly/1Chr21:30), נִבְעָת [Est 7:6](https://ref.ly/Esth7:6); נִבְעַתִּי [Dn 8:17](https://ref.ly/Dan8:17); —*be terrified*, abs. [Dn 8:17](https://ref.ly/Dan8:17); c. מפני [1 Ch 21:30](https://ref.ly/1Chr21:30), [Est 7:6](https://ref.ly/Esth7:6). **Pi.** (mostly poet.) *Pf.* 3 fs. sf. בִּעֲתָתְנִי [Is 21:4](https://ref.ly/Isa21:4), וּבִעֲתַ֫תּוּ [1 S 16:14](https://ref.ly/1Sam16:14); 3 pl. sf. בִּעֲתֻהוּ [Jb 18:11](https://ref.ly/Job18:11); *Impf.* 3 fs. תְּבַעֵת [Jb 13:11](https://ref.ly/Job13:11); sf. תְּבַעֲתַָנִּי [Jb 9:34](https://ref.ly/Job9:34), [13:21](https://ref.ly/Job13:21), תְּבַעֲתֶָךָּ [Jb 33:7](https://ref.ly/Job33:7); 2 ms. sf. תְּבַעֲתַָנּי [Jb 7:14](https://ref.ly/Job7:14); 3 mpl. sf. יְבַעֲתוּנִי [Psalm 18:5](https://ref.ly/Ps18:5) \= יְבַעֲתֻנִי [2 S 22:5](https://ref.ly/2Sam22:5), יְבַעֲתֻהוּ [Jb 3:5](https://ref.ly/Job3:5), [15:24](https://ref.ly/Job15:24); —**1\.** *fall upon* [1 S 16:14](https://ref.ly/1Sam16:14), [16:15](https://ref.ly/1Sam16:15) (only here in prose); *overwhelm* [Jb 3:5](https://ref.ly/Job3:5) (cf. לקח v:[6](https://ref.ly/Job3:6)) [9:34](https://ref.ly/Job9:34), [13:11](https://ref.ly/Job13:11) (‖פחד נפל על) v:[21](https://ref.ly/Job3:21) (cf. ‖) [15:24](https://ref.ly/Job15:24) (‖תקף) [18:11](https://ref.ly/Job18:11) (‖הפיץ) [33:7](https://ref.ly/Job33:7) (‖כבד על) [Is 21:4](https://ref.ly/Isa21:4); *assail* [Psalm 18:5](https://ref.ly/Ps18:5) \= [2 S 22:5](https://ref.ly/2Sam22:5). **2\.** *terrify* [Jb 7:14](https://ref.ly/Job7:14) (‖חתת).

> \[Str1205] † בְּעָתָה **n.f.** terror, dismay [Je 8:15](https://ref.ly/Jer8:15) \= [14:19](https://ref.ly/Jer14:19).

> \[Str1161] † \[בִּעוּתִים] **n.m.pl.** terrors, alarms, occasioned by God בִּעוּתֵי אֱלוֺהַּ [Jb 6:4](https://ref.ly/Job6:4) (‖חִצֵּי שַׁדַּי); בִּעוּתֶיךָ [Psalm 88:17](https://ref.ly/Ps88:17) (‖חֲרוֺנֶיךָ).

> \[Str1206] בֹּץ v. בצץ. p. 130

## בצל (id: BDB01382)

בחל (*strip, strip off*, Eth. በጸለ፡ I. 2; Ar. بَصٌلَ, appar. denom.)

> \[Str1211] † \[בָּצָל] **n.m.** onion (NH בָּצֵל or בֶּ֫צֶל, Ar. بَصَلٌ, Eth. በጸል፡ Aram. בּוּצְלָא, ܒܶܨܠܴܐ)—בְּצָלִים [Nu 11:5](https://ref.ly/Num11:5) (אֵת הַקִּשֻּׁאִים וְאֵת הָאֲבַטִּתִים וְאֶת־הֶחָצִיר וְאֵת־הַשּׁוּמִים ´וְאֶת־הַבּ).

> \[Str1212] † בְּצַלְאֵל **n.pr.m.** (*in the shadow (protection) of Ēl;* cf. cuneif. *Ṣil\-Bêl,* a king of Gaza, COT [Jos 11:22](https://ref.ly/Josh11:22))—**1\.** a skilled artisan of tribe of Judah [Ex 31:2](https://ref.ly/Exod31:2), [35:30](https://ref.ly/Exod35:30), [36:1](https://ref.ly/Exod36:1); [36:2](https://ref.ly/Exod36:2), [37:1](https://ref.ly/Exod37:1), [38:22](https://ref.ly/Exod38:22) (all P) [1 Ch 2:20](https://ref.ly/1Chr2:20), [2 Ch 1:5](https://ref.ly/2Chr1:5). **2\.** an Israelite [Ezr 10:30](https://ref.ly/Ezra10:30).

> \[Str1213] † בַּצְלוּת **n.pr.m.** (*stripping*)—head of Isr. fam. at return from exile; ´בני־ב [Ezr 2:52](https://ref.ly/Ezra2:52) \= [Ne 7:54](https://ref.ly/Neh7:54) Kt; בַּצְלִית [Ne 7:54](https://ref.ly/Neh7:54) Qr.

* **Associated Passages:** Exodus 37:1; 1 Chronicles 2:20; Exodus 36:2; Ezra 10:30; Exodus 38:22; Exodus 36:1; Exodus 31:2; Ezra 2:52; Exodus 35:30; Joshua 11:22; Nehemiah 7:54; Numbers 11:5; 2 Chronicles 1:5


## בָּצַע (id: BDB01386)

\[Str1214] † \[בָּצַע] **vb.** cut off, break off, gain by violence (so NH, Ar. بَضَعَ, Eth. በጽዐ፡ Aram. בְּצַע)—**Qal** *Impf.* יִבְצָע [Jb 27:8](https://ref.ly/Job27:8), יִבְצָעוּ [Jo 2:8](https://ref.ly/Joel2:8); *Imv.* sf. בְּצָ֫עַם בְּרֹאשׁ [Am 9:1](https://ref.ly/Amos9:1); *Inf.* בְּצֹעַ Ez [22:27](https://ref.ly/Ezek22:27); *Pt.* בּוֺצֵעַ [Pr 15:27](https://ref.ly/Prov15:27), [Je 6:13](https://ref.ly/Jer6:13), בֹּצֵעַ [Psalm 10:3](https://ref.ly/Ps10:3) \+ 3 t.;—*cut off, break off* (c. acc. capitals of pillars) [Am 9:1](https://ref.ly/Amos9:1) (but LagProv. v. vi בְּחַעַס \= בְּוַעַם Hb [3:12](https://ref.ly/Hab3:12) *in wrath*); so fig. [Jb 27:8](https://ref.ly/Job27:8) *when Eloah cutteth off, draweth out, his soul;* obj. om. *their course*, i.e. stop [Jo 2:8](https://ref.ly/Joel2:8) (cf. Hi\-St); usually *gain by violence* or in gen. *wrongfully* Ez [22:27](https://ref.ly/Ezek22:27); *Pt.* abs. [Psalm 10:3](https://ref.ly/Ps10:3) \= *greedy getter, robber;* \& c. acc. cogn. בֶּ֫צַע [Pr 1:19](https://ref.ly/Prov1:19), [15:27](https://ref.ly/Prov15:27), [Je 6:13](https://ref.ly/Jer6:13), [8:10](https://ref.ly/Jer8:10), Hb [2:9](https://ref.ly/Hab2:9). **Pi.** *Pf.* בִּצַּע [La 2:17](https://ref.ly/Lam2:17); *Impf.* יְבַצַּע [Is 10:12](https://ref.ly/Isa10:12); sf. יְבַצְּעֵנִי [Is 38:12](https://ref.ly/Isa38:12), [Jb 6:9](https://ref.ly/Job6:9); 2 fs. וַתְּבַצְּעִי Ez [22:12](https://ref.ly/Ezek22:12); 3 fpl. תְּבַצָּעְנָה [Zc 4:9](https://ref.ly/Zech4:9); —*cut off*, (*dis*) *sever* (i.e. from life) [Jb 6:9](https://ref.ly/Job6:9) cf. [Is 10:19](https://ref.ly/Isa10:19) (מִדַּלָּה); \= *finish, complete* [Is 10:12](https://ref.ly/Isa10:12), [Zc 4:9](https://ref.ly/Zech4:9); *accomplish* (\= carry out, fulfil) [La 2:17](https://ref.ly/Lam2:17) (obj. אמרתו); *violently make gain of*, obj. Pers. Ez [22:12](https://ref.ly/Ezek22:12) (instr. בּעשׁק).

> \[Str1215] † בֶּ֫צַע **n.m.** gain made by violence, unjust gain, profit—[Gn 37:26](https://ref.ly/Gen37:26) \+ 7 t. (cstr. Ju [5:19](https://ref.ly/Judg5:19) \+); בָּצַע [Ex 18:21](https://ref.ly/Exod18:21) \+ 7 t.; sf. בִּצֱעָךָ [Je 22:17](https://ref.ly/Jer22:17); בִּצְעֵךְ [Je 51:13](https://ref.ly/Jer51:13), Ez [22:13](https://ref.ly/Ezek22:13); בִּצְעוֺ [Is 56:11](https://ref.ly/Isa56:11) [57:17](https://ref.ly/Isa57:17); בִּצְעָם Ez [33:31](https://ref.ly/Ezek33:31), [Mi 4:13](https://ref.ly/Mic4:13); —*gain made by violence* (nearly \= plunder) Ju [5:19](https://ref.ly/Judg5:19), [Mi 4:13](https://ref.ly/Mic4:13); more generally, *unjust gain* [Ex 18:21](https://ref.ly/Exod18:21), [1 S 8:3](https://ref.ly/1Sam8:3), [Psalm 119:36](https://ref.ly/Ps119:36), [Pr 28:16](https://ref.ly/Prov28:16), [Is 33:15](https://ref.ly/Isa33:15), [56:11](https://ref.ly/Isa56:11), [57:17](https://ref.ly/Isa57:17), [Je 22:17](https://ref.ly/Jer22:17), [51:13](https://ref.ly/Jer51:13), Ez [22:13](https://ref.ly/Ezek22:13), [33:21](https://ref.ly/Ezek33:21); as acc. cogn. c. בָּצַע [Pr 1:19](https://ref.ly/Prov1:19), [15:27](https://ref.ly/Prov15:27), [Je 6:13](https://ref.ly/Jer6:13), [8:10](https://ref.ly/Jer8:10), Ez [22:27](https://ref.ly/Ezek22:27), Hb [2:9](https://ref.ly/Hab2:9); *profit* (with selfish suggestion) ´מה־ב [Gn 37:26](https://ref.ly/Gen37:26), [Mal 3:14](https://ref.ly/Mal3:14) cf. [Psalm 30:10](https://ref.ly/Ps30:10), [Jb 22:3](https://ref.ly/Job22:3).

## בָּצֵק (id: BDB01392)

\[Str1216] † בָּצֵק **vb.** swell—**Qal** *Pf.* 3 fs. בָּעֵָקָה [Dt 8:4](https://ref.ly/Deut8:4); 3 pl. בָּצֵָקוּ [Ne 9:21](https://ref.ly/Neh9:21); —*swell*, or *receive swellings, blisters*, of foot, Di [Dt 8:4](https://ref.ly/Deut8:4), Ry [Ne 9:21](https://ref.ly/Neh9:21).

> \[Str1217] † בָּצֵק **n.\[m.]** dough—בָּצֵק [Ex 12:39](https://ref.ly/Exod12:39) \+ 4 t.; sf. בְּצֵקוֺ [Ex 12:34](https://ref.ly/Exod12:34); —*dough*, not fermented [Ex 12:34](https://ref.ly/Exod12:34), [12:39](https://ref.ly/Exod12:39) (E); no restriction [2 S 13:8](https://ref.ly/2Sam13:8), [Ho 7:4](https://ref.ly/Hos7:4), [Je 7:18](https://ref.ly/Jer7:18).

> \[Str1218] † בָּצְקַת **n.pr.loc.** (? cf. Ar. بَصْقَةٌ an elevated region covered with volcanic stones) city of Judah toward Philistines, בָּצְקַת [Jos 15:39](https://ref.ly/Josh15:39); בָּצְקַתָ [2 K 22:1](https://ref.ly/2Kgs22:1) (home of Josiah's mother).

## בָּצַר (id: BDB01395)

\[Str1219] † \[בָּצַר] **vb.** cut off, make inaccessible (esp. by fortifying), enclose (NH *id.*, Aram. בְּצַר, ܒܨܰܪ (Pa. *diminish, substract*), perh. cf. Ar. بُصْرٌ *side, edge*, بِصْرٌ, etc., *rough stone*, أَرْضٌ بَصِرَةٌ *land in wh. are sharp stones* (cf. Lane))—**Qal** *Impf.* יִבְצֹר [Psalm 76:13](https://ref.ly/Ps76:13); 2 ms. תִּבְצֹר [Lv 25:5](https://ref.ly/Lev25:5), [Dt 24:21](https://ref.ly/Deut24:21), וַיִּבְצְרוּ Ju [9:27](https://ref.ly/Judg9:27), תִּבְצְרוּ [Lv 25:11](https://ref.ly/Lev25:11); *Pt.* act. בּוֺצֵר [Je 6:9](https://ref.ly/Jer6:9), בֹּֽחְרִיס [Je 49:9](https://ref.ly/Jer49:9), Ob:[5](https://ref.ly/Obad1:5); pass. m. בָּצוּר [Zc 11:2](https://ref.ly/Zech11:2) Kt (Qr בָּצִיר), f. בְּצוּרָה [Is 2:15](https://ref.ly/Isa2:15) \+ 3 t. \+ Ez [21:25](https://ref.ly/Ezek21:25) v. infr.; pl. f. בְּצוּרוֺת Ez [36:35](https://ref.ly/Ezek36:35), בְּצֻרוֺת [Nu 13:28](https://ref.ly/Num13:28) \+ 14 t., בְּצוּרֹת [Dt 1:28](https://ref.ly/Deut1:28), [Ne 9:25](https://ref.ly/Neh9:25), בְּצֻרֹת [Dt 3:5](https://ref.ly/Deut3:5), [9:1](https://ref.ly/Deut9:1); —*cut off,* grape\-clusters, ענבים [Lv 25:5](https://ref.ly/Lev25:5), cf. v:[11](https://ref.ly/Lev25:11) (obj. נזיר), [Dt 24:21](https://ref.ly/Deut24:21), Ju [9:27](https://ref.ly/Judg9:27) (obj. in both, כרם); hence Pt. act. *grape\-gathering, \-gatherer* [Je 6:9](https://ref.ly/Jer6:9), [49:9](https://ref.ly/Jer49:9), Ob:[5](https://ref.ly/Obad1:5); fig. *cut off* (\= take away) [Psalm 76:13](https://ref.ly/Ps76:13) (obj. רות נגידים); most often Pt. pass. *cut off, made inaccessible*, De [Is 2:15](https://ref.ly/Isa2:15) \= *fortified*, always f.; generally adj. c. עיר, עריס; [Nu 13:28](https://ref.ly/Num13:28), [Dt 1:28](https://ref.ly/Deut1:28), [3:5](https://ref.ly/Deut3:5), [9:1](https://ref.ly/Deut9:1), [Jos 14:12](https://ref.ly/Josh14:12), [2 S 20:6](https://ref.ly/2Sam20:6), [2 K 18:13](https://ref.ly/2Kgs18:13) \= [Is 36:1](https://ref.ly/Isa36:1), [2 K 19:25](https://ref.ly/2Kgs19:25) \= [Is 37:26](https://ref.ly/Isa37:26), [2 Ch 17:2](https://ref.ly/2Chr17:2), [19:5](https://ref.ly/2Chr19:5), [32:1](https://ref.ly/2Chr32:1), [33:14](https://ref.ly/2Chr33:14), [Ne 9:25](https://ref.ly/Neh9:25), [Is 25:2](https://ref.ly/Isa25:2), [27:10](https://ref.ly/Isa27:10), Ez [36:35](https://ref.ly/Ezek36:35), [Ho 8:14](https://ref.ly/Hos8:14), [Zp 1:16](https://ref.ly/Zeph1:16); rarely c. חומה [Dt 28:52](https://ref.ly/Deut28:52), [Is 2:15](https://ref.ly/Isa2:15), [Je 15:20](https://ref.ly/Jer15:20); ´יער הב [Zc 11:2](https://ref.ly/Zech11:2) (rd. Kt); once, subst. of *secrets, mysteries* (\= unattainable things) [Je 33:3](https://ref.ly/Jer33:3); —´ב Ez [21:25](https://ref.ly/Ezek21:25) LXX Sm Co בְּתוֺבָהּ, doubtless right. **Niph.** *Impf.* יִבָּצֵר *be withheld* [Gn 11:6](https://ref.ly/Gen11:6) (מֵהֶס), [Jb 42:2](https://ref.ly/Job42:2) (מִמְּךָ). **Pi.** *Impf.* 3 fs. תְּבַצֵּר [Je 51:53](https://ref.ly/Jer51:53) *fortify;* so *Inf.* לְבַּצֵר [Is 22:10](https://ref.ly/Isa22:10).

> \[Str1220,1222] † I. \[בֶּ֫צֶר] **n.\[m.]** precious ore (A W105 Thes), \> gold, ring\-gold HoffmZA 1887, 48 f. Hiob 70 (A W Thes *ore* as *that broken off;* Hoffm comp. Ar. بَظَرٌ *ring*, Heb. בָּצְרָה *enclosure*, Talm. בטרא *finger\-measure*, etc.; a √ II. בצר must then be assumed, \= بظر)—בָּצֶָר [Jb 22:24](https://ref.ly/Job22:24) (‖אוֺפִיר q.v.); בְּצָרֶיךָ v:[25](https://ref.ly/Job22:25) (‖כֶּסֶף) possibly also בִּצְרֵי כֶּסֶף [Psalm 68:31](https://ref.ly/Ps68:31) for MT ´בְּרַצֵּי־כ, cf. ## בָּקַע (id: BDB01411)

\[Str1234] † בָּקַע **vb.** cleave, break open or through (NH *id.,* MI15 מבקע השחרת *from break of dawn;* Aram. בְּקַע; cf. Eth. በቍዐ፡ *profit, be useful*, orig. *findere, aperire*, Di)—**Qal** *Pf.* בָּקַע [Psalm 78:13](https://ref.ly/Ps78:13), בָּֽקְעָה [Is 34:15](https://ref.ly/Isa34:15), בָּקַעְתָּ [Ne 9:11](https://ref.ly/Neh9:11), [Psalm 74:15](https://ref.ly/Ps74:15), וּבָֽקַעְתָּ֫ Ez [29:7](https://ref.ly/Ezek29:7); *Impf.* וַיִּבְקַע Ju [15:19](https://ref.ly/Judg15:19), [Is 48:21](https://ref.ly/Isa48:21), וַיִּבְקְעוּ [2 S 23:16](https://ref.ly/2Sam23:16) \= [1 Ch 11:18](https://ref.ly/1Chr11:18), וַיִּ בְקָעוּהָ [2 Ch 21:17](https://ref.ly/2Chr21:17); *Imv.* וּבְקָעֵהוּ [Ex 14:16](https://ref.ly/Exod14:16); *Inf. cstr.* sf. בִּקְעָם [Am 1:13](https://ref.ly/Amos1:13), [2 Ch 32:1](https://ref.ly/2Chr32:1); *Pt. act.* בּוֺקֵעַ [Is 63:12](https://ref.ly/Isa63:12), [Ec 10:9](https://ref.ly/Eccl10:9), בֹּקֵעַ [Psalm 141:7](https://ref.ly/Ps141:7); —**1\.** *cleave, cleave open*, sq. acc., Ju [15:19](https://ref.ly/Judg15:19) *God cleft open the hollow* (הַמֵּכְתֵּשׁ), and water came out, cf. [Is 48:21](https://ref.ly/Isa48:21) (obj. צוּר), also [Psalm 74:15](https://ref.ly/Ps74:15) *bring forth by cleaving*, obj. מַעְיָן יָנָחַָל, all three of divine operation; *cleave* or *rip open* pregnant women [Am 1:13](https://ref.ly/Amos1:13); of a broken staff, tearing the shoulder Ez [29:7](https://ref.ly/Ezek29:7) (but rd. כַּף *hand* for כָּתֵף, LXX Vulgate Sm (?) Co.); *cleave* wood [Ec 10:9](https://ref.ly/Eccl10:9) (‖מַסִּיעַ אֲבָנִיס); of ploughing (furrowing) the earth [Psalm 141:7](https://ref.ly/Ps141:7) (‖פֹּלֵחֵ; in sim.); esp. of *dividing* the sea, [Ex 14:16](https://ref.ly/Exod14:16) (P) [Ne 9:11](https://ref.ly/Neh9:11), [Psalm 78:13](https://ref.ly/Ps78:13), cf. [Is 63:12](https://ref.ly/Isa63:12), obj. מַיִם;—in all these subj. ´י exc. [Ex 14:16](https://ref.ly/Exod14:16) where he commands Moses; of *hatching out* (a brood, but no obj. expr.) [Is 34:15](https://ref.ly/Isa34:15), subj. קִפּוֺו *arrow\-snake.* **2\.** *break through* or *into*, sq. בְּ [2 S 23:16](https://ref.ly/2Sam23:16) \= [1 Ch 11:18](https://ref.ly/1Chr11:18); sq. acc. [2 Ch 21:17](https://ref.ly/2Chr21:17) וַיַעֲלוּ בִיהוּדָה וֵיִבְקָעוּהָ; also [32:1](https://ref.ly/2Chr32:1), obj. suff. ref. to cities, ויאמר לְבִקְעָס אֵלָיו *and he thought to break into them* and so bring them *unto himself*. **Niph.** *Pf.* נִבְקַע [Jb 26:8](https://ref.ly/Job26:8), [Zc 14:4](https://ref.ly/Zech14:4) (וְ consec.); נִבְקְעוּ [Gn 7:11](https://ref.ly/Gen7:11), [Is 35:6](https://ref.ly/Isa35:6), נִבְקָעוּ [2 Ch 25:11](https://ref.ly/2Chr25:11), [Pr 3:20](https://ref.ly/Prov3:20); *Impf.* יִבָּקַע [Is 58:8](https://ref.ly/Isa58:8), יִבֵָּקָעַ [Jb 32:19](https://ref.ly/Job32:19); 3 fs. חִבָּקַע [Is 59:5](https://ref.ly/Isa59:5), וַחִבָּקַע [Nu 16:31](https://ref.ly/Num16:31) \+ 3 t.; *Inf.* cstr. לְהִבָּקֵעַ Ez [30:16](https://ref.ly/Ezek30:16); —**1\.** *be cleft, rent open*, subj. the ground, הָֽאֲדָמָה [Nu 16:31](https://ref.ly/Num16:31) (J), הָאָרֶץ [1 K 1:40](https://ref.ly/1Kgs1:40) (hyperb.); mountain [Zc 14:4](https://ref.ly/Zech14:4); *burst open*, of men hurled from rock [2 Ch 25:12](https://ref.ly/2Chr25:12); of cloud beneath its weight of water [Jb 26:8](https://ref.ly/Job26:8); hyperb. of belly full of words seeking a vent, [Jb 32:19](https://ref.ly/Job32:19) *like new wine\-skins it will burst open;* so of the water\-receptacles (מעינות) of the great deep, at the flood [Gn 7:11](https://ref.ly/Gen7:11); of the water\-masses themselves, תהומות [Pr 3:20](https://ref.ly/Prov3:20), מים ונהלים [Is 35:6](https://ref.ly/Isa35:6); also of the Red Sea, וַיִבָּקְעוּ המים [Ex 14:21](https://ref.ly/Exod14:21); of light breaking forth [Is 58:8](https://ref.ly/Isa58:8) (fig.); of serpent's egg *hatching out* as a viper [Is 59:5](https://ref.ly/Isa59:5) הַזּוּרֶה תִּבָּקַע אֶפְעֶה. **2\.** *be broken into*, of city captured by breaches in walls [2 K 25:4](https://ref.ly/2Kgs25:4) \= [Je 52:7](https://ref.ly/Jer52:7), Ez [30:16](https://ref.ly/Ezek30:16). **Pi.** *Pf.* בִּקֵּעַ [2 K 15:16](https://ref.ly/2Kgs15:16), [Jb 28:10](https://ref.ly/Job28:10), וּבִקַּעְתִּ֫י Ez [13:13](https://ref.ly/Ezek13:13), בִּקֵָּעוּ [Is 59:5](https://ref.ly/Isa59:5); *Impf.* יְבַקַּע [Psalm 78:15](https://ref.ly/Ps78:15), וַיְבַקַּע [Gn 22:3](https://ref.ly/Gen22:3); 3 fs. תְּבַקֵּעַ Ez [13:11](https://ref.ly/Ezek13:11), תְּבַקְּעֵם [Ho 13:8](https://ref.ly/Hos13:8); 2 ms. תְּבַקַּ־ע Hb [3:9](https://ref.ly/Hab3:9), תְּבַקֵָּעַ [2 K 8:12](https://ref.ly/2Kgs8:12); 2 fpl. וַתְּבַקַּעְנָה [2 K 2:24](https://ref.ly/2Kgs2:24); —*cleave, cut to pieces*, or *rend open* (oft. more complete or more violent than Qal), sq. acc., of cleaving wood [Gn 22:3](https://ref.ly/Gen22:3) i.e. cut it up for burning, so [1 S 6:14](https://ref.ly/1Sam6:14); of ripping open pregnant women [2 K 8:12](https://ref.ly/2Kgs8:12), [15:16](https://ref.ly/2Kgs15:16); of tearing in pieces children [2 K 2:24](https://ref.ly/2Kgs2:24); cf. also [Ho 13:8](https://ref.ly/Hos13:8) (fig.); of cleaving open rocks, to bring forth water [Psalm 78:15](https://ref.ly/Ps78:15) (subj. God); of cutting mining\-shafts [Jb 28:10](https://ref.ly/Job28:10) ´בַּצּוּרוֺת יְאֹרִיס בּ; בְהָרוֺת תְּבַקַּע־אָרֶץ Hb [3:9](https://ref.ly/Hab3:9) *into rivers thou cleavest (the) earth; break through* or *down* (a wall, but no obj. expr.), Ez [13:11](https://ref.ly/Ezek13:11) רוּחַ סְעָרוֺת תְּבַקִָּעַ (but Co תִּבָּקֵעַ), cf. v:[13](https://ref.ly/Ezek13:13) וּבִקַּעְתִּי רוּחַ סְעָרוֺת בַחֲמָתִי; of *hatching* eggs [Is 59:5](https://ref.ly/Isa59:5) בֵּיצֵי צִפְעוֺנִי בֵּקִָּעוּ (fig.) **Pu.** *Impf.* יְבָֻקָּעוּ [Ho 14:1](https://ref.ly/Hos14:1); *Pt.* מְבֻקָּעָה Ez [26:10](https://ref.ly/Ezek26:10), מְבֻקָּעִיס [Jos 9:4](https://ref.ly/Josh9:4); —*be ripped open*, of women [Ho 14:1](https://ref.ly/Hos14:1) (vb. of masc. form); *rent*, of old wine\-skins [Jos 9:4](https://ref.ly/Josh9:4); *broken into*, of a city in whose walls a breach has been made Ez [26:10](https://ref.ly/Ezek26:10). **Hiph.** *Impf.* 1 pl. sf. וְנַבְקִעֶנָּה [Is 7:6](https://ref.ly/Isa7:6); *Inf. cstr.* לְהַבְקִיעַ [2 K 3:26](https://ref.ly/2Kgs3:26); —*break into*, sq. sf. ref. to Judah, [Is 7:6](https://ref.ly/Isa7:6) אֵלֵינוּ ´ונב *let us break into it*, lay it open, and so bring it *unto ourselves* (cf. Qal [2 Ch 32:1](https://ref.ly/2Chr32:1)); *break through* (abs.) with sword, שֹׁלֵף חֶרֶבּ לְהַבְקִיעַ אֶל־מֶלֶחאֱדוֺף [2 K 3:26](https://ref.ly/2Kgs3:26). **Hoph.** *Pf.* 3 fs. הָבְקְעָה הָעִיר [Je 39:2](https://ref.ly/Jer39:2) *the city was broken into*, entrance was made by a breach. **Hithp.** *Pf.* הִתְכַּקָּעוּ [Jos 9:13](https://ref.ly/Josh9:13), *Impf.* יִתְבּקָּעוּ [Mi 1:4](https://ref.ly/Mic1:4); —*burst (themselves) open*, of wine\-skins [Jos 9:13](https://ref.ly/Josh9:13); *cleave asunder*, of valleys [Mi 1:4](https://ref.ly/Mic1:4).

> \[Str1235] † בֶּ֫קִַע **n.\[m.]** fraction, half, i.e. half\-shekel, a weight; בֶּקַע מִשְׁקָלוֺ [Gn 24:22](https://ref.ly/Gen24:22), cf. Hesychius in LagGes. Abh 199, 1\. 18 βακαίον \[Lag βέκαον] μέρον τι; v. also בֶּקַע [Ex 38:26](https://ref.ly/Exod38:26) (\= מַחֲצִית הַשֶּׁקֶל).

> \[Str1237] † בִּקְעָה **n.f.** valley (cleft), plain—abs. ´ב [Gn 11:2](https://ref.ly/Gen11:2) \+ 8 t.; cstr. בִּקְעַת [Dt 34:3](https://ref.ly/Deut34:3) \+ 7 t.; pl. בְּקָעוֺת [Is 41:18](https://ref.ly/Isa41:18), [Psalm 104:8](https://ref.ly/Ps104:8); בְּקָעֹת [Dt 11:11](https://ref.ly/Deut11:11); —**1\.** *valley* (opp. הַר mountain) [Dt 8:7](https://ref.ly/Deut8:7), [11:11](https://ref.ly/Deut11:11) cf. Is [41:18](https://ref.ly/Isa41:18); also [63:14](https://ref.ly/Isa63:14) כַּבְּהֵמָה בַּבִּקְעָח תֵרֵד; in creation\-poem [Psalm 104:8](https://ref.ly/Ps104:8) יַעֲלוּ הָרִים יֵרְֽדוּ בְקָעוֺת. **2\.** *plain* (sts. valley\-plain, broad valley) [Gn 11:2](https://ref.ly/Gen11:2); also Ez [3:22](https://ref.ly/Ezek3:22), [3:23](https://ref.ly/Ezek3:23), [8:4](https://ref.ly/Ezek8:4), [37:1](https://ref.ly/Ezek37:1), [37:2](https://ref.ly/Ezek37:2) (´עַל־פְּבֵי הַבּ) as level, opp. הָֽרְכָּ סִים [Is 40:4](https://ref.ly/Isa40:4) (‖מִישׁוֺר); elsewhere cstr. mostly with n. pr. [Dt 34:3](https://ref.ly/Deut34:3) יְרֵחוֺ ´ב (appos. הַכִּכָּר), מִצְפֶה ´ב [Jos 11:8](https://ref.ly/Josh11:8), הַלְּבָנוֺן ´ב [11:17](https://ref.ly/Josh11:17), [12:7](https://ref.ly/Josh12:7), מִגִדּוֺ ´ב [2 Ch 35:22](https://ref.ly/2Chr35:22) cf. [Zc 12:11](https://ref.ly/Zech12:11), אוֺנוֺ ´ב [Ne 6:2](https://ref.ly/Neh6:2); ־אָוֶן´ב [Am 1:5](https://ref.ly/Amos1:5) *plain of idolatry* \= Baalbek (Damascus, acc. to Wetzst in DeJes 3, 702; LXX πέδιον Ὤη).

> \[Str1233] † \[בְּקִיע, בָּקִיעַ] **n.\[m.]** fissure, breach, [Am 6:11](https://ref.ly/Amos6:11) בְּקִעִים, into which the small house is to be smitten (‖רְסִיסִים); בְּקִיעֵי עִיר־דָּוִד [Is 22:9](https://ref.ly/Isa22:9).

## I בָּקַק (id: BDB01415)

\[Str1238] † I. \[בָּקַק] **vb.** be luxuriant (Ar. بَقَّ *be profuse, abundant* (v. esp. Conjj. I. IV, Lane))—**Qal** *Pt.* בֹּאֵא *luxuriant* [Ho 10:1](https://ref.ly/Hos10:1) (fig. of Isr. as vine).

* **Associated Passages:** Hosea 10:1


## II בָּקַק (id: BDB01416)

\[Str1238] † II. \[בָּקַק] **vb.** empty (cf. probably Ar. بَقَّ *make a gurgling noise*, of a mug dipped in water, or emptied of water)—**Qal** *Pf.* וּבַקֹּתִ֫י [Je 19:7](https://ref.ly/Jer19:7), בָּֽקֲקוּ [Na 2:3](https://ref.ly/Nah2:3); *Pt.* בּוֺקֵק [Is 24:1](https://ref.ly/Isa24:1), בִּקֲקִים [Na 2:3](https://ref.ly/Nah2:3); *empty, lay waste* land, acc., [Is 24:1](https://ref.ly/Isa24:1), [Na 2:3](https://ref.ly/Nah2:3), also abs. v:[3](https://ref.ly/Nah2:3); fig. *make void* (obj. עֵעֶה) [Je 19:7](https://ref.ly/Jer19:7). **Niph.** *Pf.* וְנָֽבְקָה (cf. Ges§ 67, R. 11\) [Is 19:3](https://ref.ly/Isa19:3); *Impf.* 3 fs. תִּבּוֺק [Is 24:3](https://ref.ly/Isa24:3); *Inf. abs.* הִבּוֺק [Is 24:3](https://ref.ly/Isa24:3); —*be emptied* (laid waste) [Is 24:3](https://ref.ly/Isa24:3) הִבּוֺק תִבּוֺק, of land (‖הִבּוֺז יִבּוֺו); fig. of spirit, courage [Is 19:3](https://ref.ly/Isa19:3). **Po.** *Impf.* יְבֹקֲקוּ [Je 51:2](https://ref.ly/Jer51:2) *empty out* (devastate) land.

> \[Str1228] † בַּקְבֻּק **n.\[m.]** flask (from gurgling sound of emptyIng, cf. Ar. بَقْبَقَةٌ *gurgling sound;* also Syr. ܒܰܓܒܽܘܓܳܐ, *cantharus*, etc.)—abs. בַּקְבֻּק [Je 19:10](https://ref.ly/Jer19:10); cstr. *id.* [1 K 14:3](https://ref.ly/1Kgs14:3), [Je 19:1](https://ref.ly/Jer19:1).

> \[Str1227] † בַּקְבּוּק **n.pr.m.** head of a family of Nethinim; ´בני־ב [Ezr 2:51](https://ref.ly/Ezra2:51), [Ne 7:53](https://ref.ly/Neh7:53).

> \[Str1229] † בַּקְבֻּקְיָה **n.pr.m.** a Levite [Ne 11:17](https://ref.ly/Neh11:17), [12:9](https://ref.ly/Neh12:9), [12:25](https://ref.ly/Neh12:25).

> \[Str2999] † יַבֹּק **n.pr.fl.** (√ prob. בקק; so Thes after Simonis, Sam. Di) יבק(ה)מַעֲבַר [Gn 32:23](https://ref.ly/Gen32:23) (where perhaps connected with אבק \= יאבק); ´נַ֫חַל יַבּ [Dt 2:37](https://ref.ly/Deut2:37), הַנַּחַל ´יַבּ [Dt 3:16](https://ref.ly/Deut3:16), [Jos 12:2](https://ref.ly/Josh12:2), יַבֹּק [Nu 21:24](https://ref.ly/Num21:24) (‖אַרְנֹּן, cf. [Dt 3:16](https://ref.ly/Deut3:16), [Jos 12:2](https://ref.ly/Josh12:2)), הַיַּבֹּק Ju [11:13](https://ref.ly/Judg11:13), [11:22](https://ref.ly/Judg11:22) (in both ‖ארנון); it empties into Jordan from East, in latitude of Shechem; called (southern) boundary of Ammon [Dt 3:16](https://ref.ly/Deut3:16), and (northern) of Amorites [Jos 12:2](https://ref.ly/Josh12:2); but some confusion (Di [Nu 21:24](https://ref.ly/Num21:24), [Dt 2:37](https://ref.ly/Deut2:37));—mod. *Wady Zerqa*, BDPal:181\.

* **Associated Passages:** Deuteronomy 2:37; 1 Kings 14:3; Numbers 21:24; Nehemiah 12:25; Jeremiah 19:10; Nehemiah 7:53; Genesis 32:23; Isaiah 24:3; Nehemiah 11:17; Nahum 2:3; Jeremiah 19:1; Jeremiah 19:7; Ezra 2:51; Isaiah 19:3; Deuteronomy 3:16; Judges 11:13; Jeremiah 51:2; Nehemiah 12:9; Isaiah 24:1; Joshua 12:2; Judges 11:22


## בָּקַר (id: BDB01421)

\[Str1239] † \[בָּקַר] **vb.** inquire, seek (NH *id.,* Aram. בְּקַר, ܒܩܰܪ; also Eth. በቀለ፡ in deriv.; orig. *divide discern,* cf. Ar. بَقَرَ *slit, rip, split*)—only **Pi.** *Pf.* 1 S. sf. וּבִקַּרְתִּים consec. Ez [34:11](https://ref.ly/Ezek34:11); *Impf.* יְבַקֵּר [Lv 13:36](https://ref.ly/Lev13:36), [27:33](https://ref.ly/Lev27:33), אֲבַקֵּר Ez [34:12](https://ref.ly/Ezek34:12); *Inf. cstr.* לְבַקֵּר [2 K 16:15](https://ref.ly/2Kgs16:15) \+ 2 t.;—*seek, look for,* sq. לְ [Lv 13:36](https://ref.ly/Lev13:36); *seek* (to distinguish) sq. בֵּין־טוֺב לָרַע [Lv. 27:33](https://ref.ly/Lev27:33); *seek* (in order to care for) sq. acc. צאֹן Ez [34:11](https://ref.ly/Ezek34:11) (‖דרשׁ) v:[12](https://ref.ly/Ezek34:12), fig. of ´י seeking his people; *contemplate,* sq. בְּ [Psalm 27:4](https://ref.ly/Ps27:4) (‖חָזָה בְּ);*consider, reflect,* abs. [Pr 20:25](https://ref.ly/Prov20:25) אַחַר נְדָרִים i.e. whether the vows were wise, or should be kept (cf. Str ad loc. \& reff.); cf. perh. [2 K 16:15](https://ref.ly/2Kgs16:15) *consider* (what shall be done with the old altar); *look at* Klo, (so לְבַקֵּר [1 K 3:21](https://ref.ly/1Kgs3:21) for 2nd בַּבֹּקֶר), AV RV *to inquire by,* SyrVer *for praying;* perh. denoting some religious service to be performed by king himself, cf. esp. RS Sem. i. 467\.

> \[Str1241] בָּקָר:182 **n.m.**:[1 K 5:3](https://ref.ly/1Kgs5:3) \+ (**f.**:[Gn 33:16](https://ref.ly/Gen33:16); [Jb 1:14](https://ref.ly/Job1:14) cf. [Dt 32:14](https://ref.ly/Deut32:14), [2 S 17:29](https://ref.ly/2Sam17:29)) cattle, herd, ox (Ar. بَقَرٌ, Aram. בַּקְרָא, בַּקְרְתָא, ܒܰܩܪܳܐ (cf. also HomNS 222 ff.); name from *ploughing,* so Thes LagBN 51 al.)—abs. ´בּ [Gn 12:16](https://ref.ly/Gen12:16) \+; cstr. בְּקַר [Nu 7:88](https://ref.ly/Num7:88); sf. בְּקָֽרְךָ [Gn 45:10](https://ref.ly/Gen45:10) \+; בְּקָרֶָךָ [Ex 20:24](https://ref.ly/Exod20:24), [Je 5:17](https://ref.ly/Jer5:17); בְּקָרוֺ [1 S 11:7](https://ref.ly/1Sam11:7), [2 S 12:4](https://ref.ly/2Sam12:4); בְּקַרְכֶם [Dt 12:6](https://ref.ly/Deut12:6); בְּקָרָם [Je 3:24](https://ref.ly/Jer3:24) \+; pl. בְּקָרִים [Am 6:12](https://ref.ly/Amos6:12) (al. rd. בקר ים) [2 Ch 4:3](https://ref.ly/2Chr4:3) (but rd. פְּקָעִים v. ‖ [1 K 7:24](https://ref.ly/1Kgs7:24) infr.); sf. בְּקָרֵינוּ [Ne 10:37](https://ref.ly/Neh10:37); rd. בקריכם for בחוריכם [1 S 8:16](https://ref.ly/1Sam8:16) LXX We Dr;—**1\.** mostly coll. **a.** *cattle,* generic (never pl. in form) [Gn 12:6](https://ref.ly/Gen12:6), [13:5](https://ref.ly/Gen13:5), [20:14](https://ref.ly/Gen20:14), [21:27](https://ref.ly/Gen21:27), [24:35](https://ref.ly/Gen24:35), [Lv 1:2](https://ref.ly/Lev1:2), [Dt 8:13](https://ref.ly/Deut8:13), [1 S 11:5](https://ref.ly/1Sam11:5), [Ho 5:6](https://ref.ly/Hos5:6), [Jo 1:18](https://ref.ly/Joel1:18), [1 Ch 27:29](https://ref.ly/1Chr27:29), [27:29](https://ref.ly/1Chr27:29) \+ oft. (frequently ‖צאֹן); as grazing, [1 Ch 27:29](https://ref.ly/1Chr27:29) \+; in sim. ´כַּבּ [Jb 40:15](https://ref.ly/Job40:15), [Is 11:7](https://ref.ly/Isa11:7), [65:25](https://ref.ly/Isa65:25); as lowing (´קוֺל הַבּ) [1 S 15:14](https://ref.ly/1Sam15:14); מִקְנֵה בָקָר *possession of* (i.e. property in) cattle [Gn 6:14](https://ref.ly/Gen6:14), [47:17](https://ref.ly/Gen47:17) (both J); ´עֶדְרֵי ב [Jo 1:18](https://ref.ly/Joel1:18) *herds of cattle;* esp. בֶּן־בָּקָר *son of cattle* (i.e. *belonging to the* בקר), to denote a single ox, calf, etc.; as used for food [Gn 18:7](https://ref.ly/Gen18:7) cf. v:[8](https://ref.ly/Gen18:8) (J);—in these prob. \= *calf* (v. also [1 S 14:32](https://ref.ly/1Sam14:32) בָּקָר וּבְנֵי בָקָר); usually for sacrifice (Hex only P) [Nu 15:8](https://ref.ly/Num15:8), [15:9](https://ref.ly/Num15:9); ´בֶּן־הַבּ [Lv 1:5](https://ref.ly/Lev1:5); appos. עֵגֶל †[Lv 9:2](https://ref.ly/Lev9:2); —cf. עֶגְלַת בָּקָר †[Dt 21:3](https://ref.ly/Deut21:3), [1 S 16:2](https://ref.ly/1Sam16:2), [Is 7:21](https://ref.ly/Isa7:21); —mostly appos. פַּר [Ex 29:1](https://ref.ly/Exod29:1) \+ 27 t.; also pl. † פָּרִיםַ בני בקר [Nu 28:11](https://ref.ly/Num28:11), [28:19](https://ref.ly/Num28:19), [28:27](https://ref.ly/Num28:27), [29:13](https://ref.ly/Num29:13), [29:17](https://ref.ly/Num29:17) (on all these cf. בֵּן); also indef. *cattle, oxen*, of a number not specified [Nu 7:87](https://ref.ly/Num7:87), [7:88](https://ref.ly/Num7:88) (in both enumerated as פָּרִים); [22:40](https://ref.ly/Num22:40), [1 S 14:32](https://ref.ly/1Sam14:32), [15:21](https://ref.ly/1Sam15:21), [1 K 1:9](https://ref.ly/1Kgs1:9), [7:29](https://ref.ly/1Kgs7:29), [7:29](https://ref.ly/1Kgs7:29) (here of graven work) [8:5](https://ref.ly/1Kgs8:5), [1 Ch 12:40](https://ref.ly/1Chr12:40), [2 Ch 5:6](https://ref.ly/2Chr5:6), [18:2](https://ref.ly/2Chr18:2), [Is 22:13](https://ref.ly/Isa22:13), [Psalm 66:15](https://ref.ly/Ps66:15); also as beasts of burden † [1 Ch 12:40](https://ref.ly/1Chr12:40). **b.** a particulur *herd* of cattle [Gn 18:7](https://ref.ly/Gen18:7) (J); cf. pl. בְּקָרֵינוּ, *our herds,* only [Ne 10:37](https://ref.ly/Neh10:37). **2\.** more individually, *head of cattle,*—yet alw. of more than one (Hex mostly P; pl. only [Am 6:12](https://ref.ly/Amos6:12) \+ [2 Ch 4:3](https://ref.ly/2Chr4:3) v. supr.); of two [Nu 7:17](https://ref.ly/Num7:17) \+ 11 t. Nu 7; † cf. also ´צֶמֶד ב *yoke* (pair) *of oxen* [1 S 11:7](https://ref.ly/1Sam11:7) cf. v:[7](https://ref.ly/1Sam11:7), [1 K 19:21](https://ref.ly/1Kgs19:21) cf. v:[21](https://ref.ly/1Kgs19:21) (ploughing, cf. v:[19](https://ref.ly/1Kgs19:19)); also [2 S 6:6](https://ref.ly/2Sam6:6) \= [1 Ch 13:9](https://ref.ly/1Chr13:9) (drawing a cart, cf. [Nu 7:3](https://ref.ly/Num7:3) f. > \[Str1248] † I. בַּר **n.m.** son (Aram. בַּר, Syr. ܒܰܪ) only in late Heb. of [Pr 31:2](https://ref.ly/Prov31:2), [31:2](https://ref.ly/Prov31:2) (both cstr.), v:[2](https://ref.ly/Prov31:2) sf. בְּרִי; נשׁקו בר [Psalm 2:12](https://ref.ly/Ps2:12) *kiss the son*, SyrVer Modern Vrss Ges De Pe et al.; *receive instruction* Targum; δράξασθε παιδείας LXX; *apprehendite disciplinam* Vulgate Ew; *kiss purely, do sincere homage* Aq Sym Jer BrMP 136; emend. Lag (מוֺסָרוֺ)נַשְּׁקוּ מוֺסֵרוֺ *put on his bonds* (cf. v:[3](https://ref.ly/Ps2:3)) so Kmp CheOP 351\.

> \[Str1249–50] II. בַּר corn, III. בַּר pure, בָּר, I. II. בֹּר v. ברר.

## I בָּרָא (id: BDB01431)

\[Str1254] † I. בָּרָא:53 **vb.** shape, create (cf. Ar. ܒܪܝ, *form, fashion by cutting, shape out, pare a reed for writing, a stick for an arrow,* but also بَرَى, *create;* Ph. הברא CISi. 347 *incisor, a trade involving cutting;* As. *barû, make, create*, COTGloss \& Hpt KAT2 Gloss 1 but dub.; Sab. ברא *found, build*, DHMZMG 1883, 413, synon. בנה; BaZA. 1888, 58, comp. As. *banû, create, beget*, with change of liquid; Aram. בְּרָא, ܒܪܳܐ, *create*)—**Qal** *Pf.* [Gn 1:1](https://ref.ly/Gen1:1) \+ 19 t.; *Impf.* יִבְרָא [Gn 1:21](https://ref.ly/Gen1:21), [1:27](https://ref.ly/Gen1:27), [Nu 16:30](https://ref.ly/Num16:30); *Inf.* בְּראֹ [Gn 5:1](https://ref.ly/Gen5:1); *Imv.* בְּרָא [Psalm 51:12](https://ref.ly/Ps51:12); *Pt.* בּוֺרֵא [Is 42:5](https://ref.ly/Isa42:5) \+ 10 t.; sf. בֹּרַאֲךָ [Is 43:1](https://ref.ly/Isa43:1); בּוֺרְאֶיךָ [Ec 12:1](https://ref.ly/Eccl12:1); —*shape, fashion, create*, always of divine activity, with acc. rei, seldom except in P and Is:2. **1\.** obj. heaven and earth [Gn 1:1](https://ref.ly/Gen1:1), [2:3](https://ref.ly/Gen2:3) (P) [Is 45:18](https://ref.ly/Isa45:18), [45:18](https://ref.ly/Isa45:18); mankind [Gn 1:27](https://ref.ly/Gen1:27), [1:27](https://ref.ly/Gen1:27), [1:27](https://ref.ly/Gen1:27), [5:1](https://ref.ly/Gen5:1), [5:2](https://ref.ly/Gen5:2) (P) [6:7](https://ref.ly/Gen6:7) (J) [Dt 4:32](https://ref.ly/Deut4:32), [Psalm 89:48](https://ref.ly/Ps89:48), [Is 45:12](https://ref.ly/Isa45:12); the host of heaven [Is 40:26](https://ref.ly/Isa40:26); heavens [Is 42:5](https://ref.ly/Isa42:5); ends of the earth [Is 40:28](https://ref.ly/Isa40:28); north and south [Psalm 89:13](https://ref.ly/Ps89:13); wind [Am 4:13](https://ref.ly/Amos4:13); the תנינים [Gn 1:21](https://ref.ly/Gen1:21) (P). **2\.** the individual man [Mal 2:10](https://ref.ly/Mal2:10) (‖ father) [Ec 12:1](https://ref.ly/Eccl12:1); the smith and the waster [Is 54:16](https://ref.ly/Isa54:16), [54:16](https://ref.ly/Isa54:16); Israel as a nation [Is 43:15](https://ref.ly/Isa43:15); Jacob [Is 43:1](https://ref.ly/Isa43:1); the seed of Israel [Is 43:7](https://ref.ly/Isa43:7). **3\.** new conditions and circumstances: righteousness and salvation [Is 45:8](https://ref.ly/Isa45:8); darkness and evil [Is 45:7](https://ref.ly/Isa45:7); fruit of the lips [Is 57:19](https://ref.ly/Isa57:19); a new thing חֲדָשָׁה (a woman encompassing a man) [Je 31:22](https://ref.ly/Jer31:22); בְּרִיאָה (swallowing up the Korahites) [Nu 16:30](https://ref.ly/Num16:30) (J); cloud and flame over Zion [Is 4:5](https://ref.ly/Isa4:5). **4\.** of transformations: a clean heart [Psalm 51:12](https://ref.ly/Ps51:12) (‖חִדֵּשׁ); new heaven and earth [Is 65:17](https://ref.ly/Isa65:17) (in place of old); transformation of nature [Is 41:20](https://ref.ly/Isa41:20); with double acc. בורא ירושׁלם גילה *transform Jerusalem into rejoicing* [Is 65:18](https://ref.ly/Isa65:18). **Niph.** *Pf.* 2 fs. נִבְרֵאתְ Ez [21:35](https://ref.ly/Ezek21:30); 3 pl. נִבְרְאוּ [Ex 34:10](https://ref.ly/Exod34:10) \+ 2 t.; *Impf.* יִבָּרֵאָוּן [Psalm 104:30](https://ref.ly/Ps104:30); *Inf.* sf. הִבָּרַאֲךָ Ez [28:13](https://ref.ly/Ezek28:13); הִבָּֽרְאָךְ Ez [28:15](https://ref.ly/Ezek28:15); הִבָּֽרְאָם [Gn 2:4](https://ref.ly/Gen2:4), [5:2](https://ref.ly/Gen5:2); *Pt.* נִבְרָא [Psalm 102:19](https://ref.ly/Ps102:19); —*Pass.* **1\.** *be created:* heaven and earth [Gn 2:4](https://ref.ly/Gen2:4) (P); creatures [Psalm 104:30](https://ref.ly/Ps104:30); mankind [Gn 5:2](https://ref.ly/Gen5:2) (P); heavens [Psalm 148:5](https://ref.ly/Ps148:5). **2\.** with reference to birth: במקום אשׁר נבראת *in the place where thou wast created* (i.e. native land) Ez [21:35](https://ref.ly/Ezek21:30); יום הבראך *day when thou wast created* (king of Tyre) Ez [28:13](https://ref.ly/Ezek28:13), [28:15](https://ref.ly/Ezek28:15) (cf. היום ילדתיך [Psalm 2:7](https://ref.ly/Ps2:7)); עַם נִבְרָא [Psalm 102:19](https://ref.ly/Ps102:19) (‖עַם נוֺלָד [Psalm 22:32](https://ref.ly/Ps22:32)). **3\.** of something new, astonishing: miracles [Ex 34:10](https://ref.ly/Exod34:10) (J); new things, חדשׁות [Is 48:6](https://ref.ly/Isa48:6). **Pi.** *Pf.* בֵּרֵאתָ [Jos 17:15](https://ref.ly/Josh17:15); בֵּרֵאתוֺ [Jos 17:18](https://ref.ly/Josh17:18); *Inf.* abs. בָּרֵא Ez [21:24](https://ref.ly/Ezek21:24), [21:24](https://ref.ly/Ezek21:24), [23:47](https://ref.ly/Ezek23:47); —**1\.** *cut down:* a forest [Jos 17:15](https://ref.ly/Josh17:15), [17:18](https://ref.ly/Josh17:18) (J); וּבָרֵא אוֺתְהֶן בְּחַרְבוֺתָם Ez [23:47](https://ref.ly/Ezek23:47). **2\.** *cut out:* יָד hand, as an index Ez [21:24](https://ref.ly/Ezek21:24), [21:24](https://ref.ly/Ezek21:24).

135:a. I. בָּרָא: Ar. بَرَأَ prob. loan\-word.

> \[Str1278] † בְּרִיאָה **n.f.** a creation, thing created, as preternatural, unparalleled; acc. cogn. ´אִם־ב ´יִבְרָא י [Nu 16:30](https://ref.ly/Num16:30), cf. בָּרָא **Qal 3, Niph. 3\.**

> \[Str1256] † בְּרָאיָה **n.pr.m.** (´י *hath created*) a Benjamite [1 Ch 8:21](https://ref.ly/1Chr8:21).

* **Associated Passages:** Joshua 17:15; Isaiah 45:8; Isaiah 45:18; Numbers 16:30; Psalms 89:13; Ezekiel 28:13; Psalms 148:5; Isaiah 48:6; Genesis 1:1; Isaiah 40:26; Genesis 6:7; Ezekiel 23:47; Isaiah 45:12; Ezekiel 28:15; Malachi 2:10; Joshua 17:18; Isaiah 43:7; Psalms 89:48; Isaiah 43:15; Genesis 1:21; Isaiah 45:7; Psalms 104:30; Isaiah 57:19; Isaiah 65:18; Amos 4:13; Genesis 5:2; Genesis 2:4; 1 Chronicles 8:21; Isaiah 4:5; Jeremiah 31:22; Psalms 51:12; Isaiah 43:1; Isaiah 65:17; Isaiah 42:5; Genesis 1:27; Deuteronomy 4:32; Exodus 34:10; Ezekiel 21:24; Psalms 2:7; Genesis 2:3; Psalms 102:19; Ecclesiastes 12:1; Isaiah 41:20; Isaiah 54:16; Ezekiel 21:30; Psalms 22:32; Genesis 5:1; Isaiah 40:28


## II בָּרָא (id: BDB01434)

\[Str1254] † II. \[בָּרָא] **vb.** be fat (Ar. بَرِئَ *be free of a thing, sound, healthy;* v. מרא)—**Hiph.** *Inf.* לְהַבְרִיאֲכֶם [1 S 2:29](https://ref.ly/1Sam2:29) *to make yourselves fat*.

> \[Str1277,1274,1279] † בָּרִיא **adj.** fat—[Psalm 73:4](https://ref.ly/Ps73:4), Ju [3:17](https://ref.ly/Judg3:17); pl. בְּרִיאִים [1 K 5:3](https://ref.ly/1Kgs5:3); cstr. בְּרִיאֵי [Dn 1:15](https://ref.ly/Dan1:15); f. בְּרִיאָה Hb [1:16](https://ref.ly/Hab1:16) \+ 2 t. \+ Ez [34:20](https://ref.ly/Ezek34:20) בִּרְיָה; Hi Ol:§ 171 a בְּרִיָּה, \< בריאה; pl. בְּרִיאוֺת [Gn 41:5](https://ref.ly/Gen41:5) \+ 3 t.; בְּרִיאֹת [Gn 41:2](https://ref.ly/Gen41:2), [41:4](https://ref.ly/Gen41:4); —*fat*, cattle בקר [Gn 41:2](https://ref.ly/Gen41:2), [41:4](https://ref.ly/Gen41:4), [41:5](https://ref.ly/Gen41:5), [41:7](https://ref.ly/Gen41:7), [41:18](https://ref.ly/Gen41:18), [41:20](https://ref.ly/Gen41:20) (E) [1 K 5:3](https://ref.ly/1Kgs5:3); sheep Ez [34:3](https://ref.ly/Ezek34:3), [34:20](https://ref.ly/Ezek34:20), [Zc 11:16](https://ref.ly/Zech11:16); food Hb [1:16](https://ref.ly/Hab1:16); ´אישׁ ב *fat man* Ju [3:17](https://ref.ly/Judg3:17); בריאי בשר [Dn 1:15](https://ref.ly/Dan1:15); אולם ´ב *their body fat* [Psalm 73:4](https://ref.ly/Ps73:4).

> \[Str1255] בְּרֹאדַךְ בַּלְאֲדָן **n.pr.m.** v. sub מְרֹדַךְ. p. 597

> \[Str1011] בִּרְאִי v. בית בראי sub בית

> \[Str1257] בַּרְבֻּרִים v. sub ברר. p. 141

## ברד (id: BDB01439)

ברד (cf. Ar. بَرُدَ *be* or *become cold*).

[Psalm 78:47](https://ref.ly/Ps78:47), [78:48](https://ref.ly/Ps78:48), [105:32](https://ref.ly/Ps105:32); another great hailstorm [Jos 10:11](https://ref.ly/Josh10:11) (E), where ´אַבְנֵי הַבּ; further, in theoph. [Psalm 18:13](https://ref.ly/Ps18:13) (‖גחלי־אשׁ) [2 S 22:13](https://ref.ly/2Sam22:13) om. by error; del. however in [Psalm 18:14](https://ref.ly/Ps18:14) cf. De Che etc.; ´אוצרות ב [Jb 38:22](https://ref.ly/Job38:22), שׁלג וקיטור ´אשׁ וב [Psalm 148:8](https://ref.ly/Ps148:8), ´ירקון וב [Hg 2:17](https://ref.ly/Hag2:17); in sim. [Is 28:2](https://ref.ly/Isa28:2) שׁער ´זרם ב; fig. [Is 28:17](https://ref.ly/Isa28:17), [30:30](https://ref.ly/Isa30:30) (´אבן ב)—fig. of judgment of ´י.

LagBN 29; Syr. ܒܳܪܕܳܐ, i.e. *grandinatus, grêlê*, PS), mpl. of sheep \& goats עֲקֻדִּים נְקֻדִּים וּבְרֻדִּים [Gn 31:10](https://ref.ly/Gen31:10), [31:12](https://ref.ly/Gen31:12); horses [Zc 6:3](https://ref.ly/Zech6:3), [6:6](https://ref.ly/Zech6:6).

## I בָּרָה (id: BDB01444)

ZimBP 31\)—**Qal** *Pf.* [2 S 12:17](https://ref.ly/2Sam12:17); *Impf.* אֶבְרֶה [2 S 13:6](https://ref.ly/2Sam13:6), [13:10](https://ref.ly/2Sam13:10) *eat bread* (בְּרוּ־לְכֶם [1 S 17:8](https://ref.ly/1Sam17:8) scribal error for בחרו לכם [1 K 18:25](https://ref.ly/1Kgs18:25) DrSm 107\). **Pi.** *Inf.* לְבָרוֺת [La 4:10](https://ref.ly/Lam4:10) *for devouring*. **Hiph.** *Impf.* תַּבְרֵנִי [2 S 13:5](https://ref.ly/2Sam13:5); *Inf.* לְהַבְרוֺת [2 S 3:35](https://ref.ly/2Sam3:35) *cause to eat bread*.

## II ברה (id: BDB01447)

II. ברה (cf. As. *barû, bind*, whence *birîtu, fetter* ZimBP 59, 82, \& *treaty, covenant* DlK 7\).

> \[Str1285] בְּרִית:285 **n.f.** covenant (‖ Aram. קְיָם, διαθήκη; *constitutio*)—´ב [Gn 9:13](https://ref.ly/Gen9:13) \+ 199 t.; sf. בְּרִיתִי [Gn 6:18](https://ref.ly/Gen6:18) \+ 50 t.; בְּרִיתְךָ [Dt 33:9](https://ref.ly/Deut33:9); בְּרִיתֶָךָ [Psalm 44:18](https://ref.ly/Ps44:18) \+ 11 t.; בְּרִיתְכֶם [Is 28:18](https://ref.ly/Isa28:18); בְּרִיתֵךְ Ez [16:61](https://ref.ly/Ezek16:61); בְּרִיתוֺ [Ex 2:24](https://ref.ly/Exod2:24) \= 18 t.;—*pact, compact, covenant*. **I.** *between men*. **1\.** *trety, alliance, league:* Abraham and Amorites [Gn 14:13](https://ref.ly/Gen14:13); Edom and its allies Ob:[7](https://ref.ly/Obad1:7); with Philistines [Gn 21:27](https://ref.ly/Gen21:27), [21:32](https://ref.ly/Gen21:32) (E) [26:28](https://ref.ly/Gen26:28) (J); Jacob and Laban [Gn 31:44](https://ref.ly/Gen31:44) (J); Joshua and Gibeonites [Jos 9:6](https://ref.ly/Josh9:6), [9:7](https://ref.ly/Josh9:7), [9:11](https://ref.ly/Josh9:11), [9:15](https://ref.ly/Josh9:15), [9:16](https://ref.ly/Josh9:16) (J); Israel and Canaanites [Ex 23:32](https://ref.ly/Exod23:32), [34:12](https://ref.ly/Exod34:12), [34:15](https://ref.ly/Exod34:15) (JE) [Dt 7:2](https://ref.ly/Deut7:2), Ju [2:2](https://ref.ly/Judg2:2); Ammonites and Jabesh [1 S 11:1](https://ref.ly/1Sam11:1); Solomon and Hiram [1 K 5:26](https://ref.ly/1Kgs5:26); Ahab and Benhadad [1 K 20:34](https://ref.ly/1Kgs20:34); Syria and Israel [1 K 15:19](https://ref.ly/1Kgs15:19) \= [2 Ch 16:3](https://ref.ly/2Chr16:3); Nebuchadnezzar and Zedekiah Ez [17:13–19](https://ref.ly/Ezek17:13-Ezek17:19); nations against Israel [Psalm 83:6](https://ref.ly/Ps83:6); nations with Egypt Ez [30:5](https://ref.ly/Ezek30:5); Ephraim and Assyria [Ho 12:2](https://ref.ly/Hos12:2); Judah and Israel Ez [16:61](https://ref.ly/Ezek16:61); Judah and Tyre [Am 1:9](https://ref.ly/Amos1:9); Assyria and Judah [Is 33:8](https://ref.ly/Isa33:8); נגיד ברית *a prince in league* (with him) [Dn 11:22](https://ref.ly/Dan11:22) (so He Ew; Hi Meinh Bev ref. to h. p. Onias III, \& translate *prince of cov't*, cf. AV RV); fig., with death [Is 28:15](https://ref.ly/Isa28:15), [28:18](https://ref.ly/Isa28:18); with stones of the field [Jb 5:23](https://ref.ly/Job5:23). **2\.** *constitution, ordinance*, between monarch and subjects: David and Abner [2 S 3:12](https://ref.ly/2Sam3:12), [3:13](https://ref.ly/2Sam3:13), [3:21](https://ref.ly/2Sam3:21); David and the elders of Israel [2 S 5:3](https://ref.ly/2Sam5:3) \= [1 Ch 11:3](https://ref.ly/1Chr11:3); Zedekiah and his people [Je 34:8–18](https://ref.ly/Jer34:8-Jer34:18); hostile prince and Israelites [Dn 9:27](https://ref.ly/Dan9:27). **3\.** *agreement, pledge:* Jehoiada and captains [2 K 11:4](https://ref.ly/2Kgs11:4) \= [2 Ch 23:1](https://ref.ly/2Chr23:1); with onself [Jb 31:1](https://ref.ly/Job31:1); with Leviathan to be a servant [Jb 40:28](https://ref.ly/Job40:28); between man and man [Ho 10:4](https://ref.ly/Hos10:4) cf. בעל ברית Ju [8:33](https://ref.ly/Judg8:33), [9:4](https://ref.ly/Judg9:4) \= אל ברית Ju [9:46](https://ref.ly/Judg9:46). **4\.** *alliance* of friendship between David and Jonathan [1 S 18:3](https://ref.ly/1Sam18:3), [20:8](https://ref.ly/1Sam20:8), [23:18](https://ref.ly/1Sam23:18) cf. [Psalm 55:21](https://ref.ly/Ps55:21). **5\.** *alliance* of marriage [Pr 2:17](https://ref.ly/Prov2:17), [Mal 2:14](https://ref.ly/Mal2:14).—In all cases כרת ברית is the technical phrase for making covenant except [Je 34:10](https://ref.ly/Jer34:10) באו בברית; [Dn 9:27](https://ref.ly/Dan9:27) הגביר ברית לְ. Various preps. are used, most oft. לְ [Ex 23:32](https://ref.ly/Exod23:32) \+, but also עִם [Gn 26:28](https://ref.ly/Gen26:28) \+, אֵת [2 S 3:12](https://ref.ly/2Sam3:12) \+, בֵּין [2 Ch 16:3](https://ref.ly/2Chr16:3). **II.** *between God and man*. **1\.** *alliance* of friendship (‖סוֺד) [Psalm 25:14](https://ref.ly/Ps25:14). **2\.** *covenant*, as a divine constitution or ordinance with signs or pledges (vid. אוֺת). **a.** *with Noah* [Gn 9:9–17](https://ref.ly/Gen9:9-Gen9:17) (P) [Is 54:10](https://ref.ly/Isa54:10), [Je 33:20](https://ref.ly/Jer33:20), [33:25](https://ref.ly/Jer33:25); a divine promise that there would be no other deluge. **b.** *with Abraham, Isaac and Jacob* [Gn 15:18](https://ref.ly/Gen15:18) (J) [17:2–21](https://ref.ly/Gen17:2-Gen17:21), [Ex 2:24](https://ref.ly/Exod2:24), [6:4](https://ref.ly/Exod6:4), [6:5](https://ref.ly/Exod6:5), [Lv 26:42](https://ref.ly/Lev26:42) (P) [2 K 13:23](https://ref.ly/2Kgs13:23), [1 Ch 16:15](https://ref.ly/1Chr16:15) \= [Psalm 105:8](https://ref.ly/Ps105:8), [105:10](https://ref.ly/Ps105:10), [Ne 9:8](https://ref.ly/Neh9:8), [Je 34:18](https://ref.ly/Jer34:18); a promise to multiply their seed, give them the land of Canaan, and make them a blessing to the nations. **c.** *with Israel at Sinai* \= Horeb, with a covenant sacrifice [Ex 19:5](https://ref.ly/Exod19:5), [24:7](https://ref.ly/Exod24:7), [24:8](https://ref.ly/Exod24:8) (E) [34:10](https://ref.ly/Exod34:10), [34:27](https://ref.ly/Exod34:27), [34:28](https://ref.ly/Exod34:28) (J) [31:16](https://ref.ly/Exod31:16), [Lv 2:13](https://ref.ly/Lev2:13) (P) [24:8](https://ref.ly/Lev24:8), [26:9](https://ref.ly/Lev26:9), [26:15](https://ref.ly/Lev26:15), [26:25](https://ref.ly/Lev26:25), [26:44](https://ref.ly/Lev26:44), [26:45](https://ref.ly/Lev26:45) (H) [Dt 4:13](https://ref.ly/Deut4:13); renewed in plains of Moab [Dt 28:69](https://ref.ly/Deut28:69); with blessings and curses [Dt 29:20](https://ref.ly/Deut29:20); frequently referred to in other books [2 Ch 34:32](https://ref.ly/2Chr34:32), [Psalm 25:10](https://ref.ly/Ps25:10), [44:18](https://ref.ly/Ps44:18), [50:5](https://ref.ly/Ps50:5), [50:16](https://ref.ly/Ps50:16), [74:20](https://ref.ly/Ps74:20), [78:10](https://ref.ly/Ps78:10), [78:37](https://ref.ly/Ps78:37), [103:18](https://ref.ly/Ps103:18), [106:45](https://ref.ly/Ps106:45), [111:5](https://ref.ly/Ps111:5), [111:9](https://ref.ly/Ps111:9), [Is 56:4](https://ref.ly/Isa56:4), [56:6](https://ref.ly/Isa56:6), [Je 1:2](https://ref.ly/Jer1:2), [1:3](https://ref.ly/Jer1:3), [1:6](https://ref.ly/Jer1:6), [1:8](https://ref.ly/Jer1:8), [1:10](https://ref.ly/Jer1:10), [14:21](https://ref.ly/Jer14:21), [22:9](https://ref.ly/Jer22:9), [31:32](https://ref.ly/Jer31:32), Ez [16:8](https://ref.ly/Ezek16:8), [16:59](https://ref.ly/Ezek16:59), [16:60](https://ref.ly/Ezek16:60), [44:7](https://ref.ly/Ezek44:7), [Dn 9:4](https://ref.ly/Dan9:4), [11:28](https://ref.ly/Dan11:28), [11:30](https://ref.ly/Dan11:30), [11:32](https://ref.ly/Dan11:32), [Ho 6:7](https://ref.ly/Hos6:7), [8:1](https://ref.ly/Hos8:1), [Zc 9:11](https://ref.ly/Zech9:11), [11:10](https://ref.ly/Zech11:10); a divine constitution given to Israel with promises on condition of obedience and penalties for disobendience, in the form of tables of the covenant [Dt 9:9](https://ref.ly/Deut9:9), [9:11](https://ref.ly/Deut9:11), [9:15](https://ref.ly/Deut9:15), inscribed with the ten words, placed in (´י)ארון ברית the ark of the covenant [Nu 10:33](https://ref.ly/Num10:33) \+ 40 t. (vid. ארון; in [1 S 4:3](https://ref.ly/1Sam4:3), [4:4](https://ref.ly/1Sam4:4), [4:4](https://ref.ly/1Sam4:4), [4:5](https://ref.ly/1Sam4:5) om. ברית after ארון LXX We Dr); set forth in דברי הברית *words of the covenant* [Ex 34:28](https://ref.ly/Exod34:28) (J) [Dt 28:69](https://ref.ly/Deut28:69), [29:8](https://ref.ly/Deut29:8), [2 K 23:3](https://ref.ly/2Kgs23:3) (\= [2 Ch 34:31](https://ref.ly/2Chr34:31)) [Je 11:2–8](https://ref.ly/Jer11:2-Jer11:8); written in ספר הברית *the book of the covenant* [Ex 24:7](https://ref.ly/Exod24:7) (E. cf. [34:27](https://ref.ly/Exod34:27) J) [2 K 23:2](https://ref.ly/2Kgs23:2), [23:21](https://ref.ly/2Kgs23:21) (cf. [2 Ch 34:30](https://ref.ly/2Chr34:30)). **d.** *with Phinehas* [Nu 25:12](https://ref.ly/Num25:12), [25:13](https://ref.ly/Num25:13) (P), a constitution, establishing an everlasting priesthood in his line; cf. ברית הכהנים [Ne 13:29](https://ref.ly/Neh13:29) \& ברית הלוי [Mal 2:4](https://ref.ly/Mal2:4), [2:8](https://ref.ly/Mal2:8). **e.** *with Joshua and Israel* [Jos 24:25](https://ref.ly/Josh24:25) (E), an ordinance or constitutional agreement to serve Yahweh only. **f.** *with David* [Psalm 89:4](https://ref.ly/Ps89:4), [89:29](https://ref.ly/Ps89:29), [89:34](https://ref.ly/Ps89:34), [89:39](https://ref.ly/Ps89:39), [132:12](https://ref.ly/Ps132:12), [Je 33:21](https://ref.ly/Jer33:21) (cf. 2 S 7 \= 1 Ch 17); a divine promise to the seed of David of an everlasting kingdom, the relation of sonship, and the superintendence of the temple (cf. Psalm 2.) **g.** *Jehoiada and the people* [2 K 11:17](https://ref.ly/2Kgs11:17) \= [2 Ch 23:3](https://ref.ly/2Chr23:3), a constitutional agreement to be the people of Yahweh. **h.** *Hezekiah and the people* [2 Ch 29:10](https://ref.ly/2Chr29:10), a constitutional agreement to reform the worship. **i.** *Josiah and the people* [2 K 23:3](https://ref.ly/2Kgs23:3), a constitutional agreement to obey the book of the covenant. **j.** *Ezra and the people* [Ezr 10:3](https://ref.ly/Ezra10:3), a constitutional agreement to put away foreign wives and observe the Law. **k.** *the prophetic covenant*, a divine promise through a series of prophets to establish a new constitution ברית חדשׁה [Je 31:31](https://ref.ly/Jer31:31), with new institutions and precepts [Is 42:6](https://ref.ly/Isa42:6), [49:8](https://ref.ly/Isa49:8), [55:3](https://ref.ly/Isa55:3), [59:21](https://ref.ly/Isa59:21), [61:8](https://ref.ly/Isa61:8), [Je 31:31](https://ref.ly/Jer31:31), [31:33](https://ref.ly/Jer31:33), [32:40](https://ref.ly/Jer32:40), [50:5](https://ref.ly/Jer50:5), Ez [16:60](https://ref.ly/Ezek16:60), [16:62](https://ref.ly/Ezek16:62), [20:37](https://ref.ly/Ezek20:37), [34:25](https://ref.ly/Ezek34:25), [37:26](https://ref.ly/Ezek37:26), [Ho 2:20](https://ref.ly/Hos2:20). ## ברז (id: BDB01452)

ברז (? cf. Aram. כרז *bore, pierce*, or Ar. بَرَزَ, *appear*).

> \[Str1269] † ברזות **n.pr.f.** (?) descend. of Asher (perh. בִּרְזָא *foramen*, Thes, or بَرْزَةٌ woman whose beauties are *apparent*) [1 Ch 7:31](https://ref.ly/1Chr7:31) Kt (Qr בִּרְָ\+זיִת).

## בָּרַךְ (id: BDB01468)

but rather a blessing overdone and so really a curse as in vulgar English as well as in the Shemitic cognates: [1 K 21:10](https://ref.ly/1Kgs21:10), [21:13](https://ref.ly/1Kgs21:13), [Jb 1:5](https://ref.ly/Job1:5), [1:11](https://ref.ly/Job1:11), [2:5](https://ref.ly/Job2:5), [2:9](https://ref.ly/Job2:9), [Psalm 10:3](https://ref.ly/Ps10:3). **Pu.** *Impf.* יְבֹרַח [2 S 7:29](https://ref.ly/2Sam7:29) \+ 3 t.; —**1\.** pass. *to be blessed, adored:* ´שׁם י [Jb 1:21](https://ref.ly/Job1:21), [Psalm 113:2](https://ref.ly/Ps113:2) **2\.** *prospered by God:* **a.** persons [2 S 7:29](https://ref.ly/2Sam7:29), [1 Ch 17:27](https://ref.ly/1Chr17:27), [Psalm 37:22](https://ref.ly/Ps37:22), [112:2](https://ref.ly/Ps112:2), [128:4](https://ref.ly/Ps128:4), [Pr 20:21](https://ref.ly/Prov20:21). **b.** things [Dt 33:13](https://ref.ly/Deut33:13). **3\.** *have prosperity invoked,* by Balaam [Nu 22:6](https://ref.ly/Num22:6). **4\.** *in gratitude* [Pr 22:9](https://ref.ly/Prov22:9), Ju [5:24](https://ref.ly/Judg5:24). **Hiph.** וַיַּבְרֵח חַגְּמַלִּים *and he made his camels kneel* [Gn 24:11](https://ref.ly/Gen24:11) (J). **Hithp.** הִתְבָּרֵח [Dt 29:18](https://ref.ly/Deut29:18) \+ 3 t.; *Impf.* יִתְבָּרֵח [Is 65:16](https://ref.ly/Isa65:16), [Psalm 72:17](https://ref.ly/Ps72:17); *Pt.* מִתְבָּרֵח [Is 65:16](https://ref.ly/Isa65:16); —*bless oneself,* congratulate onself בלבבו in his heart [Dt 29:18](https://ref.ly/Deut29:18); בזרעך with or by (cf. בְּ III. **2\. d**) thy seed (invoke for oneself the blessing of the see of Abraham) [Gn 22:18](https://ref.ly/Gen22:18), [26:4](https://ref.ly/Gen26:4) (J); by the Messianic king [Psalm 72:17](https://ref.ly/Ps72:17); באלהי אמן [Is 65:16](https://ref.ly/Isa65:16); by ´י [Je 4:2](https://ref.ly/Jer4:2).

> \[Str1290] † בֶּ֫רֶךְ **n.f.**:[Is. 45:23](https://ref.ly/Isa45:23) Knee (As. *birku* COTGloss; Eth. ብርክ፡ Aram. בֶּרֶךְ ܒܪܽܘܟ) [Is 45:23](https://ref.ly/Isa45:23); du. בִּרְכַּיִם [Jb 3:12](https://ref.ly/Job3:12) \+ 10 t.; sf. בִּרְכַּי etc. [Gn 30:3](https://ref.ly/Gen30:3) \+ 11 t.;—*knee, knees:* מים ברכים water reaching to the knees Ez [47:4](https://ref.ly/Ezek47:4); וַתְּנִיעֵנִי על ברכי [Dn 10:10](https://ref.ly/Dan10:10); יַכְּכָה בשׁחין רע על הברכים [Dt 28:35](https://ref.ly/Deut28:35); כרע על ברכים *kneel on knees* in worship [1 K 8:54](https://ref.ly/1Kgs8:54), [Ezr 9:5](https://ref.ly/Ezra9:5) cf. [Is 45:23](https://ref.ly/Isa45:23), [1 K 19:18](https://ref.ly/1Kgs19:18), [2 Ch 6:13](https://ref.ly/2Chr6:13), in entreaty [2 K 1:13](https://ref.ly/2Kgs1:13), to drink of a fountain Ju [7:5](https://ref.ly/Judg7:5), [7:6](https://ref.ly/Judg7:6); שם פניו בין ברכיו *put his face between his knees* in prayer [1 K 18:42](https://ref.ly/1Kgs18:42); על ברכים *upon the knees,* [Gn 30:3](https://ref.ly/Gen30:3) (E; on ´תֵּלֵד עַל ב v. PlossDas Weib Aufi. 2\. ii. 180 ff. StaZAW 1886, 143 ff.) [50:23](https://ref.ly/Gen50:23) (E) [Jb 3:12](https://ref.ly/Job3:12), Ju [16:10](https://ref.ly/Judg16:10), [2 K 4:20](https://ref.ly/2Kgs4:20), [Is 66:12](https://ref.ly/Isa66:12); הוציא מעם ברכים [Gn 48:12](https://ref.ly/Gen48:12) (E); knees as seat of strength, weak from terror [Jb 4:4](https://ref.ly/Job4:4), [Is 35:3](https://ref.ly/Isa35:3), Ez [7:17](https://ref.ly/Ezek7:17), [21:12](https://ref.ly/Ezek21:12), [Na 2:11](https://ref.ly/Nah2:11); or fasting [Psalm 109:24](https://ref.ly/Ps109:24).

> \[Str1293] † I. בְּרָכָה **n.f.** blessing (Ar. بَرَكَةٌ; Eth. በረከት፡ Aram. בִּרְכָּא ܒܘܪܟܬܳܐ; NH as Heb.)—´ב [Gn 12:2](https://ref.ly/Gen12:2) \+ 39 t.; cstr. בִּרְכַּת [Gn 28:4](https://ref.ly/Gen28:4) \+ 9 t.; sf. בִּרְכָתוֺ [Gn 49:28](https://ref.ly/Gen49:28) \+ 7 t.; pl. בְּרָכוֺת [Psalm 21:7](https://ref.ly/Ps21:7) \+ 4 t.; cstr. בִּרְכוֺת, בִּרְכֹת [Gn 49:25](https://ref.ly/Gen49:25) \+ 5 t.; sf. בִּרְכוֺתֵיכֶם [Mal 2:2](https://ref.ly/Mal2:2); —**1\.** *blessing:* **a.** *of parent* [Gn 27:12](https://ref.ly/Gen27:12), [27:41](https://ref.ly/Gen27:41), [49:28](https://ref.ly/Gen49:28) (JE), of Moses [Dt 33:1](https://ref.ly/Deut33:1). **b.** *of God* [Ex 32:29](https://ref.ly/Exod32:29) (E) LV [25:21](https://ref.ly/Exod25:21) (P) [Dt 11:26](https://ref.ly/Deut11:26), [11:27](https://ref.ly/Deut11:27), [11:29](https://ref.ly/Deut11:29), [23:6](https://ref.ly/Deut23:6) (\= [Ne 13:2](https://ref.ly/Neh13:2)) [28:2](https://ref.ly/Deut28:2), [28:8](https://ref.ly/Deut28:8), [30:1](https://ref.ly/Deut30:1), [30:19](https://ref.ly/Deut30:19), [Jos 8:34](https://ref.ly/Josh8:34) (D) [2 S 7:29](https://ref.ly/2Sam7:29), [Psalm 3:9](https://ref.ly/Ps3:9), [21:4](https://ref.ly/Ps21:4), [133:3](https://ref.ly/Ps133:3), [Is 44:3](https://ref.ly/Isa44:3), Ez [34:26](https://ref.ly/Ezek34:26), [44:30](https://ref.ly/Ezek44:30), [Jo 2:14](https://ref.ly/Joel2:14), [Mal 3:10](https://ref.ly/Mal3:10); ברכת יהוה [Gn 39:5](https://ref.ly/Gen39:5) (J) [Dt 12:15](https://ref.ly/Deut12:15), [16:17](https://ref.ly/Deut16:17), [33:23](https://ref.ly/Deut33:23), [Psalm 129:8](https://ref.ly/Ps129:8), [Pr 10:22](https://ref.ly/Prov10:22); ´ברכה מאת י [Psalm 24:5](https://ref.ly/Ps24:5); ברכת אברהם [Gn 28:4](https://ref.ly/Gen28:4) (P), the blessing given to Abraham. **c.** *of the people,* in recognition of good men [Psalm 109:17](https://ref.ly/Ps109:17), [Pr 10:6](https://ref.ly/Prov10:6), [11:26](https://ref.ly/Prov11:26), [24:25](https://ref.ly/Prov24:25), [28:20](https://ref.ly/Prov28:20). **d.** *of a poor man,* in recognition of benefits [Jb 29:13](https://ref.ly/Job29:13). **2\.** *source of blessing:* Abraham [Gn 12:2](https://ref.ly/Gen12:2) (J); Israel [Is 19:24](https://ref.ly/Isa19:24), Ez [34:26](https://ref.ly/Ezek34:26), [Zc 8:13](https://ref.ly/Zech8:13); seed of the righteous [Psalm 37:26](https://ref.ly/Ps37:26); the king [Psalm 21:7](https://ref.ly/Ps21:7); memory of the righteous [Pr 10:7](https://ref.ly/Prov10:7); new wine [Is 65:8](https://ref.ly/Isa65:8). **3\.** *blessing, prosperity:* בברכת ישׁרים *by the prosperity of the upright* (the city is exalted) [Pr 11:11](https://ref.ly/Prov11:11); גם ברכות יעטה מורה *yea, the early rain covereth with blessings* [Psalm 84:7](https://ref.ly/Ps84:7) cf. [Gn 49:25](https://ref.ly/Gen49:25), [49:26](https://ref.ly/Gen49:26); וארותי את ברכותיכם *and I will curse your prosperity* [Mal 2:2](https://ref.ly/Mal2:2). **4\.** *blessing, praise of God* [Ne 9:5](https://ref.ly/Neh9:5). **5\.** *a gift, present* [Gn 33:11](https://ref.ly/Gen33:11) (E) [Jos 15:19](https://ref.ly/Josh15:19) (J) Ju [1:15](https://ref.ly/Judg1:15), [1 S 25:27](https://ref.ly/1Sam25:27), [30:26](https://ref.ly/1Sam30:26), [2 K 5:15](https://ref.ly/2Kgs5:15); נֶפֶשׁ בְּרָכָה *a liberal person* [Pr 11:25](https://ref.ly/Prov11:25) (cf. Syr. ܒܽܘܪܟܳܐ, Eth. በረከት፡). **6\.** *treaty of peace* [2 K 18:31](https://ref.ly/2Kgs18:31) \= [Is 36:16](https://ref.ly/Isa36:16).

> \[Str1294] † II. בְּרָכָה **1\. n. pr. loc.** valley in wilderness by Tekoa [2 Ch 20:26](https://ref.ly/2Chr20:26), [20:26](https://ref.ly/2Chr20:26); mod. *Bereikût* cf. Be \& reff. **2\. n.pr.m.** one of David's band [1 Ch 12:3](https://ref.ly/1Chr12:3).

> \[Str1263] † בָּרוּךְ **n.pr.m.** (*blessed*) **1\.** friend and amanuensis of Jeremiah [Je 32:12](https://ref.ly/Jer32:12), [32:13](https://ref.ly/Jer32:13), [32:16](https://ref.ly/Jer32:16), [36:4–32](https://ref.ly/Jer36:4-Jer36:32), [43:3](https://ref.ly/Jer43:3), [43:6](https://ref.ly/Jer43:6), [45:1](https://ref.ly/Jer45:1), [45:2](https://ref.ly/Jer45:2). **2\.** a priest, son of Zabbai (Zaccai) [Ne 3:20](https://ref.ly/Neh3:20), [10:7](https://ref.ly/Neh10:7). **3\.** son of Colhozeh, of the tribe of Judah [Ne 11:5](https://ref.ly/Neh11:5).

> \[Str1295] † בְּרֵכָה **n.f.** pool, pond (הברכה SI5; Ar. بِرْكَةٌ; Sab. ברכת Sab. Denkm.:73; Aram. בְּרֵיכְתָּא)—´ב [2 S 2:13](https://ref.ly/2Sam2:13), [2:13](https://ref.ly/2Sam2:13), [4:12](https://ref.ly/2Sam4:12), [2 K 18:17](https://ref.ly/2Kgs18:17) (\= [Is 36:2](https://ref.ly/Isa36:2)) [20:20](https://ref.ly/2Kgs20:20), [Ne 3:16](https://ref.ly/Neh3:16), [Is 7:3](https://ref.ly/Isa7:3), [22:9](https://ref.ly/Isa22:9), [22:11](https://ref.ly/Isa22:11); cstr. בְּרֵכַת [2 S 2:13](https://ref.ly/2Sam2:13), [1 K 22:38](https://ref.ly/1Kgs22:38), [Ne 2:14](https://ref.ly/Neh2:14), [3:15](https://ref.ly/Neh3:15), [Na 2:9](https://ref.ly/Nah2:9); pl. בְּרֵכוֺת [Ec 2:6](https://ref.ly/Eccl2:6), Ct [7:5](https://ref.ly/Song7:5).

> \[Str1292] † בָּרַכְאֵל **n.pr.m.** (*Ēl doth bless*, cf. Ph. ברכבעל, Palm. בלברך Vog117, Bab. *Bariki\-ili* OppJas 1887 Nov.\-Dec., 596 f.) father of Elihu [Jb 32:2](https://ref.ly/Job32:2), [32:6](https://ref.ly/Job32:6).

> \[Str3000] † יְבֶרֶכְיָ֫הוּ **n.pr.m.** (\= יְבָרֶכְיָהוּ s. *Yah blesseth* Ges§ 27, 3\) father of a Zechariah in Isaiah's time [Is 8:2](https://ref.ly/Isa8:2); usually in abbreviated form as foll.: בֶּרֶכְיָה **1\.** son of Zerubbabel [1 Ch 3:20](https://ref.ly/1Chr3:20). **2\.** a Levite gruard of the ark [1 Ch 9:16](https://ref.ly/1Chr9:16), [15:23](https://ref.ly/1Chr15:23). **3\.** father of Meshullam, one of Nehemian's chiefs [Ne 3:4](https://ref.ly/Neh3:4), [3:30](https://ref.ly/Neh3:30), [6:18](https://ref.ly/Neh6:18). **4\.** father of the prophet Zechariah [Zc 1:1](https://ref.ly/Zech1:1) \= בֶּרֶכְיָהוּ v:[7](https://ref.ly/Zech1:7); בֶּרֶכְיָהוּ also **5\.** father of Asaph [1 Ch 6:24](https://ref.ly/1Chr6:24), [15:17](https://ref.ly/1Chr15:17). **6\.** Ephraimite chief [2 Ch 28:12](https://ref.ly/2Chr28:12).

> \[Str1296] בֶּרֶכְיָה, בֶּרֶכְיָהוּ v. יְבֶרֶכְיָהוּ supra.

## ברם (id: BDB01477)

ברם (cf. ar. بَرَمَ *twist a rope of two strands*).

> \[Str1264] † בְּרֹמִים **n.\[m.]** variegated cloth (Ar. بَرِيمٌ *rope* (or fabric) *of two strands* or *colours;* cf. As. *birmu*, a kind of clothing COTGloss, *burmu, iris,* ZimBP 82; on *burūmu* cf. JenKosmol. 6 ff.) וּבְגִנְזֵי בְּרֹמִים Ez [27:24](https://ref.ly/Ezek27:24).

> \[Str6947] בַּרְנֵעַ cf. ´קדשׁ ב. p. 873

> \[Str1298] † בֶּ֫רַע **n.pr.m.** king of Sodom [Gn 14:2](https://ref.ly/Gen14:2) (√ unknown; LXX Βαλλα).

> \[Str1284] † בְּרִיעִי **adj. gent.** c. art. as n. coll. משׁפחת ´הַבְּ [Nu 26:44](https://ref.ly/Num26:44).

## בָּרַק (id: BDB01483)

\[Str1299] † בָּרַק **vb.** flash, of lightning (Ar. بَرَقَ *gleam, flash, lighten,* As. *barâḳy* ZimBP 76, Aram. בְּרַק ܒܪܰܩ, Eth. በረቀ፡ Sab. ברק Hal252 cf. DHMZMG 1875, 597\)—**Qal** *Pf.* בָּרַק so rd. after LXXL [2 S 22:15](https://ref.ly/2Sam22:15) \& also in ‖ [Psalm 18:15](https://ref.ly/Ps18:15) (cf. Klo Che crit. n.); *Imv.* בְּרֹק [Psalm 144:6](https://ref.ly/Ps144:6); —all c.acc. cogn. בָּרָק, בְּרָקִים;—*flash*, trans. *flash lightning*, subj. ´י.

> \[Str1300] † בָּרָק **n.m.**:Ez [1:13](https://ref.ly/Ezek1:13) lightning (chiefly in poetry) (Ar. بَرْقٌ, As. *birḳu* COTGloss, ZimBP 76, 82 Aram. בְּרַק, ܒܰܪܩܳܐ)—abs. ´ב [Jb 20:25](https://ref.ly/Job20:25) \+ 7 t. [2 S 22:15](https://ref.ly/2Sam22:15) (cf. infr.); cstr. בְּרַק [Dt 32:41](https://ref.ly/Deut32:41) \+ 2 t.; pl. בְּרָקִים [Ex 19:16](https://ref.ly/Exod19:16) \+ 7 t., בְּרָקָיו [Psalm 97:4](https://ref.ly/Ps97:4); —*lightning,* **1\.** lit. mostly pl. \= *lightnings, lightning\-flashes* [Ex 19:16](https://ref.ly/Exod19:16), [Psalm 18:15](https://ref.ly/Ps18:15) (in theoph., on [2 S 22:15](https://ref.ly/2Sam22:15) v. infr.) [77:19](https://ref.ly/Ps77:19), [97:4](https://ref.ly/Ps97:4), [135:7](https://ref.ly/Ps135:7), [Jb 38:35](https://ref.ly/Job38:35), [Je 10:13](https://ref.ly/Jer10:13), [51:16](https://ref.ly/Jer51:16); so in sim. of swift brightness [Na 2:5](https://ref.ly/Nah2:5), sg. only [Psalm 144:6](https://ref.ly/Ps144:6), [2 S 22:15](https://ref.ly/2Sam22:15) (where however rd. בְּרָקִים בָּרַק cf. בָּרַק—so LXXL Klo Che, cf. his crit. n. [Psalm 18:15](https://ref.ly/Ps18:15)), Ez [1:13](https://ref.ly/Ezek1:13) (in vision), and in sim. of brightness [Dn 10:6](https://ref.ly/Dan10:6); swift destruction [Zc 9:14](https://ref.ly/Zech9:14). **2\.** fig. (always sing.) of flashing arrow\-head [Jb 20:25](https://ref.ly/Job20:25), cf. חרב ´ב [Dt 32:41](https://ref.ly/Deut32:41), חנית ´ב [Na 3:3](https://ref.ly/Nah3:3), Hb [3:11](https://ref.ly/Hab3:11); cf. *glitter* of weapon [Ex 21:15](https://ref.ly/Exod21:15), [21:20](https://ref.ly/Exod21:20), [21:33](https://ref.ly/Exod21:33).

> \[Str1301] † בָּרָק **n.pr.m.** (*lightning\-flash*, cf. Pun. *Barcas,* surname of Hamilcar, cf. NeposHamilcar 1, 1; Sab. ברקם DHMZMG, 1875, 592; Palm. ברק VogPalm. 76\) son of Abinoam, \& leader of Israel Ju [4:6](https://ref.ly/Judg4:6), [4:8](https://ref.ly/Judg4:8), [4:9](https://ref.ly/Judg4:9), [4:10](https://ref.ly/Judg4:10), [4:12](https://ref.ly/Judg4:12), [4:14](https://ref.ly/Judg4:14), [4:14](https://ref.ly/Judg4:14), [4:15](https://ref.ly/Judg4:15), [4:16](https://ref.ly/Judg4:16), [4:22](https://ref.ly/Judg4:22), [5:1](https://ref.ly/Judg5:1), [5:12](https://ref.ly/Judg5:12), [5:15](https://ref.ly/Judg5:15).

> \[Str1139] בְּרַק cf. בני ברק. p. 122

> \[Str1304] † בָּרֶ֫קֶת **n.f.** a precious stone, emerald, acc. to LXX Vulgate Josephus; (from *flashing, sparkling;* LagRel. Jur. Eccl. x. comp. Skr. *markata*, Gk. μάραγδος, ζμύραγδος) [Ex 28:17](https://ref.ly/Exod28:17), [39:10](https://ref.ly/Exod39:10) (both P).

> \[Str1304] † בָּֽרְקַת **n.f.** id., Ez [28:13](https://ref.ly/Ezek28:13), cf. Ges§ 80 R. 2 PinskEinl. 73\.

> \[Str1302] † בַּרְקוֺס **n.pr.m.** (√ \& mng. unknown; Bab. *Barḳûsu* DlPr 212\) head of a family of Nethinim, ´בני־ב [Ezr 2:53](https://ref.ly/Ezra2:53) \= [Ne 7:55](https://ref.ly/Neh7:55).

> \[Str1303] † בַּרְקָנִים **n.m.pl.** briers (so Vrss Ki al., also Stu q.v., Be, √ unknown; cf. ‖קוֺצִים; \> J. D. Michaelis, Thes al. *threshing\-sledges,* furnished with sharp (*glittering*) stones) Ju [8:7](https://ref.ly/Judg8:7) וְדַשְׁתִּ֫י ´אֶת־בְּשַרְכֶם אֶת־קוֺצֵי הַמִּדְבָּר וְאֶת־הַבּ (vid. also דושׁ)  *and I will thresh your flesh together with the thorns of the wilderness and the briers;* v:[16](https://ref.ly/Judg8:16) וַיֹּדַע ´וַיִּקַּח אֶת־זִקְנֵי הָעִיר וְאֶת־קוֺצֵי הַמִּדְבָּר וְאֶת הַבּ בָּהֶם אֵת אַנְשֵׁי סֻכּוֺת (rd. וַיָּדָשׁ for וַיֹּדַע LXX BuRS 114; cf. Stu Be) *and he took the thorns of the wilderness and the briers and threshed,* etc.

## בָּרַר (id: BDB01491)

\[Str1305] † \[בָּרַר] **vb.** purify, select (cf. As. *barâru, be shining,* in deriv. ZimBP 46\. 73 BelserBAS ii. 154; Ar. بِرٌّ *pious, kind, true;* بَرَّ *he was pious, good, virtuous, honest*)—**Qal** *Pf.* בָּרוֺתִי Ez [20:38](https://ref.ly/Ezek20:38); *Inf.* sf. לְבָרָם [Ec 3:18](https://ref.ly/Eccl3:18), Ges§ 67 R, 3; cf. לָבוּר [9:1](https://ref.ly/Eccl9:1) (si vera l.) as metaplastic form, but on text vid. בּוּר supra p. 101; *Pt.* pass. m. בָּרוּר [Jb 33:3](https://ref.ly/Job33:3) \+ 4 t.; f. בְּרוּרָה [Ne 5:18](https://ref.ly/Neh5:18), [Zp 3:9](https://ref.ly/Zeph3:9); —**1\.** *purge out, purify:* וּבָרוֺתִי מִכֶּם הַמֹּרְדִים *and I will purge out from among you the rebels* Ez [20:38](https://ref.ly/Ezek20:38); שָפָה בְּרוּרָה *a purified lip* [Zp 3:9](https://ref.ly/Zeph3:9); בָּרוּר מִלֵּלָוּ *utter in a pure, sincere manner* [Jb 33:3](https://ref.ly/Job33:3). **2\.** *choose, select,* only *Pt.* and in Chronicler: chosen, valiant men [1 Ch 7:40](https://ref.ly/1Chr7:40); porters [1 Ch 9:22](https://ref.ly/1Chr9:22); musicians [1 Ch 16:41](https://ref.ly/1Chr16:41); sheep [Ne 5:18](https://ref.ly/Neh5:18). **3\.** *cleanse, make shining, polish,* pt. pass. חֵץ בָּרוּר *polished arrow* [Is 49:2](https://ref.ly/Isa49:2) (cf. De \& [Je 51:11](https://ref.ly/Jer51:11) infr.) **4\.** *test, prove* [Ec 3:18](https://ref.ly/Eccl3:18) לְבָרָם הָאֱלֽהִים *that God may prove them* (RV);—on [9:1](https://ref.ly/Eccl9:1) v. בּוּר. **Niph.** הִבָּרוּ [Is 52:11](https://ref.ly/Isa52:11); *Pt.* נָבָר [2 S 22:27](https://ref.ly/2Sam22:27) (\= [Psalm 18:27](https://ref.ly/Ps18:27));—*purify oneself:* **a.** ceremonially, the bearers of the sacred vessels [Is 52:11](https://ref.ly/Isa52:11). **b.** morally [2 S 22:27](https://ref.ly/2Sam22:27) \= [Psalm 18:27](https://ref.ly/Ps18:27). **Pi.** *Inf.* לְבָרֵר [Dn 11:35](https://ref.ly/Dan11:35) *purify* (‖לִצְרוֺף, לַלְבֵּן). **Hiph. 1\.** *Inf.* לְהָבַר [Je 4:11](https://ref.ly/Jer4:11) *purify, cleanse.* **2\.** *Imv.* הָבֵרוּ הַחִצִּים [Je 51:11](https://ref.ly/Jer51:11) *polish arrows* (vid. **3** above). **Hithp. 1\.** *Impf.* יִתְבָּרֲרוּ [Dn 12:10](https://ref.ly/Dan12:10) *purify oneself.* **2\.** תִּתְבָּרַר [Psalm 18:27](https://ref.ly/Ps18:27) \= תִּתָּבָר [2 S 22:27](https://ref.ly/2Sam22:27) *shew oneself pure, just, kind.*

> \[Str1249] † II. בַּר **adj.** pure, clean, [Jb 11:4](https://ref.ly/Job11:4), [Psalm 24:4](https://ref.ly/Ps24:4); בָּר [Pr 14:4](https://ref.ly/Prov14:4); pl. cstr. בָּרֵי [Psalm 73:1](https://ref.ly/Ps73:1); f. בָּרָה [Psalm 19:9](https://ref.ly/Ps19:9), Ct [6:9](https://ref.ly/Song6:9), [6:10](https://ref.ly/Song6:10); —**1\.** *pure, clear:* בר לבב *pure in heart* [Psalm 24:4](https://ref.ly/Ps24:4); ברי לבב [Psalm 73:1](https://ref.ly/Ps73:1); a pure damsel Ct [6:9](https://ref.ly/Song6:9), [6:10](https://ref.ly/Song6:10), man [Jb 11:4](https://ref.ly/Job11:4), commands of God [Psalm 19:9](https://ref.ly/Ps19:9). **2\.** *clean:* אֵבוּס בָּר *crib is clean* [Pr 14:4](https://ref.ly/Prov14:4). **3\.** perh. adv. נַשְּׁקו־בַר *kiss purely,* of *sincere* homage [Psalm 2:12](https://ref.ly/Ps2:12) but cf. I. בַּר, p. 135\.
> 
> 141:a. I. בֹּר: [Is 1:25](https://ref.ly/Isa1:25) read prob. כַּכֻּר *as in a furnace* ([Is 48:10](https://ref.ly/Isa48:10)); there seems no evidence that ‘ lye ’ (i.e. water mixed with the burnt ashes of plants containing potash, used formerly for washing) was ever used in refining gold or silver: cf. J. NapierAnc. Workers in Metal (1856\), 15, 20, 25 WAM in Smith DB ii. 368; Amer. Rev. Ed. (1871, vol, iii), 1939\.

> \[Str1250] † III. בַּר **n.m.** grain, corn (cf. Ar. بُرٌّ *wheat, grain of wheat*), [Psalm 72:16](https://ref.ly/Ps72:16), [Am 5:11](https://ref.ly/Amos5:11), [8:5](https://ref.ly/Amos8:5), [8:6](https://ref.ly/Amos8:6); בָּר [Gn 41:35](https://ref.ly/Gen41:35), [41:49](https://ref.ly/Gen41:49), [42:3](https://ref.ly/Gen42:3), [42:25](https://ref.ly/Gen42:25), [45:23](https://ref.ly/Gen45:23) (E) [Psalm 65:14](https://ref.ly/Ps65:14), [Pr 11:26](https://ref.ly/Prov11:26), [Je 23:28](https://ref.ly/Jer23:28), [Jo 2:24](https://ref.ly/Joel2:24).

> \[Str1253] † I. בֹּר **n.m.** lye, potash, alkali used in smelting metals [Is 1:25](https://ref.ly/Isa1:25) (see בֹּרִית).

> \[Str1252] † II. בֹּר **n.m.** cleanness, pureness: בֹּר יָדַי *cleanness of my hands* [2 S 22:21](https://ref.ly/2Sam22:21) (\= [Psalm 18:21](https://ref.ly/Ps18:21)) [Psalm 18:25](https://ref.ly/Ps18:25) (\= בֹּרִי *my cleanness* [2 S 22:25](https://ref.ly/2Sam22:25)); בֹּר כַּף [Jb 9:30](https://ref.ly/Job9:30), [22:30](https://ref.ly/Job22:30).

> \[Str1287] † בֹּרִית **n.f.** lye, alkali, potash, soap, used in washing [Je 2:22](https://ref.ly/Jer2:22), [Mal 3:2](https://ref.ly/Mal3:2).

> \[Str1250] † בָּר **n.m.** field (BAram. בָּרָא, Syr. ܒܰܪܳܐ, Ar. بَرٌّ *open country, land*)—יִרְבּוּ בַבָּר *they grow up in the open field* [Jb 39:4](https://ref.ly/Job39:4) (Aram. usage).

> \[Str1257] † בַּרְבֻּרִים **n.m.pl.** birds fattened for table of Solomon [1 K 5:3](https://ref.ly/1Kgs5:3); *capons* Ki, *geese* (from their pure white feathers) TargumJer Thes, *swans* Ew, *guinea\-hens* Th, *fowls* Tristr. AV RV (ברברי \= ינשׁוף water\-birds [Lv 11:17](https://ref.ly/Lev11:17) TargumSam).
