## ברשׁ (id: BDB01499)

ברשׁ (√ of foll., meaning unknown).

> \[Str1265] † בְּרוֺשׁ **n.m.**:[Ho 14:9](https://ref.ly/Hos14:9) cypress or fir (As. *burâšu* COTGloss. Aram. בּרוֺתא, ܒܪܽܘܬܳܐ, cf. בְּרוֺת infr., v. also Gr. βράθυ, Lat. *bratum, cypress, juniper* (PS); in favour of *cypress,* also LXX SyrVer \& so Thes; in favour of *fir* or *pine,* Vulgate Rob in RobGes (because cypress not now indigenous on Lebanon); cf. also RSProph. iv. n. 20 who lays stress on Ph. n.pr.loc. אי בשם (\= איברושים) \= Gr. Πιτυοῦσαι i.e. *isle of firs;*—v. further SchroedPhön. Spr. 99 LöwNo. 59 \& *Brathu* as name of Hermon Philo Bybl. in EusebPraep. Evang. i. 10\)—בְּרוֺשׁ [Ho 14:9](https://ref.ly/Hos14:9) \+ 4 t.; בִּרוֺשִׁים [2 S 6:5](https://ref.ly/2Sam6:5) \+ 11: t., בְּרשִׁים [Na 2:4](https://ref.ly/Nah2:4), בְּרוֺשָׁיו [Is 37:24](https://ref.ly/Isa37:24), בְּרשָׁיו [2 K 19:23](https://ref.ly/2Kgs19:23); —**1\.** (lit.) a noble tree, usually ‖ארז (exc. [2 S 6:5](https://ref.ly/2Sam6:5), [1 K 6:34](https://ref.ly/1Kgs6:34), [2 Ch 3:5](https://ref.ly/2Chr3:5), [Psalm 104:17](https://ref.ly/Ps104:17), [Is 55:13](https://ref.ly/Isa55:13), [Ho 14:9](https://ref.ly/Hos14:9), [Na 2:4](https://ref.ly/Nah2:4)); as standing and growing [Is 14:8](https://ref.ly/Isa14:8), [37:24](https://ref.ly/Isa37:24) \= [2 K 19:23](https://ref.ly/2Kgs19:23), [Is 41:19](https://ref.ly/Isa41:19), [55:13](https://ref.ly/Isa55:13) (‖הֲדַס) [60:13](https://ref.ly/Isa60:13) (‖כבוד לבנון \= ארז) [Psalm 104:17](https://ref.ly/Ps104:17). **2\.** sim. of luxuriance, stateliness Ez [31:8](https://ref.ly/Ezek31:8), productiveness [Ho 14:9](https://ref.ly/Hos14:9) (Now thinks *cypress*), fig. for spear\-shafts [Na 2:4](https://ref.ly/Nah2:4) Hi\-St, but LXX SyrVer פרשׁים; fig. for mighty men [Zc 11:2](https://ref.ly/Zech11:2). **3\.** as material (always pl.), for building temple (´עצי ב) [1 K 5:22](https://ref.ly/1Kgs5:22), [5:24](https://ref.ly/1Kgs5:24), [6:34](https://ref.ly/1Kgs6:34) cf. [9:11](https://ref.ly/1Kgs9:11), [2 Ch 2:7](https://ref.ly/2Chr2:7); ´עץ ב (sing.) [2 Ch 3:5](https://ref.ly/2Chr3:5) (cf. Baer's n.); ´צלעות ב [1 K 6:15](https://ref.ly/1Kgs6:15); for ships (no עץ) Ez [27:5](https://ref.ly/Ezek27:5); —בְּכֹל עֲצֵי ברושׁים [2 S 6:5](https://ref.ly/2Sam6:5) appar. \= *with all musical instruments made of fir,* but ‖ [1 Ch 13:8](https://ref.ly/1Chr13:8) בְּכָל־עֹז וּבְשִׁירִים \& so here LXX We Dr.

> \[Str1266] † \[בְּרוֺת] **n.m.** id. (Aramaic (prob. North\- Palest.) form of same) only pl. בְּרוֺתִים ‖אֲרָזִים Ct [1:17](https://ref.ly/Song1:17); ref. to arbour of trees as their home, cf. אֶרֶז.

> \[Str1306] † בִּרְשַׁע **n.pr.m.** (√ \& meaning unknown) king of Gomorrha [Gn 14:2](https://ref.ly/Gen14:2).

> \[Str1307] בֵּרֹתַי, בֵּרֹתִי, בֵּר֫וֺתָה v. sub בארות, p. 92\.

## בשם (id: BDB01504)

בשם (*have a sweet odour,* cf. Aram. בְּסִים, ܒܣܶܡ *be sweet, pleasant,* Pa. ܒܰܣܶܡ *to delight,* בַּסִּימָא, *sweet;* Palm. בשימא (משחא) VogJAs 1883, Août. Sept. 153 1\. 12, 155 1\. 16, 18, 20 etc. *spiced* (*oil*), cf. ReckendorfZMG 1888, 403; As. *bašâmu,* Pa. *make fine, beautiful* COTGloss).

> \[Str1314,1313] בֶּ֫שֶם, \[בָּשָם] v. בּשֶֹׁם. below

> \[Str1314] † בּ֫שֶֹם **n.m.**:[1 K 10:10](https://ref.ly/1Kgs10:10) spice, balsam, balsam\-tree (Ar. بَش, Aram. בּוּסְמָא, ܒܶܣܡܳܐ, Gk. βάλσαμον; cf. Löw53)—´ב [Is 3:24](https://ref.ly/Isa3:24) \+, בֶּ֫שֶם [Ex 30:23](https://ref.ly/Exod30:23) v. infr., בְּשָמִי Ct [5:1](https://ref.ly/Song5:1) (as if fr. בָּשָם); pl. בְּשָמִים [Ex 25:6](https://ref.ly/Exod25:6) \+ 18 t., בְּשָמָיו Ct [4:16](https://ref.ly/Song4:16); —**1\.** *spice, perfume, sweet odour* [Is 3:24](https://ref.ly/Isa3:24); קִנְּמָן־בֶּשֶם [Ex 30:23](https://ref.ly/Exod30:23) (´ב perh. so pointed to distinguish it from) קְנֵה־בשֶֹׁם v:[23](https://ref.ly/Exod30:23); —with this cf. Aram. ܩܢܝܐ ܕܒܣܡܐ, קני בוסמא—*spices* as costly, token of wealth [2 K 20:13](https://ref.ly/2Kgs20:13) \= [Is 39:9](https://ref.ly/Isa39:9) cf. [2 Ch 32:27](https://ref.ly/2Chr32:27); royal gifts [1 K 10:2](https://ref.ly/1Kgs10:2), [10:10](https://ref.ly/1Kgs10:10), [10:25](https://ref.ly/1Kgs10:25) \= [2 Ch 9:1](https://ref.ly/2Chr9:1), [9:19](https://ref.ly/2Chr9:19), [9:24](https://ref.ly/2Chr9:24), cf. sg. [1 K 10:10](https://ref.ly/1Kgs10:10) \= [2 Ch 9:9](https://ref.ly/2Chr9:9); as article of commerce Ez [27:22](https://ref.ly/Ezek27:22); burnt at burial [2 Ch 16:14](https://ref.ly/2Chr16:14); appointed for ingredient of the anointing oil [Ex 25:6](https://ref.ly/Exod25:6), [35:8](https://ref.ly/Exod35:8) cf. v:[28](https://ref.ly/Exod25:28) (all P), cf. [1 Ch 9:30](https://ref.ly/1Chr9:30) (Palm. v. supr.); stored in temple [1 Ch 9:29](https://ref.ly/1Chr9:29); used for purifying the women of Ahasuerus [Est 2:12](https://ref.ly/Esth2:12); elsewhere only Ct [4:10](https://ref.ly/Song4:10), [4:14](https://ref.ly/Song4:14) *spices*, v:[16](https://ref.ly/Song4:16) *balsam\-juice*, gathered Ct [5:1](https://ref.ly/Song5:1); *balsam\-tree* ´הָרֵי ב Ct [8:14](https://ref.ly/Song8:14); *beds of balsam* ´עֲרֻגוֺת בּ Ct [6:2](https://ref.ly/Song6:2); cf. ´עֲרֻוּגַת בּ Ct [5:13](https://ref.ly/Song5:13) sim. of lover's cheeks.

> \[Str1315] † בָּֽשְמִת **n.pr.f.** (*perfume* ?)—**1\.** Hittite woman, a wife of Esau [Gn 26:34](https://ref.ly/Gen26:34) (P); called daughter of Ishmael, and sister of Nebaioth [Gn 36:3](https://ref.ly/Gen36:3) (but due prob. to R; this daughter of Ishmael is מַחֲלַת in [28:9](https://ref.ly/Gen28:9) P); v. also [36:4](https://ref.ly/Gen36:4), [36:10](https://ref.ly/Gen36:10), [36:13](https://ref.ly/Gen36:13), [36:17](https://ref.ly/Gen36:17) (all P) (Sam. has מחלת throughout Gn 36). **2\.** daughter of Solomon, wife of Ahimaaz [1 K 4:15](https://ref.ly/1Kgs4:15).

> \[Str3005] † יִבְשָם **n.pr.m.** a descendant of Issachar [1 Ch 7:2](https://ref.ly/1Chr7:2).

> \[Str4017] † מִבְשָם **n.pr.m. 1\.** a son of Ishmael [Gn 25:13](https://ref.ly/Gen25:13) \= [1 Ch 1:29](https://ref.ly/1Chr1:29). **2\.** a descendant of Simeon [1 Ch 4:25](https://ref.ly/1Chr4:25).

## בָּשַר (id: BDB01510)

\[Str1319] † \[בָּשַר] **vb.** bear tidings (√ *rub smooth the face;* cf. Ar. بَشَرَ *remove the face of surface of a thing*, cf. Ar. بَشِرَ *be glad, joyful;* بَشَّرَهُ بِمَوْلُودٍ *he rejoiced him with the message of the birth of a son;* Eth. በሰረ፡ *bring a joyful message*, so AS. *bussuru* (Pa.) DlPr 170, Sab. תבשר DHMMV, also בשר n.pr.dei,بشير DHMZMG 1883, 358, CISiv. 1, 41, 1\. 3\)—**Pi.** *Pf.* בִּשַּר [Je 20:15](https://ref.ly/Jer20:15) \+ 2 t.; *Impf.* תְּבַשֵּר [2 S 18:20](https://ref.ly/2Sam18:20) \+ 4 t.; אֲבַשְּרָה [2 S 18:19](https://ref.ly/2Sam18:19); *Imv.* בַּשְּרוּ [1 Ch 16:23](https://ref.ly/1Chr16:23) \= [Psalm 96:2](https://ref.ly/Ps96:2); *inf.* לְבַשֵּר [1 S 31:9](https://ref.ly/1Sam31:9) \+ 2 t.; *Pt.* מְבַשֵּר [Is 41:27](https://ref.ly/Isa41:27) \+ 6 t.; f. מְבַשֶּרֶת [Is 40:9](https://ref.ly/Isa40:9), [40:9](https://ref.ly/Isa40:9); pl. מְבַשְּרוֺת [Psalm 68:12](https://ref.ly/Ps68:12); —**1\.** *gladden with good tidings:* birth of a son [Je 20:15](https://ref.ly/Jer20:15); victory [1 S 31:9](https://ref.ly/1Sam31:9), [2 S 1:20](https://ref.ly/2Sam1:20), [1 Ch 10:9](https://ref.ly/1Chr10:9), [Psalm 68:12](https://ref.ly/Ps68:12); היה כמבשר בעיניו *he was in his eyes as a bearer of good tidings* [2 S 4:10](https://ref.ly/2Sam4:10). **2\.** *bear tidings* [2 S 18:19](https://ref.ly/2Sam18:19), [18:20](https://ref.ly/2Sam18:20), [18:20](https://ref.ly/2Sam18:20), [18:26](https://ref.ly/2Sam18:26); even of evil 1 S 4:l7, and so with acc. בשר טוב [1 K 1:42](https://ref.ly/1Kgs1:42). **3\.** *herald as glad tidings:* the salvation of God, *peach* (chiefly exilic usage) the advent of ´י in salvation [Na 2:1](https://ref.ly/Nah2:1), [Is 40:9](https://ref.ly/Isa40:9), [40:9](https://ref.ly/Isa40:9), [41:27](https://ref.ly/Isa41:27), [52:7](https://ref.ly/Isa52:7), [52:7](https://ref.ly/Isa52:7); the praises of Yahweh [60:6](https://ref.ly/Isa60:6); His righteousness in the great congregation [Psalm 40:10](https://ref.ly/Ps40:10); His salvation daily [Psalm 96:2](https://ref.ly/Ps96:2) \= [1 Ch 16:23](https://ref.ly/1Chr16:23); the Messianic servant preaches good tidings to the meek [Is 61:1](https://ref.ly/Isa61:1). **Hithp.** *Impf.* יִתְבַּשֵּר [2 S 18:31](https://ref.ly/2Sam18:31) *receive good tidings* (so Kirkp. Klo; cf. Ar. بَشِرَ IV. X; otherwise AV).

The flesh of the body is contrasted with stone Ez [11:19](https://ref.ly/Ezek11:19), [36:26](https://ref.ly/Ezek36:26). **2\.** *flesh* for *the body itself* (esp. in P): על בשר אדם לא ייסך *upon the body of man it shall not be poured* [Ex 30:32](https://ref.ly/Exod30:32) (P); מכנסי בד ילבשׁ על בשרו *linen drawers shall he put on his body* [Lv 6:3](https://ref.ly/Lev6:3), [16:4](https://ref.ly/Lev16:4) (P); וישם שק על בשרו [1 K 2:27](https://ref.ly/1Kgs2:27); ורחץ את בשרו *and he shall bath his body* [Lv 14:9](https://ref.ly/Lev14:9), [15:13](https://ref.ly/Lev15:13), [15:16](https://ref.ly/Lev15:16), [16:24](https://ref.ly/Lev16:24), [16:26](https://ref.ly/Lev16:26), [16:28](https://ref.ly/Lev16:28), [17:16](https://ref.ly/Lev17:16), [22:6](https://ref.ly/Lev22:6), [Nu 19:7](https://ref.ly/Num19:7), [19:8](https://ref.ly/Num19:8) (P); שרט לנפשׁ לא תתנו בכשרכם *ye shall not put any cutting for any one in your body* [Lv 19:28](https://ref.ly/Lev19:28) cf. [Lv 21:5](https://ref.ly/Lev21:5) (P); והעבירו תער על כל בשרם *and they shall pass a razor over all their body* [Nu 8:7](https://ref.ly/Num8:7) (P). Ec. uses בשר only in this sense [2:3](https://ref.ly/Eccl2:3), [4:5](https://ref.ly/Eccl4:5), [5:5](https://ref.ly/Eccl5:5), [11:10](https://ref.ly/Eccl11:10), [12:12](https://ref.ly/Eccl12:12); elsewhere this usage only in poetry; the body antith. to נפשׁ [Jb 14:22](https://ref.ly/Job14:22), [Is 10:18](https://ref.ly/Isa10:18), [Psalm 63:2](https://ref.ly/Ps63:2); לב [Psalm 16:9](https://ref.ly/Ps16:9), [84:3](https://ref.ly/Ps84:3), [Pr 14:30](https://ref.ly/Prov14:30) (only here emphatic pl. \= *enṭire body* Bö§ 695, *Leiblichkeit* De) ; מִבְּשָרִי *apart from my body*, in disembodied state [Jb 19:26](https://ref.ly/Job19:26); סמר מפחדך בשרי *my body trembleth for fear of thee* [Psalm 119:120](https://ref.ly/Ps119:120). **3\.** *make organ of generation* (euphemism): בְּשַר עָרְלַתְכֶם [Gn 17:11](https://ref.ly/Gen17:11), [17:14](https://ref.ly/Gen17:14), [17:23](https://ref.ly/Gen17:23), [17:24](https://ref.ly/Gen17:24), [17:25](https://ref.ly/Gen17:25) (P); בשר ערוה [Ex 28:42](https://ref.ly/Exod28:42) (P), but בשר [Gn 17:14](https://ref.ly/Gen17:14), [Lv 15:2–19](https://ref.ly/Lev15:2-Lev15:19) (P) Ez [16:26](https://ref.ly/Ezek16:26), [23:20](https://ref.ly/Ezek23:20), [44:7](https://ref.ly/Ezek44:7), [44:9](https://ref.ly/Ezek44:9). **4\.** *flesh for kindred, blood\-relations:* עצם מעצמי ובשר מבשרי *bone of my bone and flesh of my flesh* [Gn 2:23](https://ref.ly/Gen2:23) (J); והיו לבשר אחד *and they shall become one flesh* [Gn 2:24](https://ref.ly/Gen2:24) (J); עצמי ובשרי [Gn 29:14](https://ref.ly/Gen29:14) (J) Ju [9:2](https://ref.ly/Judg9:2), [2 S 5:1](https://ref.ly/2Sam5:1), [19:13](https://ref.ly/2Sam19:13), [19:14](https://ref.ly/2Sam19:14), [1 Ch 11:1](https://ref.ly/1Chr11:1); בשר with sf. in same sense [Gn 37:27](https://ref.ly/Gen37:27) (J) [Ne 5:5](https://ref.ly/Neh5:5), [Is 58:7](https://ref.ly/Isa58:7), for which שְׁאֵר בשרו *near of kin*, man or woman [Lv 18:6](https://ref.ly/Lev18:6), [25:49](https://ref.ly/Lev25:49) (both H; [25:49](https://ref.ly/Lev25:49) ‖מִשְׁפָּחָה, cf. RS K149). **5\.** *man over against God as frail* or *erring* [Gn 6:3](https://ref.ly/Gen6:3) (J) [Psalm 56:5](https://ref.ly/Ps56:5), [78:39](https://ref.ly/Ps78:39); eyes of flesh [Jb 10:4](https://ref.ly/Job10:4); arm of flesh [2 Ch 32:8](https://ref.ly/2Chr32:8), [Je 17:5](https://ref.ly/Jer17:5); horses are flesh not spirit [Is 31:3](https://ref.ly/Isa31:3) **6\.** the phrase כָּל־בָּשָר: **a.** *all living beings* [Gn 6:17](https://ref.ly/Gen6:17), [6:19](https://ref.ly/Gen6:19), [7:21](https://ref.ly/Gen7:21), [9:11](https://ref.ly/Gen9:11), [9:15](https://ref.ly/Gen9:15), [9:16](https://ref.ly/Gen9:16), [9:17](https://ref.ly/Gen9:17), [Lv 17:14](https://ref.ly/Lev17:14), [Nu 18:15](https://ref.ly/Num18:15) (P) [Jb 34:15](https://ref.ly/Job34:15), [Psalm 136:25](https://ref.ly/Ps136:25). **b.** *animals* [Gn 7:15](https://ref.ly/Gen7:15), [7:16](https://ref.ly/Gen7:16), [8:17](https://ref.ly/Gen8:17) (P). **c.** *mankind* [Gn 6:12](https://ref.ly/Gen6:12), [6:18](https://ref.ly/Gen6:18), [Nu 16:22](https://ref.ly/Num16:22), [27:16](https://ref.ly/Num27:16) (P) [Dt 5:28](https://ref.ly/Deut5:28), [Psalm 65:3](https://ref.ly/Ps65:3), [145:21](https://ref.ly/Ps145:21), [Is 40:5](https://ref.ly/Isa40:5), [40:6](https://ref.ly/Isa40:6), [49:26](https://ref.ly/Isa49:26), [66:16](https://ref.ly/Isa66:16), [66:23](https://ref.ly/Isa66:23), [66:24](https://ref.ly/Isa66:24), [Je 12:12](https://ref.ly/Jer12:12), [25:31](https://ref.ly/Jer25:31), [32:27](https://ref.ly/Jer32:27), [45:5](https://ref.ly/Jer45:5), Ez [21:4](https://ref.ly/Ezek21:4), [21:9](https://ref.ly/Ezek21:9), [21:10](https://ref.ly/Ezek21:10), [Jo 3:1](https://ref.ly/Joel3:1), [Zc 2:17](https://ref.ly/Zech2:17); cf. כל בשר אישׁ [Jb 12:10](https://ref.ly/Job12:10).

> \[Str1308] † בְּשוֺר **n.pr.** of brook in Philistine territory; alw. נַחַל הַבְּשוֺר [1 S 30:9](https://ref.ly/1Sam30:9), [30:10](https://ref.ly/1Sam30:10), [30:21](https://ref.ly/1Sam30:21); LXX Βοσορ (v:[21](https://ref.ly/1Sam30:21) Βεανα, but LXXL Βοσορ); mod. *Wady Ṛazze* (Gaza) acc. to GuêrinJudêe ii. 213;—it empties into sea SW of Gaza. ## בָּשַׁל (id: BDB01514)

\[Str1310] † בָּשַׁל **vb.** boil, seethe (intr.), grow ripe (Aram. בְּשַׁל, ܒܫܶܠ *ripen*, Nas. ܒܫܰܠ *boil*, NSyr. ܒܵܫܸܠ *be boiled, cooked*, cf. As. *bašâlu* (*bašlu, cooked*) ZimBP 76\)—**Qal** *Pf.* בָּשַׁל [Jo 4:13](https://ref.ly/Joel4:13), בָּֽשְׁלוּ Ez [24:5](https://ref.ly/Ezek24:5); —*boil, cook* (intr.) Ez [24:5](https://ref.ly/Ezek24:5); *grow ripe* (of קציר) [Jo 4:13](https://ref.ly/Joel4:13); —**Pi.** *Pf.* 3 ms. sf. בִּשְּׁלָם [1 K 19:21](https://ref.ly/1Kgs19:21); וּבִשַּׁלְתָּ֫ [Ex 29:31](https://ref.ly/Exod29:31), [Dt 16:7](https://ref.ly/Deut16:7), בִּשְּׁלוּ [La 4:10](https://ref.ly/Lam4:10) \+ 2 t., וּבִשְּׁלוּ consec. [Zc 14:21](https://ref.ly/Zech14:21); *Impf.* 3 fs. וַתְּבַשֵּׁל [2 S 13:8](https://ref.ly/2Sam13:8); 2 ms. תְּבַשֵּׁל [Ex 23:19](https://ref.ly/Exod23:19) \+ 2 t., יְבַשְּׁלוּ Ez [46:20](https://ref.ly/Ezek46:20), [46:24](https://ref.ly/Ezek46:24), וַיְבַשְּׁלוּ [2 Ch 35:13](https://ref.ly/2Chr35:13), תְּבַשְּׁלוּ [Ex 16:23](https://ref.ly/Exod16:23), וַנְּבַשֵּׁל [2 K 6:29](https://ref.ly/2Kgs6:29); *Imv.* בַּשֵּׁל [2 K 4:38](https://ref.ly/2Kgs4:38), בַּשְּׁלוּ [Lv 8:31](https://ref.ly/Lev8:31), בַּשֵָּׁלוּ [Ex 16:23](https://ref.ly/Exod16:23); *Inf.* בַּשֵּׁל [1 S 2:13](https://ref.ly/1Sam2:13); *Pt.* מְבַשְּׁלִים Ez [46:24](https://ref.ly/Ezek46:24); —**1\.** *boil* (tr.) obj. om. [Nu 11:8](https://ref.ly/Num11:8), בָּשָר [1 S 2:13](https://ref.ly/1Sam2:13); גְּדִי [Ex 23:19](https://ref.ly/Exod23:19), [34:26](https://ref.ly/Exod34:26), [Dt 14:21](https://ref.ly/Deut14:21); נזיד [2 K 4:38](https://ref.ly/2Kgs4:38); of offerings, obj. הקדשׁים [2 Ch 35:13](https://ref.ly/2Chr35:13); obj. אשׁם Ez [46:20](https://ref.ly/Ezek46:20), זבח Ez [46:24](https://ref.ly/Ezek46:24) (no obj.) v:[24](https://ref.ly/Ezek46:24), cf. [Zc 14:21](https://ref.ly/Zech14:21) (no obj.); [Ex 16:23](https://ref.ly/Exod16:23), [16:23](https://ref.ly/Exod16:23) opp. אפה *bake.* **2\.** *cook*

 (general), obj. בשר [1 K 19:21](https://ref.ly/1Kgs19:21), [Ex 29:31](https://ref.ly/Exod29:31), [Lv 8:31](https://ref.ly/Lev8:31) (both P); obj. בֵּן [2 K 6:29](https://ref.ly/2Kgs6:29) cf. יֶלֶד [La 4:10](https://ref.ly/Lam4:10); hence also of הפסח [Dt 16:7](https://ref.ly/Deut16:7) (cf. Di; ‖ [Ex 12:8](https://ref.ly/Exod12:8), [12:9](https://ref.ly/Exod12:9) has צְלִי אֵשׁ opp. מְבֻשָּׁל בַּמַּיִם) [2 Ch 35:13](https://ref.ly/2Chr35:13) (בָּאֵשׁ), cakes (הלבבות) [2 S 13:8](https://ref.ly/2Sam13:8). **Pu.** *Pf.* 3 fs. בֻּשָּׁלָה [Lv 6:21](https://ref.ly/Lev6:21); *Impf.* 3 fs. תְּכֻשַּׁ־ל [Lv 6:21](https://ref.ly/Lev6:21); *Pt.* מְבֻשָּׁל [Ex 12:9](https://ref.ly/Exod12:9), [1 S 2:15](https://ref.ly/1Sam2:15); —*be boiled, sodden*, במים [Ex 12:9](https://ref.ly/Exod12:9) cf. [Lv 6:21](https://ref.ly/Lev6:21), [6:21](https://ref.ly/Lev6:21) (all P) [1 S 2:15](https://ref.ly/1Sam2:15). **Hiph.** *Pf.* הִבְשִׁילוּ *ripened, brought to ripeness* [Gn 40:10](https://ref.ly/Gen40:10) (a vine, its grape\-clusters). 

> \[Str1311] † בָּשֵׁל **adj.** cooked, boiled (As. *basûlu, ripe* COTGloss)—[Ex 12:9](https://ref.ly/Exod12:9) בָּשֵׁל מְבֻשָּׁל במים; f. בְּשֵׁלָה [Nu 6:19](https://ref.ly/Num6:19), (both P).

> \[Str4018] † מְבַשְּׁלוֺת **n.f.pl.** cooking\-places Ez [46:23](https://ref.ly/Ezek46:23) (cf. בֵּית הִמְַבַשְּׁלִים v:[24](https://ref.ly/Ezek46:24)).

> \[Str1312] † בִּשְׁלָם **n.pr.m.** (\= בֶּן־שְׁלָם *son of peace*? cf. sub בן) a Persian officer in Canaan [Ezr 4:7](https://ref.ly/Ezra4:7).

## בשׁן (id: BDB01518)

בשׁן (√ of foll. \= *smooth, soft*? cf. Ar. بَثْنَةٌ, *soft and smooth ground*).

> \[Str1316] בָּשָׁן:60 **n.pr.terr.m.**:[Dt 3:3](https://ref.ly/Deut3:3) (*smooth* (\& fertile) land) Bashan, first mentioned as kingdom of Og, E. of Jordan, stretching from stream Jabboḳ (thus including northern Gilead) northward to Hermon, between Gennesaret (W) and mts of Hauran (E); cf. Di [Nu 21:33](https://ref.ly/Num21:33) WetzstHauran 82; later a type of fertility;—usually c. art. ´הַבּ [Nu 21:33](https://ref.ly/Num21:33), [21:33](https://ref.ly/Num21:33), [32:33](https://ref.ly/Num32:33), [Dt 1:4](https://ref.ly/Deut1:4) \+ 30 t. Dt Jos; [1 K 4:13](https://ref.ly/1Kgs4:13), [4](https://ref.ly/1Kgs4:4); [19](https://ref.ly/1Kgs4:19), [2 K 10:33](https://ref.ly/2Kgs10:33), [1 Ch 5:11](https://ref.ly/1Chr5:11), [5:12](https://ref.ly/1Chr5:12), [5:16](https://ref.ly/1Chr5:16), [5:23](https://ref.ly/1Chr5:23), [6:47](https://ref.ly/1Chr6:47), [6:56](https://ref.ly/1Chr6:56), [Ne 9:22](https://ref.ly/Neh9:22), [Psalm 68:23](https://ref.ly/Ps68:23), [135:11](https://ref.ly/Ps135:11), [136:20](https://ref.ly/Ps136:20), [Je 22:20](https://ref.ly/Jer22:20), [50:19](https://ref.ly/Jer50:19) (‖הַכַּרְמֶל), [Mi 7:14](https://ref.ly/Mic7:14) (‖ *id.*), [Na 1:4](https://ref.ly/Nah1:4) (‖ *id.;* personif., subj. of אֻמְלַל), [Is 33:9](https://ref.ly/Isa33:9) (‖ *id.;* personif. subj. of נֹעֵר); הַר־בָּשָׁן (i.e. range of Hauran) [Psalm 68:16](https://ref.ly/Ps68:16), [68:16](https://ref.ly/Ps68:16) (called הַר־אלהים,—from a sanctuary there?—\& הַר־גַּבְנֻנִּים *many\-peaked mt.*); of stately trees ´אַלּוֺנֵי הַבּ [Is 2:13](https://ref.ly/Isa2:13) (still seen on western slope of mts. of Hauran, WetzstHauran 88\); ´אַלּוֺנֵי ב [Zc 11:2](https://ref.ly/Zech11:2) (fig. of prominent men); ´אַלּוֺנִים מִבּ Ez [27:6](https://ref.ly/Ezek27:6); of bulls ´אַבִּידֵי ב [Psalm 22:13](https://ref.ly/Ps22:13) (‖פָּרִים fig.); so ´בְּנֵי־ב Dt 32:l4 (\+ כָּ רִים וְאֵילִים also עַתּוּדִים, cf. בָּקָר \& צאֹן va; vid. בֵּן **1\. j.** (ν)); appar. more general is ´מְרִיאֵי ב Ez [39:18](https://ref.ly/Ezek39:18) (ref. to כֻּלָּם…אֵילִים כָּ רִים וְעַתּוּדִים פָּרִים); once of kine (fem., fig. of luxurious and haughty women of Samaria) [Am 4:1](https://ref.ly/Amos4:1) ´פָּאוֺת הַבּ.

> \[Str1328] † I. בְּתוּאֵל **n.pr.m.** (? \= מְתוּאֵל *man of God*) [Gn 22:22](https://ref.ly/Gen22:22), [22:23](https://ref.ly/Gen22:23), [24:15](https://ref.ly/Gen24:15), [24:24](https://ref.ly/Gen24:24), [24:47](https://ref.ly/Gen24:47), [24:50](https://ref.ly/Gen24:50) (J) son of Nahor, neph. of Abr., living in Aram\-Naharaim, in city of Nahor; [25:20](https://ref.ly/Gen25:20), [28:2](https://ref.ly/Gen28:2), [28:5](https://ref.ly/Gen28:5) (P) Aramaean of Paddan\-Aram.

> \[Str1329] בְּתוּל v. II. בְּתוּאֵל. above

## בָּתַק (id: BDB01534)

\[Str1333] † \[בָּתַק] **vb.** cut, cut off, cut down (As. *batâḳu*, COTGloss. ZimBP 104 n.; cf. also Ar. بَتَكَ *secuit, amputavit*)—only **Pi.** *Pf.* consec. וּבִתְּקוּח בחרבותם Ez [16:40](https://ref.ly/Ezek16:40) *and they shall cut thee to pieces with their swords*.

* **Associated Passages:** Ezekiel 16:40


## בָּתַר (id: BDB01535)

\[Str1334] † \[בָּתַר] **vb.** cut in two (Ar. بَتَرَ *cut off* prematurely; *extirpate by cutting*)—**Qal** *Pf.* בָּתָר [Gn 15:10](https://ref.ly/Gen15:10); **Pi.** *Impf.* וַיְבַתֵּר [Gn 15:10](https://ref.ly/Gen15:10) *id.*

> \[Str1335,1336] † \[בֶּ֫תֶר] **n.m.**:[Gn 15:10](https://ref.ly/Gen15:10) part, piece. **1\.** בִּתְרוֺ [Gn 15:10](https://ref.ly/Gen15:10); pl. sf. בְּתָרָיו [Je 34:18](https://ref.ly/Jer34:18), בִּתְרֵי הָעֵגֶל [Je 34:19](https://ref.ly/Jer34:19); always of halves of animals cut in two in making covenants. **2\.** הָרֵי בָּתֶר Ct [2:17](https://ref.ly/Song2:17) *mountains of cutting*, i.e. *cleft mountains* LXX Thes al.; or *of separation* (between us) Ew al.; acc. to WeProl. 415, Eng. Tr. 391 ´ב \= *malobathron*.—*Bether* as n.pr. AV RV.

> \[Str1338] † בִּתְרוֺן prob. **n.pr.terr.** (*cleft, ravine*) E. of Jordan; ´כָּל־הַבּ [2 S 2:29](https://ref.ly/2Sam2:29).

* **Associated Passages:** Jeremiah 34:18; Song of Songs 2:17; Jeremiah 34:19; Genesis 15:10; 2 Samuel 2:29


## בתת (id: BDB01538)

בתת (? *cut off, sever*, cf. Ar. ܐܬܓܐܝ, *secuit, resecuit, abrupit*).

> \[Str1324] † II. בַּת **n.m.**:Ez [45:14](https://ref.ly/Ezek45:14) (**f.**:[Is 5:10](https://ref.ly/Isa5:10)) bath (Thes al. fr. above √ in sense of *define, measure;* cf. τέμνω; LagOr. ii. 10 f. makes \= בדת, \= בד \+ fem. ת; cf. Syr. بَتَّ, instrument for pressing olives; cf. Epiphan. βάδος \= ἐλαιοτριβεῖον; \& βάδος also Hesych. JosAnt. viii. 2\. 9 but ed. Niese βάτους, βάτος—´בּ abs. [Is 5:10](https://ref.ly/Isa5:10) \+ 6 t.; (בָּת Ez [45:11](https://ref.ly/Ezek45:11)) cstr. Ez [45:10](https://ref.ly/Ezek45:10); pl. בַּתִּים [2 Ch 2:9](https://ref.ly/2Chr2:9), [2:9](https://ref.ly/2Chr2:9) \+ 3 t.;—a liquid measure \= אֵיפָה of dry measure, each being 1/10 חֹמֶר (q.v.) Ez [45:11](https://ref.ly/Ezek45:11), [45:11](https://ref.ly/Ezek45:11), [45:14](https://ref.ly/Ezek45:14), [45:14](https://ref.ly/Ezek45:14); also [Is 5:10](https://ref.ly/Isa5:10), [1 K 7:26](https://ref.ly/1Kgs7:26), [7:38](https://ref.ly/1Kgs7:38), [2 Ch 2:9](https://ref.ly/2Chr2:9), [2:9](https://ref.ly/2Chr2:9), [4:5](https://ref.ly/2Chr4:5) \+ Ez [45:14](https://ref.ly/Ezek45:14), [45:14](https://ref.ly/Ezek45:14) (4 t. in this v., Co del. *a* \& *d*). Ez [45:10](https://ref.ly/Ezek45:10) בַּת־צֶדֶק *a righteous* (right, accurate, full) *bath* (‖אֵיפַת־צֶדֶק). The actual size of bath (\= ephah) is appar. c. 40 litres (\= Attic *metretes* cf. JosAnt. viii. 2\. 9; \= 39\.39 lit. cf. BoeckhMetrol. Untersuch. 259 f.; v. also J. BrandisMünz\- Mass\- u. Gewichtswesen 29 f. SmithDict. Bible, art. Weights \& Measures RiHWB 934 f.).—1\. בַּת v. sub בֵּן.

> \[Str1327] † \[בַּתָּה] **n.f.** precipice, steep (as *cut off, abrupt*)—בְּנַחֲלֵי הַבַּתּוֺת [Is 7:9](https://ref.ly/Isa7:9) *in the ravines of the precipices*.

> \[Str1326] † בָּתָה **n.f.** end, destruction (for בַּתָּה, perh. on account of difference of meaning, perh. fr. analogy of כָּלָה with like sense; cf. Di)—וַאֲשִׁיתֵהוּ בָּתָה [Is 5:6](https://ref.ly/Isa5:6) *and I will make it* (the vineyard) *a destruction*, a waste, or (Che) *make an end of it*.

* **Associated Passages:** 1 Kings 7:38; 1 Kings 7:26; Isaiah 5:10; Isaiah 5:6; 2 Chronicles 2:9; Isaiah 7:9; Ezekiel 45:14; Ezekiel 45:11; 2 Chronicles 4:5; Ezekiel 45:10
